只根据下面显示的摘要判断。同话题不等于有人研究了这个问题。 中文为机器翻译的辅助阅读版，以英文原文为准——AI 裁判读的是英文原文， 两边看同一份材料，对比才成立。 答案会自动保存在本浏览器里，随时可以关掉再回来；全部做完点右上角 Export CSV 导出文件。
Judge only from the abstracts shown; same topic is not engagement. Answers autosave locally; press Export CSV when done.

标注手册 / Codebook — 点开看判断标准和例子

你看到的是：一个写于 2021 年之前的科研问题，加上最多 8 篇 之后发表的论文摘要。只根据这些摘要判断——不要上网查， 因为 AI 裁判当时看到的就是这几篇，你多用外部知识对比就不公平了。

You see a question written before 2021 and up to 8 abstracts published after it. Judge only from these abstracts — do not look anything up.

⚠️ 最容易犯的错：把「同话题」当成「有人研究了这个问题」。
摘要讲的是同一颗行星、同一个分子，不算。必须真的碰到这个问题要检验的东西， 或者碰到它依赖的那个假设。
The single most common error is confusing topic overlap with engagement.

例子 / Worked examples

问题：「HD 209458 b 大气里检索出的极低含水量，是真实的大气性质，还是检索方法造成的假象？」
摘要：三篇独立重新分析，都得出含水量接近太阳组成。
→ answered + refuted（问题解决了，而且是靠推翻它质疑的那个假设解决的）

同一个问题，但摘要：几篇测量别的热木星含水量的论文。
→ not_addressed（同话题、不同天体，跟这个检验无关）

问题：「不均匀的晨昏线会怎样系统性地影响检索出的丰度？」
摘要：几篇证明这种偏差普遍存在，但没有解决问题里问的那个具体情形。
→ partially_addressed + still_plausible

两个标签是独立的 / The two labels are independent

一个问题可以正因为它的假设被推翻而得到解决—— answered + refuted 是合理组合，不是矛盾。

信心度 / Confidence

1 = 没把握，2 = 一般，3 = 很有把握。该给 1 就给 1，低信心的条目会单独分析， 它们是有用的信息，不是失败。犹豫的地方请在 notes 里写一句你在纠结什么。

item_001 · sys_A
Analyses [2016koa..prop..367C] and [2017hst..prop15127B] of 55 CNC E's atmosphere reach potentially conflicting conclusions regarding CO: one reports "A detection of the extended atmosphere would be a first for super-Earths and could provide constraints on planetary formation and evoluti...", while the other reports "A non-detection of escaping hydrogen, as with 55 Cnc e and HD 97658b, would bring useful constraints on the nature of the planetary atmos...". Under what observational or methodological conditions can these results be reconciled, and which currently untested assumption drives the disagreement?
对55 CNC E大气的分析[2016koa..prop..367C]和[2017hst..prop15127B]在CO方面得出了可能相互矛盾的结论：一项报告称“对扩展大气的探测将是超地球的首次，并可为行星形成和演化提供约束……”，而另一项报告称“如同55 Cnc e和HD 97658b，未探测到逃逸氢将为行星大气性质带来有用约束……”。在什么观测或方法学条件下这些结果可以调和，目前尚未检验的哪个假设驱动了这种分歧？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] High-resolution transmission spectroscopy of the super-Earth 55 Cnc e

2021-10-00

The comparison of exoplanets with well-studied Solar System planets can provide answers to the fundamental questions regarding their evolutionary path. The study of exoplanet atmospheres via high-resolution transmission spectroscopy has shown great success, especially for Jupiter-type planets. In the last years, this study also continued with promising investigations towards the atmospheric characterization of terrestrial exoplanets. One possible target for such investigation is the super Earth-sized exoplanet 55 Cnc e, which is one of the most studied terrestrial exoplanets to date. Currently, there are two possible atmospheric scenarios for 55 Cnc e: Either there is a heavy-weighted envelope with a significant amount of water or there is no gaseous envelope at all i.e. a rocky surface similar to Mercury, of our Solar System.Here, we present high-resolution (R = 120 000) transit observation of this planet, acquired with the PEPSI instrument at the Large Binocular Telescope, aiming to search for absorption signatures from various species in the puzzling atmosphere of 55 CNC e.

[1] 超级地球 55 Cnc e 的高分辨率透射光谱

将系外行星与太阳系中研究充分的行星进行比较，有助于回答其演化路径的基本问题。通过高分辨率透射光谱研究系外行星大气已取得巨大成功，尤其是类木星行星。近年来，这一研究也在向类地系外行星的大气特性探索方向取得了有前景的进展。55 Cnc e是一颗超地球大小的系外行星，也是迄今研究最充分的类地系外行星之一，目前对其大气存在两种可能情景：要么存在一个富含水的大质量包层，要么完全没有气体包层，即类似于太阳系中的水星那样的岩石表面。本文展示了利用大双筒望远镜上的PEPSI仪器获得的该行星高分辨率（R = 120 000）凌日观测，旨在搜索55 Cnc e大气中各种组分的吸收特征。

[2] A Detailed Investigation of HD 209458 b HST and JWST Transmission Spectra with SANSAR

2025-08-00

HD 209458 b is the first exoplanet on which an atmosphere was detected. Since then, its atmosphere has been investigated using multiple telescopes and instruments. However, many of its atmospheric constraints remain debatable. While Hubble Space Telescope (HST) observations suggested a highly subsolar metallicity, recent JWST NIRCam observations by Q. Xue et al. constrained a supersolar metallicity with highly subsolar C/O. In this work, we show a detailed investigation of HD 209458 b transmission spectra observations from JWST and HST using SANSAR, a newly developed planetary atmosphere modeling framework, with free, equilibrium chemistry and self-consistent grid retrievals. The overall best-fitting model with free retrievals (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>χ</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>red</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> = 1.21) constrains its metallicity and C/O to be highly subsolar, while equilibrium chemistry and grid retrievals (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>χ</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>red</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> = 1.27 and 1.30, respectively) are consistent with solar values using Space Telescope Imaging Spectrograph (STIS)+WFC3+NIRCam observations. The retrieved abundances of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO<SUB>2</SUB> are almost 3 orders of magnitude lower (highly subsolar) with STIS+WFC3+NIRCam compared to just NIRCam, using free retrievals. NIRCam observations alone also result in misleading constraints on metallicity and C/O, with equilibrium chemistry and grid retrieval. We find that the model choice of varying C/H or O/H to vary the C/O in equilibrium chemistry retrievals leads to different metallicity constraints with NIRCam, but similar constraints with STIS+WFC3+NIRCam. We conclude that NIRCam observations alone can lead to overestimation of abundances for exoplanet atmospheres and, therefore, should be used in combination with UV/optical and near-infrared observations to obtain robust constraints on abundances, C/O, and metallicity. In particular, even though we can detect the CO<SUB>2</SUB> feature with just NIRCam, we cannot constrain its abundances robustly without the optical baseline.

[2] 利用SANSAR对HD 209458 b的HST和JWST透射光谱的详细研究

HD 209458 b 是首个被探测到大气的系外行星。自那以后，其大气已通过多台望远镜和仪器进行了研究。然而，其许多大气约束仍存在争议。尽管哈勃空间望远镜（HST）观测表明其金属丰度远低于太阳，但最近 Q. Xue 等人利用 JWST NIRCam 观测约束其金属丰度高于太阳且 C/O 极低。在本工作中，我们利用新开发的行星大气建模框架 SANSAR，对 JWST 和 HST 的 HD 209458 b 透射光谱观测进行了详细分析，采用自由、平衡化学和自洽网格检索。整体上，自由检索的最佳拟合模型（χ_red^2 = 1.21）约束其金属丰度和 C/O 均远低于太阳，而平衡化学和网格检索（χ_red^2 = 1.27 和 1.30）则与利用 STIS+WFC3+NIRCam 观测得到的太阳值一致。利用自由检索，STIS+WFC3+NIRCam 得到的 H2O 和 CO2 丰度比仅用 NIRCam 时低近3个数量级（极低于太阳）。仅用 NIRCam 观测也会导致金属丰度和 C/O 的误导性约束（平衡化学和网格检索）。我们发现，在平衡化学检索中，通过改变 C/H 或 O/H 来调整 C/O 的模型选择，会导致 NIRCam 下金属丰度约束不同，但在 STIS+WFC3+NIRCam 下约束相似。我们得出结论，仅用 NIRCam 观测可能会高估系外行星大气的丰度，因此应与紫外/光学和近红外观测结合使用，以获得对丰度、C/O 和金属丰度的稳健约束。特别地，尽管仅用 NIRCam 可以探测到 CO2 特征，但没有光学基线无法稳健约束其丰度。

[3] Four HD 209458 b transits through CRIRES+: Detection of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and non-detections of C<SUB>2</SUB>H<SUB>2</SUB>, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, and HCN

2024-10-00

Context. HD 209458 b is one of the most studied exoplanets to date. Despite this, atmospheric characterisation studies yielded inconsistent species detections and abundances. Values reported for the C/O ratio range from ≍0.1 to 1.0. Of particular interest is the simultaneous detection of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and HCN reported by some studies using high-resolution ground-based observations, which would require the atmospheric C/O ratio to be fine-tuned to a narrow interval around 1. HCN has however not been detected from recent space-based observations. Aims. We aim to provide an independent study of HD 209458 b's atmosphere with high-resolution observations, in order to infer the presence of several species, including H<SUB>2</SUB>O and HCN. Methods. We observed four primary transits of HD 209458 b at a high resolution (ℛ ≍ 92000) with CRIRES+ in the near infrared (band H, 1.431243─1.837253 μm). After reducing the data with pycrires, we prepared the data using the SysRem algorithm and performed a cross-correlation (CCF) analysis of the transmission spectra. We also compared the results with those obtained from simulated datasets constructed by combining the Exo-REM self-consistent model with the petitRADTRANS package. Results. Combining the four transits, we detect H<SUB>2</SUB>O with a signal-to-noise CCF metric of 8.7σ. This corresponds to a signal emitted at K<SUB>p</SUB> = 151.3<SUB>−23.4</SUB><SUP>+31.1</SUP> km s<SUP>−1</SUP> and blueshifted by −6<SUB>−2</SUB><SUP>+1</SUP> km s<SUP>−1</SUP>, consistent with what is expected for HD 209458 b. We do not detect any other species among C<SUB>2</SUB>H<SUB>2</SUB>, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, CO, CO<SUB>2</SUB>, H<SUB>2</SUB>S, HCN, and NH<SUB>3</SUB>. Comparing this with our simulated datasets, this result is consistent with a C/O ratio of 0.1 and an opaque cloud top pressure of 50 Pa, at a 3 times solar metallicity. This would also be consistent with recent JWST observations. However, none of the simulated results obtained with a bulk C/O ratio of 0.8, a value suggested by previous studies using GIANO-B and CRIRES, are consistent with our observations.

[3] HD 209458 b的四次CRIRES+凌日观测：探测到H2O，未探测到C2H2、CH4和HCN

背景。HD 209458 b 是迄今为止研究最为深入的系外行星之一。尽管如此，大气特征研究在分子种类探测和丰度方面结果并不一致。已报道的 C/O 比值范围从约 0.1 到 1.0。特别值得关注的是，部分基于高分辨率地面观测的研究报告了 H2O 和 HCN 的同时探测，这要求大气 C/O 比值被精确调节到接近 1 的狭窄区间。然而，近期的空间观测并未探测到 HCN。目标。我们旨在利用高分辨率观测对 HD 209458 b 的大气进行独立研究，以推断包括 H2O 和 HCN 在内的多种分子的存在。方法。我们使用 CRIRES+ 在近红外（H 波段，1.431243─1.837253 μm）以高分辨率（ℛ ≍ 92000）观测了 HD 209458 b 的四次凌日。数据经 pycrires 处理后，采用 SysRem 算法进行预处理，并对透射光谱进行了互相关（CCF）分析。我们还将结果与结合 Exo-REM 自洽模型和 petitRADTRANS 软件包构建的模拟数据集进行了比较。结果。结合四次凌日观测，我们以 8.7σ 的信噪比 CCF 指标探测到 H2O。该信号对应于 Kp = 151.3−23.4+31.1 km s−1，蓝移 −6−2+1 km s−1，与 HD 209458 b 的预期一致。我们未探测到 C2H2、CH4、CO、CO2、H2S、HCN 和 NH3 等其他分子。与我们的模拟数据集比较，该结果与 C/O 比值为 0.1、云顶压强为 50 Pa、金属丰度为三倍太阳值的情形一致。这也与近期 JWST 观测结果相符。然而，采用 GIANO-B 和 CRIRES 先前研究建议的整体 C/O 比值 0.8 的所有模拟结果均与我们的观测不符。

[4] Can We Reconcile Space- and Ground-Based Atmospheric Interpretations of Two Classic Hot Jupiters? A comprehensive reanalysis of HD209458b and HD189733b using IGRINS2 spectrograph

2026-02-00

Hot Jupiters present a major challenge to planet formation theories, and their atmospheric compositions are keys to understanding their origins. Despite extensive observations from both space and ground based facilities, the molecular makeup of keystone hot Jupiters - HD 209458 b and HD 189733 b has long been a topic of intense debate. More importantly, these two planets occupy the moderate hot Jupiter regime (1000 - 1500 K), cooler than the ultra-hot (T &gt; 2000 K) planets where molecules dissociate but hotter than warm Neptunes where condensation dominates, placing them in the critical temperature range where species such as H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, HCN, and NH<SUB>3</SUB> can coexist—ideal for probing chemical transitions between oxygen-rich and carbon-rich atmospheres. I will present our campaign to revisit these classic exoplanets using the simultaneous high-resolution H- and K-band coverage of IGRINS2. Our observations target the dayside thermal emission mitigating the biases plaguing transit geometry such as limb asymmetry, Rossiter-McLaughlin effects and clouds. I will present the preliminary result of a strong H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO signals in HD209458b and an ongoing analysis of HD189733b with a strong H<SUB>2</SUB>O signal. Today, JWST is remarkable in detecting broadband exoplanet atmospheric features, but recovering crucial narrow band signals (e.g., CO) has proven to be challenging for planets with robust ground-based CO detection (e.g., WASP-18b, HD209458b). Our analysis of HD209458b and HD189733b further emphasizes the reasoning that ground-based observations are complementary to spaced-based facilities and possess unique advantages in providing unprecedented view of exoplanet atmospheres.

[4] 我们能否调和两颗经典热木星的空间与地面大气解释？利用IGRINS2光谱仪对HD209458b和HD189733b的综合再分析

热木星对行星形成理论提出了重大挑战，其大气成分是理解其起源的关键。尽管来自空间和地面设施的观测非常丰富，关键热木星 HD 209458 b 和 HD 189733 b 的分子组成长期以来一直存在激烈争议。更重要的是，这两颗行星处于中等温度热木星区间（1000-1500 K），比分子解离主导的超热行星（T > 2000 K）更冷，但又比冷凝主导的温暖海王星更热，正处于 H2O、CO、CH4、HCN 和 NH3 等物种可以共存的关键温度区间——非常适合研究富氧与富碳大气之间的化学转变。我将介绍我们利用 IGRINS2 同步高分辨率 H- 和 K-波段观测重新研究这些经典系外行星的计划。我们的观测针对日面热发射，减小了凌星几何带来的偏差，如边缘不对称、Rossiter-McLaughlin 效应和云的影响。我将展示 HD209458b 中强 H2O 和 CO 信号的初步结果，以及 HD189733b 的持续分析（同样有强 H2O 信号）。目前，JWST 在探测宽带系外行星大气特征方面表现出色，但对于那些地面已稳健探测到 CO 的行星（如 WASP-18b、HD209458b），恢复关键的窄带信号（如 CO）仍然具有挑战性。我们对 HD209458b 和 HD189733b 的分析进一步强调，地面观测与空间设施互为补充，并在提供前所未有的系外行星大气视角方面具有独特优势。

[5] The ESO SupJup Survey. III. Confirmation of <SUP>13</SUP>CO in YSES 1 b and Atmospheric Detection of YSES 1 c with CRIRES<SUP>+</SUP>

2024-12-00

High-resolution spectroscopic characterization of young super-Jovian planets enables precise constraints on elemental and isotopic abundances of their atmospheres. As part of the ESO SupJup Survey, we present high-resolution spectral observations of two wide-orbit super-Jupiters in YSES 1 (or TYC 8998-760-1) using the upgraded VLT/CRIRES<SUP>+</SUP> (<inline-formula> </inline-formula>) in K-band. We carry out free atmospheric retrieval analyses to constrain chemical and isotopic abundances, temperature structures, rotation velocities (<inline-formula> </inline-formula>), and radial velocities. We confirm the previous detection of <SUP>13</SUP>CO in YSES 1 b at a higher significance of 12.6σ, but point to a higher <SUP>12</SUP>CO/<SUP>13</SUP>CO ratio of 88 ± 13 (1σ confidence interval), consistent with the primary's isotope ratio 66 ± 5. We retrieve a solar-like composition in YSES 1 b with a C/O = 0.57 ± 0.01, indicating a formation via gravitational instability or core accretion beyond the CO iceline. Additionally, the observations lead to detections of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO in the outer planet YSES 1 c at 7.3σ and 5.7σ, respectively. We constrain the atmospheric C/O ratio of YSES 1 c to be either solar or subsolar (C/O = 0.36 ± 0.15), indicating the accretion of oxygen-rich solids. The two companions have distinct <inline-formula> </inline-formula>, 5.34 ± 0.14 km s<SUP>−1</SUP> for YSES 1 b and 11.3 ± 2.1 km s<SUP>−1 </SUP>for YSES 1 c, despite their similar natal environments. This may indicate different spin axis inclinations or effective magnetic braking by the long-lived circumplanetary disk around YSES 1 b. YSES 1 represents an intriguing system for comparative studies of super-Jovian companions and linking present atmospheres to formation histories.

[5] ESO SupJup巡天III：确认YSES 1 b中的13CO及利用CRIRES+探测YSES 1 c的大气

对年轻超木星行星进行高分辨率光谱特性研究，可以精确约束其大气中的元素和同位素丰度。作为ESO SupJup Survey的一部分，我们利用升级后的VLT/CRIRES+在K波段对YSES 1（或TYC 8998-760-1）系统中的两颗宽轨道超木星进行了高分辨率光谱观测。我们进行了自由大气检索分析，以约束化学和同位素丰度、温度结构、自转速度和径向速度。我们以更高的显著性（12.6σ）确认了此前在YSES 1 b中探测到的13CO，但指出12CO/13CO比值为88 ± 13（1σ置信区间），与主星的同位素比值66 ± 5一致。我们在YSES 1 b中检索到类太阳组成，C/O = 0.57 ± 0.01，表明其形成过程可能为引力不稳定或在CO雪线之外的核吸积。此外，观测还在外侧行星YSES 1 c中分别以7.3σ和5.7σ的显著性探测到H2O和CO。我们约束了YSES 1 c的大气C/O比为类太阳或亚太阳（C/O = 0.36 ± 0.15），表明其吸积了富氧固体。尽管两颗伴星的形成环境相似，但它们的自转速度明显不同，YSES 1 b为5.34 ± 0.14 km s−1，YSES 1 c为11.3 ± 2.1 km s−1。这可能表明自转轴倾角不同，或YSES 1 b周围长寿命的类卫星盘产生了有效的磁制动。YSES 1系统为超木星伴星的对比研究及将当前大气与形成历史联系起来提供了有趣的案例。

[6] The only known atmosphere on a rocky exoplanet?

2025-03-00

55 Cnc e is so far the only rocky exoplanet with convincing--though not uncontroversial--evidence of an atmosphere, in the form of a MIRI/LRS eclipse 8 sigma shallower than that of an airless zero-albedo world. It is also the only exoplanet with evidence of emission variability from multiple telescopes spanning multiple years. We propose to observe three eclipses of the planet with MIRI/MRS in the Medium sub-band. These observations will allow us to confirm the existence of an atmosphere, probe the wavelength dependence of its emission variability, and constrain its carbon dioxide abundance through the 15 um CO2 absorption feature. As an old, ultra-hot (Teq=2000 K), and ultra-short-period planet, 55 Cnc e may seem a-priori like a particularly hostile place for any gaseous envelope. Understanding whether and/or how such an envelope exists on 55 Cnc e, the most observationally favorable super-Earth, has strong implications for the survivability of rocky planet atmospheres more generally.

[6] 岩石系外行星上唯一已知的大气？

55 Cnc e 迄今是唯一有令人信服（尽管仍有争议）大气证据的岩石系外行星，其证据表现为 MIRI/LRS 食相深度比无大气零反照率模型浅 8 sigma。它也是唯一一个在多台望远镜、跨越多年观测中表现出发射可变性的系外行星。我们提议使用 MIRI/MRS 中波段观测该行星的三次食相。这些观测将使我们能够确认大气的存在，探测其发射可变性的波长依赖性，并通过 15 μm CO2 吸收特征约束其二氧化碳丰度。作为一颗年老、超高温（Teq=2000 K）、超短周期的行星，55 Cnc e 乍看之下似乎极不适合存在任何气体包层。理解 55 Cnc e 这样一颗观测条件极佳的超级地球是否以及如何拥有大气包层，对于岩石行星大气的存活性具有重要意义。

[7] No Escaping Helium from 55 Cnc e

2021-04-00

We search for escaping helium from the hot super-Earth 55 Cnc e by taking high-resolution spectra of the 1083 nm line during two transits using Keck/NIRSPEC. We detect no helium absorption down to a 90% upper limit of 250 ppm in excess absorption or 0.27 mÅ in equivalent width. This corresponds to a mass-loss rate of less than ∼10<SUP>9</SUP> g s<SUP>-1</SUP> assuming a Parker wind model with a plausible exosphere temperature of 5000-6000 K, although the precise constraint is heavily dependent on model assumptions. We consider both hydrogen- and helium-dominated atmospheric compositions and find similar bounds on the mass-loss rate in both scenarios. Our hydrodynamical models indicate that if a lightweight atmosphere exists on 55 Cnc e, our observations would have easily detected it. Together with the nondetection of Lyα absorption by Ehrenreich et al., our helium nondetection indicates that 55 Cnc e either never accreted a primordial atmosphere in the first place or lost its primordial atmosphere shortly after the dissipation of the gas disk. * The data presented herein were obtained at the W. M. Keck Observatory, which is operated as a scientific partnership among the California Institute of Technology, the University of California, and the National Aeronautics and Space Administration. The Observatory was made possible by the generous financial support of the W. M. Keck Foundation.

[7] 55 Cnc e 未探测到氦逃逸

我们利用Keck/NIRSPEC在两个凌星期间对1083 nm谱线进行高分辨率观测，搜索热超地球55 Cnc e的大气逃逸氦。我们未探测到氦吸收，90%上限为额外吸收250 ppm或等效宽度0.27 mÅ。假设Parker风模型和合理的外逸层温度（5000–6000 K），这对应的质量损失率小于约10^9 g s^-1，尽管具体约束高度依赖模型假设。我们同时考虑了以氢和氦为主的大气成分，发现两种情形下的质量损失率上限相似。我们的流体动力学模型表明，如果55 Cnc e存在轻质大气，我们的观测本应能轻松探测到。结合Ehrenreich等人未探测到Lyα吸收的结果，我们的氦未探测表明，55 Cnc e要么从未获得过原始大气，要么在气体盘消散后不久就失去了原始大气。* 本文所用数据由W. M. Keck天文台获得，该台由加州理工学院、加州大学和美国国家航空航天局合作运营，并由W. M. Keck基金会慷慨资助建成。

[8] A Nondetection of Iron in the First High-resolution Emission Study of the Lava Planet 55 Cnc e

2023-10-00

Close-in lava planets represent an extreme example of terrestrial worlds, but their high temperatures may allow us to probe a diversity of crustal compositions. The brightest and most well-studied of these objects is 55 Cancri e, a nearby super-Earth with a remarkably short 17 hr orbit. However, despite numerous studies, debate remains about the existence and composition of its atmosphere. We present upper limits on the atmospheric pressure of 55 Cnc e derived from high-resolution time-series spectra taken with Gemini-N/MAROON-X. Our results are consistent with current crustal evaporation models for this planet which predict a thin ~100 mbar atmosphere. We conclude that, if a mineral atmosphere is present on 55 Cnc e, the atmospheric pressure is below 100 mbar.

[8] 熔岩行星55 Cnc e首次高分辨率发射研究中未探测到铁

近轨道熔岩行星是类地世界的极端例子，但其高温可能使我们能够探测多样的地壳组成。其中最亮、研究最充分的是55 Cancri e，这是一颗距离较近、轨道周期仅17小时的超地球。尽管已有大量研究，关于其大气的存在与组成仍有争议。我们基于Gemini-N/MAROON-X高分辨率时序光谱，对55 Cnc e的大气压强给出了上限。我们的结果与当前针对该行星的地壳蒸发模型一致，该模型预测其大气为~100 mbar的稀薄大气。我们得出结论，如果55 Cnc e存在矿物大气，其大气压强低于100 mbar。

Do the planets in the GJ 9827 system exhibit atmospheric features (such as CO) that can be detected and characterized via transit spectroscopy, or are their atmospheres undetectable or ambiguous with current observational techniques?
GJ 9827 系统的行星是否表现出可通过凌日光谱探测和表征的大气特征（如 CO），还是以当前观测技术其大气不可探测或结果不明确？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] A Multiwavelength Look at the GJ 9827 System: No Evidence of Extended Atmospheres in GJ 9827b and d from HST and CARMENES Data

2021-03-00

GJ 9827 is a bright star hosting a planetary system with three transiting planets. As a multiplanet system with planets that sprawl within the boundaries of the radius gap between terrestrial and gaseous planets, GJ 9827 is an optimal target to study the evolution of the atmospheres of close-in planets with a common evolutionary history and their dependence from stellar irradiation. Here we report on the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and CARMENES transit observations of GJ 9827 planets b and d. We performed a stellar and ISM characterization from the ultraviolet HST spectra, obtaining fluxes for Lyα and Mg II of F(Lyα) = (5.42 ${}_{-0.75}^{+0.96}$ ) × 10<SUP>-13</SUP> erg cm<SUP>-2</SUP> s<SUP>-1</SUP> and F(MgII) = (5.64 ± 0.24) × 10<SUP>-14</SUP> erg cm<SUP>-2</SUP> s<SUP>-1</SUP>. We also investigated a possible absorption signature in Lyα in the atmosphere of GJ 9827b during a transit event from HST spectra, as well as Hα and He I signature for the atmosphere of GJ 9827b and d from CARMENES spectra. We found no evidence of an extended atmosphere in either of the planets. This result is also supported by our analytical estimations of mass loss based on the measured radiation fields for all three planets of this system, which led to a mass-loss rate of 0.4, 0.3, and 0.1 planetary masses per Gyr for GJ 9827b, c, and d, respectively. These values indicate that the planets could have lost their volatiles quickly in their evolution and probably do not retain an atmosphere at the current stage.

[1] GJ 9827 系统的多波段观测：HST 与 CARMENES 数据未发现 GJ 9827b 和 d 存在扩展大气

GJ 9827是一颗明亮恒星，拥有一个包含三颗凌星行星的行星系统。作为一个多行星系统，其行星半径分布跨越类地行星与气态行星之间的半径鸿沟，GJ 9827是研究近轨道行星大气演化及其对恒星辐射依赖性的理想目标。本文报告了GJ 9827 b和d的哈勃空间望远镜（HST）和CARMENES凌星观测。我们利用HST紫外光谱对恒星和星际介质进行了特性分析，获得了Lyα和Mg II的通量，分别为F(Lyα) = (5.42 ${}_{-0.75}^{+0.96}$ ) × 10^-13 erg cm^-2 s^-1和F(MgII) = (5.64 ± 0.24) × 10^-14 erg cm^-2 s^-1。我们还研究了HST光谱中GJ 9827b凌星期间大气中Lyα可能的吸收特征，以及CARMENES光谱中GJ 9827b和d大气的Hα和He I特征。我们未发现两颗行星存在扩展大气的证据。基于对该系统三颗行星测得辐射场的质量损失分析结果也支持这一结论，三颗行星的质量损失率分别为每十亿年0.4、0.3和0.1个行星质量。这些数值表明，这些行星在演化早期可能已迅速失去挥发物，目前阶段很可能不再保有大气。

[2] Double Trouble: Two Transits of the Super-Earth GJ 1132 b Observed with JWST NIRSpec G395H

2023-12-00

The search for rocky planet atmospheres with JWST has focused on planets transiting M dwarfs. Such planets have favorable planet-to-star size ratios, enhancing the amplitude of atmospheric features. Since the expected signal strength of atmospheric features is similar to the single-transit performance of JWST, multiple observations are required to confirm any detection. Here, we present two transit observations of the rocky planet GJ 1132 b with JWST NIRSpec G395H, covering 2.8-5.2 μm. Previous Hubble Space Telescope WFC3 observations of GJ 1132 b were inconclusive, with evidence reported for either an atmosphere or a featureless spectrum based on analyses of the same data set. Our JWST data exhibit substantial differences between the two visits. One transit is consistent with either an H<SUB>2</SUB>O-dominated atmosphere containing ~1% CH<SUB>4</SUB> and trace N<SUB>2</SUB>O ( ${\chi }_{\nu }^{2}=1.13$ ) or stellar contamination from unocculted starspots ( ${\chi }_{\nu }^{2}=1.36$ ). However, the second transit is consistent with a featureless spectrum. Neither visit is consistent with a previous report of HCN. Atmospheric variability is unlikely to explain the scale of the observed differences between the visits. Similarly, our out-of-transit stellar spectra show no evidence of changing stellar inhomogeneity between the two visits-observed 8 days apart, only 6.5% of the stellar rotation rate. We further find no evidence of differing instrumental systematic effects between visits. The most plausible explanation is an unlucky random noise draw leading to two significantly discrepant transmission spectra. Our results highlight the importance of multivisit repeatability with JWST prior to claiming atmospheric detections for these small, enigmatic planets.

[2] 双重麻烦：利用JWST NIRSpec G395H观测到的超级地球GJ 1132 b的两次凌日

利用 JWST 搜寻岩石行星大气的工作主要集中在凌日 M 矮星的行星。这类行星具有有利的行星-恒星尺寸比，增强了大气特征的幅度。由于大气特征的预期信号强度与 JWST 单次凌日观测性能相当，因此需要多次观测以确认任何探测结果。本文报告了 JWST NIRSpec G395H 对岩石行星 GJ 1132 b 的两次凌日观测，波段覆盖 2.8-5.2 μm。此前 Hubble Space Telescope WFC3 对 GJ 1132 b 的观测结果并不确定，同一数据集的分析报告了大气存在或无特征光谱。我们的 JWST 数据在两次观测之间表现出显著差异。一次凌日观测结果与 H2O 主导、含约 1% CH4 和微量 N2O 的大气（χν2=1.13）或未被遮挡的恒星黑子污染（χν2=1.36）均一致。然而，第二次凌日观测结果与无特征光谱一致。两次观测均不支持此前关于 HCN 的报告。大气变化不太可能解释两次观测之间的差异。同样，我们凌日外的恒星光谱也未显示两次观测之间恒星非均匀性有变化——两次观测间隔 8 天，仅为恒星自转周期的 6.5%。我们也未发现两次观测之间仪器系统误差有差异。最合理的解释是偶然的随机噪声导致两次透射光谱显著不同。我们的结果强调了在宣称这些小型、难以捉摸的行星存在大气之前，JWST 多次观测重复性的重要性。

[3] Featureless transmission spectra of 12 giant exoplanets observed by GTC/OSIRIS

2023-07-00

Context. Exoplanet atmospheres are the key to understanding the nature of exoplanets. To this end, transit spectrophotometry provides us opportunities to investigate the physical properties and chemical compositions of exoplanet atmospheres. <BR /> Aims: We aim to detect potential atmospheric signatures in 12 gaseous giant exoplanets using transit spectrophotometry and we try to constrain their atmospheric properties. <BR /> Methods: The targets of interest were observed using transit spectrophotometry with the GTC OSIRIS instrument. We estimated the transit parameters and obtained the optical transmission spectra of the target planets using a Bayesian framework. We analyzed the spectral features in the transmission spectra based on atmospheric retrievals. <BR /> Results: Most of the observed transmission spectra were found to be featureless, with only the spectrum of CoRoT-1b showing strong evidence for atmospheric features. However, in combination with the previously published near-infrared transmission spectrum, we found multiple interpretations for the atmosphere of CoRoT-1b due to the lack of decisive evidence for alkali metals or optical absorbers. <BR /> Conclusions: Featureless spectra are not necessarily indicative of cloudy atmospheres if they poorly constrain the altitudes of cloud decks. Precise constraints on the models of hazes and clouds strongly depend on the significance of the observed spectral features. Further investigations on these exoplanets, especially CoRoT-1b, are required to confirm the properties of their atmospheres.

[3] GTC/OSIRIS观测的12颗巨型系外行星无特征透射光谱

背景：系外行星大气是理解系外行星本质的关键。为此，凌日光谱测光为我们提供了研究系外行星大气物理性质和化学组成的机会。 目标：我们旨在利用凌日光谱测光探测12颗气态巨行星可能存在的大气特征，并尝试约束其大气性质。 方法：目标行星通过GTC OSIRIS仪器进行凌日光谱测光观测。我们采用贝叶斯框架估算凌日参数并获得目标行星的光学透射光谱。基于大气检索分析透射光谱中的光谱特征。 结果：大多数观测到的透射光谱表现为无特征，仅CoRoT-1b的光谱显示出强烈的大气特征证据。然而，结合先前发表的近红外透射光谱，由于缺乏对碱金属或光学吸收体的决定性证据，我们对CoRoT-1b大气存在多种解释。 结论：无特征光谱并不一定意味着多云大气，如果它们对云层高度的约束较弱。对薄雾和云模型的精确约束高度依赖于观测到的光谱特征的显著性。对这些系外行星，尤其是CoRoT-1b的大气性质，需要进一步研究以确认。

[4] Detailed Atmospheric Characterization of a Unique Low-Temperature Exo-Saturn

2024-02-00

Astronomers have learned a great deal about the atmospheres of hot extrasolar Jupiters and Saturns using ground-based, HST, Spitzer, and JWST observations. These planets are however in very different atmospheric circulation and chemistry regimes than their solar-system counterparts, and so far, there lacks an extrasolar analog of Jupiter or Saturn where we could test the basic theories of giant planet atmospheres. Here we identify TOI-199 b, a 350-K, Saturn-sized planet orbiting a G star, as a highly favorable target for detailed atmospheric characterization via transmission spectroscopy. We propose to observe one transit of the planet using NIRSpec/G395M. With a TSM of &gt;100 and a long transit duration of nearly 8 hours, the observation will result in exquisite photometric precision and precisely constrain the abundances of H2O, CH4, NH3, HCN, CO2, and CO in the planet's atmosphere. This unprecedented information will not only determine the atmospheric metallicity and C/O ratio, but also assess whether the vertical mixing in the planet's atmosphere is closer to the Jupiter regime or the hot Jupiter regime, and if the planet has a Jupiter-like vertical mixing, constrain the photochemical production mechanisms of HCN. The constraints on the metallicity and vertical mixing will also determine the fraction of nitrogen as NH3 (or indirectly as HCN), leading to a comprehensive picture of the planet's C, N, and O inventories. This program pushes the frontier of exoplanet characterization to low-temperature planets and advances the understanding of atmospheric physics and chemistry in unique planetary environments.

[4] 独特低温类土星系外行星的大气详细表征

天文学家利用地面观测、HST、Spitzer 和 JWST 对系外热木星和土星的大气进行了大量研究。然而，这些行星在大气环流和化学环境上与太阳系中的木星和土星有很大不同，目前还缺乏一个可以用于检验巨行星大气基本理论的系外木星或土星类似物。在此，我们确定 TOI-199 b（一个350 K、类土星大小、绕G型恒星运行的行星）是通过透射光谱进行详细大气特性研究的极佳目标。我们提议使用 NIRSpec/G395M 观测该行星的一次凌日。该目标的 TSM 大于100，凌日持续时间接近8小时，观测将获得极高的光度精度，并能精确约束该行星大气中 H2O、CH4、NH3、HCN、CO2 和 CO 的丰度。这些前所未有的信息不仅可以确定大气的金属丰度和 C/O 比，还可以评估该行星大气的垂直混合是更接近木星还是热木星的状态；如果该行星具有类似木星的垂直混合，还能约束 HCN 的光化学生成机制。对金属丰度和垂直混合的约束还将决定氮以 NH3（或间接以 HCN）形式存在的比例，从而全面揭示该行星的 C、N、O 储量。该项目将系外行星特性研究推进到低温行星领域，促进对独特行星环境下大气物理和化学的理解。

[5] JWST NIRISS transmission spectroscopy of the super-Earth GJ 357b, a favourable target for atmospheric retention

2025-07-00

We present a JWST Near Infrared Imager and Slitless Spectrograph/Single Object Slitless Spectroscopy transmission spectrum of the super-Earth GJ 357 b: the first atmospheric observation of this exoplanet. Despite missing the first <inline-formula><tex-math>$\sim$</tex-math></inline-formula>40 per cent of the transit due to using an out-of-date ephemeris, we still recover a transmission spectrum that does not display any clear signs of atmospheric features. We perform a search for Gaussian-shaped absorption features within the data but find that this analysis yields comparable fits to the observations as a flat line. We compare the transmission spectrum to a grid of atmosphere models and reject, to 3<inline-formula><tex-math>$\sigma$</tex-math></inline-formula> confidence, atmospheres with metallicities <inline-formula><tex-math>$\lesssim 100\times$</tex-math></inline-formula> solar (<inline-formula><tex-math>$\sim$</tex-math></inline-formula>4 g mol<SUP>−1</SUP>) with clouds at pressures down to 0.01 bar. We analyse how the retention of a secondary atmosphere on GJ 357 b may be possible due to its higher escape velocity compared to an Earth-sized planet and the exceptional inactivity of its host star relative to other M2.5V stars. The star's XUV luminosity decays below the threshold for rapid atmospheric escape early enough that the volcanic revival of an atmosphere of several bars of CO<inline-formula><tex-math>$_2$</tex-math></inline-formula> is plausible, though subject to considerable uncertainty. Finally, we model the feasibility of detecting an atmosphere on GJ 357 b with MIRI/LRS, MIRI photometry, and NIRSpec/G395H. We find that, with two eclipses, it would be possible to detect features indicative of an atmosphere or surface. Further to this, with three to four transits, it would be possible to detect a 1 bar nitrogen-rich atmosphere with 1000 ppm of CO<inline-formula><tex-math>$_2$</tex-math></inline-formula>.

[5] JWST NIRISS对超级地球GJ 357b的透射光谱观测：大气保留的有利目标

我们展示了JWST近红外成像仪与无缝光谱仪/单目标无缝光谱对超级地球GJ 357 b的透射光谱观测：这是该系外行星的首次大气观测。尽管由于采用过时的星历表，错过了约40%的凌日初始阶段，我们仍然获得了一个未显示明显大气特征的透射光谱。我们在数据中搜索高斯形状的吸收特征，但发现该分析与平坦线拟合效果相当。我们将透射光谱与一组大气模型进行比较，并以3σ置信度排除了金属丰度≲100倍太阳（约4 g mol^−1），云层压力低至0.01 bar的大气。我们分析了GJ 357 b由于其逃逸速度高于地球大小行星，以及其母星相较其他M2.5V恒星极为不活跃，可能保留次生大气的情况。该恒星的XUV光度在大气逃逸加速阈值以下衰减得足够早，因此火山活动可使大气CO2浓度达到数巴，尽管存在较大不确定性。最后，我们模拟了利用MIRI/LRS、MIRI光度和NIRSpec/G395H探测GJ 357 b大气的可行性。结果显示，通过两次凌星可探测到大气或表面特征；进一步地，通过三到四次凌日可探测到含1000 ppm CO2、1 bar氮丰富大气。

[6] L 98-59: A Benchmark System of Small Planets for Future Atmospheric Characterization

2021-10-00

The M3V dwarf star L 98-59 hosts three small (R &lt; 1.6 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>) planets. The host star is bright (K = 7.1) and nearby (10.6 pc), making the system a prime target for follow-up characterization with the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and the upcoming James Webb Space Telescope (JWST). Herein, we use simulated transmission spectroscopy to evaluate the detectability of spectral features with HST and JWST assuming diverse atmospheric scenarios (e.g., atmospheres dominated by H<SUB>2</SUB>, H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO<SUB>2</SUB>, or O<SUB>2</SUB>). We find that H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CH<SUB>4</SUB> present in a low mean molecular weight atmosphere could be detected with HST in one transit for the two outermost planets, while H<SUB>2</SUB>O in a clear steam atmosphere could be detected in six transits or fewer with HST for all three planets. We predict that observations using JWST/NIRISS would be capable of detecting a clear steam atmosphere in one transit for each planet and H<SUB>2</SUB>O absorption in a hazy steam atmosphere in two transits or less. In a clear, desiccated atmosphere, O<SUB>2</SUB> absorption may be detectable for all three planets with NIRISS. If the L 98-59 planets possess a clear, Venus-like atmosphere, NIRSpec could detect CO<SUB>2</SUB> within 26 transits for each planet, but the presence of H<SUB>2</SUB>SO<SUB>4</SUB> clouds would significantly suppress CO<SUB>2</SUB> absorption. The L 98-59 system is an excellent laboratory for comparative planetary studies of transiting multiplanet systems, and observations of the system via HST and JWST would present a unique opportunity to test the accuracy of the models presented in this study.

[6] L 98-59：未来大气特征研究的小型行星基准系统

M3V矮星L 98-59拥有三颗小型（R < 1.6 R⊕）行星。母星亮（K = 7.1）且距离近（10.6 pc），使该系统成为Hubble Space Telescope（HST）和即将发射的James Webb Space Telescope（JWST）后续特征分析的优选目标。本文利用模拟透射光谱，评估在不同大气情景（如以H2、H2O、CO2或O2为主的大气）下，HST和JWST对光谱特征的探测能力。我们发现，在低平均分子量大气中，H2O和CH4可在两颗最外侧行星的一次凌星中被HST探测到，而在清晰的水蒸气大气中，三颗行星均可在六次或更少凌星中被HST探测到H2O。我们预测，JWST/NIRISS观测可在每颗行星的一次凌星中探测到清晰的水蒸气大气，在两次或更少凌星中探测到有薄雾的水蒸气大气中的H2O吸收。在清晰、干燥的大气中，NIRISS有望对三颗行星均探测到O2吸收。如果L 98-59行星拥有清晰、类金星大气，NIRSpec可在每颗行星26次凌星内探测到CO2，但若存在H2SO4云则CO2吸收将被显著抑制。L 98-59系统是凌星多行星系统比较行星学的极佳实验室，通过HST和JWST观测该系统将为检验本研究模型的准确性提供独特机会。

[7] The Study of Atmosphere of Hot Jupiters and Their Host Stars

2022-12-00

What makes the study of exoplanetary atmospheres so hard is the extraction of its tiny signal from observations, usually dominated by telluric absorption, stellar spectrum and instrumental noise. The High Resolution Spectroscopy has emerged as one of the leading techniques for detecting atomic and molecular species (\citealt{2018arXiv180604617B}), but although it is particularly robust against contaminant absorption in the Earth's atmosphere, the non-stationary stellar spectrum - in the form of either Doppler shift or distortion of the line profile during planetary transits - creates a non-negligible source of noise that can alter or even prevent the detection. Recently, significant improvements have been achieved by using 3D, radiative hydrodynamical (RHD) simulations for the star and Global Circulation Models (GCM) for the planet (e.g., \citealt{2019A&amp;A...631A.100C}, \citealt{2019AJ....157..209F}). However, these numerical simulations have been computed independently so far, while acquired spectra are the result of the natural coupling at each phase along the planet orbit. With our work, we aim at generating emission spectra of G,F, and K-type stars and Hot Jupiters and coupling them at any phase of the orbit. This approach is expected to be particularly advantageous for those molecules that are present in both the atmospheres (e.g., CO) and form in the same region of the spectrum, resulting in mixed and overlapped spectral lines. We also present the analysis of transmission spectra of the Hot Saturn WASP-20b, observed in the K-band of the recently upgraded spectrograph VLT/CRIRES+ at a resolution R $\sim$ 92, 000 during the first night of the Science Verification of the instrument and that led to a tentative detection of H$_2$O.

[7] 热木星及其母星大气研究

系外行星大气研究的难点在于从观测数据中提取其微弱信号，这些观测通常被地球大气吸收、恒星光谱和仪器噪声主导。高分辨率光谱已成为探测原子和分子的重要技术之一（\citealt{2018arXiv180604617B}），尽管该方法对地球大气中的污染吸收具有较强的鲁棒性，但恒星光谱的非平稳性——无论是多普勒位移还是行星凌日过程中谱线轮廓的畸变——都会产生不可忽略的噪声源，可能改变甚至阻碍信号的探测。近期，通过对恒星进行三维辐射流体力学（RHD）模拟和对行星进行全球环流模型（GCM）模拟（如\citealt{2019A&A...631A.100C}, \citealt{2019AJ....157..209F}），取得了显著进展。然而，这些数值模拟目前仍是独立计算的，而实际获得的光谱是在行星轨道各相位自然耦合的结果。我们的工作旨在生成G、F、K型恒星及热木星的发射光谱，并在轨道任意相位进行耦合。这一方法对于那些在恒星和行星大气中均存在、且在同一光谱区域形成的分子（如CO）尤其有利，因为它们会产生混合和重叠的谱线。我们还展示了对热土星WASP-20b的透射光谱分析，该光谱是在VLT/CRIRES+升级后、分辨率R $\sim$ 92,000的K波段，在仪器科学验证首夜观测获得，并初步探测到H$_2$O。

[8] Detecting Atmospheric Molecules of Nontransiting Temperate Terrestrial Exoplanets Using High-resolution Spectroscopy in the Mid-infrared Domain

2021-04-00

Motivated by the development of high-dispersion spectrographs in the mid-infrared (MIR) range, we study their application to the atmospheric characterization of nearby nontransiting temperate terrestrial planets around M-type stars. We examine the detectability of CO<SUB>2</SUB>, H<SUB>2</SUB>O, N<SUB>2</SUB>O, and O<SUB>3 </SUB>features in high-resolution planetary thermal emission spectra at 12-18 μm assuming an Earth-like profile and a simplified thermal structure. The molecular line width of such planets can be comparable to or broader than the Doppler shift due to the planetary orbital motion. Given the likely difficulty in knowing the high-resolution MIR spectrum of the host star with sufficient accuracy, we propose observing the target system at two quadrature phases and extracting the differential spectra as the planetary signal. In this case, the signals can be substantially suppressed compared with the case where the host star spectrum is perfectly known, as some parts of the spectral features do not remain in the differential spectra. Despite this self-subtraction, the CO<SUB>2</SUB> and H<SUB>2</SUB>O features of nearby (≲5 pc) systems with mid-/late-M host stars would be feasible with a 6.5 m class cryogenic space telescope, and orbital inclination could also be constrained for some of them. For CO<SUB>2</SUB> and N<SUB>2</SUB>O in a 1 bar Earth-like atmosphere, this method would be sensitive when the mixing ratio is 1-10<SUP>3</SUP> ppm. The detectability of molecules except O<SUB>3</SUB> is not significantly improved when the spectral resolution is higher than ${ \mathcal R }\gtrsim {\rm{10,000}}$ &lt;!-- --&gt; ,although the constraint on the orbital inclination is improved. This study provides some benchmark cases useful for assessing the value of MIR high-resolution spectroscopy in terms of characterization of potentially habitable planets.

[8] 利用中红外高分辨率光谱探测非凌日温带类地系外行星大气分子

受中红外（MIR）高分辨率光谱仪发展的推动，我们研究了其在邻近非凌星温带类地行星（围绕M型恒星）大气特征分析中的应用。我们假设地球类大气剖面和简化的热结构，考察了在12–18 μm高分辨率行星热发射光谱中CO2、H2O、N2O和O3特征的可探测性。这类行星的分子谱线宽度可与行星轨道运动引起的多普勒位移相当或更宽。鉴于难以充分准确地获得母星的高分辨率MIR光谱，我们提出在两个象限相位观测目标系统，并将差分光谱作为行星信号提取。在这种情况下，信号相比于母星光谱已知的理想情况会被大幅抑制，因为部分光谱特征不会保留在差分光谱中。尽管存在这种自减效应，对于距离≲5 pc、母星为中/晚M型的系统，使用6.5 m级低温空间望远镜仍有可能探测到CO2和H2O特征，并可对部分系统的轨道倾角加以约束。对于1 bar地球类大气中的CO2和N2O，当混合比为1–10^3 ppm时该方法具有灵敏度。除O3外，分子可探测性在光谱分辨率高于𝓡 ≳ 10,000时提升不显著，但对轨道倾角的约束有所改善。本研究为评估MIR高分辨率光谱在潜在宜居行星特征分析中的价值提供了基准案例。

item_003 · sys_F
What are the observable consequences of magma ocean dynamos and interior magnetic fields on the atmospheric properties and potential habitability of rocky exoplanets with thick atmospheres?
岩石系外行星具有厚大气层时，岩浆海洋发电机和内部磁场对其大气性质及潜在宜居性有哪些可观测影响？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] How does magma ocean evolution affect the habitability of terrestrial exoplanets?

2023-12-00

Whether the long-term habitability of Earth is exceptional or typical for terrestrial planets has immense significance for the search for life elsewhere. One potentially underexplored aspect to habitability is how initial volatile inventories may shape subsequent geochemical and climate evolution. Exoplanet exploration has revealed that many—perhaps most—terrestrial exoplanets formed with substantial H2-rich envelopes, seemingly in contrast to the solar system terrestrials, for which there is scant evidence of prolonged H2-rich envelopes. How might the presence of a long-lived primary atmosphere and resultant molten surface affect the subsequent habitability of terrestrial and super-Earth planets? Answering this question requires a self-consistent model of how high molecular weight volatiles partition into the magma ocean and fractionate during primary atmosphere loss, as well as how terrestrial planets that previously possessed thick primary atmospheres might subsequently evolve. Of particular relevance to ongoing JWST observations is how coupled magma ocean + atmosphere systems evolve when subjected to high bolometric and XUV fluxes during the extended pre-main sequence of late M-dwarfs. Here, we will present a new model of atmosphere-interior early evolution that self-consistently tracks atmospheric escape, magma ocean solidification, and redox evolution. Specifically, the model tracks how C, O, H, and N-bearing volatile reservoirs evolve as an initial H2-rich envelope is lost to space and the magma ocean solidifies. The model will be applied to several imminently characterizable, rocky exoplanets to explore how post-magma ocean volatile reservoirs (and subsequent geochemical cycling + climate evolution) could be affected by the presence or absence of a substantial primary atmosphere.

[1] 岩石系外行星的岩浆海演化如何影响其宜居性？

地球的长期宜居性是特殊还是类地行星的普遍现象，对于寻找地外生命具有重大意义。影响宜居性的一个可能被低估的方面是初始挥发分库存如何塑造后续地球化学和气候演化。系外行星探索显示，许多（甚至大多数）类地系外行星形成时拥有大量富 H2 包层，这与太阳系类地行星形成鲜有长期富 H2 包层的证据形成对比。长期存在的原始大气及其导致的熔融表面会如何影响类地和超级地球行星的后续宜居性？回答这一问题需要自洽建模高分子量挥发分在岩浆洋中的分配及其在原始大气损失过程中的分馏，以及曾经拥有厚原始大气的类地行星后续如何演化。与 JWST 正在进行的观测密切相关的是，岩浆洋+大气系统在晚型 M 矮星延长前主序阶段经历高总辐射和 XUV 通量时的耦合演化。我们将在此展示一个新的大气-内部早期演化模型，该模型自洽追踪大气逃逸、岩浆洋固化和氧化还原演化。具体而言，该模型追踪 C、O、H、N 含挥发分库在初始富 H2 包层丧失至太空及岩浆洋固化过程中的演化。该模型将应用于若干即将可表征的岩石系外行星，以探讨岩浆洋后挥发分库（及后续地球化学循环和气候演化）如何受有无大量原始大气的影响。

[2] Evaporation of a magma ocean in presence of hydrogen &amp; observational implications

2023-10-00

With the new observational capabilities of space telescopes, it should be possible to better characterize the atmospheres of exoplanets, and provide constraints on interiors. Ultra hot rocky exoplanets, for which the stellar irradiation may maintain a magma ocean at the surface for a long period of time, are candidates for such observations. It has been suggested that the primary hydrogen envelope that is captured during the formation of a planet could be kept in the magma ocean, and therefore we could observe planets with a silicate atmosphere mixed with hydrogen. Our model relies on a Gibbs free-energy minimization to find the vapor composition in equilibrium with the magma ocean (a modified version of the CEA/NASA code (Gordon &amp; McBride (1996)). The vapor composition is then used in an atmospheric model, ATMO (Amundsen et al. 2014), which solves for the pressure-temperature profile by finding the energy flux balance in each layer of the model. Synthetic observations are generated via ATMO. We confirm the thermal inversion of silicate atmospheres and the associated emission features of SiO (Ito et al. 2015, Zilinskas et al. 2022), as well as MgO, Na, K, Fe, which are the strongest candidates for detection. We show that hydrogen will water down the other species, and the thermal inversion is reduced or removed, depending on the temperature of the planet. Cases with a lot of hydrogen will be linked to absorption features of H2O. Surface temperatures will also be affected, and increase for higher content of hydrogen.

[2] 氢存在下岩浆海的蒸发及其观测意义

随着空间望远镜观测能力的提升，我们有望更好地刻画系外行星大气，并对其内部结构加以约束。超高温岩石系外行星由于恒星辐射可能在表面长期维持岩浆海，是此类观测的候选目标。有研究提出，行星形成过程中捕获的原始氢包层可能被保存在岩浆海中，因此我们可能观测到含氢的硅酸盐大气。我们的模型基于Gibbs自由能最小化，确定与岩浆海平衡的蒸气成分（采用改进版CEA/NASA代码（Gordon & McBride (1996)））。随后将蒸气成分输入大气模型ATMO（Amundsen等，2014），通过各层能量通量平衡求解压强-温度剖面。合成观测数据由ATMO生成。我们证实了硅酸盐大气的热反转及其相关的SiO发射特征（Ito等，2015；Zilinskas等，2022），以及MgO、Na、K、Fe，这些是最有希望被探测到的分子。我们显示，氢的存在会稀释其他组分，热反转会减弱或消失，具体取决于行星温度。高氢含量的情形将与H2O的吸收特征相关。表面温度也会受到影响，氢含量越高，表面温度越高。

[3] Convective shutdown in the atmospheres of lava worlds

2025-01-00

Atmospheric energy transport is central to the cooling of primordial magma oceans. Theoretical studies of atmospheres on lava planets have assumed that convection is the only process involved in setting the atmospheric temperature structure. This significantly influences the ability for a magma ocean to cool. It has been suggested that convective stability in these atmospheres could preclude permanent magma oceans. We develop a new 1D radiative-convective model in order to investigate when the atmospheres overlying magma oceans are convectively stable. Using a coupled interior-atmosphere framework, we simulate the early evolution of two terrestrial-mass exoplanets: TRAPPIST-1 c and HD 63433 d. Our simulations suggest that the atmosphere of HD 63433 d exhibits deep isothermal layers which are convectively stable. However, it is able to maintain a permanent magma ocean and an atmosphere depleted in <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0001" notation="LaTeX">$\mathrm{H_{2}O}$</tex-math></inline-formula>. It is possible to maintain permanent magma oceans underneath atmospheres without convection. Absorption features of <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0002" notation="LaTeX">$\mathrm{CO_{2}}$</tex-math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0003" notation="LaTeX">$\mathrm{SO_{2}}$</tex-math></inline-formula> within synthetic emission spectra are associated with mantle redox state, meaning that future observations of HD 63433 d may provide constraints on the geochemical properties of a magma ocean analogous with the early Earth. Simulations of TRAPPIST-1 c indicate that it is expected to have solidified within <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0004" notation="LaTeX">$100 \,\mathrm{M}\rm {yr}$</tex-math></inline-formula>, outgassing a thick atmosphere in the process. Cool isothermal stratospheres generated by low-molecular-weight atmospheres can mimic the emission of an atmosphere-less body. Future work should consider how atmospheric escape and chemistry modulates the lifetime of magma oceans, and the role of tidal heating in sustaining atmospheric convection.

[3] 熔岩世界大气中的对流关闭

大气能量输运对于原始岩浆海洋的冷却至关重要。关于熔岩行星大气的理论研究通常假设对流是决定大气温度结构的唯一过程，这对岩浆海洋的冷却能力有重要影响。有观点认为，这些大气中的对流稳定性可能阻止永久性岩浆海洋的存在。我们开发了一个新的1D辐射-对流模型，以研究覆盖岩浆海洋的大气在何种条件下为对流稳定。利用耦合的内部-大气框架，我们模拟了两颗类地质量系外行星（TRAPPIST-1 c和HD 63433 d）的早期演化。模拟结果表明，HD 63433 d的大气表现出深层等温层且对流稳定，但仍能维持永久性岩浆海洋和H2O亏损的大气。在无对流的大气下，仍有可能维持永久性岩浆海洋。合成发射光谱中CO2和SO2的吸收特征与地幔氧化还原态相关，意味着未来对HD 63433 d的观测可为类似早期地球的岩浆海洋地球化学性质提供约束。TRAPPIST-1 c的模拟表明其预计在100 Myr内固化，并在此过程中释放出厚大气。低分子量大气产生的冷等温平流层可模拟无大气天体的发射特征。未来工作应考虑大气逃逸和化学过程如何调节岩浆海洋寿命，以及潮汐加热在维持大气对流中的作用。

[4] Redox hysteresis of super-Earth exoplanets from magma ocean circulation

2021-12-00

Ongoing and near-future exoplanet surveys will significantly widen our view of the distribution and variability of rocky exoplanets. From an astronomical perspective, planets that formed under similar conditions should exhibit comparable compositional trends, such as volatile inventory, which can be compared to hypothetical M-R relations. However, internal redox reactions may irreversibly alter the mantle composition and volatile inventory of terrestrial and super-Earth exoplanets, which can affect their outgassed atmospheres and decouple the initial accreted composition from long-term climate. The global efficacy of these mechanisms hinges on the transfer of reduced iron from the molten silicate mantle to the metal core. Using scaling analysis I demonstrate that turbulent diffusion in the internal magma oceans of sub-Neptune exoplanets can kinetically entrain liquid iron droplets and quench core formation. This suggests that the chemical equilibration between core, mantle, and atmosphere may be energetically limited by convective overturn in the magma flow. Hence, molten super-Earths possibly retain a compositional memory of their accretion path. Redox control by magma ocean circulation positively correlates with planetary heat flow, internal gravity, and planet size. The presence and speciation of remanent atmospheres, surface mineralogy, and core-mass fraction of atmosphere-stripped exoplanets may thus constrain magma ocean dynamics, which can be probed observationally.

[4] 来自岩浆海洋环流的超级地球系外行星氧化还原迟滞

正在进行和即将到来的系外行星巡天将极大拓展我们对岩石系外行星分布和多样性的认识。从天文学角度看，在类似条件下形成的行星应表现出可比较的组成趋势，如挥发物储量，这可以与假设的质量-半径关系进行比较。然而，内部氧化还原反应可能不可逆地改变类地和超级地球系外行星的地幔组成和挥发物储量，这会影响其逸出大气，并使初始吸积组成与长期气候脱钩。这些机制的全球效率取决于还原态铁从熔融硅酸盐地幔向金属核的转移。通过尺度分析，我证明了亚海王星系外行星内部岩浆洋中的湍流扩散可以动力学地夹带液态铁滴并抑制核心形成。这表明，核、地幔和大气之间的化学平衡可能受到岩浆流对流翻转的能量限制。因此，熔融超级地球可能保留其吸积路径的组成记忆。岩浆洋环流对氧化还原的控制与行星热流、内部重力和行星大小正相关。大气剥离后的系外行星的残余大气的存在与形态、表面矿物学和核心质量分数等，可能因此约束岩浆洋动力学，这些可以通过观测手段加以探测。

[5] Early Habitability Under Different Molten Interior Outgassing Scenarios

2023-12-00

The volatile budget of rocky exoplanets is highly unconstrained. In addition, recent models have suggested that the early magma ocean outgassing of volatile species highly soluble in the melt, in particular water, may not be as efficient as previously thought. A substantial amount of water could then be trapped in the planetary interior before being progressively released through long-term volcanic outgassing. The long-term evolution of a planet may then start with a broad range of atmospheric compositions. Yet, the inner edge of the habitable zone is classically defined for and restricted to steam, water-dominated atmospheres. It does not encompass the diversity of atmospheric composition resulting from alternative magma ocean and volcanic outgassing scenarios. Together with exoplanets findings and characterizations, there is a pressing need to broaden our understanding of planetary habitability in the frame of realistic coupled interior-atmosphere evolution. <P />Here we use a 1D radiative-convective climate model, with self-consistent patchy clouds, to simulate the atmospheric thermal structure of Earth-sized planets of different oxidized atmospheric compositions. We thus investigate the climate and resulting surface conditions associated with a broad diversity of interior outgassing scenarios. We then assess how the location of the inner edge of the habitable zone may vary as a function of the relative abundance of water and carbon dioxide in the atmosphere. Finally, we evaluate the influence of the star type and of clouds on our predictions.

[5] 不同熔融内部逸气情景下的早期宜居性

岩石系外行星的挥发分预算高度不确定。此外，最新模型表明，早期岩浆洋中对熔体高度可溶的挥发分（尤其是水）的释气效率可能远低于此前认为的水平。因此，大量水分可能被困于行星内部，随后通过长期火山释气逐步释放。行星的长期演化可能由多种大气成分组成的初始状态开始。然而，宜居带内缘传统上是针对并仅限于水蒸气主导的大气定义的，并未涵盖由不同岩浆洋和火山释气情景导致的大气成分多样性。结合系外行星的发现与表征，亟需在真实的内部-大气耦合演化框架下拓展我们对行星宜居性的理解。 在此，我们使用带有自洽斑块云的 1D 辐射-对流气候模型，模拟不同氧化大气成分的类地行星大气热结构。我们研究了多样化内部释气情景下的气候及其导致的表面条件。随后，我们评估了宜居带内缘位置如何随大气中水和二氧化碳相对丰度变化而变化。最后，我们还评估了恒星类型和云对预测结果的影响。

[6] Redox Hysteresis of Super-Earth Exoplanets from Magma Ocean Circulation

2021-06-00

Internal redox reactions may irreversibly alter the mantle composition and volatile inventory of terrestrial and super-Earth exoplanets and affect the prospects for atmospheric observations. The global efficacy of these mechanisms, however, hinges on the transfer of reduced iron from the molten silicate mantle to the metal core. Scaling analysis indicates that turbulent diffusion in the internal magma oceans of sub-Neptunes can kinetically entrain liquid iron droplets and quench core formation. This suggests that the chemical equilibration between core, mantle, and atmosphere may be energetically limited by convective overturn in the magma flow. Hence, molten super-Earths possibly retain a compositional memory of their accretion path. Redox control by magma ocean circulation is positively correlated with planetary heat flow, internal gravity, and planet size. The presence and speciation of remanent atmospheres, surface mineralogy, and core mass fraction of primary envelope-stripped exoplanets may thus constrain magma ocean dynamics.

[6] 来自岩浆海洋环流的超级地球系外行星氧化还原迟滞

内部氧化还原反应可能不可逆地改变类地和超级地球系外行星的地幔组成和挥发物储量，并影响其大气观测前景。然而，这些机制的整体效率取决于还原态铁从熔融硅酸盐地幔向金属核的转移。尺度分析表明，亚海王星内部岩浆洋中的湍流扩散可以动力学夹带液态铁滴并抑制核心形成。这表明，核、地幔和大气之间的化学平衡可能受到岩浆流动中对流翻转的能量限制。因此，熔融超级地球可能保留其吸积路径的组成记忆。岩浆洋环流对氧化还原的控制与行星热流、内部重力和行星尺寸正相关。原始包层被剥离的系外行星的残余大气的存在及其形态、表面矿物学和核质量分数，因而可以约束岩浆洋动力学。

[7] Large-scale hydrogen reactions with magma oceans change the atmosphere of exoplanets

2025-09-00

Exoplanet super-Earths and sub-Neptunes are likely more prevalent than stars, yet their nature remains uncertain. Notably, sub-Neptunes orbit in close proximity to their host stars, often enveloped in substantial and hot hydrogen-rich atmospheres. These conditions, characterized by intense irradiation and the presence of greenhouse gases, accelerate the melting of the underlying silicate surface and facilitate the exchange of volatiles with the atmosphere. In this study, we conduct extensive large-scale ab initio simulations to investigate the reaction between hydrogen gas and molten multi-component silicates. Our findings reveal that magma oceans possess the capability to dissolve up to 2 wt% hydrogen at typical conditions pertinent to the interface between the atmosphere and the condensed surface of such planets. The influx of hydrogen into the molten silicate results in a reduction in magma density, leading to the formation of a substantial buffer layer between the condensed interior and the outer atmosphere. This layer effectively suppresses convection and restricts chemical exchanges within these planets.Furthermore, the incorporation of hydrogen into the molten silicate alters the redox state of the magma ocean, causing it to become reduced. Consequently, a substantial outflux of oxygen is generated, which combines with atmospheric hydrogen to produce substantial amounts of water vapor. Consequently, the release of oxygen will profoundly alter the atmospheric chemistry and manifest spectral signatures detectable from space telescopes. These atmospheric composition modifications constitute a testable signature of concealed magma oceans on exoplanets. The uptake of volatiles by the magma implies that sub-Neptunes exhibit a higher volatility compared to previously assumed. Additionally, our atomistic simulations suggest that the dissolution and evaporation process is chemically intricate, resulting in the formation of hundreds of distinct molecular species within the atmosphere. These magma-buffered atmospheric recipes will necessitate consideration in future studies focused on the origins of exoplanet atmospheres.

[7] 大尺度氢与岩浆海洋反应改变系外行星大气

系外行星中的超级地球和亚海王星可能比恒星更为常见，但它们的本质仍不确定。值得注意的是，亚海王星通常围绕其母星近距离运行，往往包裹着大量且高温的富氢大气。这些条件下，强烈的辐射和温室气体的存在加速了下方硅酸盐表面的熔融，并促进了挥发分与大气之间的交换。在本研究中，我们进行了大规模从头算模拟，研究氢气与多组分熔融硅酸盐之间的反应。我们的结果显示，在典型的大气与凝聚表面界面条件下，岩浆海洋能够溶解高达2 wt%的氢。氢进入熔融硅酸盐会导致岩浆密度降低，从而在凝聚内部与外层大气之间形成一个显著的缓冲层。该层有效抑制了对流，并限制了这些行星内部的化学交换。此外，氢掺入熔融硅酸盐会改变岩浆海洋的氧化还原状态，使其变得更还原。结果，产生了大量氧气外流，氧与大气中的氢结合生成大量水蒸气。因此，氧的释放将深刻改变大气化学，并在空间望远镜可探测的光谱中表现出特征信号。这些大气组成的变化构成了可检验的系外行星隐匿岩浆海洋的特征信号。岩浆对挥发分的吸收意味着亚海王星的挥发性比此前认为的更高。此外，我们的原子级模拟表明，溶解和蒸发过程在化学上极为复杂，导致大气中形成数百种不同的分子种类。这些岩浆缓冲型大气的成分将在未来关于系外行星大气起源的研究中需要加以考虑。

[8] Redox hysteresis of super-Earth exoplanets from magma ocean circulation

2021-09-00

Internal redox reactions may irreversibly alter the mantle composition and volatile inventory of terrestrial and super-Earth exoplanets and affect the prospects for atmospheric observations. The global efficacy of these mechanisms, however, hinges on the transfer of reduced iron from the molten silicate mantle to the metal core. Scaling analysis indicates that turbulent diffusion in the internal magma oceans of sub- Neptunes can kinetically entrain liquid iron droplets and quench core formation. This suggests that the chemical equilibration between core, mantle, and atmosphere may be energetically limited by convective overturn in the magma flow. Hence, molten super-Earths possibly retain a compositional memory of their accretion path. Redox control by magma ocean circulation is positively correlated with planetary heat flow, internal gravity, and planet size. The presence and speciation of remanent atmospheres, surface mineralogy, and core mass fraction of atmosphere-stripped exoplanets may thus constrain magma ocean dynamics.

[8] 来自岩浆海洋环流的超级地球系外行星氧化还原迟滞

内部氧化还原反应可能不可逆地改变类地和超地球系外行星的地幔组成和挥发物储量，并影响大气观测的前景。然而，这些机制的全球效率取决于还原态铁从熔融硅酸盐地幔向金属核的转移。尺度分析表明，亚海王星内部岩浆洋中的湍流扩散可以动力学地夹带液态铁滴，并抑制核心的形成。这表明，核心、地幔和大气之间的化学平衡可能受到岩浆流对流翻转的能量限制。因此，熔融的超地球可能保留其吸积路径的组成记忆。岩浆洋环流对氧化还原的控制与行星热流、内部重力和行星大小呈正相关。大气被剥离的系外行星的残余大气的存在及其形态、表面矿物学和核心质量分数，因而可以约束岩浆洋动力学。

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Revealing patchy clouds on WASP-43b and WASP-121b through coupled microphysical and hydrodynamical models

2025-09-00

Hot and ultra-hot Jupiters are currently the best observational targets to study the effects of clouds on exoplanet atmospheres. Observations have reported westward optical phase curve offsets, weak spectral features, and nightside temperatures remaining constant with increasing stellar flux, which may together be explained by the presence of exoplanetary clouds. Although there are many models that simulate the 3D structure and circulation of hot/ultra-hot Jupiters and many microphysical models describing the formation of clouds, very few models exist that couple these two approaches. This gap, along with recent JWST observations unmatched by models, suggests a need for more accurate models to track the formation of clouds as well as their radiative feedback on atmospheric circulation and dynamics. In this work, we couple two models to better understand how atmospheric dynamics and cloud microphysics in hot Jupiter atmospheres affect each other and the observable properties of such planets in the context of JWST data. We run cloudless 3D general circulation model (GCM) simulations using the SPARC/MITgcm for WASP-43b and WASP-121b, two hot/ultra-hot Jupiters that already have high-quality data from HST and recent JWST observations. We then feed the temperature-pressure profile outputs from the GCM simulations into 1D CARMA, which models the microphysics of mineral clouds in hot and ultra-hot Jupiter atmospheres. Finally, we use our coupled circulation and cloud formation results to calculate synthetic spectra with a ray-striking radiative transfer code and compare our results to emission and transmission observations of WASP-43b and WASP-121b. We find that various cloud species, including corundum, forsterite, and iron, form everywhere on WASP-43b and on the nightside and west limb of WASP-121b, perhaps explaining the most recent phase curve observations of these planets. We discuss implications for the interpretation of JWST/MIRI and JWST/NIRSpec observations of WASP-43b and WASP-121b respectively, with implications for the broader planetary population.

[1] 通过耦合微物理和流体动力学模型揭示WASP-43b和WASP-121b上的斑块状云

热木星和超热木星目前是研究云对系外行星大气影响的最佳观测目标。观测结果报告了光学相位曲线向西偏移、光谱特征较弱，以及夜面温度随恒星通量增加而保持恒定，这些现象可能都可以通过系外行星大气中存在云来解释。尽管已有许多模拟热/超热木星三维结构和环流的模型，以及描述云形成的微物理模型，但将这两种方法耦合的模型却很少。这一空白，加上近期JWST观测结果与模型不符，表明需要更精确的模型来追踪云的形成及其对大气环流和动力学的辐射反馈。在本工作中，我们耦合了两种模型，以更好地理解热木星大气中的大气动力学与云微物理如何相互影响，以及它们在JWST数据背景下对这些行星可观测性质的影响。我们使用SPARC/MITgcm对WASP-43b和WASP-121b这两颗已有HST和JWST高质量观测数据的热/超热木星进行了无云三维大气环流模型（GCM）模拟。随后，我们将GCM模拟得到的温度-压强剖面输入到一维CARMA模型中，后者模拟热木星和超热木星大气中矿物云的微物理过程。最后，我们利用耦合后的环流和云形成结果，结合射线追踪辐射传输代码计算合成光谱，并将结果与WASP-43b和WASP-121b的发射和透射观测进行比较。我们发现，包括刚玉、橄榄石和铁在内的多种云种类在WASP-43b的各处以及WASP-121b的夜面和西侧边缘均有形成，这或许可以解释这些行星最新的相位曲线观测结果。我们还讨论了这些结果对JWST/MIRI和JWST/NIRSpec对WASP-43b和WASP-121b观测解释的意义，并对更广泛的行星种群提出了启示。

[2] Patchy clouds on the nightsides of ultra-hot Jupiters

2022-06-00

The atmospheres of ultra-hot Jupiters have been characterized in detail through recent phase curve and low- and high-resolution transmission and emission spectroscopy observations. These observations have found that the daysides of ultra-hot Jupiters are likely hot enough to be cloud-free and their nightsides are not uniformly cloudy, as expected for cooler hot Jupiters. Recent three-dimensional modeling of ultra-hot Jupiter atmospheres with clouds either coupled to the dynamics or post-processed have found that the nightsides of ultra-hot Jupiters are likely partially covered by a patchy cloud deck of high-temperature condensates and silicates. In this work, we study the patchy cloud coverage in ultra-hot Jupiter atmospheres with the three-dimensional ultra-hot Jupiter MITgcm coupled to radiatively and dynamically active cloud tracers. To determine our cloud condensate properties, we simulate the dominant species from CARMA cloud microphysics models along with their approximate cloud particle size distribution, single scattering albedo, and asymmetry parameter. We model the newly discovered ultra-hot Jupiter TOI-1431b, which has an equilibrium temperature of 2370 K and an inflated radius typical of ultra-hot Jupiters. We conduct a suite of GCM simulations varying cloud microphysical and scattering properties and gas opacity, and find that partial cloud coverage on the nightside and western limb is a ubiquitous outcome of our models in the parameter regime considered. We find that condensate clouds on the nightside and western limb are sequestered at depth due to a thermodynamically driven thermal inversion as atomic hydrogen recombines to molecular form, warming the nightside atmosphere at low pressures. We study the vertical transport of condensate clouds, finding that the patchy spatial distribution of cloud tracers does not directly track the local temperature conditions in the atmosphere. Rather, the cloud distribution is set by vertical mixing driven by the large-scale atmospheric dynamics, necessitating a coupled understanding of cloud formation and transport to predict the patchy cloud coverage of ultra-hot Jupiters. We post-process these GCMs with the gCMCRT Monte Carlo radiative transfer model in order to both compare our results with the observed TESS phase curve of TOI-1431b and make observational predictions for JWST characterization of the patchy cloud coverage of ultra-hot Jupiters.

[2] 超热木星夜面上的斑块状云

超热木星的大气已通过近期的相位曲线以及低分辨率和高分辨率透射光谱与发射光谱观测得到了详细刻画。这些观测发现，超热木星的白昼面很可能足够高温而无云，而夜晚面不像较冷的热木星那样均匀多云。近期对超热木星大气的三维建模（云与动力学耦合或后处理）发现，超热木星的夜晚面很可能被高温冷凝物和硅酸盐组成的斑块状云层部分覆盖。在本工作中，我们利用三维超热木星MITgcm模型，耦合辐射和动力学活跃的云示踪物，研究超热木星大气中的斑块状云覆盖。为确定云冷凝物性质，我们模拟了CARMA云微物理模型中的主要物种，并给出了其近似云粒径分布、单次散射反照率和不对称因子。我们对新发现的超热木星TOI-1431b进行建模，其平衡温度为2370 K，半径膨胀，符合超热木星的典型特征。我们进行了一系列GCM模拟，改变云微物理和散射特性以及气体不透明度，发现夜晚面和西侧边缘的部分云覆盖是我们所考虑参数范围内模型的普遍结果。我们发现，夜晚面和西侧边缘的冷凝云由于热力学驱动的温度反转而被困在大气深处，随着原子氢重组为分子形式，低压下夜晚面大气变暖。我们研究了冷凝云的垂直输运，发现云示踪物的斑块状空间分布并不直接追踪大气中的局部温度条件。相反，云的分布由大尺度大气动力学驱动的垂直混合决定，因此需要耦合理解云的形成和输运，才能预测超热木星的斑块状云覆盖。我们用gCMCRT蒙特卡洛辐射传输模型对这些GCM进行后处理，以便将我们的结果与TOI-1431b的TESS相位曲线观测进行比较，并为JWST对超热木星斑块状云覆盖的刻画提供观测预测。

[3] Patchy Nightside Clouds on Ultra-hot Jupiters: General Circulation Model Simulations with Radiatively Active Cloud Tracers

2022-07-00

The atmospheres of ultra-hot Jupiters have been characterized in detail through recent phase curve and low- and high-resolution emission and transmission spectroscopic observations. Previous numerical studies have analyzed the effect of the localized recombination of hydrogen on the atmospheric dynamics and heat transport of ultra-hot Jupiters, finding that hydrogen dissociation and recombination lead to a reduction in the day-to-night contrasts of ultra-hot Jupiters relative to previous expectations. In this work, we add to previous efforts by also considering the localized condensation of clouds in the atmospheres of ultra-hot Jupiters, their resulting transport by the atmospheric circulation, and the radiative feedback of clouds on the atmospheric dynamics. To do so, we include radiatively active cloud tracers into the existing MITgcm framework for simulating the atmospheric dynamics of ultra-hot Jupiters. We take cloud condensate properties appropriate for the high-temperature condensate corundum from CARMA cloud microphysics models. We conduct a suite of general circulation model (GCM) simulations with varying cloud microphysical and radiative properties, and we find that partial cloud coverage is a ubiquitous outcome of our simulations. This patchy cloud distribution is inherently set by atmospheric dynamics in addition to equilibrium cloud condensation, and causes a cloud greenhouse effect that warms the atmosphere below the cloud deck. Nightside clouds are further sequestered at depth due to a dynamically induced high-altitude thermal inversion. We post-process our GCMs with the Monte Carlo radiative transfer code gCMCRT and find that the patchy clouds on ultra-hot Jupiters do not significantly impact transmission spectra but can affect their phase-dependent emission spectra.

[3] 超热木星夜面斑块状云：带有辐射活性云示踪剂的一般环流模型模拟

通过近期的相位曲线以及低分辨率和高分辨率的发射与透射光谱观测，超热木星的大气已被详细刻画。先前的数值研究分析了氢的局部复合对超热木星大气动力学和热量输运的影响，发现氢的解离与复合导致超热木星昼夜温差较以往预期减小。在本工作中，我们在前人基础上进一步考虑了超热木星大气中云的局部凝结、其随大气环流的输运以及云对大气动力学的辐射反馈。为此，我们在现有的MITgcm超热木星大气动力学模拟框架中引入了具有辐射活性的云示踪物。我们采用CARMA云微物理模型中适用于高温冷凝物刚玉的云冷凝物性质。我们进行了一系列广义环流模型（GCM）模拟，改变云的微物理和辐射属性，发现部分云覆盖是模拟的普遍结果。这种斑块状云分布不仅由平衡云凝结决定，还受到大气动力学的本质影响，并在云层下方引发云温室效应使大气变暖。夜面云由于动力学诱导的高空热反转而被进一步沉积到深层。我们用Monte Carlo辐射传输代码gCMCRT对GCM结果进行后处理，发现超热木星上的斑块状云对透射光谱影响不大，但会影响其相位相关的发射光谱。

[4] Signatures of Clouds in Hot Jupiter Atmospheres: Modeled High-resolution Emission Spectra from 3D General Circulation Models

2021-03-00

Observations of scattered light and thermal emission from hot Jupiter exoplanets have suggested the presence of inhomogeneous aerosols in their atmospheres. 3D general circulation models (GCMs) that attempt to model the effects of aerosols have been developed to understand the physical processes that underlie their dynamical structures. In this work, we investigate how different approaches to aerosol modeling in GCMs of hot Jupiters affect high-resolution thermal emission spectra throughout the duration of the planet's orbit. Using results from a GCM with temperature-dependent cloud formation, we calculate spectra of a representative hot Jupiter with different assumptions regarding the vertical extent and thickness of clouds. We then compare these spectra to models in which clouds are absent or simply post-processed (i.e., added subsequently to the completed clear model). We show that the temperature-dependent treatment of clouds in the GCM produces high-resolution emission spectra that are markedly different from the clear and post-processed cases—both in the continuum flux levels and line profiles—and that increasing the vertical extent and thickness of clouds leads to bigger changes in these features. We evaluate the net Doppler shifts of the spectra induced by global winds and the planet's rotation and show that they are strongly phase dependent, especially for models with thicker and more extended clouds. This work further demonstrates the importance of radiative feedback in cloudy atmospheric models of hot Jupiters, as this can have a significant impact on interpreting spectroscopic observations of exoplanet atmospheres.

[4] 热木星大气中云的特征：基于三维大气环流模型的高分辨率发射光谱模拟

对热木星系外行星散射光和热发射的观测表明其大气中存在不均匀气溶胶。为理解其动力结构背后的物理过程，已经开发了考虑气溶胶效应的三维广义环流模型（GCM）。在本工作中，我们研究了在热木星GCM中采用不同气溶胶建模方法对整个轨道周期内高分辨热发射光谱的影响。我们利用包含温度依赖型云形成的GCM结果，计算了代表性热木星在不同云垂直分布和厚度假设下的光谱，并将这些光谱与无云或后处理（即在清晰模型完成后再添加云）模型进行比较。我们表明，GCM中温度依赖的云处理会产生与无云和后处理模型在连续谱通量水平和谱线轮廓上均有显著差异的高分辨发射光谱，且随着云的垂直范围和厚度增加，这些特征的变化更为显著。我们评估了由全球风和行星自转引起的光谱净多普勒位移，并显示其具有强烈的相位依赖性，尤其是在云层更厚、更扩展的模型中。本工作进一步证明了在热木星大气云模型中考虑辐射反馈的重要性，因为这对系外行星大气的光谱观测解释有显著影响。

[5] Modeling the High-resolution Emission Spectra of Clear and Cloudy Nontransiting Hot Jupiters

2021-12-00

The advent of high-resolution spectroscopy (R ≳ 25,000) as a method for characterization of exoplanet atmospheres has expanded our capability to study nontransiting planets, vastly increasing the number of planets accessible for observation. Many of the most favorable targets for atmospheric characterization are hot Jupiters, where we expect large spatial variation in physical conditions such as temperature, wind speed, and cloud coverage, making viewing geometry important. Three-dimensional models have generally simulated observational properties of hot Jupiters assuming edge-on viewing, which can be compared to observations of transiting planets, but neglected the large fraction of planets without nearly edge-on orbits. As the first investigation of how orbital inclination manifests in high-resolution emission spectra from three-dimensional models, we use a general circulation model to simulate the atmospheric structure of Upsilon Andromedae b, a typical nontransiting hot Jupiter with high observational interest, due the brightness of its host star. We compare models with and without clouds, and find that cloud coverage intensifies spatial variations by making colder regions dimmer and relatedly enhancing emission from the clear, hotter regions. This increases both the net Doppler shifts and the variation of the continuum flux amplitude over the course of the planet's orbit. In order to accurately capture scattering from clouds, we implement a generalized two-stream radiative transfer routine for inhomogeneous multiple scattering atmospheres. As orbital inclination decreases, four key features of the high-resolution emission spectra also decrease in both the clear and cloudy models: (1) the average continuum flux level, (2) the amplitude of the variation in continuum with orbital phase, (3) net Doppler shifts of spectral lines, and (4) Doppler broadening in the spectra. Models capable of treating inhomogeneous cloud coverage and different viewing geometries are critical in understanding results from high-resolution emission spectra, enabling an additional avenue to investigate these extreme atmospheres.

[5] 非凌日热木星清晰与多云大气的高分辨率发射光谱建模

高分辨率光谱（R ≳ 25,000）作为系外行星大气表征方法的出现，极大拓展了对非凌星的研究能力，大幅增加了可观测行星的数量。许多最适合大气表征的目标是热木星，其物理条件（如温度、风速和云覆盖）空间变化显著，因此观测几何非常重要。三维模型通常假设边缘视线（edge-on）观测热木星的观测性质，可与凌星观测对比，但忽略了大量非近边缘轨道的行星。作为首个研究轨道倾角如何在三维模型高分辨率发射光谱中体现的工作，我们利用全球环流模型模拟了Upsilon Andromedae b（一颗典型的非凌热木星，因其宿主星亮度高而观测兴趣大）的大气结构。我们比较了有云和无云模型，发现云覆盖增强了空间变化，使冷区更暗，同时增强了晴空热区的发射。这增加了行星轨道过程中净多普勒位移和连续谱通量幅度的变化。为准确描述云的散射，我们实现了适用于非均匀多重散射大气的广义双流辐射传输算法。随着轨道倾角减小，无论晴空还是有云模型，高分辨率发射光谱的四个关键特征均减小：（1）平均连续谱通量水平，（2）连续谱随轨道相位变化的幅度，（3）光谱线的净多普勒位移，（4）光谱中的多普勒展宽。能够处理非均匀云覆盖和不同观测几何的模型对于理解高分辨率发射光谱结果至关重要，为研究这些极端大气提供了新的途径。

[6] The coupled impact of atmospheric dynamics and cloud microphysics on WASP-43b and WASP-121b

2025-01-00

Hot and ultra-hot Jupiters are currently the best observational targets to study the effects of clouds on exoplanet atmospheres. Observations have reported westward visible phase curve offsets, weak absorption features, and nightside temperatures remaining constant with increasing instellation, which may together be explained by the presence of clouds. Although there are many models that simulate the 3D structure and circulation of hot/ultra-hot Jupiters and many microphysical models describing the formation of clouds, very few models exist that couple these two approaches. This, along with recent JWST observations unmatched by models, suggests a need for more accurate models to track the formation and transport of clouds as well as their radiative feedback on atmospheric circulation and dynamics. In this work, we couple a series of increasingly complex models by running 3D SPARC/MITgcm simulations of WASP-43b and WASP-121b, feeding the resulting temperature-pressure profiles into a 1D CARMA model simulating mineral cloud microphysics, and calculating resulting synthetic spectra with a radiative transfer code. We find that various cloud species form on the nightside and limbs of WASP-43b, perhaps explaining the most recent phase curve observations of this planet. We discuss implications for the interpretation of JWST/MIRI and JWST/NIRSpec observations of WASP-43b and WASP-121b, respectively.

[6] 大气动力学与云微物理对WASP-43b和WASP-121b的耦合影响

热木星和超热木星目前是研究云对系外行星大气影响的最佳观测目标。观测结果报告了可见光相位曲线向西偏移、吸收特征较弱以及夜面温度随入射辐射增强而保持恒定，这些现象可能都可以通过云的存在来解释。尽管已有许多模拟热/超热木星三维结构和环流的模型，以及许多描述云形成的微物理模型，但将这两种方法耦合的模型却很少。这一现状，加上近期JWST观测结果与模型不符，表明需要更精确的模型来追踪云的形成与输运，以及云对大气环流和动力学的辐射反馈。在本工作中，我们通过运行WASP-43b和WASP-121b的三维SPARC/MITgcm模拟，将得到的温度-压强剖面输入到一维CARMA模型中以模拟矿物云的微物理过程，并利用辐射传输代码计算合成光谱，从而耦合了一系列日益复杂的模型。我们发现多种云种类在WASP-43b的夜面和晨昏线形成，这或许可以解释该行星最新的相位曲线观测结果。我们还讨论了这些结果对JWST/MIRI和JWST/NIRSpec对WASP-43b和WASP-121b观测的解释意义。

[7] The golden mean: how a balanced approach to cloud modelling powers atmospheric retrieval

2024-04-00

With the launch of JWST, we are embracing an era of high-precision measurements of exoplanet atmospheres' transmission and emission spectra. In interpreting these data, the presence of clouds plays a crucial role, as clouds obscures the spectral signature and their formation consumes vapor, changing the molecular inventory. Hence, understanding clouds is instrumental to properly characterize exoplanets. However, in retrieval methods, clouds are typically imposed manually through parameters such as the cloud deck pressure, the mixing ratio, sedimentation strength, etc, which is too simplistic. At the other extreme, fully consistent modeling of clouds, which involves following their nucleation and size distribution from first principles, is prohibitively slow. Here, we have developed a streamlined 1D cloud model that solves for the steady state cloud profile, following, but vastly improving on, Ormel &amp; Min (2019). Our condensation model accommodates an arbitrary number of condensate species and operates synergistically in particle collisional growth and particle transport processes (i.e., particle settling and diffusion). A novel relaxation method ensures rapid and robust convergence under arbitrary conditions. The general design of our model enables computation of cloud structures in various planetary types, including hot-Jupiters, super-Earths and self-luminous planets. For hot-Jupiter planets, we find that cloud layers of MgSiO3, Fe and Al2O3 are formed top-down in the atmosphere, in line with the condensation sequence of these species. This result is consistent with more complex cloud models. Cloud particle radii increase with depth, reaching micron sizes in the lower regions of the cloud. Under the condition of efficient diffusion (high Kzz), the formation of clouds might dominate the planet's transmission spectrum, suppressing vapor molecular lines while concurrently displaying a distinct 10-micron silicate feature. With the computational cost on the order of seconds on a regular PC, the cloud model can easily be embedded into the majority of existing atmosphere retrieval codes.

[7] 黄金分割：平衡的云模型如何提升大气检索能力

随着JWST的发射，我们迎来了对系外行星大气透射和发射光谱进行高精度测量的时代。在解释这些数据时，云的存在起着关键作用，因为云会遮蔽光谱特征，其形成还会消耗气态分子，改变分子组成。因此，理解云对于正确表征系外行星至关重要。然而，在检索方法中，云通常通过如云层压力、混合比、沉降强度等参数手动设定，这过于简化。另一极端是完全自洽的云建模，即从第一性原理追踪云的成核和粒径分布，但这在计算上极为缓慢。我们在此开发了一种简化的1D云模型，能够求解稳态云剖面，遵循并大幅改进了Ormel & Min (2019)的方法。我们的凝结模型可处理任意数量的冷凝物种，并协同考虑粒子碰撞生长和粒子输运过程（即粒子沉降和扩散）。一种新颖的松弛方法确保在任意条件下都能快速且稳健地收敛。该模型的通用设计使其能够计算多种行星类型（包括热木星、超级地球和自发光行星）的云结构。对于热木星，我们发现MgSiO3、Fe和Al2O3的云层自上而下在大气中形成，符合这些物种的凝结序列。这一结果与更复杂的云模型一致。云粒径随深度增加，在云层下部可达微米级。在高效扩散（高Kzz）条件下，云的形成可能主导行星的透射光谱，抑制气态分子谱线，同时呈现明显的10微米硅酸盐特征。该云模型的计算成本仅需普通PC上几秒钟，可轻松嵌入现有大多数大气检索代码中。

[8] Under the magnifying glass: A combined 3D model applied to cloudy warm Saturn-type exoplanets around M dwarfs

2024-12-00

Context. Warm Saturn-type exoplanets orbiting M dwarfs are particularly suitable for an in-depth cloud characterisation through transmission spectroscopy because the contrast of their stellar to planetary radius is favourable. The global temperatures of warm Saturns suggest efficient cloud formation in their atmospheres which in return affects the temperature, velocity, and chemical structure. However, a consistent modelling of the formation processes of cloud particles within the 3D atmosphere remains computationally challenging. Aims. We explore the combined atmospheric and micro-physical cloud structure and the kinetic gas-phase chemistry of warm Saturn-like exoplanets in the irradiation field of an M dwarf. The combined modelling approach supports the interpretation of observational data from current (e.g. JWST and CHEOPS) and future missions (PLATO, Ariel, and HWO). Methods. A combined 3D cloudy atmosphere model for HATS-6b was constructed by iteratively executing the 3D general circulation model (GCM) expeRT/MITgcm and a detailed kinetic cloud formation model, each in its full complexity. The resulting cloud particle number densities, particle sizes, and material compositions were used to derive the local cloud opacity which was then used in the next GCM iteration. The disequilibrium H/C/O/N gas-phase chemistry was calculated for each iteration to assess the resulting transmission spectrum in post-processing. Results. We present the first model atmosphere that iteratively combines cloud formation and 3D GCM simulation and applied it to the warm Saturn HATS-6b. The cloud opacity feedback causes a temperature inversion at the sub-stellar point and at the evening terminator at gas pressures higher than 10<SUP>−2</SUP> bar. Furthermore, clouds cool the atmosphere between 10<SUP>−2</SUP> bar and 10 bar, and they narrow the equatorial wind jet. The transmission spectrum shows muted gas-phase absorption and a cloud particle silicate feature at ~10 μm. Conclusions. The combined atmosphere-cloud model retains the full physical complexity of each component and therefore enables a detailed physical interpretation with JWST NIRSpec and MIRI LRS observational accuracy. The model shows that warm Saturn-type exoplanets around M dwarfs are ideal candidates for a search for limb asymmetries in clouds and chemistry, for identifying the cloud particle composition by observing their spectral features, and for identifying in particular the cloud-induced strong thermal inversion that arises on these planets.

[8] 放大镜下：应用于M矮星周围多云温暖土星型系外行星的联合三维模型

背景：围绕M型矮星运行的温暖土星型系外行星由于恒星与行星半径对比度有利，非常适合通过透射光谱进行深入的云特征研究。温暖土星的全球温度表明其大气中能够高效形成云，这反过来又影响温度、速度和化学结构。然而，在三维大气中一致地模拟云粒子的形成过程在计算上仍具有挑战性。目标：我们探索在M型矮星辐照场下，温暖土星类系外行星的大气与微物理云结构及动力学气相化学。该联合建模方法有助于解释当前（如JWST和CHEOPS）及未来任务（PLATO、Ariel和HWO）的观测数据。方法：我们为HATS-6b构建了一个联合的三维多云大气模型，通过反复运行三维大气环流模型（GCM）expeRT/MITgcm和详细的动力学云形成模型，每次都保持其全部复杂性。所得的云粒子数密度、粒径和材料组成用于推导局部云不透明度，并在下一次GCM迭代中使用。每次迭代都计算非平衡H/C/O/N气相化学，以在后处理中评估最终的透射光谱。结果：我们首次提出了将云形成与三维GCM模拟迭代结合的大气模型，并将其应用于温暖土星HATS-6b。云不透明度反馈在亚恒星点和傍晚晨昏线处（气压高于10^{-2} bar）引发了温度反转。此外，云在10^{-2} bar到10 bar之间冷却大气，并收窄了赤道风射流。透射光谱显示气相吸收减弱，并在约10 μm处出现云粒子硅酸盐特征。结论：该联合大气-云模型保留了各自组分的全部物理复杂性，因此能够以JWST NIRSpec和MIRI LRS的观测精度进行详细物理解释。模型显示，围绕M型矮星的温暖土星型系外行星是寻找晨昏线云和化学不对称性、通过观测光谱特征识别云粒子组成，以及识别这些行星上由云引发的强烈热反转的理想目标。
