8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] The Hazy and Metal-rich Atmosphere of GJ 1214 b Constrained by Near- and Mid-infrared Transmission Spectroscopy

2023-07-00

The near-infrared transmission spectrum of the warm sub-Neptune exoplanet GJ 1214 b has been observed to be flat and featureless, implying a high metallicity atmosphere with abundant aerosols. Recent JWST MIRI Low Resolution Spectrometer observations of a phase curve of GJ 1214 b showed that its transmission spectrum is flat out into the mid-infrared. In this paper, we use the combined near- and mid-infrared transmission spectrum of GJ 1214 b to constrain its atmospheric composition and aerosol properties. We generate a grid of photochemical haze models using an aerosol microphysics code for a number of background atmospheres spanning metallicities from 100 to 1000× solar, as well as a steam atmosphere scenario. The flatness of the combined data set largely rules out atmospheric metallicities ≤300× solar due to their large corresponding molecular feature amplitudes, preferring values ≥1000× solar and column haze production rates ≥10<SUP>−10</SUP> g cm<SUP>−2</SUP> s<SUP>−1</SUP>. The steam atmosphere scenario with similarly high haze production rates also exhibits sufficiently small molecular features to be consistent with the transmission spectrum. These compositions imply that atmospheric mean molecular weights ≥15 g mol<SUP>−1</SUP> are needed to fit the data. Our results suggest that haze production is highly efficient on GJ 1214 b and could involve non-hydrocarbon, non-nitrogen haze precursors. Further characterization of GJ 1214 b's atmosphere would likely require multiple transits and eclipses using JWST across the near- and mid-infrared, potentially complemented by ground-based high-resolution transmission spectroscopy.

[1] GJ 1214 b的有雾且富金属大气：近红外和中红外透射光谱约束

温暖的亚海王星系外行星GJ 1214 b的近红外透射光谱表现为平坦且无特征，暗示其大气具有高金属丰度并富含气溶胶。近期JWST MIRI低分辨率光谱仪对GJ 1214 b的相位曲线观测显示，其透射光谱在中红外波段同样平坦。本文利用GJ 1214 b的近红外和中红外透射光谱联合约束其大气成分和气溶胶性质。我们利用气溶胶微物理代码，针对背景大气金属丰度从100到1000倍太阳金属丰度，以及蒸汽大气情景，生成了一组光化学薄雾模型。联合数据的平坦性基本排除了金属丰度≤300倍太阳的情形，因为其分子特征幅度过大，更倾向于金属丰度≥1000倍太阳和柱状薄雾生成速率≥10^−10 g cm^−2 s^−1。蒸汽大气情景下，若薄雾生成速率同样很高，也能产生足够小的分子特征，与透射光谱一致。这些成分意味着拟合数据需要大气平均分子量≥15 g mol^−1。我们的结果表明，GJ 1214 b上的薄雾生成效率极高，且可能涉及非烃类、非氮类的薄雾前体。进一步表征GJ 1214 b的大气可能需要JWST在近红外和中红外多次凌日和掩食观测，并可辅以地基高分辨率透射光谱。

[2] A Possible Metal-dominated Atmosphere below the Thick Aerosols of GJ 1214 b Suggested by Its JWST Panchromatic Transmission Spectrum

2025-01-00

GJ 1214b is the archetype sub-Neptune for which thick aerosols have prevented us from constraining its atmospheric properties for over a decade. In this study, we leverage the panchromatic transmission spectrum of GJ 1214b established by the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and JWST to investigate its atmospheric properties using a suite of atmospheric radiative transfer, photochemistry, and aerosol microphysical models. We find that the combined HST, JWST/NIRSpec, and JWST/MIRI spectrum can be well explained by atmospheric models with an extremely high metallicity of [M/H] ∼ 3.5 and an extremely high haze production rate of F<SUB>haze</SUB> ∼ 10<SUP>−8</SUP> to 10<SUP>−7</SUP> g cm<SUP>−2</SUP> s<SUP>−1</SUP>. Such high atmospheric metallicity is suggested by the relatively strong CO<SUB>2</SUB> feature compared to the haze absorption feature or the CH<SUB>4</SUB> feature in the NIRSpec-G395H bandpass of 2.5─5 μm. The flat 5─12 μm MIRI spectrum also suggests a small scale height with a high atmospheric metallicity that is needed to suppress a prominent ∼6 μm haze feature. We tested the sensitivity of our interpretation to various assumptions for uncertain haze properties, such as optical constants and production rate, and all models tested here consistently suggest extremely high metallicity. Thus, we conclude that GJ 1214b likely has a metal-dominated atmosphere where hydrogen is no longer the main atmospheric constituent. We also find that different assumptions for the haze production rate lead to distinct inferences for the atmospheric C/O ratio. We stress the importance of high-precision follow-up observations to confirm the metal-dominated atmosphere, as it challenges the conventional understanding of interior structure and evolution of sub-Neptunes.

[2] JWST全波段透射光谱暗示GJ 1214 b厚气溶胶下可能为金属主导大气

GJ 1214b是亚海王星的典型代表，十多年来其厚气溶胶层一直阻碍我们约束其大气性质。在本研究中，我们利用哈勃空间望远镜（HST）和JWST建立的GJ 1214b全波段透射光谱，结合一系列大气辐射传输、光化学和气溶胶微物理模型，研究其大气性质。我们发现，HST、JWST/NIRSpec和JWST/MIRI联合光谱可以通过大气金属丰度极高（[M/H] ∼ 3.5）且气溶胶生成率极高（Fhaze ∼ 10⁻⁸至10⁻⁷ g cm⁻² s⁻¹）的模型很好地解释。如此高的大气金属丰度由NIRSpec-G395H波段（2.5─5 μm）中CO2特征相对气溶胶吸收特征或CH4特征的强度所指示。平坦的5─12 μm MIRI光谱也表明尺度高度较小，需要高金属丰度以抑制显著的约6 μm气溶胶特征。我们测试了对气溶胶性质（如光学常数和生成率）不同假设下的解释敏感性，所有模型均一致指向极高金属丰度。因此，我们认为GJ 1214b很可能拥有以金属为主的大气，氢已不再是主要成分。我们还发现，不同的气溶胶生成率假设会导致对大气C/O比的不同推断。我们强调需要高精度后续观测以确认金属主导大气，因为这对亚海王星的内部结构和演化的传统理解提出了挑战。

[3] Clouds and Hazes in GJ 1214 b's Metal-rich Atmosphere

2025-04-00

The sub-Neptune GJ 1214 b has an infamously flat transmission spectrum, likely due to thick aerosols in its atmosphere. A recent JWST/MIRI spectroscopic phase curve of GJ 1214 b added to this picture, suggesting a highly reflective and metal-rich atmosphere. Using a 3D general circulation model with both photochemical hazes and condensate clouds, we characterize how different aerosol types affect the atmospheric structure of GJ 1214 b and manifest in its spectroscopic phase curve. Additionally, we reanalyze the original GJ 1214 b JWST phase curve. The reanalysis shows a hotter nightside, similar dayside temperature, and a lower, but still elevated, Bond albedo (0.42 ± 0.11) in comparison to the original results. We find that a scenario with both clouds and hazes is most consistent with the JWST phase curve. Reflective clouds or hazes are needed to explain the large Bond albedo, and hazes or a supersolar metallicity help account for the several hundred kelvin day─night temperature difference measured by the phase curve.

[3] GJ 1214 b富金属大气中的云和薄雾

亚海王星GJ 1214 b以其极为平坦的透射光谱著称，这很可能是由于其大气中存在大量气溶胶。最近的JWST/MIRI光谱相位曲线观测进一步丰富了这一图景，表明其大气高度反射且金属丰度较高。我们利用包含光化学烟雾和冷凝云的三维大气环流模型，研究了不同类型气溶胶如何影响GJ 1214 b的大气结构，并在其光谱相位曲线中表现出来。此外，我们还重新分析了GJ 1214 b最初的JWST相位曲线。重新分析结果显示夜面温度更高，日面温度相似，Bond反照率较原始结果更低但仍然较高（0.42 ± 0.11）。我们发现同时存在云和烟雾的情景与JWST相位曲线最为一致。需要反射性云或烟雾来解释较高的Bond反照率，而烟雾或超太阳金属丰度有助于解释相位曲线测得的数百开尔文的昼夜温差。

[4] Characterising the atmosphere of GJ1214 b with JWST observations.

2024-09-00

GJ-1214b, discovered by Charbonneau et al. (2009), is a transiting super-Earth/mini-Neptune with an M4.5-type host star at 14.6pc from the Sun. GJ-1214b is notable for its featureless transmission spectrum (Gillon et al., 2014; Kreidberg et al., 2014), which has been interpreted as evidence for the presence of clouds (ZnS, KCL) or photochemical hazes (Morley et al. 2013, 2015; Charnay et al. 2015; Gao and Benneke 2018; Ohno &amp; Okuzumi 2018, Adam et al. 2019; Kawasima et al. 2019; Lavvas et al. 2019; Ohno et al. 2020; Christie et al. 2022). Latest observations of GJ 1214 b with JWST reveal a high-albedo atmosphere (Kempton et al. 2023), possibly rich in photochemical hazes (Gao et al. 2023). We use a self-consistent model coupling among photochemistry, radiative transfer, thermal structure and haze &amp; cloud microphysics to characterise the atmosphere of GJ1214b. We explore different metallicity cases to constrain the atmospheric composition and evaluate different scenarios for the photochemical haze mass fluxes and the eddy mixing efficient. We particularly focus on the interplay between photochemical hazes and clouds, considering the formation of ZnS, KCL and NaCL clouds on hazes.We demonstrate that the novel JWST thermal emission observations provide much better constraints on the atmospheric metallicity compared to transit spectra alone, limiting the retrieved metallicity to values between ~1000x-3000x solar. Our results demonstrate that the atmospheric albedo is in the order of 10-20%, considering both the contributions of hazes and clouds. Transit spectra are mostly affected by hazes rather than clouds, but the implications of the clouds on the atmospheric thermal structure provide an indirect effect to affect the transit signature. We further discuss the role of sulfur chemistry in the formation of the haze and its potential signature in the transit spectra.

[4] 利用JWST观测对GJ1214 b大气的表征

GJ-1214b由Charbonneau等（2009）发现，是一颗凌日的超级地球/迷你海王星，母星为M4.5型，距离太阳14.6pc。GJ-1214b以其无特征的透射光谱（Gillon等，2014；Kreidberg等，2014）而著称，这被解释为存在云（ZnS, KCL）或光化学薄雾的证据（Morley等2013, 2015；Charnay等2015；Gao和Benneke 2018；Ohno & Okuzumi 2018，Adam等2019；Kawasima等2019；Lavvas等2019；Ohno等2020；Christie等2022）。JWST对GJ 1214 b的最新观测显示其大气具有高反照率（Kempton等2023），可能富含光化学薄雾（Gao等2023）。我们使用一个自洽模型，耦合光化学、辐射传输、热结构以及薄雾和云的微物理过程，对GJ1214b的大气进行刻画。我们探索不同金属丰度情形，以约束大气组成，并评估不同光化学薄雾质量通量和湍流混合效率的情景。我们特别关注光化学薄雾与云之间的相互作用，考虑ZnS、KCL和NaCL云在薄雾上的形成。我们展示了新颖的JWST热发射观测相比仅用凌日光谱能更好地约束大气金属丰度，将检索到的金属丰度限制在约1000倍至3000倍太阳值之间。我们的结果表明，大气反照率在10-20%量级，考虑了薄雾和云的共同贡献。透射光谱主要受薄雾影响而非云，但云对大气热结构的影响会间接影响凌日信号。我们进一步讨论了硫化学在薄雾形成中的作用及其在透射光谱中的潜在特征。

[5] Photochemical hazes &amp; clouds in the atmosphere of GJ 1214 b in view of recent JWST observations

2023-10-00

GJ-1214b, discovered by Charbonneau et al. (2009), is a transiting super-Earth/mini-Neptune with an M4.5-type host star at 14.6pc from the Sun. GJ-1214b is notable for its featureless transmission spectrum (Gillon et al., 2014; Kreidberg et al., 2014), which has been interpreted as evidence for the presence of clouds (ZnS, KCL) or photochemical hazes (Morley et al. 2013, 2015; Charnay et al. 2015; Gao and Benneke 2018; Ohno &amp; Okuzumi 2018, Adam et al. 2019; Kawasima et al. 2019; Lavvas et al. 2019; Ohno et al. 2020; Christie et al. 2022). Latest observations of GJ 1214 b with JWST reveal a high-albedo atmosphere (Kempton et al. 2023), possibly rich in photochemical hazes (Gao et al. 2023). We explore in this study if the atmospheric photochemistry could generate sufficient haze to explain these observations, or if additional cloud contributions are necessary. We particularly focus on the interplay between photochemical hazes and clouds, considering the formation of ZnS, KCL and NaCL on hazes. We explore how different haze mass flux assumptions affect the resulting cloud particle properties, as well as, the roles of atmospheric mixing and atmospheric metallicity on the resulting particles distribution, and how each scenario compares with the available observational constraints.

[5] GJ 1214 b大气中的光化学烟雾和云——结合近期JWST观测

GJ-1214b 由 Charbonneau 等（2009）发现，是一颗凌日的超级地球/迷你海王星，母星为 M4.5 型，距离太阳 14.6pc。GJ-1214b 以其无特征的透射光谱（Gillon et al., 2014; Kreidberg et al., 2014）著称，这被解释为存在云（ZnS, KCL）或光化学薄雾（Morley et al. 2013, 2015; Charnay et al. 2015; Gao and Benneke 2018; Ohno & Okuzumi 2018, Adam et al. 2019; Kawasima et al. 2019; Lavvas et al. 2019; Ohno et al. 2020; Christie et al. 2022）。最新 JWST 对 GJ 1214 b 的观测显示其大气具有高反照率（Kempton et al. 2023），可能富含光化学薄雾（Gao et al. 2023）。本研究探讨大气光化学过程是否能生成足够的薄雾以解释这些观测，或是否还需要额外的云贡献。我们特别关注光化学薄雾与云的相互作用，考虑 ZnS、KCL 和 NaCL 在薄雾上的形成。我们研究不同薄雾质量通量假设对最终云粒子性质的影响，以及大气混合和金属丰度对粒子分布的作用，并将每种情景与现有观测约束进行比较。

[6] Can we Keep Assuming Grey Exoclouds?

2023-07-00

Clouds and hazes like those in most of the Solar System atmospheres are probably present in many of the exoplanetary atmospheres. They leave a clear signal in the transmission spectrum as the starlight passes through them, sometimes reducing, or even removing, gaseous absorption bands [Kreidberg, L., 2018]. Determining the presence and effect of such clouds is therefore essential to retrieve other atmospheric parameters. Most of the previous spectroscopic studies of giant exoplanet atmospheres have assumed grey clouds with a flat transmittance all over the spectrum [Barstow, J. K. et al, 2017]. This is done for simplicity, although it does not realistically represent any known aerosol layer in the solar system, at least when viewed through a wide enough spectral range. However, the use of such an approximation has been very useful to study atmospheric properties of other planetary bodies, as the use of more complex models does not lead to a representative improvement of the results, besides being much easier to simulate. The launch of the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) has provided the opportunity to obtain spectra over a wider wavelength-range, allowing a more thorough exploration of exoplanet clouds. The wide spectral range of JWST observations allows disentangling gaseous absorption in the atmosphere from the extinction generated by the clouds, which is usually flatter and smoother, in the absence of particle absorption bands. In this study, we will use a Bayesian approach to analyze the publicly available JWST data from the primary transit spectrum of WASP-39b [Rustamkulov, Z. et al, 2023]. We not only retrieve temperature, planetary radius and relevant abundances but also study the statistical significance of adding a more complex and realistic representation of atmospheric aerosols, in order to answer the opening question: can we keep assuming grey exoclouds? Or should we start moving to something more resembling the solar system clouds and hazes? <P />Kreidberg, L., 2018. [6] 我们还能继续假设灰色系外云吗？

类似于太阳系大多数行星大气中的云和薄雾，很可能也存在于许多系外行星大气中。当恒星光穿过这些云和薄雾时，它们会在透射光谱中留下明显信号，有时会减弱甚至完全消除气体吸收带 [Kreidberg, L., 2018]。因此，确定此类云的存在及其影响对于检索其他大气参数至关重要。此前对巨型系外行星大气的光谱学研究大多假设灰色云，即在整个光谱范围内具有平坦透射率的云层 [Barstow, J. K. et al, 2017]。这样做是为了简化处理，尽管这并不能真实反映太阳系中已知的任何气溶胶层，至少在足够宽的光谱范围内并非如此。然而，这种近似的使用对于研究其他行星天体的大气性质非常有用，因为采用更复杂的模型并不会显著提升结果的代表性，同时模拟也更为容易。詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）的发射为获取更宽波长范围的光谱提供了机会，使得对系外行星云层的研究更加深入。JWST观测的宽光谱范围能够将大气中的气体吸收与云层产生的消光区分开来，后者在没有粒子吸收带的情况下通常更平坦、更平滑。在本研究中，我们将采用贝叶斯方法分析WASP-39b初级凌日光谱的公开JWST数据 [Rustamkulov, Z. et al, 2023]。我们不仅检索温度、行星半径和相关丰度，还研究采用更复杂、更真实的大气气溶胶表示方式的统计显著性，以回答开篇问题：我们还能继续假设灰色系外云吗？还是应该开始转向更类似于太阳系云和薄雾的模型？ Kreidberg, L., 2018. [7] Resolving the flat-spectrum conundrum: clumpy aerosol distributions in sub-Neptune atmospheres

2026-01-00

Transmission spectroscopy of sub-Neptunes was expected to reveal their compositions and hence origins, yet many show flat near- to mid-infrared spectra. Such spectra can be explained either by metal-dominated atmospheres or by high-altitude grey aerosols. Observations of escaping hydrogen and helium from several of these planets rule out metal-dominated atmospheres, while homogeneous distributions of small aerosols cannot produce flat spectra and large particles require unphysically high production rates. We investigate the role of heterogeneous 'clumpy' aerosol distributions in shaping transmission spectra. Modestly optically thick clumps at high altitudes can produce flat spectra even with small particles and physically realistic production rates. Clumping increases the effective photon mean-free path while reducing wavelength dependence, allowing the aerosol distribution to behave as an effective grey absorber. Applying this framework to the sub-Neptune TOI-776c, we show that clumpy aerosols can reconcile the observed flattening of its transmission spectrum with a primordial H/He-dominated atmosphere. We further discuss implications for emission spectra, where enhanced stellar radiation penetration and altered scattering in a clumpy medium could produce observable signatures. These results suggest that clumpy aerosol distributions naturally resolve the tension between flat spectra and low-metallicity atmospheres and may be a common feature of sub-Neptune exoplanets. More broadly, our results highlight the need to consider aerosol heterogeneity when interpreting high-altitude microphysics and the spectral appearance of exoplanet atmospheres with James Webb Space Telescope, and motivate theoretical work to identify the physical mechanisms capable of generating clumpy aerosol distributions.

[7] 解决平坦光谱难题：亚海王星大气中的团簇气溶胶分布

人们原本期望通过对亚海王星的透射光谱研究揭示其组成及起源，然而许多亚海王星在近红外到中红外波段表现出平坦的光谱。这类光谱既可由金属主导的大气解释，也可由高空灰色气溶胶造成。对其中若干行星逃逸的氢和氦的观测排除了金属主导的大气，而均匀分布的小气溶胶无法产生平坦光谱，大颗粒则需要不现实的高生成率。我们研究了非均匀“团簇”气溶胶分布在塑造透射光谱中的作用。高空中适度光学厚度的团簇，即使由小颗粒组成且生成率物理上合理，也能产生平坦光谱。团簇结构增加了有效光子平均自由程，同时减弱了波长依赖性，使气溶胶分布表现为有效的灰色吸收体。将该框架应用于亚海王星TOI-776c，我们表明团簇气溶胶可以使其观测到的透射光谱变平，与原始H/He主导大气相符。我们进一步讨论了对发射光谱的影响，在团簇介质中增强的恒星辐射穿透和改变的散射可能产生可观测特征。这些结果表明，团簇气溶胶分布可自然解决平坦光谱与低金属丰度大气之间的矛盾，并可能是亚海王星系外行星的普遍特征。更广泛地说，我们的结果强调了解释JWST等望远镜观测的系外行星大气高空微物理和光谱外观时，必须考虑气溶胶的非均匀性，并激励理论工作去识别能够产生团簇气溶胶分布的物理机制。

[8] Tightest Constraints to Date on the Cloudy Atmosphere of GJ 436 B with Carmenes

2025-00-00

Recent advancements in exoplanet demographics have revealed that sub-Neptunes (2-3 R⨁) are the most frequent outcome for planet formation in the Milky Way. However, little is known about their interior and atmospheric composition, as most observable candidates exhibit featureless transmission spectra, making their formation and evolution conditions rather unknown. In fact, studies of planets such as 55 Cnc e, HD 106315 c, and HD 3167 c have ruled out the possibility of cloud-free or low-metallicity atmospheres in these worlds. Fortunately, high-resolution spectroscopy (HRS) provides a way to probe above potential cloud decks by resolving the cores of deep molecular absorption lines. This approach complements space-based observations and allows for tighter constraints on the interplay between abundance and cloud pressure through injection-recovery tests, reducing uncertainties in retrieved metallicities and C/O ratios. Theoretical studies predict the formation of opaque clouds in the infrared to depend on equilibrium temperatures, and UV irradiation in Neptunes and sub-Neptunes. This is the case for the warm Neptune GJ 436 b, whose atmosphere has been recently analysed with James Webb Space Telescope (JWST), with a tentative detection of CO2 (2σ). However, the absence of strong transmission features at HRS does suggest a role for cloud opacity. In this talk, I will present our analysis of the atmosphere of GJ 436 b, based on five transits observed with the NIR channel of the CARMENES spectrograph. Using the cross-correlation technique, we explore its atmospheric composition at high resolution, aiming to uncover key molecular signatures. After detecting no significant molecular absorption features, our injection-recovery tests suggest that GJ 436 b likely hosts a cloudy, metal-enriched atmosphere, consistent with recent JWST and CRIRES⁺ findings. Moreover, while comparing our results with a single-transit CRIRES⁺ study, our multi-night HRS analysis significantly enhances constraints on cloudy exoplanet atmospheres, highlighting the power of ground-based HRS in characterising atmospheres that remain elusive at lower resolutions.

[8] 利用Carmenes对GJ 436 B多云大气的迄今最严格约束

系外行星人口统计的最新进展显示，亚海王星（2-3 R⨁）是银河系行星形成最常见的结果。然而，我们对其内部和大气成分知之甚少，因为大多数可观测候选体表现出无特征的透射光谱，使其形成和演化条件变得难以了解。事实上，对55 Cnc e、HD 106315 c和HD 3167 c等行星的研究已经排除了这些天体存在无云或低金属丰度大气的可能性。幸运的是，高分辨率光谱（HRS）通过解析深层分子吸收线的线芯，提供了一种探测潜在云层之上的方法。这一方法可与空间观测互补，并通过注入-恢复测试对丰度与云层压力之间的相互作用施加更严格的约束，从而减少检索金属丰度和C/O比的不确定性。理论研究预测，红外波段不透明云的形成取决于平衡温度，以及海王星和亚海王星中的紫外照射。温暖的海王星GJ 436 b就是这种情况，其大气最近已通过James Webb Space Telescope（JWST）分析，CO2有待确认的探测（2σ）。然而，在HRS下未见强透射特征，表明云的不透明性可能起到作用。在本报告中，我将介绍我们基于CARMENES光谱仪NIR通道观测的五次凌日，对GJ 436 b大气的分析。我们利用互相关技术，在高分辨率下探索其大气成分，旨在揭示关键分子特征。在未探测到显著分子吸收特征后，我们的注入-恢复测试表明，GJ 436 b很可能拥有多云且金属富集的大气，这与JWST和CRIRES⁺的最新结果一致。此外，将我们的结果与单次凌日CRIRES⁺研究对比，我们的多夜HRS分析显著增强了对多云系外行星大气的约束，突显了地基HRS在表征低分辨率下难以探测大气方面的能力。

How do atmospheric tides and core-mantle friction interact to determine the spin evolution and equilibrium rotation states of Earth-sized exoplanets, especially in the habitable zone?

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Improving tidal modeling for rocky worlds

2022-09-00

The high number of discovered close-in planets motivates the improvement of tidal modeling.Among the five thousand exoplanets discovered up to now, half of them have an orbital period of less than 10 days and therefore experience some form of tidal evolution. Besides, the habitable zone of ultra-cool dwarfs is located close-in (e.g. TRAPPIST-1, Gillon et al. 2017), so planets in the habitable zone of these dwarfs are expected to undergo tidal evolution. By impacting the orbital and rotational dynamics of close-in planets, tides have an impact on their habitability. Indeed, tides drive the spin evolution of planets, influence their thermal state by internal friction (Henning &amp; Hurford 2014, Bolmont et al. 2020), and drive their orbital evolution by exchange of angular momentum. This leads to the migration of the planets, their eccentricity and obliquity damping, and the precession of their orbits ( e.g Hut 1981, Bolmont et al. 2011, 2012, Ragozzine &amp; Wolf 2009). Solid planetary tides also act to synchronize the spin of planets on circular orbits with their orbital motion. It is generally assumed that close-in planets (i.e. experiencing strong tidal interactions) have reached this state of synchronization, also called the 1:1 spin-orbit resonance (hereafter SOR). However, in the case of an eccentric or inclined orbit, tides can trap the spin in higher rotation rates, e.g. in the 3:2 or the 2:1 SOR. If a planet has an atmosphere, another tidal mechanism should be taken into account: the thermal tide, which is caused by the differential heating between day and night sides (Correia &amp; Laskar 2001, Auclair-Desrotour 2017a). In the case of thick atmospheres such as that of Venus, the thermal tides acting on the atmosphere can be as strong as the gravitational tides acting on the solid core (Auclair-Desrotour et al. 2017b) and can both de-synchronize the planet and increase the spin inclination. The competition between the two tides, solid and thermal can be seen in figure [1]. Figure [1]:The two tidal contributions, gravitational and thermal, acting in opposition to each other. The solide tide drives the spin to synchronization, while the thermal tide drives it away from it.Tidal modelTo understand the evolution of exoplanets, we need a consistent tidal model which encapsulates the complex response of rocky planets to stress. Most models assume that planets are made of weakly viscous fluid (e.g. Hut 1981, Goldreich &amp; Soter 1966) even for rocky planets. However, it has been shown that they do not reproduce the correct behavior for highly viscous solid bodies (Henning et al. 2009; Efroimsky &amp; Makarov 2013). In contrast, we use here a model which accounts for more realistic anelastic behavior for the rocks (such as the Andrade rheology) for a Venus-like planet (Dumoulin et al. 2017). We choose here an approach developed by Kaula (1964), which consists in using a decomposition of the tidal potential into Fourier harmonic modes. For the thermal tide, we use the analytical formulation of Leconte et al (2015), which aims at reproducing Venus today. Our developments were added to the ESPEM code (Benbakoura et al. 2019) to compute the orbital and rotational evolution of a Venus-like planet around a Sun-like star. 2) A complex rotational evolution:Figure [2] shows the evolution of a Venus-like planet, with and without an atmosphere, orbiting a Sun-like planet with an initial obliquity of 50 degrees and a rotation of about 100 days and on a circular orbit. No atmosphere (red curve):When the planet does not have an atmosphere, the rotation spins down to synchronization in 100 million years. Interestingly and despite the absence of an eccentricity, Figure [2] shows that the obliquity of the planet allows the presence of the 2:0 spin-orbit resonance in which the planet spends a few 10 million years. The planet leaves this resonance when the obliquity becomes too low to maintain this configuration stable. With atmosphere (blue curve):The blue curve of Fig. [2] shows the effect of a strong CO2 atmosphere on the evolution of the spin and the obliquity. The evolution here is more complex. First, the combination of the two tides damps the spin toward the 2:1 SOR while increasing the obliquity. When the obliquity becomes high enough, the thermal torque begins to counter-balance the solid torque. The thermal tides continue to increase the obliquity and the spin of the planet until an equilibrium state between the solid and thermal tides is found. Depending on the atmospheric parameters, the evolutions we obtain are complex, with spin-orbit resonances, coming either from the eccentricity or from the obliquity of the planet. I will show the variety of different spin evolutions of a Venus-like planet, and how it depends on the presence of an eccentricity or obliquity and the presence or not of a thick atmosphere.Figure [2]Simulation of a Venus-like planet orbiting a Sun-like star, without an atmosphere (in red) and with an atmosphere (in blue). Top panel: evolution of the ratio of the planetary spin Ω and the mean motion n. Bottom panel: evolution of the obliquity of the planet (defined as the angle between the equatorial plane and the orbital plane).

[1] 改进岩石类行星的潮汐建模

大量近轨系外行星的发现推动了潮汐建模的改进。在迄今发现的五千颗系外行星中，有一半的轨道周期小于10天，因此会经历某种形式的潮汐演化。此外，超冷矮星的宜居带位于近轨（如TRAPPIST-1，Gillon等2017），因此这些矮星宜居带内的行星预计也会经历潮汐演化。潮汐通过影响近轨行星的轨道和自转动力学，进而影响其宜居性。实际上，潮汐驱动行星自转演化，通过内部摩擦影响其热状态（Henning & Hurford 2014, Bolmont等2020），并通过角动量交换驱动轨道演化。这导致行星的迁移、偏心率和自转轴倾角的阻尼，以及轨道的进动（如Hut 1981, Bolmont等2011, 2012, Ragozzine & Wolf 2009）。固体行星潮汐还会使圆轨道上的行星自转与轨道运动同步。通常认为，近轨行星（即经历强潮汐作用的行星）已达到这种同步状态，也称为1:1自转-轨道共振（以下简称SOR）。然而，在偏心或倾斜轨道的情况下，潮汐可将自转锁定在更高的自转速率，如3:2或2:1 SOR。如果行星有大气，还需考虑另一种潮汐机制：热潮汐，由昼夜面之间的差异加热引起（Correia & Laskar 2001, Auclair-Desrotour 2017a）。对于如金星那样厚大气的行星，作用于大气的热潮汐可与作用于固体核的引力潮汐一样强（Auclair-Desrotour等2017b），并可使行星去同步化并增加自转轴倾角。两种潮汐（固体和热潮汐）之间的竞争见图[1]。图[1]：两种潮汐贡献，引力潮汐和热潮汐，相互对抗。固体潮汐驱动自转同步，而热潮汐则使其远离同步。潮汐模型：为理解系外行星的演化，我们需要一个能够反映岩石行星复杂应力响应的一致性潮汐模型。大多数模型假设行星由弱粘性流体组成（如Hut 1981, Goldreich & Soter 1966），即使是岩石行星也是如此。然而，已有研究表明，这些模型无法再现高粘性固体天体的正确行为（Henning等2009；Efroimsky & Makarov 2013）。相比之下，我们在此采用了能够描述岩石更真实非弹性行为（如Andrade流变学）的模型，针对类金星行星（Dumoulin等2017）。我们采用Kaula（1964）提出的方法，将潮汐势分解为傅里叶谐波模式。对于热潮汐，我们采用Leconte等（2015）的解析公式，旨在再现当今金星的情况。我们的开发已集成到ESPEM代码（Benbakoura等2019）中，用于计算类金星行星围绕类太阳恒星的轨道和自转演化。2）复杂的自转演化：图[2]展示了类金星行星在有无大气情况下，围绕类太阳恒星运行，初始自转轴倾角为50度，自转周期约100天，轨道为圆轨道的演化过程。无大气（红色曲线）：当行星没有大气时，自转在1亿年内减速至同步。有趣的是，尽管偏心率为零，图[2]显示行星的自转轴倾角允许2:0自转-轨道共振的存在，行星在该共振中停留了几千万年。当自转轴倾角降至无法维持该结构的稳定性时，行星离开该共振。有大气（蓝色曲线）：图[2]的蓝色曲线显示了强CO2大气对自转和自转轴倾角演化的影响。此时演化更为复杂。首先，两种潮汐的共同作用将自转阻尼至2:1 SOR，同时增加自转轴倾角。当自转轴倾角足够高时，热潮汐力开始抵消固体潮汐力。热潮汐继续增加行星的自转轴倾角和自转速率，直到固体和热潮汐达到平衡状态。根据大气参数的不同，我们得到的演化过程复杂，存在自转-轨道共振，既可能由偏心率也可能由自转轴倾角引起。我将展示类金星行星自转演化的多样性，以及其如何依赖于偏心率、自转轴倾角和厚大气的存在与否。图[2]：类金星行星围绕类太阳恒星运行的模拟，无大气（红色）和有大气（蓝色）。上图：行星自转Ω与平均运动n的比值演化。下图：行星自转轴倾角（定义为赤道面与轨道面的夹角）演化。

[2] The Feasibility of Asynchronous Rotation via Thermal Tides for Diverse Atmospheric Compositions

2024-10-00

The equilibrium rotation rate of a planet is determined by the sum of torques acting on its solid body. For planets with atmospheres, the dominant torques are usually the gravitational tide, which acts to slow the planet's rotation rate, and the atmospheric thermal tide, which acts to spin up the planet. Previous work demonstrated that rocky planets with thick atmospheres may produce strong enough thermal tides to avoid tidal locking, but a study of how the strength of the thermal tide depends on atmospheric properties has not been done. In this work, we use a combination of simulations from a global climate model and analytic theory to explore how the thermal tide depends on the shortwave and longwave optical depth of the atmosphere, the surface pressure, and the absorbed stellar radiation. We find that for planets in the habitable zones of M stars only high-pressure but low-opacity atmospheres permit asynchronous rotation owing to the weakening of the thermal tide at high longwave and shortwave optical depths. We conclude that asynchronous rotation may be very unlikely around low-mass stars, which may limit the potential habitability of planets around M stars.

[2] 通过热潮汐实现不同大气成分下的非同步自转的可行性

行星的平衡自转速率由作用于其固体本体的所有力矩之和决定。对于有大气的行星，主导力矩通常为减缓自转的引力潮汐和加速自转的大气热潮汐。此前研究表明，拥有稠密大气的类地行星可能产生足够强的热潮汐以避免潮汐锁定，但热潮汐强度如何依赖于大气属性尚未系统研究。本文结合全球气候模式模拟和解析理论，探讨了热潮汐对大气短波和长波光学厚度、地表压强及吸收恒星辐射的依赖性。我们发现，对于 M 型恒星宜居带内的行星，只有高压但低光学厚度的大气才能因高长波和短波光学厚度下热潮汐减弱而实现非同步自转。我们得出结论，低质量恒星周围的非同步自转可能极为罕见，这可能限制 M 型恒星周围行星的潜在宜居性。

[3] Effects of Spin-Orbit Resonances and Tidal Heating on the Inner Edge of the Habitable Zone

2021-11-00

Much attention has been given to the climate dynamics and habitable boundaries of synchronously rotating planets around low mass stars. However, other rotational states are possible, including spin-orbit resonant configurations, particularly when higher eccentricity orbits can be maintained in a system. Additionally, the oscillating strain as a planet moves from periastron to apoastron results in friction and tidal heating, which can be an important energy source. Here, we simulate the climate of ocean-covered planets near the inner edge of the habitable zone around M to solar stars with the NASA GISS ROCKE-3D general circulation model, and leverage the planetary evolution software package, VPLanet, to calculate tidal heating rates for Earth-sized planets orbiting 2600 and 3000 K stars. This study is the first to use a 3D general circulation model that implements tidal heating to investigate habitability for multiple resonant states. We find that for reference experiments without tidal heating, the resonant state has little impact on the radial position of the inner edge because for a given stellar flux, higher-order states tend to be warmer than synchronous rotators, but for a given temperature, have drier upper atmospheres. However, when strong tidal heating is present, the rotational component implies a strong dependence of habitable conditions on the system evolution and rotational state. Since tidal and stellar heating both decrease rapidly with orbital distance, this results in a compact orbital width separating temperate and uninhabitable climates. We summarize these results and also compare ROCKE-3D to previously published simulations of the inner edge.

[3] 自转-公转共振与潮汐加热对宜居带内缘的影响

同步自转行星在低质量恒星周围的气候动力学和宜居边界已受到广泛关注。然而，其他自转状态也是可能的，包括自转-公转共振构型，尤其是在系统中能维持较高偏心率轨道时。此外，行星从近日点到远日点运动时的应变振荡会导致摩擦和潮汐加热，这可能是重要的能量来源。本文利用NASA GISS ROCKE-3D全球环流模型，模拟了M型到太阳型恒星宜居带内缘附近的海洋覆盖行星气候，并结合行星演化软件包VPLanet，计算了围绕2600 K和3000 K恒星运行的类地行星的潮汐加热率。本研究首次使用实现潮汐加热的三维全球环流模型，探讨了多种共振状态下的宜居性。我们发现，在无潮汐加热的参考实验中，共振状态对内缘的径向位置影响很小，因为在给定恒星辐射通量下，高阶共振状态通常比同步自转更暖，但在给定温度下，其高层大气更干燥。然而，当存在强潮汐加热时，自转分量导致宜居条件对系统演化和自转状态高度敏感。由于潮汐加热和恒星加热均随轨道距离迅速减小，这导致温带与不可居住气候之间的轨道宽度变得非常狭窄。我们总结了这些结果，并将ROCKE-3D与先前发表的内缘模拟进行了比较。

[4] Sporadic Spin-orbit Variations in Compact Multiplanet Systems and Their Influence on Exoplanet Climate

2023-04-00

Climate modeling has shown that tidally influenced terrestrial exoplanets, particularly those orbiting M-dwarfs, have unique atmospheric dynamics and surface conditions that may enhance their likelihood to host viable habitats. However, sporadic libration and rotation induced by planetary interactions, such as those due to mean motion resonances (MMR) in compact planetary systems, may destabilize attendant exoplanets away from synchronized states (1:1 spin-orbit ratios). Here, we use a three-dimensional N-rigid-body integrator and an intermediately complex general circulation model to simulate the evolving climates of TRAPPIST-1 e and f with different orbital- and spin-evolution pathways. Planet f scenarios perturbed by MMR effects with chaotic spin variations are colder and dryer compared to their synchronized counterparts due to the zonal drift of the substellar point away from open ocean basins of their initial eyeball states. On the other hand, the differences between perturbed and synchronized planet e are minor due to higher instellation, warmer surfaces, and reduced climate hysteresis. This is the first study to incorporate the time-dependent outcomes of direct gravitational N-rigid-body simulations into 3D climate modeling of extrasolar planets, and our results show that planets at the outer edge of the habitable zones in compact multiplanet systems are vulnerable to rapid global glaciations. In the absence of external mechanisms such as orbital forcing or tidal heating, these planets could be trapped in permanent snowball states.

[4] 致密多行星系统中的偶发自转-轨道变化及其对系外行星气候的影响

气候建模显示，受潮汐影响的类地系外行星，尤其是环绕M型恒星的行星，具有独特的大气动力学和表面条件，可能提升其适宜生存环境的概率。然而，由于行星间相互作用（如致密行星系统中的均运动共振MMR）引发的偶发性摆动和自转，可能使相关系外行星偏离同步状态（1:1自转-公转比）。本研究采用三维N刚体积分器和中等复杂度的大气环流模式，模拟了TRAPPIST-1 e和f在不同轨道和自转演化路径下的气候演化。受MMR效应扰动、具有混沌自转变化的f行星情景，由于亚恒星点从初始“眼球”态的开阔海洋盆地向经向漂移，导致其比同步状态更冷更干。相比之下，e行星扰动与同步状态的差异较小，原因是其吸收恒星辐射更高、表面更温暖、气候滞后效应减弱。这是首次将直接引力N刚体模拟的时变结果引入系外行星三维气候建模。结果显示，致密多行星系统宜居带外缘的行星易受快速全球冰冻影响。在缺乏轨道强迫或潮汐加热等外部机制时，这些行星可能被困于永久雪球态。

[5] Linking the interior structure of terrestrial exoplanets to their (tidal) observables

2024-04-00

Tidal effects probe the interior of celestial bodies and are an important source of information on their thermal state and the prevailing deformation mechanisms. In the case of exoplanets, estimation of quantities related to tides might add important constraints on the interior structure of those worlds that would complement the existing measurements of masses and radii [e.g., 1]. In recent years, the fluid Love number hf, which characterises the deformed figure of a rotating celestial body, has been measured for the gaseous exoplanet WASP-103b [2], and the rate of tidal dissipation has also been estimated for several extrasolar gas giants [e.g., 3]. Although not directly detectable today, future measurements might also assess the deformation of Earth-sized exoplanets. Moreover, the measurements of thermal emission light curves, accessible to JWST [4], might shed light on the actual spin states of low-mass exoplanets, which is another parameter affecting the long-term evolution and the habitability prospects of the extrasolar worlds. Detailed analysis of the light curves can also unveil global-scale volcanism that would be indicative of the magnitude of tidal dissipation [5].Here, we discuss and illustrate the link between various aspects of the planet's interior structure and a set of potential observables related to tides on close-in rocky exoplanets without atmosphere. Specifically, we focus on the role of different rheological models and their parameters and on the major features of the interior structure, such as liquid layers or low-viscosity zones. We address the stability of various spin-orbit resonances, surface tidal heat flux, the magnitude of the tidal Love numbers h2 and k2, and the present-day effect of tides on the orbital elements. Since the tidal deformation and the rate of energy dissipation in close-in rocky exoplanets also govern the secular orbital evolution, we further discuss the effect of changes in the interior structure, induced by variations in the thermal state, on the long-term orbital dynamics of tidally loaded exoplanets or moons [6]. [5] 将类地系外行星内部结构与其（潮汐）可观测量联系起来

潮汐效应能够探测天体内部结构，是了解其热状态和主导变形机制的重要信息来源。对于系外行星，潮汐相关量的估算可能为这些世界的内部结构提供重要约束，补充现有的质量和半径测量 [如1]。近年来，流体Love数hf（表征旋转天体变形形状）已被测量于气态系外行星WASP-103b [2]，潮汐耗散率也已对若干系外气体巨行星进行了估算 [如3]。虽然目前尚无法直接探测，但未来的观测也许能够评估类地系外行星的变形。此外，JWST可获取的热发射光变曲线 [4]，有望揭示低质量系外行星的实际自转状态，这也是影响系外世界长期演化和宜居性前景的参数。对光变曲线的详细分析还可揭示全球尺度的火山活动，指示潮汐耗散的强度 [5]。在此，我们讨论并展示了近轨道无大气岩石系外行星内部结构各方面与一组潮汐相关潜在可观测量之间的联系。具体而言，我们关注不同流变模型及其参数的作用，以及内部结构的主要特征，如液态层或低粘度区。我们讨论了各种自转-轨道共振的稳定性、表面潮汐热通量、潮汐Love数h2和k2的大小，以及潮汐对轨道要素的现今影响。由于近轨道岩石系外行星的潮汐变形和能量耗散率还主导其长期轨道演化，我们进一步讨论了由热状态变化引起的内部结构变化对受潮汐加载的系外行星或卫星长期轨道动力学的影响 [6]。致谢：本项工作得到捷克科学基金会23-06513I号项目资助。参考文献：[1] Baumeister & Tosi (2023), doi:10.1051/0004-6361/202346216。[2] Barros et al. [6] Interior dynamics of small-core and coreless exoplanets

2021-09-00

Since the first exoplanet detection in 1992, the study of exoplanets has received considerable attention. It is becoming apparent that the diversity of the general exoplanet population extends beyond that of the Solar System and includes a variety of structures and compositions. For instance, recent research suggests that if a terrestrial planet forms with substantially more Al and Ca than Earth, it may be entirely rocky and lack a metallic core. Two such planets have been tentatively identified (Dorn et al., 2018).Small-core and coreless planets are expected to differ significantly from Earth. Small cores may have fundamentally different dynamics compared to larger cores, which can affect the heat flow inside the planet and the generation and retention of a magnetic dynamo. Additionally, core size has been shown to affect the development of plate tectonics in numerical models (Osterloo et al. 2021; O'Neill et al., 2017), as well as carbon cycling on a planet. Investigating small-core and coreless planets therefore illustrates the potential diversity of exoplanets and offers insight into different regimes of mantle dynamics and plate tectonics.In this study, we use the code StagYY (Tackley, 2008, 2013) to model the evolution of planets with core mass fractions ranging from 10-1 to 10-6 and compare them to an Earth-like reference model. We use mantle reference viscosities of 1020, 1021, and 1022 Pa·s and crustal yield stresses of 30, 100, and 300 MPa. The planets are set to evolve for 4.5 Gyr.In planets with smaller cores, hot plumes rising from the core-mantle boundary (CMB) are reduced in number and develop slowly compared to large-core planets. In the models with a reference viscosity of 1021 Pa·s and a crustal yield stress of 30 MPa, all planets show subduction and plate tectonic-like behavior. The onset of subduction is delayed in small-core planets compared to the Earth-like reference model. Basalt-enriched material accumulates at the CMB and forms a continuous layer in all planet models; this layer is thicker in small-core planets. Due to insulation by the basalt layer, CMB temperature is significantly higher in small-core planets at the end of the 4.5 Gyr evolution period.For a lower reference viscosity of 1020 Pa·s, subduction is initiated earlier and the basalt layer surrounding the CMB is thicker, while the reverse is true for the higher reference viscosity of 1022 Pa·s.At higher yield stresses, some of the planet models undergo subduction and later transition to a stagnant-lid state. However, for a reference viscosity of 1021 Pa·s and a yield stress of 100 MPa, there is no consistent trend regarding this transition. For a yield stress of 300 MPa, planets with core fractions of 10-3 and larger do not show any subduction, but remain in stagnant-lid state throughout the evolution period. In planets with smaller core fractions, subduction events occur starting at 2.5 Gyr. This agrees with previous research suggesting that a small core size can favor the initiation of plate tectonics (Osterloo et al. 2021; O'Neill et al., 2017). In such cases, core size may crucially influence a planet's habitability, since a large-core planet may lack the plate tectonics that are sometimes considered essential to efficient carbon cycling, while a small-core planet may lack the gravity or magnetic dynamo necessary to sustain life.We conclude that core size influences the onset of subduction and plate tectonics on terrestrial planets, as well as their thermal evolution. Small-core planets tend to initiate subduction later than their large-core counterparts and can also be subject to interior heating due to the hot basalt layers insulating the core. For high crustal yield stresses, small-core planets are more likely to develop plate tectonics, while large-core planets remain in a stagnant-lid state. However, this tendency is not evident at yield stresses below 300 MPa. Mantle dynamics in super-Earths: Post-perovskite rheology and self-regulation of viscosity, Icarus 225 (1), 50-61, doi:10.1016/j.icarus.2013.03.013, 2013.

[6] 小核与无核系外行星的内部动力学

自1992年首次发现系外行星以来，系外行星的研究受到了极大关注。现在已经明显，系外行星总体的多样性超出了太阳系的范围，涵盖了多种结构和组成。例如，最新研究表明，如果一颗类地行星形成时Al和Ca含量远高于地球，它可能完全为岩石质且缺乏金属核。已有两颗此类行星被初步识别（Dorn et al., 2018）。小核和无核行星预计与地球有显著不同。小核行星的动力学可能与大核行星有根本差异，这会影响行星内部的热流以及磁发电机的产生和保持。此外，数值模型显示，核的大小会影响板块构造的发展（Osterloo et al. 2021；O'Neill et al., 2017），以及行星上的碳循环。因此，研究小核和无核行星可以展示系外行星的潜在多样性，并为不同地幔动力学和板块构造机制提供见解。在本研究中，我们使用StagYY代码（Tackley, 2008, 2013）模拟了核质量分数从10^-1到10^-6的行星演化，并与类地球参考模型进行比较。我们采用地幔参考粘度为10^20、10^21和10^22 Pa·s，地壳屈服应力为30、100和300 MPa。行星演化时间设为4.5 Gyr。在小核行星中，从核-幔边界（CMB）上升的热羽数量减少，发展速度较大核行星慢。在参考粘度为10^21 Pa·s、地壳屈服应力为30 MPa的模型中，所有行星均表现出俯冲和类似板块构造的行为。小核行星的俯冲起始时间较类地球参考模型延迟。富含玄武岩的物质在CMB处积累，并在所有行星模型中形成连续层；该层在小核行星中更厚。由于玄武岩层的绝热作用，4.5 Gyr演化末期小核行星的CMB温度显著更高。对于较低的参考粘度10^20 Pa·s，俯冲更早发生，CMB周围的玄武岩层更厚；而对于较高的参考粘度10^22 Pa·s，则相反。在较高屈服应力下，部分行星模型经历俯冲后转变为停滞盖状态。然而，对于参考粘度10^21 Pa·s和屈服应力100 MPa，没有一致的转变趋势。对于屈服应力300 MPa，核分数为10^-3及以上的行星在整个演化期间均未出现俯冲，始终处于停滞盖状态。对于更小核分数的行星，俯冲事件自2.5 Gyr开始发生。这与先前研究一致，即小核有利于板块构造的启动（Osterloo et al. 2021；O'Neill et al., 2017）。在这种情况下，核的大小可能对行星宜居性有关键影响，因为大核行星可能缺乏高效碳循环所需的板块构造，而小核行星可能缺乏维持生命所需的重力或磁发电机。我们得出结论，核的大小影响类地行星俯冲和板块构造的启动及其热演化。小核行星的俯冲启动时间通常晚于大核行星，且由于玄武岩层对核心的绝热作用，内部加热更显著。对于高地壳屈服应力，小核行星更易发展板块构造，而大核行星则保持停滞盖状态。然而，在屈服应力低于300 MPa时，这一趋势并不明显。图1：参考粘度10^21 Pa·s、地壳屈服应力100 MPa下，不同核质量分数行星的内部分异示意图：a）类地球核大小，b）核质量分数10^-1，c）核质量分数10^-6。参考文献：Dorn, C., Harrison, J. H. D., Bonsor, A. 和 Hands, T.O. 2018, MNRAS, 484 (1), 712-727, doi:10.1093/mnras/sty3435. O'Neill, C., Lowman, J., Wasiliev, J. 2020, Icarus, 352, 114025, doi: 10.1016/j.icarus.2020.114025. Oosterloo, M., Höning, D., Kamp, I. E. E., van der Tak, F. F. S. 2021, A&A, doi:10.1051/0004-6361/202039664. Tackley, P. J. 2008, Phys. Earth Planet. Inter, 171 (1-4), 7-18, doi: 10.1016/j.pepi.2008.08.005. Tackley, P. J., Ammann, M., Brodholt, J. P., Dobson, D. P. 和 Valencia, D. 2013, Icarus 225 (1), 50-61, doi:10.1016/j.icarus.2013.03.013.

[7] Improving tidal interaction for compact N-body planetary system.

2024-04-00

Recent JWST observations of rocky planets, such as TRAPPIST-1, and the increasing number of rocky planets discovered orbiting close to their host star, strongly motivates the improvement of tidal modeling.Beside, recent JWST observations of the thermal emission from TRAPPIST-1 b and c have provided constraints on their atmospheres (Greene et al. 2023; Ih et al. 2023; Zieba et al. 2023; Lincowski et al. 2023). In this context, It is crucial to use a coherent tidal model that encapsulates the complex response of rocky planets to stress, to understand the evolution of exoplanets and interpret new data.Our presentation will focus on recent developments on the implementation of the formalism of Kaula (1964) in the N-body code Posidonius (Blanco-Cuaresma &amp; Bolmont 2017; Bolmont et al. 2020).This formalism consists in using a decomposition of the tidal potential into Fourier harmonic modes, which allows to account for the frequency dependence of the tidal response of rocky bodies.It makes it general enough to take into account for any type of internal structure, as well as the presence of ice or surface liquid water.We will present our results on the rotational state of TRAPPIST-1 planets, revisiting the assumption of the perfect synchronization state resulting from tidal evolution. Given that the rotational state influences the heat redistribution regime, precise estimation of their rotational state is critical.Various internal structures were explored with the Burnman code (Cottaar et al. 2014; Myhill et al. 2021)., considering compositions and core sizes compatible with mass and radius estimations from Agol et al. (2021).Our simulations showed that planet-planet interactions induce rapid variations in the mean motions of the planets. These variations occur too quickly for tides to maintain synchronized rotation states with the mean motion. This results in sub-stellar point drifts, causing planets to complete full solar days with periods ranging from 42 to 103 years depending on the planet. The competition between mean motion variations and tidal damping, and thus sub-stellar drifts, is contingent on the internal structure of the planet under consideration. As a result, remnant rotation is expected to facilitate the redistribution of heat on the planet's surface, modifying habitability conditions by mitigating the cold-trap effect on the night side (Turbet et al. 2016) and redistributing cloud formation on the day side (Turbet et al. 2021).Additionally, we will present preliminary results on the coupling between the spin and the orbital evolution of planets in compact mean motion resonances (MMRs), in particular with the presence of obliquity spin-orbit resonances (SOR), on time transit variations (TTV) and on the mean motion resonances for the TRAPPIST-1 system and the potential observability of such effects.

[7] 改进致密N体行星系统的潮汐相互作用

近期JWST对岩石行星（如TRAPPIST-1）的观测，以及越来越多围绕母星近轨道运行的岩石行星的发现，极大地推动了潮汐建模的改进。此外，JWST对TRAPPIST-1 b和c的热发射观测为其大气提供了约束（Greene et al. 2023；Ih et al. 2023；Zieba et al. 2023；Lincowski et al. 2023）。在此背景下，采用能够体现岩石行星对应力复杂响应的统一潮汐模型，对于理解系外行星的演化和解释新数据至关重要。本报告将聚焦于Kaula (1964)理论在N体代码Posidonius（Blanco-Cuaresma & Bolmont 2017；Bolmont et al. 2020）中的最新实现进展。该理论通过将潮汐势分解为傅里叶谐波模式，从而能够考虑岩石天体潮汐响应的频率依赖性。这使其足够通用，可以适用于任何类型的内部结构，以及存在冰或表面液态水的情形。我们将展示关于TRAPPIST-1行星自转状态的结果，重新审视潮汐演化导致完全同步自转的假设。由于自转状态影响热量再分布机制，精确估算其自转状态至关重要。我们利用Burnman代码（Cottaar et al. 2014；Myhill et al. 2021）探索了多种内部结构，考虑了与Agol et al. (2021)质量和半径估算相符的组成和核大小。我们的模拟显示，行星间相互作用会导致行星平均运动的快速变化，这些变化过快，潮汐无法维持与平均运动同步的自转状态。这导致了亚恒星点的漂移，使得行星完成一个完整太阳日的周期在42至103年之间，具体取决于行星。平均运动变化与潮汐阻尼之间的竞争，以及亚恒星点漂移，取决于所研究行星的内部结构。因此，残余自转有望促进行星表面热量再分布，通过减弱夜侧冷阱效应（Turbet et al. 2016）和改变昼侧云形成分布（Turbet et al. 2021），从而改变宜居性条件。此外，我们还将展示关于紧凑平均运动共振（MMRs）系统中自转与轨道演化耦合的初步结果，特别是存在倾角自转-轨道共振（SOR）时对凌时变化（TTV）和TRAPPIST-1系统平均运动共振的影响，以及此类效应的潜在可观测性。

[8] Atmospheric Gravitational Tides of Earth-like Planets Orbiting Low-mass Stars

2022-07-00

Temperate terrestrial planets orbiting low-mass stars are subject to strong tidal forces. The effects of gravitational tides on the solid planet and that of atmospheric thermal tides have been studied, but the direct impact of gravitational tides on the atmosphere itself has so far been ignored. We first develop a simplified analytic theory of tides acting on the atmosphere of a planet. We then implement gravitational tides into a general circulation model of a static-ocean planet in a short-period orbit around a low-mass star-the results agree with our analytic theory. Because atmospheric tides and solid-body tides share a scaling with the semimajor axis, we show that there is a maximum amplitude of the atmospheric tide that a terrestrial planet can experience while still having a solid surface; Proxima Centauri b is the poster child for a planet that could be geophysically Earth-like but with atmospheric tides more than 500× stronger than Earth's. In this most extreme scenario, we show that atmospheric tides significantly impact the planet's meteorology-but not its climate. Two possible modest climate impacts are enhanced longitudinal heat transport and cooling of the lowest atmospheric layers. The strong radiative forcing of such planets dominates over gravitational tides, unlike moons of cold giant planets, such as Titan. We speculate that atmospheric tides could be climatologically important on planets where the altitude of maximal tidal forcing coincides with the altitude of cloud formation and that the effect could be detectable for non-Earth-like planets subject to even greater tides.

[8] 类地行星绕低质量恒星运行时的大气重力潮汐

围绕低质量恒星运行的温带类地行星受到强潮汐力的影响。重力潮汐对固体行星的影响以及大气热潮汐的影响已有研究，但重力潮汐对大气本身的直接影响迄今被忽略。我们首先建立了一个作用于行星大气的潮汐简化解析理论。随后，我们将重力潮汐引入一个静态海洋行星的通用环流模型，该行星围绕低质量恒星作短周期轨道运行——结果与我们的解析理论一致。由于大气潮汐和固体潮汐与半长轴的标度关系相同，我们展示了类地行星在仍具有固体表面的情况下可经历的大气潮汐的最大幅度；比邻星b是一个典型例子，其地球物理特性可能类似地球，但大气潮汐强度超过地球500倍。在这种极端情况下，我们表明大气潮汐会显著影响行星的气象过程，但不会影响其气候。两种可能的温和气候影响是增强经向热输运和最低大气层的冷却。此类行星的强辐射强迫效应主导于重力潮汐，这与冷巨行星（如土卫六）卫星不同。我们推测，当最大潮汐强迫高度与云形成高度重合时，大气潮汐可能对行星气候具有重要影响，并且对于受更强潮汐作用的非类地行星，这一效应可能是可探测的。

[1] Probing Mini-Neptune Atmospheres: the case of K2-18b

2025-09-00

The Kepler and TESS missions found a multitude of planetary systems with Super-Earths and/or Mini-Neptunes in compact orbits around their host stars. Planets in this range of sizes and orbits appear as a common outcome of planetary formation, although they are not present in our Solar System. Remote-sensing spectroscopy of their atmospheres is key to understanding their bulk composition and possible formation pathways, such as the relative amounts of rock and volatiles, the persistence of a primordial H-He envelope, and ideally their elemental ratios. K2-18b is one of the smallest exoplanets with robust atmospheric detections (CO2 and CH4), leading to hypotheses that it is a hycean, likely inhabited, planet (Madhusudhan et al. 2023), or a gas-rich mini-Neptune (Wogan et al. 2024). The previous molecular detections, albeit with different possible interpretations, make it a particularly relevant case of study. We re-reduced and analysed the JWST NIRISS and NIRSpec data taken during two transits in 2023, confirming the detection of CO2 and CH4 in the atmosphere of K2-18 b. Moreover we quantitatively test the impact of different treatments of stellar limb-darkening, corrections for an occulted spot (and unocculted ones), instrumental ramps, spectroscopic binning and outlier rejection in the atmospheric transmission spectrum and the effect these have on possible molecular detections, highlighting the case of CO.

[1] 探测迷你海王星大气：K2-18b案例

Kepler和TESS任务发现了大量拥有超级地球和/或迷你海王星、且轨道紧凑的行星系统。这一尺寸和轨道范围的行星似乎是行星形成的常见结果，尽管在太阳系中并不存在。对其大气的遥感光谱观测是理解其整体组成及可能形成路径的关键，例如岩石与挥发物的相对含量、原始H-He包层的持续性，以及理想情况下的元素丰度比。K2-18b是目前最小的、具有稳健大气探测（CO2和CH4）的系外行星之一，因而被假设为hycean、可能宜居的行星（Madhusudhan等，2023），或富气体的迷你海王星（Wogan等，2024）。此前的分子探测，尽管存在不同的解释，使其成为特别值得研究的案例。我们重新处理并分析了2023年两次凌日期间获得的JWST NIRISS和NIRSpec数据，确认了K2-18 b大气中CO2和CH4的探测。此外，我们定量测试了不同恒星边缘变暗处理、遮挡斑点（及未遮挡斑点）校正、仪器斜坡、光谱分箱和离群值剔除对大气透射光谱及分子探测可能性的影响，重点分析了CO的情况。

[2] 1D atmospheric study of the temperate sub-Neptune K2-18b

2021-02-00

Context. The atmospheric composition of exoplanets with masses between 2 and 10 M<SUB>⊕</SUB> is poorly understood. In that regard, the sub-Neptune K2-18b, which is subject to Earth-like stellar irradiation, offers a valuable opportunity for the characterisation of such atmospheres. Previous analyses of its transmission spectrum from the Kepler, Hubble (HST), and Spitzer space telescopes data using both retrieval algorithms and forward-modelling suggest the presence of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and an H<SUB>2</SUB>-He atmosphere, but have not detected other gases, such as CH<SUB>4</SUB>. <BR /> Aims: We present simulations of the atmosphere of K2-18 b using Exo-REM, our self-consistent 1D radiative-equilibrium model, using a large grid of atmospheric parameters to infer constraints on its chemical composition. <BR /> Methods: We compared the transmission spectra computed by our model with the above-mentioned data (0.4-5 μm), assuming an H<SUB>2</SUB>-He dominated atmosphere. We investigated the effects of irradiation, eddy diffusion coefficient, internal temperature, clouds, C/O ratio, and metallicity on the atmospheric structure and transit spectrum. <BR /> Results: We show that our simulations favour atmospheric metallicities between 40 and 500 times solar and indicate, in some cases, the formation of H<SUB>2</SUB>O-ice clouds, but not liquid H<SUB>2</SUB>O clouds. We also confirm the findings of our previous study, which showed that CH<SUB>4</SUB> absorption features nominally dominate the transmission spectrum in the HST spectral range. We compare our results with results from retrieval algorithms and find that the H<SUB>2</SUB>O-dominated spectrum interpretation is either due to the omission of CH<SUB>4</SUB> absorptions or a strong overfitting of the data. Finally, we investigated different scenarios that would allow for a CH<SUB>4</SUB>-depleted atmosphere. We were able to fit the data to those scenarios, finding, however, that it is very unlikely for K2-18b to have a high internal temperature. A low C/O ratio (≈0.01-0.1) allows for H<SUB>2</SUB>O to dominate the transmission spectrum and can fit the data but so far, this set-up lacks a physical explanation. Simulations with a C/O ratio &lt;0.01 are not able to fit the data satisfactorily.

[2] 温带亚海王星 K2-18b 的一维大气研究

背景：质量介于2到10 M⊕之间的系外行星大气成分尚不清楚。在这方面，受到类似地球辐照的亚海王星K2-18b为此类大气的特性研究提供了宝贵机会。此前利用Kepler、Hubble（HST）和Spitzer空间望远镜数据，通过检索算法和前向建模分析其透射光谱，均表明存在H2O和H2-He大气，但未探测到其他气体，如CH4。 目的：我们利用自洽的一维辐射平衡模型Exo-REM，对K2-18b的大气进行模拟，采用大范围的大气参数网格以推断其化学成分的约束。 方法：我们将模型计算得到的透射光谱与上述数据（0.4-5 μm）进行比较，假设大气以H2-He为主。我们研究了辐照、湍流扩散系数、内部温度、云、C/O比和金属丰度对大气结构和凌日光谱的影响。 结果：我们的模拟结果支持大气金属丰度为40到500倍太阳值，并在某些情况下指示H2O冰云的形成，但未出现液态H2O云。我们还证实了此前研究的发现，即在HST波段范围内，CH4吸收特征在名义上主导透射光谱。我们将结果与检索算法的结果进行了比较，发现H2O主导的光谱解释要么是由于忽略了CH4吸收，要么是对数据的过拟合。最后，我们探讨了可能导致CH4贫乏大气的不同情景。我们能够用这些情景拟合数据，但发现K2-18b具有高内部温度的可能性极低。低C/O比（约0.01-0.1）可使H2O主导透射光谱并拟合数据，但目前这种设定缺乏物理解释。C/O比<0.01的模拟无法令人满意地拟合数据。

[3] Hazy with a Chance of Star Spots: Constraining the Atmosphere of Young Planet K2-33b

2023-01-00

Although all-sky surveys have led to the discovery of dozens of young planets, little is known about their atmospheres. Here, we present multiwavelength transit data for the super-Neptune sized exoplanet, K2-33b-the youngest (~10 Myr) transiting exoplanet to date. We combined photometric observations of K2-33 covering a total of 33 transits spanning &gt;2 yr, taken from K2, MEarth, the Hubble Space Telescope (HST), and Spitzer. The transit photometry spanned from the optical to the near-infrared (0.6-4.5 μm), enabling us to construct a transmission spectrum of the planet. We find that the optical transit depths are nearly a factor of 2 deeper than those from the near-infrared. This difference holds across multiple data sets taken over years, ruling out issues of data analysis and unconstrained systematics. Surface inhomogeneities on the young star can reproduce some of the difference, but required spot coverage fractions (&gt;60%) are ruled out by the observed stellar spectrum (&lt;20%). We find a better fit to the transmission spectrum using photochemical hazes, which were predicted to be strong in young, moderate-temperature, and large-radius planets like K2-33b. A tholin haze with CO as the dominant gaseous carbon carrier in the atmosphere can reasonably reproduce the data with small or no stellar surface inhomogeneities, consistent with the stellar spectrum. The HST data quality is insufficient for the detection of any molecular features. More observations would be required to fully characterize the hazes and spot properties and confirm the presence of CO suggested by current data.

[3] 有雾且可能有恒星黑子：约束年轻行星K2-33b的大气

尽管全天巡天已经发现了数十颗年轻行星，但我们对它们的大气知之甚少。本文我们展示了超海王星大小系外行星K2-33b的多波段凌星观测数据——这是迄今为止最年轻（约1000万年）的凌星系外行星。我们结合了K2-33的光度观测数据，涵盖了超过2年、共33次凌星事件，数据来自K2、MEarth、哈勃空间望远镜（HST）和Spitzer。凌星光度数据覆盖了从光学到近红外（0.6-4.5 μm）的波段，使我们能够构建该行星的透射光谱。我们发现，光学波段的凌星深度几乎是近红外波段的两倍。这一差异在多年多组数据中均有体现，排除了数据分析和系统误差的可能。年轻恒星表面的不均匀性可以部分解释这一差异，但所需的黑子覆盖率（>60%）被观测到的恒星光谱（<20%）所否定。我们发现，采用光化学薄雾模型对透射光谱拟合效果更好，这与理论预测在像K2-33b这样年轻、中等温度、大半径行星中薄雾较强的预期一致。以CO为主要气态碳载体的tholin薄雾模型，在恒星表面不均匀性很小或没有的情况下，可以较好地再现观测数据，并与恒星光谱一致。HST数据质量不足以探测任何分子特征。需要更多观测以全面刻画薄雾和黑子特性，并确认当前数据所暗示的CO的存在。

[4] Hot Rocks Survey. IV. Emission from LTT 3780 b Is Consistent with a Bare Rock

2025-10-00

It is an open question whether small planets around M dwarfs are able to maintain atmospheres. The Hot Rocks Survey aims to address this question by observing nine rocky exoplanets orbiting M dwarfs with MIRI emission photometry to constrain the onset of atmospheres. In this paper, we present two MIRI F1500W (15 μm) eclipses of LTT 3780 b, an ultrashort-period super-Earth (P = 0.768 days, R = 1.325 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>, M = 2.46 M<SUB>⊕</SUB>) that receives 111× Earth's instellation, the highest in the survey. We find a combined eclipse depth of 312 ± 38 ppm, which is consistent between different data reduction and analysis assumptions, bolstering our confidence in the eclipse detection. This eclipse depth is consistent with the thermal emission from a bare rock surface, with a dayside temperature of <inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>T</mml:mi><mml:mi>day</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>114</mml:mn><mml:msubsup><mml:mn>3</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>99</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>104</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> K, 98% ± 9% of the maximum temperature predicted for a zero-albedo, zero heat redistribution blackbody. We are able to confidently rule out CO<SUB>2</SUB>-based atmospheres down to a 0.01 bar surface pressure to greater than 3σ (ruling out an approximately Mars-like atmosphere). We are unable to rule out a pure H<SUB>2</SUB>O 1 bar atmosphere, though we argue that this composition is unlikely on such a highly irradiated planet, nor O<SUB>2</SUB> atmospheres due to the lack of features in the bandpass, though we can put constraints on CO<SUB>2</SUB>-mixture atmospheres. As a potential bare rock, we consider a variety of surface composition models, but are unable to distinguish between them. However, LTT 3780 b is an excellent target for follow-up JWST observations to determine its surface composition and rule out additional atmospheric compositions.

[4] Hot Rocks巡天IV：LTT 3780 b的发射与裸岩一致

M型矮星周围的小型行星是否能够维持大气层仍是一个悬而未决的问题。Hot Rocks Survey旨在通过MIRI发射光度观测九颗围绕M型矮星运行的岩石系系外行星，以约束大气层的出现。在本文中，我们展示了LTT 3780 b的两次MIRI F1500W（15 μm）掩食观测结果。LTT 3780 b是一颗超短周期超级地球（P = 0.768天，R = 1.325 R⊕，M = 2.46 M⊕），其接收的恒星辐射为地球的111倍，是本巡天中最高的。我们测得的合并掩食深度为312 ± 38 ppm，在不同数据处理和分析假设下结果一致，增强了我们对掩食探测的信心。该掩食深度与裸岩表面的热辐射一致，白昼面温度为Tday = 1143<sub>−99</sub><sup>+104</sup> K，为零反照率、无热量再分布黑体预测最大温度的98% ± 9%。我们能够以大于3σ的置信度排除表面压强低至0.01 bar的CO2主导大气（排除了类似火星的大气）。我们无法排除纯H2O 1 bar大气，尽管我们认为在如此高照射下这种成分不太可能；由于波段内缺乏特征，也无法排除O2大气，但我们可以对CO2混合大气施加约束。作为潜在的裸岩行星，我们考虑了多种表面组成模型，但无法区分它们。然而，LTT 3780 b是后续JWST观测以确定其表面组成并排除其他大气成分的极佳目标。

[5] A visit to the K2-3 system: models of three planetary atmospheres spanning the radius valley informed by a high energy stellar spectrum and updated system properties

2022-06-00

Multi-planet systems offer insight into a host star's influence on its planets' atmospheres. K2-3 is an M0 star (effective temperature of 3880 K) orbited by three small transiting planets, K2-3b, c, and d (2.0, 1.6, and 1.4 Earth radii, respectively). We combine stellar spectral information with self-consistently derived stellar and planetary properties to investigate the K2-3 system. We measure the UV flux of K2-3 with HST/COS and place an upper limit on its X-ray flux with XMM-Newton. We use empirical scaling techniques and a differential emission measure to fill in gaps in the high energy spectrum. We constrain the K2-3 system properties by simultaneously fitting transit photometry, radial velocity, and spectral energy density information. The radii of all three planets are constrained to 2% and the masses of the inner two planets, K2-3b and c, to 10%, while the outermost planet, K2-3d, is not detected in the RV measurements. K2-3b and c are enhanced in volatiles and consistent with sub-Neptune-like compositions, while K2-3d is likely terrestrial due to its small radius. The compositions of sub-Neptune planets are highly degenerate, and indeed we find that the radii and masses of K2-3b and c allow for solar composition envelopes making up 0.30% and 0.05% of their masses, respectively, as well as water steam atmospheres making up for 50% and 13% of their masses. However, the solar composition scenarios are unlikely given the high energy output of K2-3; the timescale for atmospheric escape (1-2 Gyr) is short compared to the estimated age of the system (3-12 Gyr). Additionally, assuming that K2-3d is terrestrial, we find that the architecture of the K2-3 planets is inconsistent with photoevaporation as the sole mechanism for sculpting their atmospheres. Rather we must invoke more stochastic processes such as gas-depleted planet formation or giant impacts to account for the observed system architecture. Finally, we present model transmission spectra that include photochemical reactions and disequilibrium chemistry for a range of atmospheric cases: for K2-3 b and c we model the aforementioned solar composition and steam atmosphere cases, while for K2-3d we model early Venus, modern Venus, and modern Earth cases. Discriminating between these atmospheric cases will require spectral information about the atmospheres of the K2-3 planets, something that is achievable with current observatories. This work serves as a template for future investigations into the atmospheres of small exoplanets in multi-planet systems in the context of their host stars.

[5] K2-3系统考察：结合高能恒星光谱和更新的系统参数对跨越半径谷的三颗行星大气建模

多行星系统为研究母星对其行星大气的影响提供了洞见。K2-3是一颗M0型恒星（有效温度3880 K），有三颗小型凌星行星K2-3b、c和d（半径分别为2.0、1.6和1.4地球半径）环绕。我们结合恒星光谱信息与自洽推导的恒星和行星属性，对K2-3系统进行研究。我们利用HST/COS测量了K2-3的紫外通量，并用XMM-Newton对其X射线通量给出了上限。我们采用经验标度技术和微分发射测度补全高能谱的空白。通过同时拟合凌星光度、径向速度和光谱能量密度信息，我们约束了K2-3系统的属性。三颗行星的半径被约束在2%以内，内侧两颗行星K2-3b和c的质量被约束在10%以内，而最外侧的K2-3d在径向速度测量中未探测到。K2-3b和c富含挥发物，成分与亚海王星类似，而K2-3d由于半径较小，很可能是类地行星。亚海王星的成分高度简并，我们发现K2-3b和c的半径和质量允许其包层分别为0.30%和0.05%太阳成分，或分别为50%和13%的水蒸气大气。然而，考虑到K2-3的高能输出，太阳成分包层情景不太可能；大气逃逸的时间尺度（1-2 Gyr）远短于系统估计年龄（3-12 Gyr）。此外，假设K2-3d为类地行星，我们发现K2-3行星的结构与光致蒸发作为唯一塑造其大气机制的情景不符。我们必须引入如气体耗竭行星形成或巨型碰撞等更随机的过程来解释观测到的系统结构。最后，我们给出了包含光化学反应和非平衡化学的大气模型透射光谱，涵盖多种大气情景：对K2-3b和c，我们模拟了上述太阳成分和水蒸气大气情景；对K2-3d，我们模拟了早期金星、现代金星和现代地球情景。区分这些大气情景需要K2-3行星大气的光谱信息，这是当前观测设备可以实现的。本工作为未来在母星背景下研究多行星系统中小型系外行星大气提供了模板。

[6] JWST observations of the cornerstone temperate sub-Neptune K2-18 b

2026-02-00

Earth-temperature, sub-Neptune-sized exoplanets are now within reach for detailed atmospheric characterization with JWST, with K2-18 b being one of the first to receive repeated observations. Initial observations revealed an H<SUB>2</SUB>-dominated atmosphere rich in CH<SUB>4</SUB> with moderate evidence for CO<SUB>2</SUB>. We have conducted additional JWST observations to capture new transmission spectra of the planet using NIRSpec's G235H and G395H grisms. The resulting high-precision transmission spectrum robustly detects both CH<SUB>4</SUB> and CO<SUB>2</SUB>, precisely measuring their abundances and firmly establishing the planet's water-rich nature. The planet could have either a thick envelope with &gt;10% H<SUB>2</SUB>O by volume or a thin atmosphere above a liquid-water ocean. The spectrum reveals no detectable H<SUB>2</SUB>O, NH<SUB>3</SUB>, or CO. The absence of atmospheric water vapor suggests an efficient cold trap, while the nondetections of NH<SUB>3</SUB> and CO support the scenario of a small H<SUB>2</SUB>-rich atmosphere overlying a liquid reservoir. However, alternative models that include these gases can also reproduce the spectrum within uncertainties and offsets, highlighting the need for deeper observations. This unprecedented dataset offers critical insights into K2-18 b's internal structure and informs an updated roadmap for characterizing temperate sub-Neptunes, advancing our understanding of their atmospheric properties and potential habitability.

[7] The first near-infrared spectroscopic phase-curve of a super-Earth

2021-03-00

The relatively rare sub-population of the so-called ultra-short period (P &lt; 1 day) rocky planets offers a unique opportunity for atmospheric characterization studies. Given the large amount of stellar irradiation these planets are bombarded with, they are expected to have lost their primordial atmospheres giving rise to thin or thick (i.e., low or high pressure) outgassed, exotic Na, O or SiO-rich atmospheres. Characterizing the surfaces and/or atmospheres of these highly irradiated rocky worlds, thus, provides a window to explore exciting atmospheric and/or surface compositions which might be completely different to the ones observed in our Solar System. Motivated by these fascinating prospects, here we propose to constrain the atmosphere of the ultra-short period (6.9-hour; Teq = 2150 K) transiting super-Earth K2-141b (Rp=1.51REarth; Mp=5.08MEarth) through a spectroscopic phase-curve observation with the James Webb Space Telescope. Our program, which will allow us to directly detect the thermal emission of this exoplanet as a function of both orbital phase and wavelength, will provide precious insights into its atmospheric and/or geological properties, which will serve as a strong leverage for future studies and characterization efforts of highly-irradiated super-Earths.

[7] 超级地球的首个近红外光谱相位曲线

[8] Demystifying K2-18 b: exploring the causes of disparate atmospheric and interior hypotheses

2026-02-00

The temperate sub-Neptune K2-18 b has emerged as a keystone target for our understanding of both the atmospheres and interiors of small exoplanets. Despite a substantial JWST observational campaign incorporating multiple instruments and covering a broad wavelength range, the nature of this planet is still heavily debated, with different studies reaching drastically different conclusions. Previous analyses have suggested that this planet may host a thin H/He-rich atmosphere atop a liquid water ocean, or that it may be a gas dwarf whose atmosphere is a product of interactions between hydrogen and a magma ocean, or that K2-18 b is a scaled-down, metal-enriched version of a gas giant planet. <P />Here, we present extensive modeling that seeks to uncover the reasons underlying these disparate interpretations. We discuss how atmospheric inferences for K2-18 b are highly sensitive to both data and model considerations, such as offsets between instruments and the treatment of clouds and hazes. We show that seemingly minor changes in the chosen modeling approach can result in dramatically different interpretations. We also discuss how the conclusions drawn about the atmosphere of K2-18 b have significant implications for the planet's interior structure. We summarise our current overall understanding of the atmosphere and interior of this planet, and present possible future directions to finally resolve the mysteries of this intriguing world.

[8] 揭开K2-18 b之谜：探索不同大气与内部假说的成因
