[1] On the robustness of atmospheric inferences. Estimating the predictive accuracy of atmospheric models for transmission spectra of transiting exoplanets.

2022-06-00

Up until now, most exoplanet transit spectroscopy observations are comprised of relatively low signal to noise and sparse wavelength coverage data. As such, atmospheric inferences are currently derived from a limited number of spectral points and, in some cases, one data-point can significantly affect the interpretation of these observations. Furthermore, when interpreting the goodness-of-fit between the data and the atmospheric models, using different statistical metrics can lead to different conclusions. In some cases, while Bayesian evidence model comparisons suggest the detection of an atomic or molecular species, frequentist metrics like the p-value suggest that the chosen atmospheric model is not supported by the data. Can we trust a chemical detection if some metric suggests that the model does not fit the data? This conundrum highlights the need for methods that can assess the sensitivity of the resulting inferences to the data. Such need becomes more urgent with the imminent wave of spectroscopic information coming from the James Webb Space Telescope for which understanding the limits of our models and our data is paramount. <P />In this work we will present a complementary goodness-of-fit metric applied to transmission spectra of transiting exoplanets. Our work leverages the power of Bayesian atmospheric retrievals to understand which specific data points are responsible for an inferred atmospheric property (e.g., chemical detection), and is robust against considerations in the model (e.g., choice of priors) and noise properties of the data. We will present our computationally practical approach and demonstrate its applicability on recent transmission spectra with unsettled atmospheric detections. We will show how this metric can help us better determine the reliability of an inference and quantify its significance. This complementary approach to estimate the goodness of fit of atmospheric models to the data opens a new avenue to determine the reliability of a given result and inform our understanding of exoplanet atmospheres.

[1] 关于大气推断的稳健性。估算凌星系外行星透射光谱大气模型的预测精度

迄今为止，大多数系外行星凌日光谱观测的数据信噪比较低且波长覆盖稀疏。因此，目前的大气推断主要基于有限数量的光谱点，在某些情况下，单个数据点就可能显著影响对观测结果的解释。此外，在评估数据与大气模型拟合优度时，采用不同的统计指标可能会导致不同的结论。有时，尽管贝叶斯证据模型比较表明探测到了某种原子或分子，但频率学指标如 p 值却显示所选大气模型并不被数据支持。如果某些指标表明模型与数据不符，我们还能相信化学成分的探测吗？这一难题凸显了需要能够评估推断结果对数据敏感性的方法。随着 JWST 即将带来大量光谱信息，理解我们模型和数据的局限性变得尤为重要。 在本工作中，我们提出了一种应用于系外行星凌日透射光谱的互补拟合优度指标。我们的工作利用贝叶斯大气检索的能力，理解哪些具体数据点导致了某一大气属性（如化学探测）的推断结果，并且对模型选择（如先验的选择）和数据噪声特性具有鲁棒性。我们将展示这种计算上可行的方法，并在近期存在争议的大气探测透射光谱上演示其适用性。我们将展示该指标如何帮助我们更好地判断推断结果的可靠性并量化其显著性。这种评估大气模型与数据拟合优度的互补方法为判断结果可靠性和加深对系外行星大气的理解开辟了新途径。

[2] Considerations for next-generation retrievals of exoplanetary transmission spectra

2021-01-00

The last decade has witnessed a revolution in our understanding of exoplanets through the procurement of exquisite spectroscopic observations of their atmospheres. Such observations are routinely interpreted by retrieval tools, frameworks in which an atmospheric model is coupled with an optimization algorithm. Atmospheric retrievals of exoplanetary transmission spectra have been able to provide important constraints on various atmospheric properties such as chemical abundances, information about the presence and structure of clouds and hazes, and characteristic temperatures, at the day-night terminator region of the atmosphere. Most transmission spectra to date have been observed for giant exoplanets due to which retrievals typically assume Hydrogen-rich atmospheres. However, recent observations of mini-Neptunes and super-Earths, and the promise of upcoming facilities including JWST, call for a new generation of retrieval frameworks that can robustly address a wide range of atmospheric compositions and related complexities. In this talk, we will present various considerations for next-generation atmospheric retrievals of exoplanetary transmission spectra with current and upcoming facilities like JWST. We investigate the performance of multiple parametric prescriptions for the presence of inhomogeneous clouds and hazes in atmospheres of transiting exoplanets and quantify their impact on the retrieved chemical abundances. Next, we consider the agreement, or lack thereof, between these prescriptions when the assumption of a H-rich atmosphere is relaxed as it may be the case for some mini-Neptunes and super-Earths. Alongside these parametric forms, we include considerations for atmospheric forward models that incorporate Mie-scattering and refraction. Furthermore, we examine the different model degeneracies present in these models using multiple optimization algorithms which have been benchmarked against each other. Lastly, we argue about the importance of exploring existing model assumptions and incorporating these and other critical model advancements to retrieval tools.

[2] 面向下一代系外行星透射光谱检索的考虑因素

过去十年，通过对系外行星大气的高质量光谱观测，我们对系外行星的理解发生了革命性变化。这类观测通常通过检索工具进行解释，即将大气模型与优化算法结合的框架。系外行星透射光谱的大气检索能够对多种大气属性提供重要约束，如化学丰度、云和薄雾的存在及结构信息，以及晨昏线区域的特征温度。迄今为止，大多数透射光谱观测对象为巨型系外行星，因此检索通常假设富氢大气。然而，近期对迷你海王星和超级地球的观测，以及包括JWST在内的新设施的到来，要求新一代检索框架能够稳健地处理更广泛的大气组成及其相关复杂性。在本报告中，我们将介绍针对当前和即将到来的设施（如JWST）进行系外行星透射光谱下一代大气检索的多项考虑。我们研究了多种参数化方案在处理凌星系外行星大气中非均匀云和薄雾存在时的表现，并量化了其对检索化学丰度的影响。接着，我们考察了在放宽富氢大气假设（如部分迷你海王星和超级地球可能的情况）时，这些参数化方案之间的一致性或不一致性。除这些参数化形式外，我们还考虑了包含Mie散射和折射的大气正向模型。此外，我们利用多种优化算法（已相互基准测试）分析了这些模型中存在的不同模型简并性。最后，我们强调探索现有模型假设并将这些及其他关键模型进展纳入检索工具的重要性。

[3] Atmospheric Retrievals: Limitations, Challenges, and the Road Ahead

2026-06-00

Atmospheric retrieval is now the default framework for interpreting exoplanet and brown dwarf spectra from space and ground-based platforms, yet its limitations often become most apparent precisely in the high-quality data regime where it is most heavily applied. In this talk, I will focus on the challenges of contemporary retrieval approaches, drawing on examples across low- and high-resolution spectroscopy, multiple wavelength regimes, and substellar benchmark atmospheres. Key challenges include degeneracies between temperature structure, composition, and aerosols; biases introduced by incomplete or overly flexible model assumptions; the influence of priors in data-limited regimes; uncertainty in the "correctness" of the data and separating planetary signal from astrophysical noise sources; and the difficulty of validating inference when forward models are only approximate representations of complex, multidimensional atmospheres. I will then outline several future directions that can move the field forward. These include leveraging combined low-and-hi-res datasets, incorporating more realistic atmospheric physics without sacrificing interpretability, and inference methods that enable more rapid computation enabling more robustness tests. I will also briefly discuss the role of machine-learning techniques as computational accelerators and diagnostic tools, rather than black-box replacements for physical models. The goal is to provide a concise, critical perspective on how retrieval can evolve from a powerful but fragile technique into a robust framework for population-level studies and the eventual characterization of potentially habitable worlds.

[3] 大气检索：局限性、挑战与未来方向

大气检索现已成为解释来自空间和地基平台的系外行星及褐矮星光谱的默认框架，但其局限性往往在高质量数据领域最为突出，而该领域也是检索应用最广泛的地方。本报告将聚焦于当前检索方法面临的挑战，涵盖低分辨率和高分辨率光谱、多波段以及亚恒星基准大气的案例。主要挑战包括温度结构、成分和气溶胶之间的退化性；不完整或过于灵活的模型假设引入的偏差；先验在数据有限情形下的影响；对数据“正确性”的不确定性以及如何将行星信号与天体噪声源区分开；以及当前前向模型仅为复杂多维大气近似时，推断验证的困难。随后我将概述推动该领域发展的若干未来方向，包括结合低分辨率和高分辨率数据集、在不牺牲可解释性的前提下引入更真实的大气物理，以及能够更快计算、从而实现更强健性测试的推断方法。我还将简要讨论机器学习技术作为计算加速器和诊断工具的作用，而非物理模型的黑箱替代品。目标是为检索方法如何从强大但脆弱的技术演变为适用于总体研究和最终潜在宜居世界表征的稳健框架，提供简明而批判性的视角。

[4] Novel methods to probe exoplanet atmospheres using ground-based spectrophotometry

2021-09-00

Ground-based spectrophotometric observations of transiting exoplanet atmospheres conventionally rely on correcting for instrumental and telluric systematics in the light curves by using reference stars that are simultaneously observed. However, this approach often leads to sub-optimal corrections due to multiple accounts on which the target and reference star spectra can be affected by systematics differently through the night, ultimately limiting the achievable precision and accuracy on the measurement of planetary atmospheric signatures. We introduce a new method based on Gaussian Processes regression to address this challenge by extracting the transmission or emission spectrum without relying explicitly on the reference stars. Our new method overcomes the necessity of using reference stars and opens up the doors to ground-based atmospheric observations of exoplanets orbiting bright host stars (e.g. those discovered by TESS) that intrinsically lack proper reference stars. We present results from the application of our method to a broad sample of exoplanets observed in the optical and near-infrared using Gemini/GMOS and Keck/MOSFIRE. We also discuss the challenges and possible solutions arising from stellar variability towards combining high precision ground-based low-resolution spectroscopy observations in complementarity with future infrared observations from HST and JWST.

[4] 利用地基光谱光度法探测系外行星大气的新方法

地基凌星系外行星大气的光谱光度观测通常依赖于同时观测的参考星来校正光曲线中的仪器和大气系统误差。然而，由于目标星和参考星的光谱在夜间可能受到不同系统误差的影响，这种方法常常导致校正效果不理想，最终限制了行星大气特征测量的精度和准确性。我们提出了一种基于高斯过程回归的新方法，通过不显式依赖参考星来提取透射光谱或发射光谱。该新方法克服了对参考星的依赖，为观测缺乏合适参考星的明亮宿主星（如TESS发现的行星）的地基大气观测打开了大门。我们展示了该方法在使用Gemini/GMOS和Keck/MOSFIRE在光学和近红外波段观测的一批系外行星样本上的应用结果。我们还讨论了恒星活动带来的挑战及其可能的解决方案，以便将高精度地基低分辨率光谱观测与未来HST和JWST的红外观测互补结合。

[5] From cross-correlations to likelihoods: a fully Bayesian approach to high-resolution retrievals

2021-10-00

High-resolution Doppler-resolved spectroscopy has opened up a new window into the atmospheres of both transiting and non-transiting exoplanets. While the 'classical' cross-correlation approach is efficient for finding atomic and molecular species, it is quite limited with its inability to recover quantitative information on the atmosphere such as abundances and temperature profiles, nor can it place statistically robust uncertainties on the quantities it can measure (e.g. velocities, detection significances). Recent pioneering efforts have sought to develop likelihood 'mappings' from the cross-correlation function, that can be used to directly compare model fits to high-resolution data sets thus solving these issues. Here, I will outline a framework based purely on a simple Gaussian likelihood, remarkably similar to the techniques that have been applied to transit light curves for over a decade. These can be used to explore the posterior distribution of parametrised model atmospheres in a statistically rigorous way. However, one important and distinct problem remains for high-resolution retrievals; the methods used for filtering out the stellar and telluric lines from the data also filters the underlying exoplanet's signal. I will discuss a simple and fast technique to address this problem that enables retrievals from data filtered through common methods such as SysRem and PCA. Finally, I will demonstrate this framework on observations of the ultra-hot Jupiter WASP-121b, including constraints on the abundances and temperature profile of the atmosphere.

[5] 从互相关到似然：高分辨率检索的全贝叶斯方法

高分辨率多普勒分辨光谱为凌日和非凌日系外行星大气研究开辟了新窗口。虽然“经典”的互相关方法在寻找原子和分子组分方面高效，但它无法获得大气的定量信息，如丰度和温度剖面，也无法对其可测量的量（如速度、探测显著性）给出统计上稳健的不确定性。近期的开创性工作尝试从互相关函数开发似然“映射”，可用于直接将模型拟合与高分辨率数据集进行比较，从而解决上述问题。本文将介绍一个完全基于简单高斯似然的框架，与十多年来应用于凌日光变曲线的技术非常相似。该方法可用于以统计严谨的方式探索参数化模型大气的后验分布。然而，高分辨率检索仍存在一个重要且独特的问题：用于滤除恒星和大气吸收线的方法也会滤除系外行星的信号。本文将讨论一种简单快速的技术，能够对通过如SysRem和PCA等常用方法滤波后的数据进行检索。最后，将以超热木星WASP-121b的观测为例，展示该框架，包括对大气丰度和温度剖面的约束。

[6] Exploring Variability in the Atmospheres of Imaged Giant Exoplanets and Brown Dwarfs

2022-00-00

I propose to examine time-resolved spectra of variable ultracool objects (exoplanets, planetary mass companions, and brown dwarfs). I will perform Bayesian atmospheric retrievals on the spectra of these objects as they vary in order to see how the atmosphere varies spatially as they rotate. I will examine objects that span the L/T transition, compare low surface gravity objects to high gravity objects with similar properties, and examine high amplitude variables outside the L/T transition. I will perform retrievals using a Bayesian retrieval scheme with a machine learning based forward model trained using the Machine Learning Algorithm for Radiative Transfer of Generated Exoplanets (MARGE). Targets will be selected from the Cloud Atlas spectral library and from similar or related studies. This will be the first study to combine spatial information from variability monitoring with the detail of a full atmospheric retrieval. This study will contribute to the understanding of atmospheric processes in gas giants and brown dwarfs, as well as the effect of surface gravity on variability of ultracool atmospheres, which is currently not well understood.

[6] 探索直接成像巨型系外行星和褐矮星大气的变化性

我提议对可变超冷天体（系外行星、行星质量伴星和褐矮星）的时序光谱进行研究。我将对这些天体在变化过程中的光谱进行贝叶斯大气检索，以了解它们自转时大气的空间变化。我将研究跨越L/T转变的天体，将低表面重力天体与具有相似性质的高重力天体进行比较，并研究L/T转变之外的高振幅变量。我将采用基于机器学习的前向模型（使用Machine Learning Algorithm for Radiative Transfer of Generated Exoplanets，MARGE训练）进行贝叶斯检索。目标将从Cloud Atlas光谱库及相关研究中选取。这将是首次将变异监测获得的空间信息与完整大气检索的细节相结合的研究。本研究将有助于理解气态巨行星和褐矮星中的大气过程，以及表面重力对超冷大气变异性的影响，而这目前尚不清楚。

[7] Impacts of high-contrast image processing on atmospheric retrievals

2023-10-00

Many post-processing algorithms have been developed in order to better separate the signal of a companion from the bright light of the host star, but the effect of such algorithms on the shape of exoplanet spectra extracted from integral field spectrograph data is poorly understood. The resulting spectra are affected by noise that is correlated in wavelength space due to both optical and data processing effects. Within the framework of Bayesian atmospheric retrievals, we aim to understand how these correlations and other systematic effects impact the inferred physical parameters. We consider three algorithms (KLIP, PynPoint, and ANDROMEDA), optimising the choice of algorithmic parameters using a series of injection tests on archival SPHERE and GPI data of the HR 8799 system. The wavelength-dependent covariance matrix was calculated to provide a measure of instrumental and algorithmic systematics. We perform atmospheric retrievals using petit RADTRANS on optimally extracted spectra to measure how these data processing systematics influence the retrieved parameter distributions. The choice of data processing algorithm and parameters significantly impact the accuracy of retrieval results, with the mean posterior parameter bias ranging from 1 to 3 σ from the true input parameters. Including the full covariance matrix in the likelihood improves the accuracy of the inferred parameters, and cannot be accounted for using ad hoc scaling parameters in the retrieval framework. Using the Bayesian information criterion and other statistical measures as heuristic goodness-of-fit metrics, the retrievals including the full covariance matrix are favoured when compared to using only the diagonal elements.

[7] 高对比度图像处理对大气检索的影响

为更好地将伴星信号与主星强光分离，已经开发了多种后处理算法，但这些算法对从积分场光谱仪数据中提取的系外行星光谱形状的影响尚不清楚。所得光谱受到波长空间相关噪声的影响，这既有光学效应，也有数据处理效应。在贝叶斯大气检索框架下，我们旨在理解这些相关性及其他系统误差如何影响推断的物理参数。我们考察了三种算法（KLIP、PynPoint和ANDROMEDA），通过在HR 8799系统的SPHERE和GPI档案数据上进行一系列注入测试，优化算法参数选择。我们计算了波长相关的协方差矩阵，以衡量仪器和算法系统误差。我们在最优提取的光谱上使用petit RADTRANS进行大气检索，以评估这些数据处理系统误差对检索参数分布的影响。数据处理算法及其参数的选择对检索结果的准确性有显著影响，平均后验参数偏差相较真实输入参数在1到3σ之间。将完整协方差矩阵纳入似然函数可提高推断参数的准确性，这一效果无法通过检索框架中的临时缩放参数来补偿。采用贝叶斯信息准则和其他统计量作为启发式拟合优度指标时，包含完整协方差矩阵的检索优于仅使用对角元素的检索。

[8] Towards Generalized Characterization of Exoplanet Atmospheres with Transit Spectroscopy

2021-07-00

The field of exoplanetary sciences has grown from an era of detection to one of characterization. To date, over 4000 exoplanets have been discovered and over 50 of them have been observed with primary transit spectroscopy methods. The current population of characterized exoplanets spans a wide range of parameter space; from ultra-hot Jupiters with atmospheric temperatures beyond 3000 K, to temperate mini Neptunes that may host water in their atmospheres. Upcoming observational facilities in the next two decades will deliver exquisite spectra of exoplanet atmospheres at wavelengths never probed before, with unprecedented precision, and at much higher resolution than currently possible, effectively expanding the number of exoplanets with observed spectra. Nonetheless, an increasingly diverse planet population and higher fidelity data necessarily demand more flexible, complex, and generalized modeling frameworks. In this thesis, we present our work on atmospheric retrievals of exoplanets, focusing on investigating the robustness of the model assumptions inevitably employed to infer basic planetary conditions, compositional trends across the exoplanet mass range, and considerations for next-generation generalized retrieval frameworks. First, we present our systematic investigation of degeneracies between different model considerations in retrievals of transmission spectra and the observations that can resolve them. This study used a combination of Bayesian atmospheric retrievals and a range of common model assumptions, focusing on H2-rich atmospheres. We find that a combination of models including variable cloud coverage, prominent opacity sources, and high-precision optical and infrared spectra with current facilities enable constraints on cloud/haze properties and chemical abundances. Second, we apply our atmospheric retrieval framework to a large sample of 19 exoplanets ranging from cool mini-Neptunes to hot Jupiters. This effort constitutes the largest (i.e., broad wavelength coverage, multiple chemical species, mini-Neptunes to Jupiter sized planets) homogeneous chemical abundance survey for transiting exoplanets to date. We find a mass─metallicity trend of increasing H2O abundances with decreasing mass, significantly lower than the mass─metallicity relation for carbon in the solar system giant planets and similar predictions for exoplanets. On the other hand, the Na and K mass─metallicity trends are generally consistent with the solar system metallicity trend. We argue that the trends observed in this sample suggest different formation pathways for these close-in exoplanets compared to the long-period solar system giants. Third, we introduce Aurora, a next-generation retrieval framework for the characterization of H-rich and H-poor atmospheres. Here, we build upon state-of-the-art architectures and incorporate the following key advancements (a) a generalized compositional retrieval allowing for H-rich and H-poor atmospheres, (b) a generalized prescription for inhomogeneous clouds/hazes, (c) multiple Bayesian inference algorithms for high-dimensional retrievals, (d) modular considerations for refraction, forward scattering, and Mie scattering, and (e) noise modeling functionalities. We then carry out an investigation of the current and future chemical composition constraints for exoplanet atmospheres using this new retrieval framework. We estimate the abundance constraints achievable for hot Jupiters, mini Neptunes, and rocky exoplanets with current and upcoming observational facilities. Lastly, we present our contribution to recent studies characterizing exoplanet atmospheres using ground and space-based facilities. We perform atmospheric retrievals on a diverse population of exoplanets from ultra-hot Jupiters to temperate mini Neptunes. Among the planets studied are WASP-127b, WASP-33b, WASP-21b, K2-18b, KELT-11b, and HAT-P-41b. Our results add to the vast chemical inventory of atomic and molecular species found in exoplanet atmospheres. Moreover, our analyses unveil some of the challenges when interpreting high-precision spectroscopic data and possible instrument systematics. The atmospheric reconnaissance presented in this work explores some of the considerations needed for generalized characterization of exoplanet atmospheres with upcoming ground-based and space-based facilities. We conclude this dissertation by summarizing our findings and their implications to the broader field of exoplanet characterization. We discuss some of the outstanding questions from our research and the prospect of future modeling and retrieval approaches to robustly characterize exoplanet atmospheres. The lessons from this work highlight that, although the inferences derived from observations are strongly influenced by model assumptions, the use of physically motivated models with minimal assumptions, and broadband transmission spectra with current and future facilities can provide plausible estimates for the atmospheric properties for planets outside our solar system.

[8] 利用透射光谱实现系外行星大气的广义特征研究

系外行星科学领域已从发现时代进入表征时代。迄今已发现4000多颗系外行星，其中50多颗通过初级凌日光谱法被观测。当前已表征的系外行星涵盖了广泛参数空间：从大气温度超过3000 K的超热木星，到可能拥有水的大气的温和型迷你海王星。未来二十年即将上线的观测设施将在前所未及的波段、以前所未有的精度和更高分辨率获取系外行星大气的精细光谱，极大扩展可观测光谱的系外行星数量。然而，日益多样化的行星种群和更高质量的数据必然要求更灵活、复杂和通用的建模框架。本论文介绍了我们在系外行星大气检索方面的工作，重点研究了推断基本行星条件时模型假设的稳健性、跨行星质量范围的组成趋势，以及下一代通用检索框架的相关考量。首先，我们系统研究了透射光谱检索中不同模型假设之间的简并性及可区分这些简并的观测条件。该研究结合了贝叶斯大气检索和一系列常见模型假设，聚焦于H2丰富大气。我们发现，结合可变云覆盖、主要不透明源以及当前设施下高精度光学和红外光谱的模型，可对云/薄雾性质和化学丰度进行约束。其次，我们将大气检索框架应用于19颗从冷迷你海王星到热木星的系外行星样本。这是迄今为止最大规模（即宽波段覆盖、多种化学物种、从迷你海王星到木星大小行星）的凌日系外行星均匀化学丰度调查。我们发现，H2O丰度随质量降低而增加的质量-金属丰度趋势，显著低于太阳系巨行星碳的质量-金属丰度关系及对系外行星的类似预测。另一方面，Na和K的质量-金属丰度趋势与太阳系金属丰度趋势大致一致。我们认为，该样本中观察到的趋势表明这些近轨道系外行星与远日太阳系巨行星有不同的形成路径。第三，我们介绍了Aurora——用于H丰富和H贫大气表征的下一代检索框架。该框架在现有架构基础上，加入了以下关键进展：（a）通用组成检索，适用于H丰富和H贫大气；（b）通用不均匀云/薄雾处理；（c）多种贝叶斯推断算法，适用于高维检索；（d）可模块化考虑折射、前向散射和Mie散射；（e）噪声建模功能。随后，我们利用该新检索框架，研究了当前和未来观测设施下系外行星大气化学组成的约束能力。我们估算了当前及未来观测设施下对热木星、迷你海王星和类地系外行星可达的丰度约束。最后，我们介绍了近期利用地基和空间设施对系外行星大气进行表征的相关研究。我们对从超热木星到温和迷你海王星的多样化行星群体进行了大气检索，研究对象包括WASP-127b、WASP-33b、WASP-21b、K2-18b、KELT-11b和HAT-P-41b。我们的结果丰富了系外行星大气中已知的原子和分子物种清单。此外，我们的分析揭示了高精度光谱数据解释及仪器系统误差方面的挑战。本论文中的大气普查探讨了未来地基和空间设施下通用系外行星大气表征所需的若干考量。最后，我们总结了本研究的主要发现及其对系外行星表征领域的意义，讨论了研究中尚未解决的问题以及未来稳健表征系外行星大气的建模与检索前景。本研究的经验表明，尽管观测推断结果受模型假设影响较大，但采用物理动机的最小假设模型，以及当前和未来设施下的宽波段透射光谱，可为太阳系外行星的大气性质提供合理估算。

[1] Comparing Results from Two Uniform Phase-curve Surveys

2026-05-00

We present a comparison of the two most recent and comprehensive Spitzer phase curve studies—L. Dang et al. and M. R. Swain et al.—which report analyses of the Spitzer 4.5 μm phase curves. The studies employ different approaches for correcting instrument systematics and they also use different approaches for selecting the optimal exoplanet system parameters. To evaluate the level of consistency between the two studies, we compared the constraints on the ratio of planet-to-star radii (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mfrac><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>R</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>P</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>R</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>⋆</mml:mo></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:mrow></mml:mfrac></mml:math> </inline-formula>), eclipse depth (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mfrac><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>F</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>P</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>F</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>⋆</mml:mo></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:mrow></mml:mfrac></mml:math> </inline-formula>), phase curve amplitude (A), and phase curve offset (ϕ). We find that the two studies produce similar results at the population level although results for individual planets can vary, especially for phase curve offset values. We examined the difference of planet system parameters to see if inconsistencies in individual planet results were due to data reduction methods or system parameter choices. We also examined whether the system parameters used by both studies were consistent with Kepler's third law. During this comparison, we identified one case where stellar mass, planet semimajor axis, and orbital period did not follow Kepler's law even though the values were all compiled from the same publication. To assess whether this kind of discrepancy was recurrent, we recalculated the orbital periods using Kepler's third law and compared them with the values listed in the NASA Exoplanet Archive. Our detailed analysis of archival system parameters strongly suggests that testing reported/selected parameters for consistency with Kepler's third law is worthwhile.

[1] 两项统一相位曲线巡天结果的比较

我们对两项最新且最全面的Spitzer相位曲线研究——L. Dang等和M. R. Swain等——进行了比较，这两项工作分析了Spitzer 4.5 μm相位曲线。两项研究在仪器系统误差校正方法上采用了不同策略，并且在最优系外行星系统参数的选择上也有所不同。为评估两项研究结果的一致性，我们比较了行星与恒星半径比（Rp/R*）、食深（Fp/F*）、相位曲线振幅（A）和相位曲线偏移（ϕ）的约束。我们发现，两项研究在总体上得出相似结果，但对单个行星的结果可能存在差异，尤其是在相位曲线偏移值上。我们检查了行星系统参数的差异，以判断单个行星结果的不一致性是否源于数据处理方法或系统参数选择。我们还检查了两项研究所用系统参数是否与开普勒第三定律一致。在比较过程中，我们发现有一例中，恒星质量、行星半长轴和轨道周期并不满足开普勒定律，尽管这些数值均来自同一文献。为评估此类差异是否普遍存在，我们利用开普勒第三定律重新计算了轨道周期，并与NASA系外行星档案库中的数值进行了比较。我们对档案系统参数的详细分析强烈表明，检验已报道/选定参数是否与开普勒第三定律一致是值得的。

[2] The Ultimate Spitzer Phase Curve Survey: Empirical Trends from a Uniform Analysis

2022-12-00

Although exoplanetary sciences were not projected to be a substantial part of its original mission, investigation of exoplanets with Spitzer has set the stage for future exploration with JWST. Spitzer pioneered a powerful technique consisting of observations spanning the full orbit of an exoplanet to reveal the longitudinal temperature structure of the planet. Embedded in thermal phase curves of exoplanets are constraints on the atmospheric structure and dynamics that continue to propel future investigations. With over 30 Spitzer phase curves of hot Jupiters spanning a wide range of equilibrium temperatures and orbital configurations, the complete data set provides an unprecedented opportunity to identify trends in the properties of hot Jupiter atmospheres. We present a uniform reduction and analysis of all 4.5 μm phase curves to minimize variations caused by using different approaches. We test theoretical and empirical trends with a large uniform sample of phase curves and conclude with how to move forward with spectroscopic phase curve observations in the era of JWST and the Ariel mission launching later this decade.

[2] 终极Spitzer相位曲线巡天：基于统一分析的经验趋势

尽管系外行星科学最初并未被认为是Spitzer任务的重要组成部分，但利用Spitzer对系外行星的研究为未来JWST的探索奠定了基础。Spitzer开创了一种强有力的技术，即对系外行星整个轨道周期进行观测，从而揭示行星的经度温度结构。系外行星的热相位曲线中蕴含着对大气结构和动力学的约束，这些约束持续推动着后续研究。Spitzer已获得30余颗热木星的相位曲线，涵盖了广泛的平衡温度和轨道构型，这一完整数据集为识别热木星大气性质的趋势提供了前所未有的机会。我们对所有4.5 μm相位曲线进行了统一的数据处理和分析，以最小化因采用不同方法带来的差异。我们利用这一大样本的相位曲线检验理论和经验趋势，并讨论在JWST时代以及本十年晚些时候即将发射的Ariel任务中，如何推进光谱学相位曲线观测。

[3] Spitzer's Exoplanet Legacy: Population Trends from Phase Curve Observations

2023-01-00

While the Spitzer telescope has officially reached the end of its mission, it remains a powerful force in exoplanet characterization. Spitzer 3.6 and 4.5 micron phase curve observations allow us to measure key parameters that result from a planet's unique atmospheric circulation such as heat redistribution efficiency and hot spot shifts due to equatorial jets. Now in the era of comparative exoplanet studies, an understanding of the observed trends in these parameters is key to our understanding of the Hot Jupiter population. However, to ensure any observed trends are accurate, we must be sure that our techniques to extract these signals from the instrumental noise are reliable and reproducible. Towards those means, we have developed a more robust intrapixel sensitivity detrending method to remove the main systematic in Spitzer IRAC observations. We are using our new fixed intrapixel sensitivity map to perform as uniform of an analysis as allowed by the phase curve data. I will discuss trends in observed Hot Jupiter atmospheric parameters, as well as how these observed trends compare to three dimensional circulation model predictions to determine what they teach us about the Hot Jupiter population. Future work will continue to extend this uniform analysis to the entire population of Spitzer phase curves and improve our modeling techniques to better compare between models and data.

[3] Spitzer系外行星遗产：来自相位曲线观测的人口统计趋势

尽管Spitzer望远镜已正式结束任务，但它在系外行星特性研究中仍然具有强大作用。Spitzer 3.6和4.5微米相位曲线观测使我们能够测量由行星独特大气环流导致的关键参数，如热量再分布效率和由于赤道喷流引起的热点偏移。如今已进入系外行星对比研究时代，理解这些参数的观测趋势对于我们理解热木星种群至关重要。然而，为确保观测趋势的准确性，我们必须确保从仪器噪声中提取这些信号的方法是可靠且可复现的。为此，我们开发了一种更为稳健的像素内灵敏度去趋势方法，以去除Spitzer IRAC观测中的主要系统误差。我们正在利用新的固定像素内灵敏度图，对相位曲线数据进行尽可能统一的分析。我将讨论热木星大气参数的观测趋势，以及这些趋势与三维环流模型预测的对比，从而探讨它们对热木星种群的启示。未来工作将继续将这种统一分析扩展到全部Spitzer相位曲线样本，并改进我们的建模技术，以更好地实现模型与数据的对比。

[4] A Comprehensive Analysis of Spitzer 4.5 μm Phase Curves of Hot Jupiters

2025-01-00

Although exoplanetary science was not initially projected to be a substantial part of the Spitzer mission, its exoplanet observations set the stage for current and future surveys with JWST and Ariel. We present a comprehensive reduction and analysis of Spitzer's 4.5 μm phase curves of 29 hot Jupiters on low-eccentricity orbits. The analysis, performed with the Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis pipeline, confirms that BLISS mapping is the best detrending scheme of the three independent schemes we tested for most, but not all, observations. Visual inspection remains necessary to ensure consistency across detrending methods due to the diversity of phase-curve data and systematics. Regardless of the model selection scheme, whether using the lowest BIC or a uniform detrending approach, we observe the same trends, or lack thereof. We explore phase-curve trends as a function of irradiation temperature, orbital period, planetary radius, mass, and stellar effective temperature. We discuss the trends that are robustly detected and provide potential explanations for those that are not observed. While it is almost tautological that planets receiving greater instellation are hotter, we are still far from confirming dynamical theories of heat transport in hot Jupiter atmospheres due to the sample's diversity. Even among planets with similar temperatures, other factors like rotation and metallicity vary significantly. Larger, curated sample sizes and higher-fidelity phase-curve measurements from JWST and Ariel are needed to firmly establish the parameters governing day─night heat transport on synchronously rotating planets.

[4] 对热木星Spitzer 4.5 μm相位曲线的综合分析

尽管系外行星科学最初并未被认为是 Spitzer 任务的重要组成部分，其系外行星观测却为 JWST 和 Ariel 的当前及未来巡天奠定了基础。我们对 29 颗低偏心轨道热木星的 Spitzer 4.5 μm 相位曲线进行了全面的数据处理与分析。分析采用 Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis 管线，确认在我们测试的三种独立去趋势方法中，BLISS mapping 对大多数观测来说是最佳的，但并非全部。由于相位曲线数据和系统误差的多样性，仍需人工检查以确保不同去趋势方法间的一致性。无论采用哪种模型选择方案（最低 BIC 或统一去趋势方法），我们都观察到相同的趋势或缺乏趋势。我们探讨了相位曲线与辐照温度、轨道周期、行星半径、质量和恒星有效温度的关系，讨论了可靠探测到的趋势，并对未观察到的趋势提出可能解释。虽然几乎可以说，获得更多辐射的行星更热，但由于样本的多样性，我们仍远未证实热木星大气中热量输运的动力学理论。即使在温度相近的行星中，自转和金属丰度等其他因素也有显著差异。需要更大、精心筛选的样本和 JWST、Ariel 提供的高保真相位曲线测量，才能确立同步自转行星昼夜热量输运的主控参数。

[6] Consistency is Key: A Uniform Reanalysis of Spitzer Phase Curves

2021-00-00

Between the first exoplanet phase curve observed with the Spitzer Space Telescope and the end of mission in January 2020, a total of nearly 60 phase curves at 3.6 and 4.5 microns have been observed for 30 unique exoplanets. Spitzer observations have been plagued by systematic signals that are typically larger than the underlying planetary signal itself ─ with the primary effect being the intra-pixel sensitivities of the IRAC instrument which cause variations in measured flux as the centroid drifts within a single pixel on the detector. Systematic models to correct for this have evolved substantially over the years, with different groups having their own preferred models. WASP-43b is a prime example of the problems imposed by the strength of Spitzer systematics - several different groups get different results on the same 4.5 -micron phase curve data using different reduction methods. Even multiple observations of the 3.6-micron phase curve seem to disagree in published results using the same systematic techniques. <P />With these examples, the need for a uniform reanalysis of published Spitzer phase curves is clear ─ we cannot hope to draw comparisons across planetary parameters or between data and models when results are inconsistent and potentially unreliable. To address this problem, here we propose to use a new fixed sensitivity map method to remove the intrapixel sensitivity systematic. This map was generated with several million observations of standard calibrator stars at 4.5 microns which were normalized and scaled relative to their date of observation to remove trends in sensitivity with time. As applied to WASP-43b this new master map results in all observations of the planet's phase curve agreeing within uncertainties ─ suggesting that this technique is accurately and reliably removing the instrument systematics regardless of centroid position. We propose to apply this master sensitivity map to all previously published and publicly available Spitzer exoplanet phase curve data sets to generate a uniform and consistent catalogue of phase offsets, heat redistribution efficiencies, nightside temperature, etc. as a function of planetary parameters. <P />We will use 3D General Circulation Models (GCM) to explain the observed phase curves ─ such as how the winds are contributing to the efficiency of heat transport from the day side to night side and how shifted the hot spot is relative to the substellar point (that of highest irradiation). However, with previous inconsistencies in results, it has been hard to match phase curve observations with current modeling efforts. Discrepancies between 3D GCMs and observed phase curves have been able to be shrugged off as systematics that are not fully understood or removed, which has been a valid argument when every published result has used different systematic removal methods. In this work, the proposed application of a uniform systematic model to the entire suite of Spitzer phase curves will allow us to better understand why our atmospheric models don't appear to match observations, and what can be done to remove the mismatch between them. A uniform data set will demand a better modeling scheme and therefore in this work we will apply relevant updates to our team's 3D GCM, including moving away from double grey radiative transfer for a more accurate representation of the energy budget in the atmosphere, as well as exploring additional parameters for atmospheric composition, wavelength dependent absorptions coefficients, and clouds and hazes. <P />This proposal addresses the XRP goal of observationally characterizing exoplanets and their atmospheres. It will greatly improve upon the currently published exoplanet phase curves to better enable exoplanet characterization, as well as provide advancements in our modeling capabilities for Hot Jupiters to better explain and understand the observations.

[6] 一致性为关键：Spitzer 相位曲线的统一再分析

自斯皮策空间望远镜首次观测到系外行星相位曲线至2020年1月任务结束，共有30颗独特系外行星在3.6和4.5微米波段获得了近60条相位曲线。斯皮策观测一直受到系统性信号的困扰，这些信号通常大于行星本身的信号——主要问题是IRAC仪器的像素内灵敏度，导致当质心在单个像素内漂移时测得的通量发生变化。用于校正这些系统性的模型多年来有了显著发展，不同研究团队有各自偏好的模型。WASP-43b是斯皮策系统性问题的典型例子——不同团队对同一4.5微米相位曲线数据采用不同数据处理方法得到不同结果。即使是3.6微米相位曲线的多次观测，采用相同系统性处理技术发表的结果也存在分歧。<P />这些例子表明，对已发表斯皮策相位曲线进行统一再分析的必要性——当结果不一致且可能不可靠时，我们无法在行星参数之间或数据与模型之间进行比较。为解决这一问题，我们提议采用一种新的固定灵敏度图方法来去除像素内灵敏度系统性。该灵敏度图由数百万次4.5微米标准校准星观测生成，并根据观测日期归一化和缩放，以去除随时间变化的灵敏度趋势。应用于WASP-43b时，这一新主灵敏度图使所有该行星的相位曲线观测在不确定度范围内一致——表明该技术能够准确可靠地去除仪器系统性，无论质心位置如何。我们计划将该主灵敏度图应用于所有已发表和公开的斯皮策系外行星相位曲线数据集，生成一个统一、一致的相位偏移、热量再分布效率、夜面温度等随行星参数变化的目录。<P />我们将使用三维大气环流模型（GCM）解释观测到的相位曲线——例如风如何影响从日面到夜面的热量输运效率，以及热点相对于亚恒星点（最高辐射点）的偏移程度。然而，由于先前结果的不一致，难以将相位曲线观测与当前建模工作相匹配。三维GCM与观测相位曲线之间的差异常被归因于尚未完全理解或去除的系统性，这在每个已发表结果都采用不同系统性去除方法时是合理的。在本工作中，拟对全部斯皮策相位曲线应用统一的系统性模型，将帮助我们更好地理解为何大气模型与观测不符，以及如何消除二者之间的不匹配。统一的数据集将对建模方案提出更高要求，因此我们将在本工作中对团队的三维GCM进行相关更新，包括摒弃双灰辐射传输以更准确地表示大气能量收支，并探索大气组成、波长相关吸收系数以及云和薄雾等附加参数。<P />本提案响应了XRP关于观测特性分析系外行星及其大气的目标。它将极大提升当前已发表的系外行星相位曲线数据，从而更好地支持系外行星特性分析，并推动我们对热木星的建模能力，以更好地解释和理解观测结果。

[7] SPCA: Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis

2025-02-00

SPCA (Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis) analyzes Spitzer/IRAC observations of exoplanets. It implements 2D polynomial, Pixel Level Decorrelation, BiLinearly-Interpolated Sub-pixel Sensitivity mapping, and Gaussian Process decorrelation methods, allowing the user to change techniques by setting a single variable. The code's modular structure enables integration of custom astrophysical models and decorrelation methods. SPCA can reduce and decorrelate multiple datasets with a single command.

[7] SPCA：Spitzer相位曲线分析

SPCA（Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis）用于分析Spitzer/IRAC的系外行星观测数据。它实现了二维多项式、像素级去相关、双线性插值亚像素灵敏度映射和高斯过程去相关方法，用户可通过设置单一变量切换技术。该代码的模块化结构支持集成自定义天体物理模型和去相关方法。SPCA可通过一条命令对多个数据集进行数据处理和去相关。

[8] An Exploration of Model Degeneracies with a Unified Phase Curve Retrieval Analysis: The Light and Dark Sides of WASP-43 b

2021-05-00

The analysis of exoplanetary atmospheres often relies upon the observation of transit or eclipse events. While very powerful, these snapshots provide mainly one-dimensional information on the planet structure and do not easily allow precise latitude-longitude characterizations. The phase curve technique, which consists of measuring the planet emission throughout its entire orbit, can break this limitation and provide useful two-dimensional thermal and chemical constraints on the atmosphere. As of today, however, computing performances have limited our ability to perform unified retrieval studies on the full set of observed spectra from phase curve observations at the same time. Here, we present a new phase curve model that enables fast, unified retrieval capabilities. We apply our technique to the combined phase curve data from the Hubble and Spitzer space telescopes of the hot Jupiter WASP-43 b. We tested different scenarios and discussed the dependence of our solution on different assumptions in the model. Our more comprehensive approach suggests that multiple interpretations of this data set are possible, but our more complex model is consistent with the presence of thermal inversions and a metal-rich atmosphere, contrasting with previous data analyses, although this likely depends on the Spitzer data reduction. The detailed constraints extracted here demonstrate the importance of developing and understanding advanced phase curve techniques, which we believe will unlock access to a richer picture of exoplanet atmospheres.

[8] 统一相位曲线检索分析下的模型简并性探索：WASP-43 b 的明暗两面

系外行星大气的分析通常依赖于凌星或掩食事件的观测。虽然这些方法非常有力，但它们主要提供关于行星结构的一维信息，难以实现精确的纬度-经度特征刻画。相位曲线技术，即在整个轨道周期内测量行星的发射，可以突破这一限制，为大气提供有用的二维热和化学约束。然而，迄今为止，计算性能限制了我们对相位曲线观测获得的全部光谱数据同时进行统一检索研究的能力。在此，我们提出了一种新的相位曲线模型，实现了快速、统一的检索能力。我们将该技术应用于热木星WASP-43 b的Hubble和Spitzer空间望远镜联合相位曲线数据。我们测试了不同情景，并讨论了模型中不同假设对解的影响。我们更全面的方法表明，该数据集存在多种解释，但我们更复杂的模型与存在热反转和富金属大气相一致，这与先前的数据分析结果相反，尽管这很可能取决于Spitzer数据的处理。这里获得的详细约束展示了发展和理解先进相位曲线技术的重要性，我们认为这将开启对系外行星大气更丰富图景的探索。

[1] Revisiting the Iconic Spitzer Phase Curve of 55 Cancri e: Not So Strange After All?

2022-06-00

Thermal phase curves of short-period planets provide the best constraints on the atmospheric dynamics and heat transport in exoplanet atmospheres. The published Spitzer phase curve of 55 Cancri e, an ultra-short period super-Earth, exhibits a large hot spot offset, suggesting significant eastward heat recirculation, unexpected on such a hot planet. We present our re-reduction and analysis of these iconic observations using the open source and modular Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis Pipeline (SPCA). In particular, we attempt to reproduce the published analysis using bi-linear interpolation, but also test alternative instrument detrending schemes. We study how the retrieved dayside temperature, nightside temperature, and longitudinal offset of the planet's hotspot depend on the reduction and detrending. Our re-analysis suggests that 55 Cancri e may have a negligible hot spot offset of 14 ± 10 degrees west, as expected from theoretical considerations and consistent with other ultra-short-period planets. We also report an eclipse depth of 195 ± 24 p.p.m., a phase semi-amplitude of 95 ± 13 p.p.m., a dayside brightness temperature of 3767 ± 424 K and a nightside brightness temperature of 1033 ± 253 K.

[1] 重访标志性Spitzer 55 Cancri e相位曲线：其实并不奇怪？

短周期行星的热相位曲线为系外行星大气动力学和热量输运提供了最佳约束。已发表的 Spitzer 55 Cancri e 相位曲线显示出显著的热点偏移，表明存在强烈的向东热再循环，这在如此高温的行星上是出乎意料的。我们利用开源模块化的 Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis Pipeline（SPCA）对这些标志性观测数据进行了重新归约和分析。我们尝试通过双线性插值复现已发表的分析结果，同时也测试了其他仪器去趋势方法。我们研究了所检索的日面温度、夜面温度和行星热点经度偏移如何依赖于数据归约和去趋势方法。我们的再分析表明，55 Cancri e 的热点偏移可能可以忽略不计，为 14 ± 10 度偏西，这与理论预期及其他超短周期行星一致。我们还报告了 195 ± 24 ppm 的食深、95 ± 13 ppm 的相位半振幅、3767 ± 424 K 的日面亮温和 1033 ± 253 K 的夜面亮温。

[2] Revisiting the Iconic Spitzer Phase Curve of 55 Cancri e: Hotter Dayside, Cooler Nightside, and Smaller Phase Offset

2022-11-00

Thermal phase curves of short-period exoplanets provide the best constraints on the atmospheric dynamics and heat transport in their atmospheres. The published Spitzer Space Telescope phase curve of 55 Cancri e, an ultra-short-period super-Earth, exhibits a large phase offset suggesting significant eastward heat recirculation, unexpected on such a hot planet. We present our rereduction and analysis of these iconic observations using the open source and modular Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis pipeline. In particular, we attempt to reproduce the published analysis using the same instrument detrending scheme as the original authors. We retrieve the dayside temperature ( ${T}_{\mathrm{day}}={3771}_{-520}^{+669}$ K), nightside temperature ( ${T}_{\mathrm{night}\,}\lt 1649$ K at 2σ), and longitudinal offset of the planet's hot spot, and quantify how they depend on the reduction and detrending. Our reanalysis suggests that 55 Cancri e has a negligible hot spot offset of $-{12}_{-18}^{+21}$ degrees east. The small phase offset and cool nightside are consistent with the poor heat transport expected on ultra-short-period planets. The high dayside 4.5 μm brightness temperature is qualitatively consistent with SiO emission from an inverted rock vapor atmosphere.

[2] 重访标志性Spitzer 55 Cancri e相位曲线：更热的昼面、更冷的夜面和更小的相位偏移

短周期系外行星的热相位曲线为其大气动力学和热量输运提供了最佳约束。超短周期超级地球 55 Cancri e 的已发表 Spitzer 空间望远镜相位曲线显示出较大的相位偏移，暗示存在显著的向东热再循环，这在如此高温行星上并不常见。我们利用开源模块化的 Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis 管道对这些标志性观测进行了重新归约和分析。特别地，我们尝试采用与原作者相同的仪器去趋势方案复现已发表的分析结果。我们检索了白昼面温度（${T}_{\mathrm{day}}={3771}_{-520}^{+669}$ K）、夜晚面温度（${T}_{\mathrm{night}\,}\lt 1649$ K，2σ）以及行星热点的经度偏移，并量化了这些参数对归约和去趋势方法的依赖性。我们的再分析表明，55 Cancri e 的热点偏移可以忽略，为 $-{12}_{-18}^{+21}$ 度向东。小的相位偏移和较冷的夜晚面与超短周期行星预期的低效热输运一致。白昼面 4.5 μm 的高亮温与反转岩石蒸气大气中的 SiO 发射在定性上相符。

[3] Spitzer's Exoplanet Legacy: Population Trends from Phase Curve Observations

2023-01-00

While the Spitzer telescope has officially reached the end of its mission, it remains a powerful force in exoplanet characterization. Spitzer 3.6 and 4.5 micron phase curve observations allow us to measure key parameters that result from a planet's unique atmospheric circulation such as heat redistribution efficiency and hot spot shifts due to equatorial jets. Now in the era of comparative exoplanet studies, an understanding of the observed trends in these parameters is key to our understanding of the Hot Jupiter population. However, to ensure any observed trends are accurate, we must be sure that our techniques to extract these signals from the instrumental noise are reliable and reproducible. Towards those means, we have developed a more robust intrapixel sensitivity detrending method to remove the main systematic in Spitzer IRAC observations. We are using our new fixed intrapixel sensitivity map to perform as uniform of an analysis as allowed by the phase curve data. I will discuss trends in observed Hot Jupiter atmospheric parameters, as well as how these observed trends compare to three dimensional circulation model predictions to determine what they teach us about the Hot Jupiter population. Future work will continue to extend this uniform analysis to the entire population of Spitzer phase curves and improve our modeling techniques to better compare between models and data.

[4] Spitzer thermal phase curve of WASP-121 b

2023-08-00

<BR /> Aims: We analyse unpublished Spitzer observations of the thermal phase-curve of WASP-121 b, a benchmark ultra-hot Jupiter. <BR /> Methods: We adopted the wavelet pixel-independent component analysis technique to remove challenging instrumental systematic effects in these datasets and we fit them simultaneously with parametric light-curve models. We also performed phase-curve retrievals to better understand the horizontal and vertical thermal structure of the planetary atmosphere. <BR /> Results: We measured planetary brightness temperatures of ~2700 K (dayside) and ~700-1100 K (nightside), along with modest peak offsets of 5.9° ± 1.6 (3.6 µm) and 5.0°<SUB>‒3.1</SUB><SUP>+3.4</SUP> (4.5 µm) after mid-eclipse. These results suggest inefficient heat redistribution in the atmosphere of WASP-121 b. The inferred atmospheric Bond albedo and circulation efficiency align well with observed trends for hot giant exoplanets. Interestingly, the measured peak offsets correspond to a westward hot spot, which has rarely been observed. We also report consistent transit depths at 3.6 and 4.5 µm, along with updated geometric and orbital parameters. Finally, we compared our Spitzer results with previous measurements, including recent JWST observations. <BR /> Conclusions: We extracted new information on the thermal properties and dynamics of an exoplanet atmosphere from an especially problematic dataset. This study probes the reliability of exoplanet phase-curve parameters obtained from Spitzer observations when state-of-the-art pipelines are adopted to remove the instrumental systematic effects. It demonstrates that Spitzer phase-curve observations provide a useful baseline for comparison with JWST observations, and shows the increase in parameters precision achieved with the newer telescope.

[4] WASP-121 b的Spitzer热相位曲线

目标：我们分析了尚未发表的WASP-121 b（一颗基准超热木星）热相位曲线的Spitzer观测数据。 方法：我们采用小波像素独立成分分析技术，以去除这些数据集中具有挑战性的仪器系统效应，并与参数化光变曲线模型同时拟合。我们还进行了相位曲线检索，以更好地理解行星大气的水平和垂直热结构。 结果：我们测得行星白昼面亮温约为2700 K，夜晚面约为700-1100 K，峰值偏移分别为5.9° ± 1.6（3.6 µm）和5.0°<SUB>‒3.1</SUB><SUP>+3.4</SUP>（4.5 µm），均发生在中掩食后。这些结果表明WASP-121 b大气中的热量再分配效率较低。推断的大气Bond反照率和环流效率与热巨行星的观测趋势一致。有趣的是，测得的峰值偏移对应于西向热点，这种现象很少被观测到。我们还报告了3.6和4.5 µm波段一致的凌日深度，以及更新后的几何和轨道参数。最后，我们将Spitzer结果与先前的观测，包括最新的JWST观测进行了比较。 结论：我们从一个特别有问题的数据集中提取了关于系外行星大气热性质和动力学的新信息。本研究检验了采用最先进数据处理流程去除仪器系统效应后，Spitzer观测获得的系外行星相位曲线参数的可靠性。结果表明，Spitzer相位曲线观测为与JWST观测的对比提供了有用的基线，并展示了新一代望远镜带来的参数精度提升。

[5] Investigation of the infrared profiles of exoplanet HD 209458 b secondary eclipses

2024-12-00

In this work we process infrared light curves for 13 secondary eclipses of the exoplanet HD 209458 b, obtained with the Spitzer Space Telescope. The objective of the study was to identify any discrepancies that might be attributed to the inhomogeneity of the planet's surface brightness or a difference of the planet's shape from a sphere. Firstly, an attempt was made to ascertain whether the observed midpoint of the secondary eclipse exhibited a shift from the ephemeris moment. This could indicate the presence of a hot spot on the planet's surface that is shifted from the central meridian. The analysis allowed for the correlated noise that remained in the IRAC detector data after aperture photometry by approximating the noise with a Gaussian random process. As a result, we detected two components of red noise with different time scales: $\sim\!10$ s and $\sim\!5$ min. In addition, we constructed a unified model of the effect of inhomogeneous pixel sensitivity as a periodic function of the brightness centroid coordinates, which works well for all the analyzed eclipses. According to the results, the main parameters of the HD 209458 b eclipse coincided with those obtained by other authors: a depth of $0.125 \pm 0.004$% and a shift of the observed midpoint of $-0.53 \pm 0.61$ min.

[5] HD 209458 b次生凌星红外光谱的研究

本工作处理了利用 Spitzer 空间望远镜获得的系外行星 HD 209458 b 13 次次生食的红外光变曲线。研究目标是识别可能归因于行星表面亮度不均或行星形状偏离球体的任何差异。首先，尝试确定观测到的次生食中点是否相对于星历时刻发生偏移，这可能表明行星表面存在偏离中央子午线的热点。分析中考虑了在光圈光度测量后 IRAC 探测器数据中残留的相关噪声，通过高斯随机过程对噪声进行近似。结果检测到两种不同时间尺度的红噪声分量：约10秒和约5分钟。此外，我们构建了一个统一模型，将像素灵敏度不均的影响表示为亮度质心坐标的周期函数，对所有分析的次生食均适用。结果显示，HD 209458 b 次生食的主要参数与其他作者获得的结果一致：食深为0.125 ± 0.004%，观测中点偏移为-0.53 ± 0.61分钟。

[6] A JWST Optical and Infrared Phase Curve of NGTS-10b

2024-02-00

Since the first photometric phase curve observation from Spitzer, hot Jupiter atmospheres have challenged our understanding of atmospheric dynamics. Large studies have shown that many optical phase curves exhibit westward offsets, while most infrared phase curves have eastward offsets. The former is attributed to the enhanced formation of reflective clouds near the morning terminator, whereas the latter is connected primarily to equatorial jets redistributing heat from the hot dayside to the cooler nightside. This inverse correlation between the cloud and temperature maps can provide strong insights into cloud formation processes in hot Jupiters. We will present results from a full-orbit phase curve of NGTS-10b, measured simultaneously at optical and near-IR wavelengths using the unique capabilities of NIRSpec/PRISM. This includes showing the planet's wavelength-dependent hotspot offsets and heat redistribution efficiencies, phase-resolved emission spectra, and transmission spectrum. We will also discuss how our isobaric map of NGTS-10b informs current theoretical models and its implications on future JWST phase curve observations.

[6] NGTS-10b的JWST光学和红外相位曲线

[7] Search for deviations in exoplanetary infrared profiles, based on the secondary eclipse analysis

2022-00-00

Aperture photometry of Spitzer space telescope observations of HD209458 b in the 3.6µm channel was conducted. To find deviations in the exoplanetary infrared emission, the modelling of secondary eclipse light curves was carried out. [8] An Analysis of Spitzer Phase Curves for WASP-77Ab and WASP-121b

2024-02-00

Although Spitzer is no longer an active mission, its collection of over 30 exoplanet phase curve observations remain a valuable resource for comparative planetology of hot Jupiters and for informing future observations with JWST. [1] Demystifying K2-18 b: exploring the causes of disparate atmospheric and interior hypotheses

2026-02-00

The temperate sub-Neptune K2-18 b has emerged as a keystone target for our understanding of both the atmospheres and interiors of small exoplanets. Despite a substantial JWST observational campaign incorporating multiple instruments and covering a broad wavelength range, the nature of this planet is still heavily debated, with different studies reaching drastically different conclusions. Previous analyses have suggested that this planet may host a thin H/He-rich atmosphere atop a liquid water ocean, or that it may be a gas dwarf whose atmosphere is a product of interactions between hydrogen and a magma ocean, or that K2-18 b is a scaled-down, metal-enriched version of a gas giant planet. <P />Here, we present extensive modeling that seeks to uncover the reasons underlying these disparate interpretations. We discuss how atmospheric inferences for K2-18 b are highly sensitive to both data and model considerations, such as offsets between instruments and the treatment of clouds and hazes. We show that seemingly minor changes in the chosen modeling approach can result in dramatically different interpretations. We also discuss how the conclusions drawn about the atmosphere of K2-18 b have significant implications for the planet's interior structure. We summarise our current overall understanding of the atmosphere and interior of this planet, and present possible future directions to finally resolve the mysteries of this intriguing world.

[1] 揭开K2-18 b之谜：探索不同大气与内部假说的成因

[2] 1D atmospheric study of the temperate sub-Neptune K2-18b

2021-02-00

Context. The atmospheric composition of exoplanets with masses between 2 and 10 M<SUB>⊕</SUB> is poorly understood. In that regard, the sub-Neptune K2-18b, which is subject to Earth-like stellar irradiation, offers a valuable opportunity for the characterisation of such atmospheres. Previous analyses of its transmission spectrum from the Kepler, Hubble (HST), and Spitzer space telescopes data using both retrieval algorithms and forward-modelling suggest the presence of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and an H<SUB>2</SUB>-He atmosphere, but have not detected other gases, such as CH<SUB>4</SUB>. <BR /> Aims: We present simulations of the atmosphere of K2-18 b using Exo-REM, our self-consistent 1D radiative-equilibrium model, using a large grid of atmospheric parameters to infer constraints on its chemical composition. <BR /> Methods: We compared the transmission spectra computed by our model with the above-mentioned data (0.4-5 μm), assuming an H<SUB>2</SUB>-He dominated atmosphere. We investigated the effects of irradiation, eddy diffusion coefficient, internal temperature, clouds, C/O ratio, and metallicity on the atmospheric structure and transit spectrum. <BR /> Results: We show that our simulations favour atmospheric metallicities between 40 and 500 times solar and indicate, in some cases, the formation of H<SUB>2</SUB>O-ice clouds, but not liquid H<SUB>2</SUB>O clouds. We also confirm the findings of our previous study, which showed that CH<SUB>4</SUB> absorption features nominally dominate the transmission spectrum in the HST spectral range. We compare our results with results from retrieval algorithms and find that the H<SUB>2</SUB>O-dominated spectrum interpretation is either due to the omission of CH<SUB>4</SUB> absorptions or a strong overfitting of the data. Finally, we investigated different scenarios that would allow for a CH<SUB>4</SUB>-depleted atmosphere. We were able to fit the data to those scenarios, finding, however, that it is very unlikely for K2-18b to have a high internal temperature. A low C/O ratio (≈0.01-0.1) allows for H<SUB>2</SUB>O to dominate the transmission spectrum and can fit the data but so far, this set-up lacks a physical explanation. Simulations with a C/O ratio &lt;0.01 are not able to fit the data satisfactorily.

[2] 温带亚海王星 K2-18b 的一维大气研究

背景：质量介于2到10 M⊕之间的系外行星大气成分尚不清楚。在这方面，受到类似地球辐照的亚海王星K2-18b为此类大气的特性研究提供了宝贵机会。此前利用Kepler、Hubble（HST）和Spitzer空间望远镜数据，通过检索算法和前向建模分析其透射光谱，均表明存在H2O和H2-He大气，但未探测到其他气体，如CH4。 目的：我们利用自洽的一维辐射平衡模型Exo-REM，对K2-18b的大气进行模拟，采用大范围的大气参数网格以推断其化学成分的约束。 方法：我们将模型计算得到的透射光谱与上述数据（0.4-5 μm）进行比较，假设大气以H2-He为主。我们研究了辐照、湍流扩散系数、内部温度、云、C/O比和金属丰度对大气结构和凌日光谱的影响。 结果：我们的模拟结果支持大气金属丰度为40到500倍太阳值，并在某些情况下指示H2O冰云的形成，但未出现液态H2O云。我们还证实了此前研究的发现，即在HST波段范围内，CH4吸收特征在名义上主导透射光谱。我们将结果与检索算法的结果进行了比较，发现H2O主导的光谱解释要么是由于忽略了CH4吸收，要么是对数据的过拟合。最后，我们探讨了可能导致CH4贫乏大气的不同情景。我们能够用这些情景拟合数据，但发现K2-18b具有高内部温度的可能性极低。低C/O比（约0.01-0.1）可使H2O主导透射光谱并拟合数据，但目前这种设定缺乏物理解释。C/O比<0.01的模拟无法令人满意地拟合数据。

[3] 利用高分辨率光谱观测KELT-20 b大气中的水解离和旋转展宽

[4] Stellar Surface Inhomogeneities as a Potential Source of the Atmospheric Signal Detected in the K2-18b Transmission Spectrum

2021-12-00

Transmission spectroscopy of transiting exoplanets is a proven technique that can yield information on the composition and structure of a planet's atmosphere. However, transmission spectra may be compromised by inhomogeneities in the stellar photosphere. The sub-Neptune-sized habitable zone planet K2-18b has water absorption detected in its atmosphere using data from the Hubble Space Telescope (HST). Herein, we examine whether the reported planetary atmospheric signal seen from HST transmission spectroscopy of K2-18b could instead be induced by time-varying starspots. We built a time-variable spectral model of K2-18 that is designed to match the variability amplitude seen in K2 photometric data, and we used this model to simulate 1000 HST data sets that follow the K2-18b observation strategy. More than 1% of these provide a better fit to the data than the best-fitting exoplanet atmosphere model. After resampling our simulations to generate synthetic HST observations, we find that 40% of random draws would produce an atmospheric detection at a level at least as significant as that seen in the actual HST data of K2-18b. This work illustrates that the inferred detection of an atmosphere on K2-18b may alternatively be explained by stellar spectral contamination due to the inhomogeneous photosphere of K2-18. We do not rule out a detection of water in the planet's atmosphere, but we provide a plausible alternative that should be considered and conclude that more observations are needed to fully rule out stellar contamination.

[4] 恒星表面不均匀性作为 K2-18b 透射光谱中大气信号的潜在来源

[5] Hazy with a Chance of Star Spots: Constraining the Atmosphere of Young Planet K2-33b

2023-01-00

Although all-sky surveys have led to the discovery of dozens of young planets, little is known about their atmospheres. Here, we present multiwavelength transit data for the super-Neptune sized exoplanet, K2-33b-the youngest (~10 Myr) transiting exoplanet to date. We combined photometric observations of K2-33 covering a total of 33 transits spanning &gt;2 yr, taken from K2, MEarth, the Hubble Space Telescope (HST), and Spitzer. The transit photometry spanned from the optical to the near-infrared (0.6-4.5 μm), enabling us to construct a transmission spectrum of the planet. We find that the optical transit depths are nearly a factor of 2 deeper than those from the near-infrared. This difference holds across multiple data sets taken over years, ruling out issues of data analysis and unconstrained systematics. Surface inhomogeneities on the young star can reproduce some of the difference, but required spot coverage fractions (&gt;60%) are ruled out by the observed stellar spectrum (&lt;20%). We find a better fit to the transmission spectrum using photochemical hazes, which were predicted to be strong in young, moderate-temperature, and large-radius planets like K2-33b. A tholin haze with CO as the dominant gaseous carbon carrier in the atmosphere can reasonably reproduce the data with small or no stellar surface inhomogeneities, consistent with the stellar spectrum. The HST data quality is insufficient for the detection of any molecular features. More observations would be required to fully characterize the hazes and spot properties and confirm the presence of CO suggested by current data.

[5] 有雾且可能有恒星黑子：约束年轻行星K2-33b的大气

[6] A visit to the K2-3 system: models of three planetary atmospheres spanning the radius valley informed by a high energy stellar spectrum and updated system properties

2022-06-00

Multi-planet systems offer insight into a host star's influence on its planets' atmospheres. K2-3 is an M0 star (effective temperature of 3880 K) orbited by three small transiting planets, K2-3b, c, and d (2.0, 1.6, and 1.4 Earth radii, respectively). We combine stellar spectral information with self-consistently derived stellar and planetary properties to investigate the K2-3 system. We measure the UV flux of K2-3 with HST/COS and place an upper limit on its X-ray flux with XMM-Newton. We use empirical scaling techniques and a differential emission measure to fill in gaps in the high energy spectrum. We constrain the K2-3 system properties by simultaneously fitting transit photometry, radial velocity, and spectral energy density information. The radii of all three planets are constrained to 2% and the masses of the inner two planets, K2-3b and c, to 10%, while the outermost planet, K2-3d, is not detected in the RV measurements. K2-3b and c are enhanced in volatiles and consistent with sub-Neptune-like compositions, while K2-3d is likely terrestrial due to its small radius. The compositions of sub-Neptune planets are highly degenerate, and indeed we find that the radii and masses of K2-3b and c allow for solar composition envelopes making up 0.30% and 0.05% of their masses, respectively, as well as water steam atmospheres making up for 50% and 13% of their masses. However, the solar composition scenarios are unlikely given the high energy output of K2-3; the timescale for atmospheric escape (1-2 Gyr) is short compared to the estimated age of the system (3-12 Gyr). Additionally, assuming that K2-3d is terrestrial, we find that the architecture of the K2-3 planets is inconsistent with photoevaporation as the sole mechanism for sculpting their atmospheres. Rather we must invoke more stochastic processes such as gas-depleted planet formation or giant impacts to account for the observed system architecture. Finally, we present model transmission spectra that include photochemical reactions and disequilibrium chemistry for a range of atmospheric cases: for K2-3 b and c we model the aforementioned solar composition and steam atmosphere cases, while for K2-3d we model early Venus, modern Venus, and modern Earth cases. Discriminating between these atmospheric cases will require spectral information about the atmospheres of the K2-3 planets, something that is achievable with current observatories. This work serves as a template for future investigations into the atmospheres of small exoplanets in multi-planet systems in the context of their host stars.

[6] K2-3系统考察：结合高能恒星光谱和更新的系统参数对跨越半径谷的三颗行星大气建模

多行星系统为研究母星对其行星大气的影响提供了洞见。K2-3是一颗M0型恒星（有效温度3880 K），有三颗小型凌星行星K2-3b、c和d（半径分别为2.0、1.6和1.4地球半径）环绕。我们结合恒星光谱信息与自洽推导的恒星和行星属性，对K2-3系统进行研究。我们利用HST/COS测量了K2-3的紫外通量，并用XMM-Newton对其X射线通量给出了上限。我们采用经验标度技术和微分发射测度补全高能谱的空白。通过同时拟合凌星光度、径向速度和光谱能量密度信息，我们约束了K2-3系统的属性。三颗行星的半径被约束在2%以内，内侧两颗行星K2-3b和c的质量被约束在10%以内，而最外侧的K2-3d在径向速度测量中未探测到。K2-3b和c富含挥发物，成分与亚海王星类似，而K2-3d由于半径较小，很可能是类地行星。亚海王星的成分高度简并，我们发现K2-3b和c的半径和质量允许其包层分别为0.30%和0.05%太阳成分，或分别为50%和13%的水蒸气大气。然而，考虑到K2-3的高能输出，太阳成分包层情景不太可能；大气逃逸的时间尺度（1-2 Gyr）远短于系统估计年龄（3-12 Gyr）。此外，假设K2-3d为类地行星，我们发现K2-3行星的结构与光致蒸发作为唯一塑造其大气机制的情景不符。我们必须引入如气体耗竭行星形成或巨型碰撞等更随机的过程来解释观测到的系统结构。最后，我们给出了包含光化学反应和非平衡化学的大气模型透射光谱，涵盖多种大气情景：对K2-3b和c，我们模拟了上述太阳成分和水蒸气大气情景；对K2-3d，我们模拟了早期金星、现代金星和现代地球情景。区分这些大气情景需要K2-3行星大气的光谱信息，这是当前观测设备可以实现的。本工作为未来在母星背景下研究多行星系统中小型系外行星大气提供了模板。

[7] Disentangling atmospheric compositions of K2-18 b with next generation facilities

2022-04-00

Recent analysis of the planet K2-18 b has shown the presence of water vapour in its atmosphere. While the H<SUB>2</SUB>O detection is significant, the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) WFC3 spectrum suggests three possible solutions of very different nature which can equally match the data. The three solutions are a primary cloudy atmosphere with traces of water vapour (cloudy sub-Neptune), a secondary atmosphere with a substantial amount (up to 50% Volume Mixing Ratio) of H<SUB>2</SUB>O (icy/water world) and/or an undetectable gas such as N<SUB>2</SUB> (super-Earth). Additionally, the atmospheric pressure and the possible presence of a liquid/solid surface cannot be investigated with currently available observations. In this paper we used the best fit parameters from Tsiaras et al. (Nat. Astron. 3, 1086, 2019) to build James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) and Ariel simulations of the three scenarios. We have investigated 18 retrieval cases, which encompass the three scenarios and different observational strategies with the two observatories. Retrieval results show that twenty combined transits should be enough for the Ariel mission to disentangle the three scenarios, while JWST would require only two transits if combining NIRISS and NIRSpec data. This makes K2-18 b an ideal target for atmospheric follow-ups by both facilities and highlights the capabilities of the next generation of space-based infrared observatories to provide a complete picture of low mass planets.

Astron. 3, 1086, 2019）给出的最佳拟合参数，构建了James Webb Space Telescope（JWST）和Ariel对三种情景的模拟。我们研究了18种检索情形，涵盖三种情景及两台望远镜的不同观测策略。检索结果显示，Ariel任务只需联合20次凌日观测即可区分三种情景，而JWST若结合NIRISS和NIRSpec数据，仅需两次凌日观测。这使K2-18 b成为两台设施进行大气后续观测的理想目标，并突显了下一代空间红外望远镜在全面揭示低质量行星方面的能力。

[8] A Disintegrating Rocky World Shrouded in Dust and Gas: Mid-infrared Observations of K2-22 b Using JWST

2025-07-00

The disintegrating ultrashort period rocky exoplanet K2-22 b periodically emits dusty clouds in a dynamically chaotic process resulting in a variable transit depth from 0% to 1.3%. The effluents that sublimate off the surface and condense out in space are probably representative of the formerly interior layers convectively transported to the molten surface. Transmission spectroscopy of these transiting clouds reveals spectral fingerprints of the interior composition of this rocky world. We used JWST's Mid-Infrared Instrument as a low-resolution slitless spectrograph to observe four predicted transit windows for K2-22 b. For each observation, we extracted a transmission spectrum over the spectral range of 4.4─11.8 μm. Over the spectral range of 4.4─8 μm, where the spectral precision is highest, we detect one transit at high significance and two at low significance. While the signal-to-noise ratio of the spectrum limits our ability to draw firm conclusions, we find that the data (1) disfavor featureless, iron-dominated core material, (2) are consistent with some form of magnesium silicate minerals, likely from mantle material, and (3) show a distinct and unexpected feature at ∼5 μm. The unexpected feature, also seen weakly in the low-significance transits, is consistent with an unknown gaseous absorber, possibly NO and/or CO<SUB>2</SUB>. These findings warrant further study to improve the constraints on the composition of this disintegrating rocky world.

[8] 被尘埃和气体包裹的解体岩石世界：利用JWST对K2-22 b的中红外观测

超短周期解体岩石系外行星K2-22 b以动力学混沌的过程周期性释放尘埃云，导致凌日深度在0%至1.3%之间变化。从表面升华并在太空中凝结的物质很可能代表了曾经的内部层，通过对流输运至熔融表面。对这些凌日尘云的透射光谱观测揭示了该岩石行星内部成分的光谱指纹。我们利用JWST中红外仪器作为低分辨率无缝光谱仪，对K2-22 b的四个预测凌日窗口进行了观测。每次观测都在4.4─11.8 μm波段提取了透射光谱。在4.4─8 μm波段（光谱精度最高），我们高显著性探测到一次凌日，低显著性探测到两次。尽管光谱的信噪比限制了我们得出明确结论的能力，但我们发现：（1）数据不支持无特征、铁主导的核心物质；（2）与某种形式的镁硅酸盐矿物一致，可能来自地幔物质；（3）在约5 μm处出现了一个明显且意外的特征。该意外特征在低显著性凌日中也有微弱表现，与未知气体吸收体一致，可能为NO和/或CO2。这些发现值得进一步研究，以更好地约束该解体岩石行星的成分。
