Does the central conclusion of the highly cited study "A Consistent Retrieval Analysis of 10 Hot Jupiters Observed in Transmission" hold under independent datasets and alternative analysis assumptions?
高被引研究“A Consistent Retrieval Analysis of 10 Hot Jupiters Observed in Transmission”的核心结论在独立数据集和不同分析假设下是否成立？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] A Uniform Reanalysis of Hot Jupiters

2023-01-00

The formation and evolution of hot Jupiters is still an open question in exoplanet research, but much of what we know about them is influenced by the assumptions and tools used in their original discovery efforts. With the success of ongoing missions like Gaia and TESS, we now have a wealth of observations for most hot Jupiters, providing a key opportunity. What is needed is a complete reanalysis of our current sample of hot Jupiters to establish a homogeneous catalog of system parameters that is key to disentangling the evolutionary mechanism of giant planets. By analyzing all available observations of hot Jupiters in a self-consistent manner along with key observations available from the same observatory (TESS), the problem of the lack of homogeneity in the data is mitigated. To prioritize our analysis, the exoplanets were sorted and ranked according to each planet's transmission spectroscopy metric (TSM), quantifying the expected signal-to-noise and thus the ease by which transmission spectra can be obtained for each exoplanet. Planning for JWST will additionally be supported by the increased accuracy and precision on future transit times. Using KELT-14 b as an example, the uncertainty in the transit time from the initial discovery data swells to ±8.2 minutes when projected out to 2025. The equivalent uncertainty from our most recent analysis is ±0.6 minutes. However, for many of the known hot Jupiters, these uncertainties in future transit times are on the order of multiple hours in 2025, making them inaccessible for scheduling precious resources like JWST. I will present our current status in reanalyzing the &gt;450 known hot Jupiters, and discuss the importance of this effort in the context of KELT-14 b.

[1] 热木星的统一再分析

热木星的形成和演化仍然是系外行星研究中的一个未解之谜，但我们对它们的许多认识都受到最初发现时所采用的假设和工具的影响。随着Gaia和TESS等正在进行的任务取得成功，我们现在对大多数热木星拥有大量观测数据，这为研究提供了关键机遇。当前亟需对现有热木星样本进行全面的重新分析，以建立一个同质的系统参数目录，这对于揭示巨行星演化机制至关重要。通过以自洽的方式分析所有可用的热木星观测数据，并结合来自同一台天文台（TESS）的关键观测，可以缓解数据同质性不足的问题。为了优先排序分析，我们根据每颗行星的透射光谱指标（TSM）对系外行星进行排序和排名，量化其预期信噪比，从而评估每颗系外行星获取透射光谱的难易程度。对JWST的观测规划还将受益于未来凌日时间精度和准确度的提升。以KELT-14 b为例，若仅用最初发现数据推算至2025年，凌日时间的不确定性将膨胀至±8.2分钟，而我们最新分析得出的等效不确定性为±0.6分钟。然而，对于许多已知热木星，2025年未来凌日时间的不确定性仍在数小时量级，这使得它们难以用于安排如JWST等宝贵资源的观测。我将介绍我们对已知>450颗热木星重新分析的最新进展，并结合KELT-14 b讨论这一工作的意义。

[2] Self-Consistent Constraints for a Population of Hot Jupiters and Implications for Planet Formation and Climate

2022-06-00

Transit and eclipse spectroscopy offer insights into the chemical and radiative properties of extrasolar planet atmospheres, but inferring these properties from their spectra requires data-model comparisons. Bayesian retrieval methods are commonly used to derive atmosphere characteristics from models. Free retrievals make minimal assumptions allowing for flexible data-model fits and potentially illuminating unknown physics, but this also creates the possibility of inferring unphysical atmosphere characteristics. In this work, we combine powerful Bayesian parameter estimation tools with novel grids of self-consistent, one-dimensional, radiative-convective equilibrium models. These models are more restrictive than free retrievals, giving them more predictive power, while maintaining the computational speed that increasingly complex models lack. Using this framework, we determine metallicities, carbon-to-oxygen ratios, and heat redistributions for a population of hot Jupiters from their dayside thermal emission spectra. Our sample is derived from a uniform data-reduction analysis of Hubble Space Telescope WFC3 and Spitzer 3.6/4.5 micron observations sourced from a variety of programs. Results are presented in the context of current trend predictions in literature — metallicity and C/O closely relate to planetary formation theories, and heat redistribution has implications for climate. Finally, we present this grid modeling method as a tool for broad trend predictions that inform observations with JWST and ground-based high-resolution instruments.

[2] 热木星种群的自洽约束及其对行星形成和气候的影响

凌星和掩食光谱学为系外行星大气的化学和辐射特性提供了洞见，但要从光谱中推断这些特性需要数据与模型的对比。贝叶斯检索方法通常用于从模型中推导大气特性。自由检索假设最少，允许灵活的数据-模型拟合，并有可能揭示未知物理过程，但这也可能导致推断出不符合物理的大气特性。在本工作中，我们将强大的贝叶斯参数估计工具与新型自洽的一维辐射-对流平衡模型网格相结合。这些模型比自由检索更具约束性，因此具有更强的预测能力，同时保持了复杂模型所缺乏的计算速度。利用这一框架，我们根据热木星白昼面热发射光谱，确定了其金属丰度、碳氧比和热量再分布。我们的样本来自对哈勃空间望远镜WFC3和Spitzer 3.6/4.5微米观测数据的统一数据处理分析，数据来源于多个项目。结果在当前文献趋势预测的背景下进行展示——金属丰度和C/O与行星形成理论密切相关，热量再分布对气候有影响。最后，我们将这种网格建模方法作为一种工具，用于进行广泛的趋势预测，为JWST和地基高分辨率仪器的观测提供参考。

[3] Self-Consistent Constraints on Elemental Abundances and Heat Redistributions for a Population of Hot Jupiters

2021-01-00

Transit and eclipse spectroscopy offer insights into the chemical and radiative properties of extrasolar planets. Inferring said properties from their spectra necessarily requires data-model comparisons. Typically, Bayesian atmospheric retrieval methods have been used to derive these properties. Retrievals generally make minimal assumptions allowing for more flexibility in the fits and potentially illuminating unknown physics, but this also creates the possibility of unphysical atmospheric determinations. In this work, we combine powerful Bayesian parameter estimation tools with a new grid of self-consistent, one dimensional radiative-convective equilibrium models. Using this framework, we determine metallicities, carbon-to-oxygen ratios, and heat redistributions for a population of hot Jupiters from their dayside thermal emission spectra. Our sample is derived from a uniform data-reduction analysis of spatial scan Hubble Space Telescope WFC3 and Spitzer 3.6/4.5 micron observations sourced from a variety of programs. The self-consistent approach mitigates the possibility of unphysical solutions, resulting in robust atmospheric inferences. We will present our self-consistent constraints on these fundamental atmospheric quantities in the context of current retrieval results and compare to atmospheric composition predictions from planet formation theories. Our nominal model fits to each object will be extrapolated to the James Webb Space Telescope wavelengths, as well as high-resolution, cross-correlation instruments, in order to identify observational setups that best address any current degeneracies.

[3] 热木星种群的元素丰度与热量再分布的自洽约束

凌星和掩食光谱学为系外行星的化学和辐射特性提供了洞见。从光谱推断这些特性必然需要数据与模型的比较。通常，贝叶斯大气检索方法被用于推导这些属性。检索通常只做最小假设，从而在拟合中更具灵活性，并有可能揭示未知物理过程，但这也带来了得到非物理大气解的可能性。在本工作中，我们将强大的贝叶斯参数估计工具与一套新的自洽一维辐射-对流平衡模型网格结合。利用这一框架，我们根据热木星的日面热发射光谱，确定了其金属丰度、C/O比和热量再分布。我们的样本来自对Hubble Space Telescope WFC3和Spitzer 3.6/4.5微米观测数据的统一数据处理分析，这些数据源自多个项目。自洽方法减小了非物理解的可能性，从而获得稳健的大气推断。我们将在当前检索结果的背景下，展示对这些基本大气参数的自洽约束，并与行星形成理论对大气组成的预测进行比较。我们对每个目标的标称模型拟合将外推到James Webb Space Telescope波段，以及高分辨率交叉相关仪器，以确定最能解决当前简并性的观测方案。

[4] Toward a multidimensional analysis of transmission spectroscopy. II. Day-night-induced biases in retrievals from hot to ultrahot Jupiters

2022-02-00

Hot Jupiters (HJs) are very good targets for transmission spectroscopy analysis. Their atmospheres have a large scale height, implying a high signal-to-noise ratio. As these planets orbit close to their stars, they often present strong thermal and chemical heterogeneities between the day- and nightside of their atmosphere. For the hottest of these planets, the thermal dissociation of several species occurs in their atmospheres, which leads to a stronger chemical dichotomy between the two hemispheres. It has already been shown that the current retrieval algorithms, which are using 1D forward models, find biased molecular abundances in ultrahot Jupiters. Here, we quantify the effective temperature domain over which these biases are present. We used a set of 12 simulations of typical HJs from T<SUB>eq</SUB> = 1000 K to T<SUB>eq</SUB> = 2100 K performed with the substellar and planetary atmospheric radiation and circulation global climate model and generate transmission spectra that fully account for the 3D structure of the atmosphere with Pytmosph3R. These spectra were then analyzed using the 1D TauREx retrieval code. We find that for James Webb Space Telescope like data, accounting for nonisothermal vertical temperature profiles is required over the whole temperature range. We further find that 1D retrieval codes start to estimate incorrect parameter values for planets with equilibrium temperatures greater than 1400 K if there are absorbers in the visible (such as TiO and VO, e.g.) that are able to create a hot stratosphere. The high temperatures at low pressures indeed entail a thermal dissociation of species that creates a strong chemical day-night dichotomy. As a byproduct, we demonstrate that when synthetic observations are used to assess the detectability of a given feature or process using a Bayesian framework (e.g., by comparing the Bayesian evidence of retrievals with different model assumptions), it is valid to use nonrandomized input data as long as the anticipated observational uncertainties are correctly taken into account.

[4] 迈向透射光谱的多维分析。II. 日夜差异对热至超热木星检索的偏差影响

热木星（HJs）是透射光谱分析的极佳目标。它们的大气具有较大的尺度高度，意味着高信噪比。由于这些行星靠近母星运行，其大气昼夜两侧常常表现出强烈的热力和化学异质性。对于最热的这类行星，大气中会发生多种物种的热解离，导致两半球之间更强的化学二分性。已有研究表明，当前采用1D前向模型的检索算法会在超热木星中得到有偏的分子丰度。本文我们量化了这些偏差存在的有效温度范围。我们利用全球气候模式对典型热木星进行了12组模拟，T_eq从1000 K到2100 K，并用Pytmosph3R生成了充分考虑大气三维结构的透射光谱。随后用1D TauREx检索代码分析这些光谱。我们发现，对于James Webb Space Telescope级别的数据，必须在整个温度范围内考虑非等温的垂直温度剖面。进一步发现，对于平衡温度大于1400 K且可见光存在吸收体（如TiO和VO等）能够形成热平流层的行星，1D检索代码开始估算出不正确的参数值。低压下的高温确实导致物种的热解离，进而产生强烈的昼夜化学二分性。作为副产品，我们证明，当用合成观测数据在贝叶斯框架下评估某一特征或过程的可探测性（例如，通过比较不同模型假设下检索的贝叶斯证据）时，只要正确考虑预期的观测不确定性，使用非随机化输入数据是有效的。

[5] An Exploration of Retrieval Choices for JWST Transit Spectra of Hot Jupiters: Varying Sampling Algorithms, Wavelength Coverage, Spectral Resolution, and Multi-Dimensionality of Models.

2024-02-00

Among the various methods for characterizing exoplanet atmospheres, transit spectroscopy has proven to be the most prolific. Hot Jupiters, while relatively rare, have emerged as favorable targets for spectral analysis. Their hot temperatures, close proximity to their host stars, and low-density atmospheres enable unparalleled insight into their structure and composition. Previous studies that investigated the atmospheres of Hot Jupiters using transmission spectroscopy were hampered by restricted wavelength coverage. Superior quality data demonstrated by JWST ERS and cycle 1 is pushing the community to upgrade their analysis methods to extract as much detail as possible. In our project, we assess to what degree the expanded wavelength coverage offered by JWST necessitates further complexity beyond the first generation of one-dimensional retrieval frameworks. We conducted several studies investigating trade-offs between wavelength coverage and signal-to-noise ratios. As part of this project, we have made significant improvements to the existing transit modeling and retrieval code, METIS, making it more suitable for application to real transit observations. Notably, we have incorporated Hamiltonian Monte Carlo techniques for parameter retrievals in our transit spectral analysis, a departure from previous Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) and nested sampling methods. We anticipate that it will be useful for multi-dimensional models and joint analysis of multiple datasets, as HMC has demonstrated to converge more robustly than MCMC for models with large numbers of parameters in other applications.

[5] JWST热木星凌日光谱检索选择探索：采样算法、波长覆盖、光谱分辨率和模型多维度的变化

在多种系外行星大气表征方法中，透射光谱已被证明是最为高产的。热木星虽然相对罕见，但因其高温、靠近母星及低密度大气，成为光谱分析的优选目标。这些特性使我们能够前所未有地深入了解其结构和成分。以往利用透射光谱研究热木星大气的工作受限于波长覆盖范围。JWST ERS和第一周期展示的高质量数据推动了社区升级分析方法，以尽可能提取更多细节。在本项目中，我们评估了JWST扩展波长覆盖是否需要超越第一代一维检索框架的复杂性。我们开展了多项研究，探讨波长覆盖与信噪比之间的权衡。作为项目的一部分，我们对现有的凌星建模与检索代码METIS进行了重大改进，使其更适用于实际凌星观测。值得注意的是，我们在凌星光谱分析中引入了Hamiltonian Monte Carlo技术进行参数检索，这与以往的Markov Chain Monte Carlo（MCMC）和嵌套采样方法不同。我们预计该方法对于多维模型和多数据集联合分析将非常有用，因为HMC在参数数量较多的模型中已被证明比MCMC收敛更稳健。

[6] A re-analysis of equilibrium chemistry in five hot Jupiters

2023-09-00

<BR /> Aims: Studies of chemistry and chemical composition are fundamental to exploring the formation histories of planets and planetary systems. We propose having another look at five targets to better determine their composition and the chemical mechanisms taking place in their atmospheres. We present a re-analysis of five hot Jupiters, combining multiple instruments and using Bayesian retrieval methods. We compare different combinations of molecules present in the simulated atmosphere and various chemistry types, as well as a range of cloud parametrizations. Following up on recent studies questioning the detection of Na and K in the atmosphere of HD 209458b as being potentially contaminated by stellar lines (when present), we study the impact on other retrieval parameters that may lead to misinterpretations of the presence of these alkali species. <BR /> Methods: We used spatially scanned observations from the grisms G102 and G141 of the Wide Field Camera 3 (WFC3) on the Hubble Space Telescope, with a wavelength coverage of ~0.8 to ~1.7 microns. We analyzed these data with the publicly available Iraclis pipeline. We added data from Space Telescope Imaging Spectrograph (STIS) observations to increase our wavelength coverage from ~0.4 to ~1.7 µm. We then performed a Bayesian retrieval analysis with the open-source TauREx using a nested sampling algorithm. We carried out the retrieval, taking into account molecular abundances that vary freely and then with equilibrium chemistry. We explored the influence of including Na and K on the retrieval of the molecules from the atmosphere. <BR /> Results: Our data re-analysis and Bayesian retrieval are consistent with previous studies, but we do find small differences in the retrieved parameters. After all, Na and K have no significant impact on the properties of the planet atmospheres. Therefore, we present here our new best-fit models, taking into account molecular abundances that are allowed to vary freely as well as the equilibrium chemistry. This work is a preparation for a future addition of a more sophisticated representation of the chemistry involved, while taking into account disequilibrium effects such as vertical mixing and photochemistry.

[6] 对五颗热木星的平衡化学进行再分析

目标：化学及化学组成的研究对于探索行星及行星系统的形成历史至关重要。我们对五个目标进行了重新分析，以更好地确定它们的大气组成及其大气中发生的化学机制。我们结合多种仪器并采用贝叶斯检索方法，对五颗热木星进行了重新分析。我们比较了模拟大气中不同分子组合、不同化学类型以及多种云参数化方案。针对近期有研究质疑在HD 209458 b大气中探测到的Na和K可能受到恒星谱线（如存在时）污染的问题，我们研究了这种情况对其他检索参数的影响，这可能导致对这些碱金属存在性的误判。 方法：我们使用了哈勃空间望远镜宽视场相机3（WFC3）G102和G141棱镜的空间扫描观测，波长覆盖约0.8至1.7微米。我们利用公开的Iraclis流程分析了这些数据。我们还加入了空间望远镜成像光谱仪（STIS）的观测数据，将波长覆盖范围扩展到约0.4至1.7微米。随后，我们使用开源TauREx和嵌套采样算法进行了贝叶斯检索分析。我们分别在分子丰度自由变化和化学平衡条件下进行了检索。我们还探讨了在大气检索中包含Na和K对其他分子的影响。 结果：我们的数据重新分析和贝叶斯检索结果与先前研究一致，但在部分检索参数上发现了小的差异。总体而言，Na和K对行星大气性质没有显著影响。因此，我们在此给出新的最佳拟合模型，考虑了分子丰度自由变化和化学平衡两种情况。本工作为未来引入更复杂化学过程的表示做准备，同时考虑非平衡效应，如垂直混合和光化学。

[7] On Atmospheric Retrievals of Exoplanets with Inhomogeneous Terminators

2022-07-00

The complexity of atmospheric retrieval models is largely data-driven, and one-dimensional models have generally been considered adequate with current data quality. However, recent studies have suggested that using 1D models in retrievals can result in anomalously cool terminator temperatures and biased abundance estimates even with existing transmission spectra of hot Jupiters. Motivated by these claims and upcoming high-quality transmission spectra, we systematically explore the limitations of 1D models using synthetic and current observations. We use 1D models of varying complexity, both analytic and numerical, to revisit claims of biases when interpreting transmission spectra of hot Jupiters with inhomogeneous terminator compositions. Overall, we find the reported biases to be resulting from specific model assumptions rather than intrinsic limitations of 1D atmospheric models in retrieving current observations of asymmetric terminators. Additionally, we revise atmospheric retrievals of the hot Jupiter WASP-43b (T <SUB>eq</SUB> = 1440 K) and the ultra-hot Jupiter WASP-103b (T <SUB>eq</SUB> = 2484 K), for which previous studies inferred abnormally cool atmospheric temperatures. We retrieve temperatures consistent with expectations. We note, however, that in the limit of extreme terminator inhomogeneities and high data quality, some atmospheric inferences may conceivably be biased-although to a lesser extent than previously claimed. To address such cases, we implement a 2D retrieval framework for transmission spectra that allows accurate constraints on average atmospheric properties and provides insights into the spectral ranges where the imprints of atmospheric inhomogeneities are strongest. Our study highlights the need for careful considerations of model assumptions and data quality before attributing biases in retrieved estimates to unaccounted atmospheric inhomogeneities.

[7] 关于晨昏线不均匀的系外行星大气反演

大气检索模型的复杂性在很大程度上受数据驱动，目前数据质量下，一维模型通常被认为足够。然而，近期研究表明，即使在现有热木星透射光谱下，检索中使用一维模型也可能导致异常偏低的晨昏线温度和有偏的丰度估计。受这些观点及即将到来的高质量透射光谱的启发，我们系统性地利用合成和现有观测数据，探讨一维模型的局限性。我们采用不同复杂度的一维解析和数值模型，重新评估在解释具有非均匀晨昏线组成的热木星透射光谱时出现偏差的说法。总体而言，我们发现这些报道的偏差主要源于特定模型假设，而非一维大气模型在检索当前非对称晨昏线观测时的固有限制。此外，我们重新检索了热木星WASP-43b（Teq = 1440 K）和超热木星WASP-103b（Teq = 2484 K）的大气参数，先前研究推断其大气温度异常偏低，而我们检索得到的温度与预期一致。但我们注意到，在极端晨昏线非均匀性和高数据质量的极限下，某些大气推断结果可能会出现偏差，尽管程度低于先前报道。为应对这类情况，我们实现了一个适用于透射光谱的二维检索框架，可对平均大气性质进行精确约束，并揭示大气非均匀性在不同波段的影响。本研究强调，在将检索偏差归因于未考虑的大气非均匀性之前，需仔细考虑模型假设和数据质量。

[8] Evolution of the biases in retrieved atmospheric composition of hot Jupiters

2021-09-00

Transmission spectroscopy provides us with information on the atmospheric properties at the limb, which is often intuitively assumed to be a narrow annulus around the planet. This is why all existing retrieval algorithms used so far to constrain the atmospheric composition from data rely either on i) a single 1D forward model, thus assuming a uniform limb or ii) a linear combination of 1D models to account for heterogeneities between different regions of the limb (e.g. east vs. west). Even full three-dimensional atmospheric models (GCMs) commonly use only the atmosphericcolumns at the terminator to predict the observable transmission spectrum for a given simulation.Here, we will present numerical experiment from hot Jupiters to ultra hot Jupiters which demonstrate that the region probed in transmission actually extends significantly toward the day and night sides of the planet and that, as a result, the real transmission spectrum computed from a GCM simulation with our fully 3D radiative transfer differs significantly from results obtained with the usual assumptions, especially for the hottest atmospheres. This comes from the fact that the terminator of hot, synchronously rotating planets is a region exhibiting sharp thermal and compositional gradients.Fig 1: Sum up of the different geometry required in retrievals code to avoid biases according to the equilibrium temperature ofthe planet and the presence or the absence of optical absorber. 1D retrieval model appears to be correct for planets with equilibrium temperature lower than 1400 K when optical absorber (such as TiO, VO, Na, K, etc) are present in the atmosphere. They are howeverbiased above this limit where 2D retrieval code are mandatory.Using realistic hot exo-atmospheres from GCM model SPARC/MIT, we will demonstrate how this effect can lead to strong biases is the temperature and abundances retrieved from actual data biases that will need to be addressed and corrected for if we want to be able to make robust inferences from future JWST and ARIEL data. We will determine a global limit where the biases due the 1D hypothesis of retrieval model start to be negligible. We have also established a hierarchy of the different 3D effects (vertical, horizontal along and/or across the limb) in order to be able to chosse the right tool to break down the biases (see Fig. 1). We will finally present our new tool, a 2D version of TauREx, which is designed to unravel these biases.

[8] 热木星大气成分检索偏差的演化

透射光谱为我们提供了晨昏线大气性质的信息，通常直观地认为这是围绕行星的一个狭窄环带。因此，迄今为止所有用于约束大气组成的检索算法都依赖于：i）单一的1D前向模型，即假设晨昏线均匀，或ii）1D模型的线性组合，以考虑晨昏线不同区域（如东西方向）之间的非均匀性。即使是完整的三维大气模型（GCM），通常也只使用晨昏线处的大气柱来预测给定模拟的可观测透射光谱。在本研究中，我们将展示从热木星到超热木星的数值实验，证明实际透射探测区域实际上显著延伸到行星的白昼面和夜晚面，因此，利用我们完全三维辐射传输的GCM模拟计算的真实透射光谱与通常假设下得到的结果存在显著差异，尤其是在最热的大气中。这是因为热的同步自转行星的晨昏线区域存在剧烈的温度和组成梯度。图1：总结了根据行星平衡温度及大气中是否存在光学吸收体，检索代码所需的不同几何结构。对于平衡温度低于1400 K且大气中存在光学吸收体（如TiO、VO、Na、K等）的行星，1D检索模型是合理的。然而，在此温度以上，1D模型会产生偏差，必须采用2D检索代码。利用GCM模型SPARC/MIT生成的真实热系外大气，我们将展示这种效应如何导致从实际数据检索的温度和丰度出现严重偏差，这些偏差需要被识别和修正，以便我们能够从未来JWST和ARIEL数据中获得可靠推断。我们将确定一个全局界限，说明1D检索模型假设导致的偏差何时可以忽略不计。我们还建立了不同3D效应（垂直、沿晨昏线和/或横跨晨昏线的水平效应）的层级，以便选择合适的工具来消除这些偏差（见图1）。最后，我们将介绍我们的新工具——TauREx的2D版本，专为揭示这些偏差而设计。

高被引研究“地基探测土星质量系外行星 WASP-69b 扩展氦大气”中的核心结论，在独立数据集和替代分析假设下是否成立？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Non-detection of Helium in the Extended Atmosphere of the Hot Jupiter WASP-48b

2023-01-00

Hot Jupiters orbiting extremely close to their host star may experience atmospheric escape due to the large amounts of high-energy radiation they receive. Understanding the conditions under which this occurs is critical, as atmospheric escape is believed to be a driving factor in sculpting planetary populations. In recent years, the near-infrared 10830 Å helium feature has been found to be a promising spectral signature of atmospheric escape, because it can be observed using ground-based telescopes, does not suffer from interstellar absorption (which is a challenge for Lyα observations), and is not as impacted by stellar activity as Hα. <P />We use transmission spectroscopy to search for excess helium absorption in the extended atmosphere of WASP-48b, a hot Jupiter orbiting a slightly evolved, rapidly-rotating F star. The data were collected using the Habitable Zone Planet Finder spectrograph on the Hobby-Eberly Telescope. Observations were taken over the course of seven nights, from which we obtain three transits. No detectable helium absorption is seen, as absorption depth is limited to -0.0030±0.0050 at 0.6 σ. This non-detection follows our current understanding of decreasing stellar activity (and thus high-energy radiation) with age. We use a 1D isothermal Parker wind model to compare with our observations and find there is still some degeneracy in the range of planetary thermosphere temperatures and mass-loss rates that could explain our findings. <P />We explore our results within the context of the full sample of helium detections and non-detections to date, which should assist in optimizing future observational parameters for helium searches. Surprisingly, comparing helium absorption with the stellar activity index log R'<SUB>HK</SUB> reveals a large spread in the correlation between these two factors, suggesting that there are additional parameters influencing the helium absorption strength.

[1] 未探测到热木星WASP-48b扩展大气中的氦

极靠近母星运行的热木星可能由于受到大量高能辐射而经历大气逃逸。理解发生大气逃逸的条件至关重要，因为大气逃逸被认为是塑造行星种群的关键因素。近年来，近红外10830 Å氦特征被发现是大气逃逸的有前景的光谱标志，因为它可以用地面望远镜观测，不受星际吸收影响（这对Lyα观测是个挑战），且不像Hα那样易受恒星活动影响。<P />我们利用透射光谱法，搜索围绕略微演化、快速自转F型恒星运行的热木星WASP-48b扩展大气中的氦过量吸收。数据由Hobby-Eberly Telescope上的Habitable Zone Planet Finder光谱仪采集，观测跨越七个夜晚，获得了三次凌日。未探测到可见的氦吸收，吸收深度上限为-0.0030±0.0050（0.6σ）。这一未探测结果符合我们对恒星活动随年龄减弱（从而高能辐射减少）的当前理解。我们使用一维等温Parker风模型与观测结果进行比较，发现可解释我们结果的行星热层温度和质量损失率范围仍存在一定简并性。<P />我们在当前所有氦探测和未探测样本的背景下探讨了我们的结果，这有助于优化未来氦搜索的观测参数。令人惊讶的是，将氦吸收与恒星活动指数log R'_HK进行比较，发现两者相关性存在较大分散，表明还有其他参数影响氦吸收强度。

[2] A Combined Ground-based and JWST Atmospheric Retrieval Analysis: Both IGRINS and NIRSpec Agree that the Atmosphere of WASP-77A b Is Metal-poor

2024-03-00

Ground-based high-resolution and space-based low-resolution spectroscopy are the two main avenues through which transiting exoplanet atmospheres are studied. Both methods provide unique strengths and shortcomings, and combining the two can be a powerful probe into an exoplanet's atmosphere. Within a joint atmospheric retrieval framework, we combined JWST NIRSpec/G395H secondary eclipse spectra and Gemini South/IGRINS pre- and post-eclipse thermal emission observations of the hot Jupiter WASP-77A b. Our inferences from the IGRINS and NIRSpec data sets are consistent with each other, and combining the two allows us to measure the gas abundances of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO, as well as the vertical thermal structure, with higher precision than either data set provided individually. We confirm WASP-77A b's subsolar metallicity ([(C+O)/H] = −0.61 ${}_{-0.09}^{+0.10})$ and solar C/O ratio (C/O = 0.57 ${}_{-0.06}^{+0.06})$ . The two types of data are complementary, and our abundance inferences are mostly driven by the IGRINS data, while inference of the thermal structure is driven by the NIRSpec data. Our ability to draw inferences from the post-eclipse IGRINS data is highly sensitive to the number of singular values removed in the detrending process, potentially due to high and variable humidity. We also search for signatures for atmospheric dynamics in the IGRINS data and find that propagated ephemeris error can manifest as either an orbital eccentricity or a strong equatorial jet. Neither are detected when using more up-to-date ephemerides. However, we find moderate evidence of thermal inhomogeneity and measure a cooler nightside that presents itself in the later phases after secondary eclipse.

[2] 地面与JWST联合大气检索分析：IGRINS和NIRSpec均认为WASP-77A b大气金属贫乏

地基高分辨率和空间低分辨率光谱是研究凌日系外行星大气的两种主要途径。两种方法各有独特优势和局限，结合使用可成为探测系外行星大气的有力手段。在联合大气检索框架下，我们结合了 JWST NIRSpec/G395H 次生凌星发射光谱和 Gemini South/IGRINS 次生凌星前后热发射观测数据，对热木星 WASP-77A b 进行了分析。IGRINS 和 NIRSpec 数据集的推断结果相互一致，联合分析使我们能比单独数据集更高精度地测量 H2O 和 CO 的气体丰度及垂直温度结构。我们确认 WASP-77A b 的亚太阳金属丰度（[(C+O)/H] = −0.61 ${}_{-0.09}^{+0.10}$）和太阳 C/O 比（C/O = 0.57 ${}_{-0.06}^{+0.06}$）。两类数据互为补充，丰度推断主要由 IGRINS 数据驱动，温度结构推断则由 NIRSpec 数据驱动。我们从次生凌星后 IGRINS 数据中推断的能力对去趋势过程中去除奇异值的数量高度敏感，可能与高且变化的湿度有关。我们还在 IGRINS 数据中搜索大气动力学特征，发现历元误差传播可表现为轨道偏心率或强赤道喷流，但使用最新历元时均未探测到。然而，我们发现中等证据表明存在热不均匀性，并测得次生凌星后期呈现较冷的夜面。

[3] HST PanCET program: non-detection of atmospheric escape in the warm Saturn-sized planet WASP-29 b

2021-05-00

Short-period gas giant exoplanets are susceptible to intense atmospheric escape due to their large scale heights and strong high-energy irradiation. This process is thought to occur ubiquitously, but to date we have only detected direct evidence of atmospheric escape in hot Jupiters and warm Neptunes. The latter planets are particularly more sensitive to escape-driven evolution as a result of their lower gravities with respect to Jupiter-sized planets. But the paucity of cases for intermediate, Saturn-sized exoplanets at varying levels of irradiation precludes a detailed understanding of the underlying physics in atmospheric escape of hot gas giants. Aiming to address this issue, our objectives here are to assess the high-energy environment of the warm (T<SUB>eq</SUB> = 970 K) Saturn WASP-29 b and search for signatures of atmospheric escape. We used far-ultraviolet observations from the Hubble Space Telescope to analyze the flux time series of H I, C II, Si III, Si IV, and N V during the transit of WASP-29 b. At 88 pc, a large portion of the Lyman-α core of the K4V-type host WASP-29 is attenuated by interstellar medium absorption, limiting our ability to probe the escape of H at velocities between ‒84 and +35 km s<SUP>‒1</SUP>. At 3σ confidence, we rule out any in-transit absorption of H I larger than 92% in the Lyman-α blue wing and 19% in the red wing. We found an in-transit flux decrease of 39%<SUB>‒11%</SUB><SUP>+12%</SUP> in the ground-state C II emission line at 1334.5 Å. But due to this signal being significantly present in only one visit, it is difficult to attribute a planetary or stellar origin to the ground-state C II signal. We place 3σ absorption upper limits of 40, 49, and 24% on Si III, Si IV, and for excited-state C II at 1335.7 Å, respectively. Low activity levels and the faint X-ray luminosity suggest that WASP-29 is an old, inactive star. Nonetheless, an energy-limited approximation combined with the reconstructed EUV spectrum of the host suggests that the planet is losing its atmosphere at a relatively large rate of 4 × 10<SUP>9</SUP> g s<SUP>‒1</SUP>. The non-detection at Lyman-α could be partly explained by a low fraction of escaping neutral hydrogen, or by the state of fast radiative blow-out we infer from the reconstructed Lyman-α line.

[3] HST PanCET 项目：未探测到温暖土星大小行星 WASP-29 b 的大气逃逸

短周期气态巨行星由于其较大的尺度高度和强烈的高能辐照，易发生剧烈的大气逃逸。该过程被认为普遍存在，但迄今为止，我们仅在热木星和暖海王星中直接探测到大气逃逸的证据。后者由于相较于木星大小行星具有更低的引力，对逃逸驱动的演化更为敏感。然而，对于不同辐照水平的中间体（土星大小）系外行星的案例稀缺，限制了我们对热气态巨行星大气逃逸物理机制的深入理解。为解决这一问题，我们的目标是评估暖（Teq = 970 K）土星WASP-29 b的高能环境，并搜索大气逃逸的迹象。我们利用哈勃空间望远镜的远紫外观测，分析了WASP-29 b凌日过程中H I、C II、Si III、Si IV和N V的通量时间序列。在88 pc处，K4V型母星WASP-29的大部分Lyman-α核心被星际介质吸收削弱，限制了我们在–84到+35 km s^‒1速度范围内探测H逃逸的能力。在3σ置信度下，我们排除了Lyman-α蓝翼大于92%和红翼大于19%的H I凌日吸收。我们在1334.5 Å基态C II发射线上发现了39%<sub>‒11%</sub><sup>+12%</sup>的凌日通量下降，但由于该信号仅在一次观测中显著出现，难以确定其为行星或恒星起源。我们对Si III、Si IV和1335.7 Å激发态C II分别给出了3σ吸收上限，分别为40%、49%和24%。低活动性和微弱的X射线光度表明WASP-29是一颗年老、非活跃恒星。尽管如此，基于能量限制近似和重建的母星EUV光谱，表明该行星以约4 × 10^9 g s^‒1的速率损失大气。Lyman-α未探测到可能部分由逃逸中性氢分数较低，或由我们从重建Lyman-α线推断的快速辐射吹出状态所致。

[4] Nondetection of Helium in the Hot Jupiter WASP-48b

2023-06-00

Hot Jupiters orbiting extremely close to their host star may experience atmospheric escape due to the large amounts of high-energy radiation they receive. Understanding the conditions under which this occurs is critical, as atmospheric escape is believed to be a driving factor in sculpting planetary populations. In recent years, the near-infrared 10833 Å helium feature has been found to be a promising spectral signature of atmospheric escape. We use transmission spectroscopy to search for excess helium absorption in the extended atmosphere of WASP-48b, a hot Jupiter orbiting a slightly evolved, rapidly rotating F star. The data were collected using the Habitable-zone Planet Finder spectrograph on the Hobby-Eberly Telescope. Observations were taken over the course of seven nights, from which we obtain three transits. No detectable helium absorption is seen, as absorption depth is measured to -0.0025 ± 0.0021, or 1.2σ from a null detection. This nondetection follows our current understanding of decreasing stellar activity (and thus high-energy radiation) with age. We use a 1D isothermal Parker wind model to compare with our observations and find that our nondetection can best be explained with a low planetary mass-loss rate and high thermosphere temperature. We explore our results within the context of the full sample of helium detections and nondetections to date. Surprisingly, comparing helium absorption with the stellar activity index $\mathrm{log}\ {R}_{\mathrm{HK}}^{{\prime} }$ reveals a large spread in the correlation between these two factors, suggesting that there are additional parameters influencing the helium absorption strength.

[4] 未探测到热木星WASP-48b中的氦

极近轨道的热木星由于受到大量高能辐射，可能经历大气逃逸。理解发生大气逃逸的条件至关重要，因为大气逃逸被认为是塑造行星种群的关键因素。近年来，近红外10833 Å氦特征被发现是大气逃逸的有前景的光谱标志。我们利用透射光谱法，搜索热木星WASP-48b（环绕一颗略微演化、快速自转的F型恒星）扩展大气中的氦过量吸收。数据由Hobby-Eberly望远镜的Habitable-zone Planet Finder光谱仪采集，观测跨越七个夜晚，获得了三次凌星。未探测到可见的氦吸收，吸收深度测得为-0.0025 ± 0.0021，即距离零点探测1.2σ。这一未探测结果符合我们当前对恒星活动随年龄降低（从而高能辐射减少）的理解。我们利用一维等温Parker风模型与观测结果对比，发现未探测结果最能用低行星质量损失率和高热层温度解释。我们在当前所有氦探测和未探测样本的背景下讨论了结果。令人惊讶的是，将氦吸收与恒星活动指数log R'_HK对比，发现两者相关性分布很大，表明还有其他参数影响氦吸收强度。

[5] Continuous helium absorption from both the leading and trailing tails of WASP-107 b

2026-02-00

The formation and evolution of giant planets remain incompletely understood, with mounting evidence that many close-in giants may have migrated from their birth locations. The detection of helium escaping the atmosphere of exoplanets has provided a powerful new tracer of atmospheric escape and exoplanetary evolution. Here, using high-precision spectroscopic observations from the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) Near Infrared Imager and Slitless Spectrograph (NIRISS) in single-object slitless spectroscopy mode (SOSS) mode, we report the detection of substantial helium absorption during the pre-transit phase of WASP-107 b (17σ), as well as in the transit and post-transit phases. This unique continuous helium absorption begins approximately 1.5 h before the planet's ingress and reveals the presence of an extended thermosphere. The observations show a maximum transit depth of 2.395 ± 0.01% near the helium triplet (36σ; at the NIRISS-SOSS resolution of ~700). Our ellipsoidal model of the planetary thermosphere matches the measured light curve well, suggesting an outflow extending to tens of planetary radii. Furthermore, we confidently detect water absorption (log<SUB>10</SUB>H<SUB>2</SUB>O = -2.5 ± 0.6), superimposed with a short-wavelength slope that we attribute to a prominent signature from unocculted stellar spots (5.2σ), rather than a small-particle haze slope. We place an upper limit on the abundance of K (log<SUB>10</SUB>K &lt; -4.86, or K/H &lt; 75× stellar) at 2σ, which is consistent with the O/H supersolar metallicity estimate. Together with the supersolar water abundance and the evidence for vigorous atmospheric escape, these findings suggest that WASP-107 b has undergone inward migration in its recent past, probably accompanied by strong tidal heating that continues to sustain its inflated atmosphere and mass loss. This investigation underscores the transformative potential of JWST for investigating planetary evolution.

[5] WASP-107 b前后拖尾的连续氦吸收

巨行星的形成与演化仍未被完全理解，越来越多的证据表明许多近轨巨行星可能经历了从其诞生地向内迁移。对系外行星大气中逃逸氦的探测为研究大气逃逸和系外行星演化提供了有力的新手段。在本研究中，我们利用詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）近红外成像仪与无缝光谱仪（NIRISS）单目标无缝光谱（SOSS）模式的高精度光谱观测，报告了在WASP-107 b凌日前阶段（17σ），以及凌日和凌日后阶段，探测到显著的氦吸收。这一独特的连续氦吸收始于行星入射前约1.5小时，揭示了一个扩展的热层。观测显示在氦三重线附近的最大凌日深度为2.395 ± 0.01%（36σ，NIRISS-SOSS分辨率约700）。我们的行星热层椭球模型与测得的光变曲线高度吻合，表明大气外流可延伸至数十个行星半径。此外，我们还可靠地探测到水吸收（log10H2O = -2.5 ± 0.6），并叠加有短波斜率，我们将其归因于未被遮挡的恒星黑子（5.2σ）产生的显著特征，而非小颗粒薄雾斜率。我们对K丰度给出了上限（log10K < -4.86，或K/H < 75×恒星）2σ，与O/H超太阳金属丰度估计一致。结合超太阳水丰度和强烈大气逃逸的证据，这些发现表明WASP-107 b在近期经历了向内迁移，可能伴随强潮汐加热，持续维持其膨胀大气和质量损失。本研究强调了JWST在行星演化研究中的变革性潜力。

[6] Keck/NIRSPEC studies of helium in the atmospheres of two inflated hot gas giants orbiting K dwarfs: WASP-52b and WASP-177b

2022-06-00

The helium triplet at 1083 nm has recently emerged as a powerful new observational probe of exoplanet atmospheric loss. We are using this new technique to conduct a survey of atmospheric escape in inflated hot gas giants using the NIRSPEC instrument on the Keck II telescope. Here we present the detection of helium in the atmosphere of WASP-52b, extending over 66 ± 5 scale heights, and find tentative evidence of helium in the atmosphere of WASP-177b at around 23 scale heights. By modeling the helium absorption profile, we find WASP-52b is losing its mass at a rate of ~1.6 × 10<SUP>11</SUP> g/s. Finally, we place our results into the context of the ~30 exoplanets that have been the focus of helium studies to date.

[6] Keck/NIRSPEC对两颗绕K型矮星运行的膨胀热气态巨行星WASP-52b和WASP-177b大气中氦的研究

1083 nm的氦三重态最近成为系外行星大气逃逸研究的有力新观测探针。我们正在利用这一新技术，结合Keck II望远镜的NIRSPEC仪器，对膨胀型热气态巨行星的大气逃逸进行巡天。在此，我们报告了在WASP-52b大气中探测到氦，其分布范围达到66 ± 5个尺度高度，并在WASP-177b大气中发现了约23个尺度高度的氦的初步证据。通过对氦吸收轮廓的建模，我们发现WASP-52b的质量损失速率约为1.6 × 10^11 g/s。最后，我们将我们的结果放在迄今为止约30颗已成为氦研究重点的系外行星背景下进行讨论。

[7] IGRINS Observations of WASP-127 b: H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO, and Super-solar Atmospheric Metallicity in the Inflated Sub-Saturn

2024-11-00

High-resolution spectroscopy of exoplanet atmospheres provides insights into their composition and dynamics from the resolved line shape and depth of thousands of spectral lines. WASP-127 b is an extremely inflated sub-Saturn (R <SUB>p</SUB> = 1.311 R <SUB>Jup</SUB>, M <SUB>p</SUB> = 0.16 M <SUB>Jup</SUB>) with previously reported detections of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO<SUB>2</SUB>. However, the seeming absence of the primary carbon reservoir expected at WASP-127 b temperatures (T <SUB>eq</SUB> ∼1400 K) from chemical equilibrium, CO, posed a mystery. In this manuscript, we present the analysis of high-resolution observations of WASP-127 b with the Immersion Grating Infrared Spectrometer on Gemini South. We confirm the presence of H<SUB>2</SUB>O (8.67σ) and report the detection of CO (4.34σ). Additionally, we conduct a suite of Bayesian retrieval analyses covering a hierarchy of model complexity and self-consistency. When freely fitting for the molecular gas volume mixing ratios, we obtain super-solar metal enrichment for H<SUB>2</SUB>O abundance of log<SUB>10</SUB> X <inline-formula> <mml:math overflow="scroll"><mml:msub><mml:mrow></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi mathvariant="normal">H</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mi mathvariant="normal">O</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:math> </inline-formula> = −1.23<inline-formula> <mml:math overflow="scroll"><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.49</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.29</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> and a lower limit on the CO abundance of log<SUB>10</SUB> X <SUB>CO</SUB> ≥─2.20 at 2σ confidence. We also report tentative evidence of photochemistry in WASP-127 b based upon the indicative depletion of H<SUB>2</SUB>S. This is also supported by the data preferring models with photochemistry over free-chemistry and thermochemistry. The overall analysis implies a super-solar (∼39× Solar; [M/H] = <inline-formula> <mml:math overflow="scroll"><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>1.59</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.30</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.30</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula>) metallicity for the atmosphere of WASP-127 b and an upper limit on its atmospheric C/O ratio as &lt; 0.68.

[7] IGRINS对WASP-127 b的观测：膨胀亚土星中H2O、CO和超太阳大气金属丰度

高分辨率光谱观测可通过成千上万条分辨的谱线形状和深度揭示系外行星大气的成分和动力学。WASP-127 b 是一颗极度膨胀的亚土星（R_p = 1.311 R_Jup, M_p = 0.16 M_Jup），此前已报道探测到 H2O 和 CO2。然而，按化学平衡预期在 WASP-127 b 温度（T_eq ∼1400 K）下应为主要碳库的 CO 却似乎缺失，这成为一个谜团。本文报告了利用 Gemini South 的 Immersion Grating Infrared Spectrometer 对 WASP-127 b 的高分辨率观测分析。我们确认了 H2O（8.67σ）的存在，并报告了 CO（4.34σ）的探测。此外，我们开展了一系列贝叶斯检索分析，涵盖不同模型复杂度和自洽性。当对分子气体体积混合比自由拟合时，得到 H2O 丰度 log10 X_H2O = −1.23_{-0.49}^{+0.29}，表现为超太阳金属富集，并对 CO 丰度给出 2σ 置信下限 log10 X_CO ≥ −2.20。我们还报告了 WASP-127 b 上 H2S 明显亏损的初步光化学证据，数据也更支持包含光化学的模型而非自由化学或热化学模型。整体分析表明 WASP-127 b 大气为超太阳金属丰度（约 39 倍太阳；[M/H] = 1.59_{-0.30}^{+0.30}），大气 C/O 比的上限为 < 0.68。

[8] Continuous helium absorption from leading and trailing tails of WASP-107 b

2025-09-00

The detection of metastable helium in exoplanet atmospheres has opened a new observational window into hydrodynamic escape processes and planetary evolution. Leveraging the unprecedented sensitivity of the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST), we present time-resolved NIRISS-SOSS spectroscopy of the warm Neptune WASP-107b, revealing a continuous and significant helium absorption feature centered on the 10833 Å triplet.We detect strong helium absorption not only during the transit but also in the pre- and post-transit phases, with a peak significance of 36σ at mid-transit and 17σ during pre-transit. The helium absorption begins approximately 1.5 hours before planetary ingress and extends over 10 planetary radii, far beyond the Roche lobe, indicating the presence of a large, escaping thermosphere. In Figure 1, we present the light curve at the helium triplet, clearly showing the extended nature of the absorption beyond the optical transit.Figure 1: Left: Light curve near the metastable helium feature (pixel corresponding to λ: 1.0830216 - 1.0839538 μm), overlaid with the best-fit solid-body model light curve (black) and the best-fit EvE model (turquoise), along with their corresponding residuals below. The binned data are highlighted for clarity. Pre-transit and post-transit absorption is evident. The out-of-transit excess absorption is particularly apparent in the middle panel, which shows the residuals from the solid-body model fit. The shaded gray region indicates the coverage of past ground-based observations of the target. Right: Transmission spectrum at instrument resolution around the metastable helium lines. Although the lines are not fully resolved (indicated by black dotted lines), the excess absorption is clearly visible.To interpret this signature, we developed an ellipsoidal outflow model of the thermosphere using our Evaporating Exoplanets (EvE) framework, which successfully reproduces the observed helium light curve (Figure 2). This model constrains the geometry of the escaping atmosphere and supports a broad, extended outflow consistent with hydrodynamic escape driven by stellar irradiation and tidal forces.Figure 2: View of the escaping metastable helium in our best-fit model. The red line denotes the planetary orbit, the blue dotted line shows the projection of the Roche lobe, and the black dashed line indicates the boundary of the confined outflow, from which escaping atoms are launched over the 3D surface. The thermospheric profile is generated with a mass-loss of ∼ 1012 g/s, a temperature of 7000 K and a H/He ratio of 0.90. The associated metastable helium mass-loss is ∼ 9 · 105 g/s. Top: Views of the system from above at mid-transit. The right panel is a zoom in over the square black region indicated in the left panel. Bottom: Views of the system along the line-of-sight at mid-transit, and 2 hours before/after.Importantly, at the NIRISS/SOSS resolution (R ~ 700), a degeneracy exists between the line shape and depth of the helium absorption signal, which cannot be fully resolved without high-resolution spectroscopy. Previous studies of metastable helium in this system relied on stellar reference spectra obtained during the pre- or post-transit phases, likely biasing their light curves and spectral interpretations. This underscores the importance of coordinating JWST observations with high-resolution ground-based spectroscopy to capture both the broad phase curve and the resolved line profile, enabling a more complete understanding of helium escape.In addition to helium, we detect water absorption in the transmission spectrum (log₁₀[H₂O] = -2.5 ± 0.6), along with a significant short-wavelength slope attributed to unocculted stellar spots (5.2σ), rather than high-altitude hazes. We also place a 2σ upper limit on potassium abundance (log₁₀[K] &lt; -4.86), consistent with a super-solar O/H metallicity. The inferred mass-loss rate of ~1-10 Earth masses per Gyr and high metallicity suggest that WASP-107b formed beyond the snowline and migrated inward recently. Ongoing tidal dissipation, due to the planet's mildly eccentric orbit, likely contributes to atmospheric inflation and enhanced escape.Figure 3: Results of free retrieval performed on the JWST /NIRISS SOSS transmission spectrum of WASP-107 b with SCARLET, TauREx, petitRADTRANS and Pyrat Bay. Top panel: Sample spectra from the posterior distributions of the SCARLET free retrievals (joint fit of planetary atmosphere and stellar contamination). The full models are shown in blue, and the atmosphere contribution is shown in purple for each sample. The best-fit SCARLET model is shown in black. The fitted NIRISS/SOSS transmission spectrum is overlaid (black points) and shifted by the best-fitting offset from the SCARLET retrieval (156 ppm). Middle panel: Best-fit spectra retrieved with SCARLET (black), TauREx (dashed pink), Pyrat Bay (blue) and petitRADTRANS (orange). Bottom panels: Posterior distributions on the H2O, NH3 and K volume mixing ratios from the SCARLET retrievals. The shaded areas indicate the 1, 2, and 3σ confidence intervals. For K and NH3, where only upper limits are obtained, we show the 1, 2, and 3σ upper limits.This study highlights the transformative potential of JWST in characterizing exoplanetary mass loss and atmospheric dynamics. Our findings provide new insight into the coupling between stellar environments and planetary atmospheres, and establish WASP-107b as a cornerstone system for future atmospheric escape and formation studies.

[8] WASP-107 b前后拖尾的连续氦吸收

在系外行星大气中探测到亚稳态氦为研究流体动力学逃逸过程和行星演化开辟了新的观测窗口。借助詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）前所未有的灵敏度，我们对温暖海王星 WASP-107b 进行了 NIRISS-SOSS 的时序光谱观测，揭示了以 10833 Å 三重态为中心的连续且显著的氦吸收特征。我们不仅在凌星期间探测到强烈的氦吸收，还在凌星前后阶段探测到，凌星中点的峰值显著性为 36σ，凌星前为 17σ。氦吸收大约在行星入射前 1.5 小时开始，延伸超过 10 个行星半径，远超 Roche 潮汐半径，表明存在一个大尺度逃逸的热层。在图 1 中，我们展示了氦三重态的光变曲线，清楚显示了吸收范围超出光学凌星的延展特性。 图 1：左图：亚稳态氦特征附近的光变曲线（像素对应 λ: 1.0830216 - 1.0839538 μm），叠加了最佳拟合的刚体模型光变曲线（黑色）和最佳拟合的 EvE 模型（蓝绿色），下方为相应残差。为便于观察，突出显示了分箱数据。凌星前后均可见吸收。中间面板显示了刚体模型拟合残差，凌星外的过量吸收尤为明显。灰色阴影区域表示以往地基观测的覆盖范围。右图：仪器分辨率下亚稳态氦线附近的透射光谱。尽管谱线未完全分辨（黑色虚线所示），但过量吸收清晰可见。 为解释该特征，我们基于 Evaporating Exoplanets (EvE) 框架开发了热层椭球形外流模型，成功再现了观测到的氦光变曲线（图 2）。该模型约束了逃逸大气的几何结构，支持由恒星辐射和潮汐力驱动的广泛、扩展的流体动力学逃逸。 图 2：最佳拟合模型中逃逸亚稳态氦的视图。红线表示行星轨道，蓝色虚线为 Roche 潮汐半径投影，黑色虚线为受限外流边界，逃逸原子从三维表面发射。热层剖面对应质量损失率约 10¹² g/s，温度 7000 K，H/He 比为 0.90。相关亚稳态氦的质量损失率约 9 × 10⁵ g/s。上方：凌星中点系统俯视图，右侧为左图黑色方框区域的放大。下方：凌星中点及前/后 2 小时沿视线方向的系统视图。 值得注意的是，在 NIRISS/SOSS 分辨率（R ~ 700）下，氦吸收信号的谱线形状和深度存在简并性，无法通过低分辨率光谱完全分辨。以往对该系统亚稳态氦的研究依赖于凌星前后获得的恒星参考光谱，可能导致光变曲线和光谱解释存在偏差。这凸显了将 JWST 观测与高分辨率地基光谱协调的重要性，以同时捕获宽广的相位曲线和分辨的谱线轮廓，从而更全面理解氦逃逸。 除氦外，我们还在透射光谱中探测到水吸收（log₁₀[H₂O] = -2.5 ± 0.6），以及归因于未被遮挡的恒星黑子（5.2σ）的显著短波斜率，而非高空薄雾。我们还对钾丰度给出了 2σ 上限（log₁₀[K] < -4.86），与超太阳 O/H 金属丰度一致。推断的质量损失率约为每十亿年 1-10 个地球质量，且高金属丰度表明 WASP-107b 形成于雪线之外，并在近期向内迁移。由于行星轨道轻微偏心，持续的潮汐耗散可能促使大气膨胀和增强逃逸。 图 3：对 WASP-107b JWST/NIRISS SOSS 透射光谱使用 SCARLET、TauREx、petitRADTRANS 和 Pyrat Bay 进行自由检索的结果。上面板：SCARLET 自由检索后验分布的样本光谱（行星大气与恒星污染联合拟合），蓝色为完整模型，紫色为每个样本的大气贡献，黑色为最佳拟合 SCARLET 模型。叠加拟合的 NIRISS/SOSS 透射光谱（黑点），并按 SCARLET 检索的最佳偏移（156 ppm）调整。中间面板：SCARLET（黑色）、TauREx（粉色虚线）、Pyrat Bay（蓝色）和 petitRADTRANS（橙色）检索的最佳拟合光谱。下方面板：SCARLET 检索的 H2O、NH3 和 K 体积混合比的后验分布。阴影区域表示 1、2、3σ 置信区间。对于仅获得上限的 K 和 NH3，显示 1、2、3σ 上限。 本研究强调了 JWST 在刻画系外行星质量损失和大气动力学方面的变革性潜力。我们的发现为恒星环境与行星大气之间的耦合提供了新见解，并确立了 WASP-107b 作为未来大气逃逸和形成研究的基石系统。

item_014 · sys_A
Analyses [2015hst..prop14090B] and [2020AAS...23612003K] of HD 209458B's atmosphere reach potentially conflicting conclusions regarding CO: one reports "With up-to-date upper-atmosphere models combined with magnetospheric simulations, we find that for the STIS results of line-integrated ab...", while the other reports "The discrepancy in circulation patterns between these three otherwise similar planets points to the importance of secondary parameters li...". Under what observational or methodological conditions can these results be reconciled, and which currently untested assumption drives the disagreement?
对HD 209458B大气的分析[2015hst..prop14090B]和[2020AAS...23612003K]在CO方面得出了可能相互矛盾的结论：一项报告称“结合最新的高层大气模型和磁层模拟，我们发现对于STIS的积分线结果……”，而另一项报告称“这三颗本质上相似行星之间环流模式的差异表明，次要参数如……的重要性”。在什么观测或方法学条件下这些结果可以调和，目前尚未检验的哪个假设驱动了这种分歧？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Can We Reconcile Space- and Ground-Based Atmospheric Interpretations of Two Classic Hot Jupiters? A comprehensive reanalysis of HD209458b and HD189733b using IGRINS2 spectrograph

2026-02-00

Hot Jupiters present a major challenge to planet formation theories, and their atmospheric compositions are keys to understanding their origins. Despite extensive observations from both space and ground based facilities, the molecular makeup of keystone hot Jupiters - HD 209458 b and HD 189733 b has long been a topic of intense debate. More importantly, these two planets occupy the moderate hot Jupiter regime (1000 - 1500 K), cooler than the ultra-hot (T &gt; 2000 K) planets where molecules dissociate but hotter than warm Neptunes where condensation dominates, placing them in the critical temperature range where species such as H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, HCN, and NH<SUB>3</SUB> can coexist—ideal for probing chemical transitions between oxygen-rich and carbon-rich atmospheres. I will present our campaign to revisit these classic exoplanets using the simultaneous high-resolution H- and K-band coverage of IGRINS2. Our observations target the dayside thermal emission mitigating the biases plaguing transit geometry such as limb asymmetry, Rossiter-McLaughlin effects and clouds. I will present the preliminary result of a strong H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO signals in HD209458b and an ongoing analysis of HD189733b with a strong H<SUB>2</SUB>O signal. Today, JWST is remarkable in detecting broadband exoplanet atmospheric features, but recovering crucial narrow band signals (e.g., CO) has proven to be challenging for planets with robust ground-based CO detection (e.g., WASP-18b, HD209458b). Our analysis of HD209458b and HD189733b further emphasizes the reasoning that ground-based observations are complementary to spaced-based facilities and possess unique advantages in providing unprecedented view of exoplanet atmospheres.

[1] 我们能否调和两颗经典热木星的空间与地面大气解释？利用IGRINS2光谱仪对HD209458b和HD189733b的综合再分析

热木星对行星形成理论提出了重大挑战，其大气成分是理解其起源的关键。尽管来自空间和地面设施的观测非常丰富，关键热木星 HD 209458 b 和 HD 189733 b 的分子组成长期以来一直存在激烈争议。更重要的是，这两颗行星处于中等温度热木星区间（1000-1500 K），比分子解离主导的超热行星（T > 2000 K）更冷，但又比冷凝主导的温暖海王星更热，正处于 H2O、CO、CH4、HCN 和 NH3 等物种可以共存的关键温度区间——非常适合研究富氧与富碳大气之间的化学转变。我将介绍我们利用 IGRINS2 同步高分辨率 H- 和 K-波段观测重新研究这些经典系外行星的计划。我们的观测针对日面热发射，减小了凌星几何带来的偏差，如边缘不对称、Rossiter-McLaughlin 效应和云的影响。我将展示 HD209458b 中强 H2O 和 CO 信号的初步结果，以及 HD189733b 的持续分析（同样有强 H2O 信号）。目前，JWST 在探测宽带系外行星大气特征方面表现出色，但对于那些地面已稳健探测到 CO 的行星（如 WASP-18b、HD209458b），恢复关键的窄带信号（如 CO）仍然具有挑战性。我们对 HD209458b 和 HD189733b 的分析进一步强调，地面观测与空间设施互为补充，并在提供前所未有的系外行星大气视角方面具有独特优势。

[2] A Detailed Investigation of HD 209458 b HST and JWST Transmission Spectra with SANSAR

2025-08-00

HD 209458 b is the first exoplanet on which an atmosphere was detected. Since then, its atmosphere has been investigated using multiple telescopes and instruments. However, many of its atmospheric constraints remain debatable. While Hubble Space Telescope (HST) observations suggested a highly subsolar metallicity, recent JWST NIRCam observations by Q. Xue et al. constrained a supersolar metallicity with highly subsolar C/O. In this work, we show a detailed investigation of HD 209458 b transmission spectra observations from JWST and HST using SANSAR, a newly developed planetary atmosphere modeling framework, with free, equilibrium chemistry and self-consistent grid retrievals. The overall best-fitting model with free retrievals (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>χ</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>red</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> = 1.21) constrains its metallicity and C/O to be highly subsolar, while equilibrium chemistry and grid retrievals (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>χ</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>red</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> = 1.27 and 1.30, respectively) are consistent with solar values using Space Telescope Imaging Spectrograph (STIS)+WFC3+NIRCam observations. The retrieved abundances of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO<SUB>2</SUB> are almost 3 orders of magnitude lower (highly subsolar) with STIS+WFC3+NIRCam compared to just NIRCam, using free retrievals. NIRCam observations alone also result in misleading constraints on metallicity and C/O, with equilibrium chemistry and grid retrieval. We find that the model choice of varying C/H or O/H to vary the C/O in equilibrium chemistry retrievals leads to different metallicity constraints with NIRCam, but similar constraints with STIS+WFC3+NIRCam. We conclude that NIRCam observations alone can lead to overestimation of abundances for exoplanet atmospheres and, therefore, should be used in combination with UV/optical and near-infrared observations to obtain robust constraints on abundances, C/O, and metallicity. In particular, even though we can detect the CO<SUB>2</SUB> feature with just NIRCam, we cannot constrain its abundances robustly without the optical baseline.

[2] 利用SANSAR对HD 209458 b的HST和JWST透射光谱的详细研究

HD 209458 b 是首个被探测到大气的系外行星。自那以后，其大气已通过多台望远镜和仪器进行了研究。然而，其许多大气约束仍存在争议。尽管哈勃空间望远镜（HST）观测表明其金属丰度远低于太阳，但最近 Q. Xue 等人利用 JWST NIRCam 观测约束其金属丰度高于太阳且 C/O 极低。在本工作中，我们利用新开发的行星大气建模框架 SANSAR，对 JWST 和 HST 的 HD 209458 b 透射光谱观测进行了详细分析，采用自由、平衡化学和自洽网格检索。整体上，自由检索的最佳拟合模型（χ_red^2 = 1.21）约束其金属丰度和 C/O 均远低于太阳，而平衡化学和网格检索（χ_red^2 = 1.27 和 1.30）则与利用 STIS+WFC3+NIRCam 观测得到的太阳值一致。利用自由检索，STIS+WFC3+NIRCam 得到的 H2O 和 CO2 丰度比仅用 NIRCam 时低近3个数量级（极低于太阳）。仅用 NIRCam 观测也会导致金属丰度和 C/O 的误导性约束（平衡化学和网格检索）。我们发现，在平衡化学检索中，通过改变 C/H 或 O/H 来调整 C/O 的模型选择，会导致 NIRCam 下金属丰度约束不同，但在 STIS+WFC3+NIRCam 下约束相似。我们得出结论，仅用 NIRCam 观测可能会高估系外行星大气的丰度，因此应与紫外/光学和近红外观测结合使用，以获得对丰度、C/O 和金属丰度的稳健约束。特别地，尽管仅用 NIRCam 可以探测到 CO2 特征，但没有光学基线无法稳健约束其丰度。

[3] The Watery Atmosphere of HD 209458 b Revealed by Joint K- and L-band High-resolution Spectroscopy

2025-10-00

We present a joint analysis of high-resolution K- and L-band observations of the benchmark hot Jupiter HD 209458 b from the Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer. One half-night of observations was obtained in each bandpass, covering similar preeclipse phases. The two epochs were then jointly analyzed using our atmospheric retrieval pipeline based on petitRADTRANS to constrain the atmospheric pressure─temperature profile and chemical composition. Consistent with recent results from JWST observations at lower spectral resolution, we obtain an oxygen-rich composition for HD 209458 b (C/O &lt; 10<SUP>−3</SUP> at 95% confidence) and a lower limit on the volatile metallicity similar to the solar value ([(C + O)/H] &gt; −0.2 at 95% confidence). Leveraging the large spectral grasp of the multiband observations, we constrain the atmospheric H<SUB>2</SUB>O mixing ratio to <inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mi>log</mml:mi><mml:msub><mml:mi>H</mml:mi><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:msub><mml:mi>O</mml:mi><mml:mi>V MR</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>&gt;</mml:mo><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>3.1</mml:mn></mml:math> </inline-formula> at 95% confidence, and obtain 95% upper limits on the atmospheric mixing ratios of CO (&lt;10<SUP>−4.8</SUP>), CH<SUB>4</SUB> (&lt;10<SUP>−4.5</SUP>), NH<SUB>3</SUB> (&lt;10<SUP>−5.8</SUP>), H<SUB>2</SUB>S (&lt;10<SUP>−3.3</SUP>), and HCN (&lt;10<SUP>−5.6</SUP>). The limits on CH<SUB>4</SUB>, NH<SUB>3</SUB>, and HCN are consistent with recent results from JWST transmission spectroscopy, demonstrating the value of multiband, ground-based high-resolution spectroscopy for precisely constraining trace-species abundances in exoplanet atmospheres. The retrieved low-C/O, moderate-metallicity composition for HD 209458 b is consistent with formation scenarios involving late accretion of substantial quantities of oxygen-rich refractory solids and/or ices.

[3] 通过联合K波段和L波段高分辨率光谱揭示HD 209458 b的富水大气

我们对基准热木星 HD 209458 b 的 Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer 获得的高分辨率 K 波段和 L 波段观测进行了联合分析。每个波段各获得了半夜的观测，覆盖了类似的凌前相位。随后，我们利用基于 petitRADTRANS 的大气检索流程对这两个历元的数据进行了联合分析，以约束大气的压强-温度剖面和化学组成。与近期 JWST 低光谱分辨率观测结果一致，我们获得了 HD 209458 b 的富氧组成（C/O < 10^−3，95%置信度）和与太阳值相当的挥发性金属丰度下限（[(C + O)/H] > −0.2，95%置信度）。借助多波段观测的广泛波长覆盖，我们将大气 H2O 混合比约束为 log(H2O_VMR) > −3.1（95%置信度），并获得了 CO（<10^−4.8）、CH4（<10^−4.5）、NH3（<10^−5.8）、H2S（<10^−3.3）和 HCN（<10^−5.6）的大气混合比 95% 上限。CH4、NH3 和 HCN 的上限与近期 JWST 透射光谱结果一致，显示多波段地基高分辨率光谱在精确约束系外行星大气微量组分丰度方面的价值。HD 209458 b 检索得到的低 C/O、中等金属丰度组成与其形成过程中晚期吸积大量富氧难挥发固体和/或冰的情景一致。

[4] The strange case of Na I in the atmosphere of HD 209458 b. Reconciling low- and high-resolution spectroscopic observations

2022-01-00

<BR /> Aims: We aim to investigate the origin of the discrepant results reported in the literature about the presence of Na I in the atmosphere of HD 209458 b, based on low- and high-resolution transmission spectroscopy. <BR /> Methods: We generated synthetic planetary atmosphere models and we compared them with the transmission light curves and spectra observed in previous studies. Our models account for the stellar limb-darkening and Rossiter-McLaughlin (RM) effects, and contemplate various possible scenarios for the planetary atmosphere. <BR /> Results: We reconciled the discrepant results by identifying a range of planetary atmospheres that are consistent with previous low- and high-resolution spectroscopic observations. Either both datasets are interpreted as consistent with a total absence of Na I in the planetary atmosphere (with Hubble Space Telescope data being affected by limb darkening), or the terminator temperature of HD 209458 b has to have an upper limit of about 1000 K. In particular, we find that 1D transmission spectra with lower-than-equilibrium temperatures can also explain the previously reported detection of absorption signal at low resolution due to differential transit depth in adjacent bands, while the cores of the Na I D lines may be masked by the strong RM signal seen at high resolution. We also rule out high-altitude clouds, which would otherwise mask the absorption signal at low resolution, as the source of the discrepancies. <BR /> Conclusions: This work highlights the synergies between different observing techniques, specifically low- and high-resolution spectroscopy, to fully characterise transiting exoplanet systems.

[4] HD 209458 b 大气中 Na I 的奇异案例。协调低分辨率与高分辨率光谱观测

目标：我们旨在基于低分辨率和高分辨率透射光谱，研究文献中关于HD 209458 b大气中Na I存在性的不同结果的起因。方法：我们生成了合成的行星大气模型，并将其与先前研究中观测到的透射光变和光谱进行比较。我们的模型考虑了恒星边缘变暗和Rossiter-McLaughlin（RM）效应，并涵盖了行星大气的多种可能情景。结果：我们通过识别一系列与先前低分辨率和高分辨率光谱观测一致的行星大气，调和了这些不同的结果。两组数据都可以解释为行星大气中完全没有Na I（Hubble Space Telescope数据受边缘变暗影响），或者HD 209458 b的晨昏线温度必须有约1000 K的上限。特别地，我们发现，具有低于平衡温度的1D透射光谱也可以解释先前在低分辨率下报告的吸收信号探测，这是由于相邻波段凌日深度的差异，而Na I D线的线芯可能被高分辨率下强烈的RM信号所掩盖。我们还排除了高空云层作为导致低分辨率下吸收信号被掩盖的原因。结论：本工作强调了不同观测技术，特别是低分辨率和高分辨率光谱之间的协同作用，以全面刻画凌日系外行星系统。

[5] Hello, Old Friend: Dayside thermal emission of HD 209458b analyzed using IGRINS2 high-resolution observations

2026-06-00

Among the Hot Jupiter population, HD209458b remains the definitive benchmark: the first transiting exoplanet, one of the most extensively observed, and a reference point against which atmospheric retrieval techniques and theoretical models are routinely calibrated. Yet, despite this privileged status, no consensus exists on its molecular composition or thermal structure (Line et al. 2016, Giacobbe et al. 2021, Xue et al. 2024, Verma et al. 2025), limiting our ability to validate chemical models, constrain elemental abundances, and extrapolate trends to broader exoplanet populations. We present new high-resolution dayside emission spectroscopy of HD209458b using the simultaneous H- and K-band coverage of IGRINS2, yielding the most chemically resolved ground-based view of this planet to date. Our analysis reveals strong and unambiguous detections of H2O and CO, along with the first evidence for H2S, uniquely enabled by targeting the dayside thermal emission and isolating narrow-band molecular features that remain challenging for broadband space-based observations alone. I will also present results from a comprehensive Bayesian investigation of the planet's atmospheric metallicity and temperature─pressure structure. Ongoing joint analyses combining IGRINS2 with archival WFC3/HST emission spectra aim to definitively resolve the composition and thermal structure of HD 209458 b, enabling us to recalibrate our understanding of this keystone hot Jupiter. This is a much-needed stride as we approach the frontier of sub-Neptune and Earth-analogue characterization. Prior to that, it is worth discussing: "Do we have a concrete understanding of benchmark Hot Jupiters, often regarded as having relatively less complex atmospheres, such as HD209458b?"

[5] 你好，老朋友：利用IGRINS2高分辨率观测分析HD 209458b白昼面热发射

[6] Revised chemistry of HD209458 b

2022-06-00

We characterize the atmosphere of the iconic giant planet HD209458 b based on a joint modeling effort of the available observations. <P />HD 209458 b is the first planet with a reported detection of a chemical species in its atmosphere, namely Na I, and also the one with the largest number of reported species (H I, O I, C II, Mg I, Fe II, Ca I, Sc I, H2O, CO, HCN, CH4, C2H2 and NH3). However, recent studies casted doubts on the authenticity of some detections. Most of the discrepant results are based on data obtained with different observing techniques, especially using low- and high-resolution spectroscopy. We performed a joint analysis of archive data to exploit the synergies between multiple observing techniques. <P />We reconciled discrepant results reported in the literature by identifying a range of planetary atmospheres that are consistent with both low- and high-resolution spectroscopic observations. The joint analysis enabled us to place tighter constraints on the chemical composition and physical state of the HD209458 b atmosphere. We discuss our findings providing an updated picture of this planetary system.

[6] HD209458 b的化学成分修订

[8] Osiris revisited: Confirming a solar metallicity and low C/O in HD 209458 b

2025-08-00

HD 209458 b is the prototypical hot Jupiter and one of the best targets available for precise atmosphere characterisation. Now that spectra from both the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) are available, we can reveal the atmospheric properties in unprecedented detail. For this study we performed a new data reduction and analysis of the original HST/WFC3 spectrum, accounting for the wavelength dependence of the instrument systematics that was not considered in previous analyses. This allowed us to precisely and robustly measure the much-debated H<SUB>2</SUB>O abundance in HD 209458 b's atmosphere. We combined the newly reduced spectrum with archival JWST/NIRCam data and ran free chemistry atmospheric retrievals over the 1.0 − 5.1 μm wavelength range, covering possible features of multiple absorbing species, including CO<SUB>2</SUB>, CO, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, NH<SUB>3</SUB>, HCN, Na, SO<SUB>2</SUB>, and H<SUB>2</SUB>S. We detected H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO<SUB>2</SUB> robustly at above 7σ significance, and found a 3.6σ preference for cloudy models compared to a clear atmosphere. For all the other absorbers we tested, only the upper limits of the abundance can be measured. We used Bayesian model averaging to account for a range of different assumptions about the cloud properties, resulting in a water volume mixing ratio of 0.95<SUB>−0.17</SUB><SUP>+0.35</SUP> × solar and a carbon dioxide abundance of 0.94<SUB>−0.09</SUB><SUP>+0.16</SUP> × solar. Both results are consistent with solar values and are comparable to predictions from the VULCAN 1D photochemistry model. Combining these values with a prior on the CO abundance from ground-based measurements, we derive an overall atmospheric composition comparable to solar metallicity of [M/H] = 0.10<SUB>−0.40</SUB><SUP>+0.41</SUP> and very low C/O of 0.054<SUB>−0.034</SUB><SUP>+0.080</SUP> with a 3 σ upper limit of 0.454. This indicates a strong enrichment in oxygen and depletion in carbon during HD 209458 b's formation.

[8] 重访Osiris：确认HD 209458 b的太阳金属丰度和低C/O

HD 209458 b是热木星的原型，也是目前最适合精确大气表征的目标之一。随着哈勃空间望远镜（HST）和詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）光谱的获取，我们能够以前所未有的细节揭示其大气性质。本研究对原始HST/WFC3光谱进行了新的数据处理和分析，考虑了此前分析未涉及的仪器系统误差的波长依赖性。这使我们能够精确且稳健地测量HD 209458 b大气中备受争议的H2O丰度。我们将新处理的光谱与归档的JWST/NIRCam数据结合，在1.0−5.1 μm波段范围内进行了自由化学大气检索，涵盖了包括CO2、CO、CH4、NH3、HCN、Na、SO2和H2S等多种吸收组分的可能特征。我们以超过7σ的显著性稳健探测到H2O和CO2，并发现与无云大气相比，云模型有3.6σ的偏好。对于其他吸收体，仅能测得丰度的上限。我们采用贝叶斯模型平均法，考虑了不同云属性假设，得到水体积混合比为0.95_{−0.17}^{+0.35} × solar，二氧化碳丰度为0.94_{−0.09}^{+0.16} × solar。两者均与太阳值一致，并与VULCAN 1D光化学模型预测相当。结合地面观测对CO丰度的先验，我们推导出整体大气组成与太阳金属丰度[M/H]=0.10_{−0.40}^{+0.41}相当，C/O极低，为0.054_{−0.034}^{+0.080}，3σ上限为0.454。这表明HD 209458 b在形成过程中氧显著富集、碳显著亏损。

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] An Exploration of Retrieval Choices for JWST Transit Spectra of Hot Jupiters: Varying Sampling Algorithms, Wavelength Coverage, Spectral Resolution, and Multi-Dimensionality of Models.

2024-02-00

Among the various methods for characterizing exoplanet atmospheres, transit spectroscopy has proven to be the most prolific. Hot Jupiters, while relatively rare, have emerged as favorable targets for spectral analysis. Their hot temperatures, close proximity to their host stars, and low-density atmospheres enable unparalleled insight into their structure and composition. Previous studies that investigated the atmospheres of Hot Jupiters using transmission spectroscopy were hampered by restricted wavelength coverage. Superior quality data demonstrated by JWST ERS and cycle 1 is pushing the community to upgrade their analysis methods to extract as much detail as possible. In our project, we assess to what degree the expanded wavelength coverage offered by JWST necessitates further complexity beyond the first generation of one-dimensional retrieval frameworks. We conducted several studies investigating trade-offs between wavelength coverage and signal-to-noise ratios. As part of this project, we have made significant improvements to the existing transit modeling and retrieval code, METIS, making it more suitable for application to real transit observations. Notably, we have incorporated Hamiltonian Monte Carlo techniques for parameter retrievals in our transit spectral analysis, a departure from previous Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) and nested sampling methods. We anticipate that it will be useful for multi-dimensional models and joint analysis of multiple datasets, as HMC has demonstrated to converge more robustly than MCMC for models with large numbers of parameters in other applications.

[1] JWST热木星凌日光谱检索选择探索：采样算法、波长覆盖、光谱分辨率和模型多维度的变化

[2] Exoplanet atmospheric retrievals through different JWST/NIRISS data reduction pipelines

2025-09-00

Since the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) became available in 2022, its observations of exoplanet transit spectra have revolutionized the field of exoplanet science. This allows to retrieve the distinct set of parameters that characterize these planetary atmospheres - and to compare how the distinct pipelines affect the retrieved parameters that characterize the planetary atmospheres across this small sample of hot Jupiters. [3] Knobs and dials of retrieving JWST transmission spectra: II. Impacts of pipeline-level differences on retrieval posteriors

2025-12-00

Context. Since the launch of the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST), observations of exoplanetary atmospheres have experienced a revolution in data quality. As atmospheric parameter inferences heavily depend on the underlying dataset, a re-evaluation of current methodologies is warranted to assess the reliability of these results. Aims. We investigate the impact of variations in input spectra on atmospheric retrievals for the hot Jupiter WASP-39 b using JWST transit data. Specifically, we analyse the reliability of parameter estimation results from random perturbations of the underlying spectrum, and their sensitivity to the use of three transmission spectra derived from the same observational data. Methods. Using the NIRSpec PRISM observation from a single transit of WASP-39 b, we performed retrievals with the TauREx framework. As an input baseline, we used a transmission spectrum derived in our work using the Eureka! data reduction pipeline. To investigate the reliability of these retrieval results, we analysed the behaviour of parameter posterior distributions under deviations of this spectrum. To mimic random noise, we performed a set of retrievals on scattered instances of the spectrum produced in this work. We compared this to differences resulting from retrievals based on existing spectra reduced from the same raw observation. Results. Our analysis identifies three types of parameter posterior distributions: (1) stable, Gaussian distributions for species constrained across the entire spectrum (e.g. H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO<SUB>2</SUB>); (2) uniform posteriors with upper bounds for species with weak constraints (e.g. CO and CH<SUB>4</SUB>); and (3) unstable, heavy-tailed posteriors for species constrained only by minor spectrum features (e.g. SO<SUB>2</SUB> and C<SUB>2</SUB>H<SUB>2</SUB>). We find that other parameters, such as the planetary radius and pressure-temperature profile, are stable under spectral perturbations. Conclusions. Parameter posterior distributions are different for atmospheric retrievals performed on independently reduced transmission spectra derived from the same raw data. This makes a robust interpretation difficult, particularly for skewed distributions. Based on this, we advocate for the careful assessment and selection of credible interval sizes to reflect this.

[3] JWST透射光谱检索的调节因子：II. 流程级差异对检索后验的影响

背景：自詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）发射以来，系外行星大气观测的数据质量发生了革命性提升。由于大气参数推断高度依赖于基础数据集，因此有必要重新评估当前方法，以检验这些结果的可靠性。目标：我们利用JWST凌星数据，研究输入光谱变化对热木星WASP-39 b大气检索的影响。具体而言，我们分析了基础光谱的随机扰动对参数估计结果的可靠性，以及使用同一观测数据导出的三组透射光谱时结果的敏感性。方法：我们采用WASP-39 b单次凌星的NIRSpec PRISM观测，利用TauREx框架进行检索。基线输入为本工作使用Eureka!数据处理流程得到的透射光谱。为检验检索结果的可靠性，我们分析了该光谱偏离情况下参数后验分布的表现。为模拟随机噪声，我们对本工作生成的光谱的多组扰动实例进行了检索，并将其与基于同一原始观测数据但不同数据处理流程得到的现有光谱的检索结果进行比较。结果：分析发现参数后验分布可分为三类：（1）对整个光谱均有约束的分子（如H<sub>2</sub>O和CO<sub>2</sub>）呈现稳定、高斯分布；（2）约束较弱的分子（如CO和CH<sub>4</sub>）呈现均匀分布并有上限；（3）仅由光谱微弱特征约束的分子（如SO<sub>2</sub>和C<sub>2</sub>H<sub>2</sub>）呈现不稳定、重尾分布。其他参数如行星半径和压强-温度剖面在光谱扰动下表现稳定。结论：基于同一原始数据独立处理得到的透射光谱进行大气检索时，参数后验分布存在差异，尤其对于偏斜分布，难以进行稳健解释。因此，我们建议在评估和选择可信区间大小时需格外谨慎，以反映这一点。

[4] Eyes on the Stars: A JWST Population Survey of Exoplanet Host Star Heterogeneities and Spectral Contributions to Transits

2024-02-00

Early JWST results have shown both the observatory's promise for revealing the properties of exoplanet atmospheres and the challenge that stellar spots and faculae pose to precise transmission spectroscopy. Without a reliable method to account for this stellar spectral contamination, stellar photospheric heterogeneity may limit JWST's scientific legacy in unveiling the atmospheres of the most exciting exoplanets. Fortunately, the same JWST datasets provide exquisite out-of-transit stellar spectra that can be leveraged to mitigate this effect, though most observations have yet to be fully exploited. Here we propose to thoroughly tap this priceless database via a large, uniform analysis of 36 NIRISS/SOSS and NIRSpec/PRISM time-series observations of 21 exoplanet hosts. Our analysis will reveal the photospheric properties of important hosts, provide insights into limitations of stellar models across spectral types, and constrain stellar variability and spectral contamination in JWST transits. As Legacy Data Products, we will produce a JWST Legacy Library of time-dependent exoplanet host star spectra, constraints on their photospheric spectral components and coverages, and transmission spectra with fully quantified uncertainties accounting for stellar spectral contributions. Our Legacy Data Products will also include open-source Python libraries we develop for the data reduction, stellar heterogeneity analysis, and transmission spectra corrections. The results of this program will pave the way for robust constraints on stellar photospheres from JWST, unlocking the observatory's full potential for characterizing transiting worlds.

[5] A Uniform Reanalysis of Hot Jupiters

2023-01-00

The formation and evolution of hot Jupiters is still an open question in exoplanet research, but much of what we know about them is influenced by the assumptions and tools used in their original discovery efforts. With the success of ongoing missions like Gaia and TESS, we now have a wealth of observations for most hot Jupiters, providing a key opportunity. What is needed is a complete reanalysis of our current sample of hot Jupiters to establish a homogeneous catalog of system parameters that is key to disentangling the evolutionary mechanism of giant planets. By analyzing all available observations of hot Jupiters in a self-consistent manner along with key observations available from the same observatory (TESS), the problem of the lack of homogeneity in the data is mitigated. To prioritize our analysis, the exoplanets were sorted and ranked according to each planet's transmission spectroscopy metric (TSM), quantifying the expected signal-to-noise and thus the ease by which transmission spectra can be obtained for each exoplanet. Planning for JWST will additionally be supported by the increased accuracy and precision on future transit times. Using KELT-14 b as an example, the uncertainty in the transit time from the initial discovery data swells to ±8.2 minutes when projected out to 2025. The equivalent uncertainty from our most recent analysis is ±0.6 minutes. However, for many of the known hot Jupiters, these uncertainties in future transit times are on the order of multiple hours in 2025, making them inaccessible for scheduling precious resources like JWST. I will present our current status in reanalyzing the &gt;450 known hot Jupiters, and discuss the importance of this effort in the context of KELT-14 b.

热木星的形成和演化仍然是系外行星研究中的一个未解之谜，但我们对它们的许多认识都受到最初发现时所采用的假设和工具的影响。随着Gaia和TESS等正在进行的任务取得成功，我们现在对大多数热木星拥有大量观测数据，这为研究提供了关键机遇。当前亟需对现有热木星样本进行全面的重新分析，以建立一个同质的系统参数目录，这对于揭示巨行星演化机制至关重要。通过以自洽的方式分析所有可用的热木星观测数据，并结合来自同一台天文台（TESS）的关键观测，可以缓解数据同质性不足的问题。为了优先排序分析，我们根据每颗行星的透射光谱指标（TSM）对系外行星进行排序和排名，量化其预期信噪比，从而评估每颗系外行星获取透射光谱的难易程度。对JWST的观测规划还将受益于未来凌日时间精度和准确度的提升。以KELT-14 b为例，若仅用最初发现数据推算至2025年，凌日时间的不确定性将膨胀至±8.2分钟，而我们最新分析得出的等效不确定性为±0.6分钟。然而，对于许多已知热木星，2025年未来凌日时间的不确定性仍在数小时量级，这使得它们难以用于安排如JWST等宝贵资源的观测。我将介绍我们对已知>450颗热木星重新分析的最新进展，并结合KELT-14 b讨论这一工作的意义。

[6] JWST COMPASS: Insights into the Systematic Noise Properties of NIRSpec/G395H from a Uniform Reanalysis of Seven Transmission Spectra

2026-03-00

JWST has already observed near-infrared transmission spectra of over a dozen super-Earths and sub-Neptunes. While some observations have allowed astronomers to characterize sub-Neptunes in unprecedented detail, small feature amplitudes and poorly understood systematics have led to ambiguous results for others. Using the first seven targets from the COMPASS program, which will survey 12 small planets using NIRSpec/G395H, we investigate timeseries systematics. We implement a model that uses the principle components of the normalized pixel fluxes to account for variations in the shape and position of the spectral trace. We find that observations with a smaller number of groups-per-integration benefit most profoundly from the use of this model, and that systematics are particularly strong between 2.8 and 3.5 μm. Despite these systematics, pandexo is a relatively accurate predictor of the precision of the spectra, with real error bars on average 5% larger in NRS1 and 12% larger in NRS2 than predicted. We compute new limits on metallicity and opaque pressure level for each target and compare these to previous results from the COMPASS program. Next, we coadd spectra from multiple targets to reduce the effective noise in the combined spectra in hopes of detecting transmission features in common between the targets, but this exercise does not yield compelling evidence of any signals. We find that a handful of additional transits are sufficient to break the degeneracy between metallicity and aerosols for the majority of our targets, pointing towards the possibility of unraveling the mysteries of these worlds with future allocations of JWST time.

[6] JWST COMPASS：对七个透射光谱统一再分析揭示NIRSpec/G395H系统噪声特性

JWST已经观测了十余颗超级地球和亚海王星的近红外透射光谱。虽然部分观测使天文学家得以前所未有地刻画亚海王星，但特征幅度较小和系统误差理解不足导致其他目标结果不明确。我们利用COMPASS项目的前七个目标（该项目将用NIRSpec/G395H观测12颗小行星），研究了时序系统误差。我们实现了一种模型，利用归一化像素通量的主成分来校正光谱迹线的形状和位置变化。我们发现，积分组数较少的观测最能从该模型中获益，且2.8至3.5 μm波段的系统误差尤为显著。尽管存在这些系统误差，pandexo对光谱精度的预测仍相对准确，实际误差条在NRS1平均大5%，在NRS2大12%。我们为每个目标计算了金属丰度和不透明压强层的新上限，并与COMPASS项目先前结果进行了比较。随后，我们将多个目标的光谱叠加，以降低合成光谱的有效噪声，期望探测到目标间共有的透射特征，但未获得有说服力的信号。我们发现，增加少量凌日观测即可打破大多数目标中金属丰度与气溶胶的简并性，表明未来JWST观测分配有望揭示这些世界的奥秘。

[7] Towards the Simultaneous Retrieval of Planetary Atmospheres and Stellar Surfaces

2023-01-00

As has been demonstrated in the past few months, we are entering a golden age of exoplanet atmospheric characterization with the capabilities of JWST. With this new precision, it is only natural to push from our current realm of giant planet atmospheric studies down to smaller, terrestrial planets. The small planets with potentially observable atmospheric signals will mainly be around small M stars, which are known for being active and having a significant number of spots and/or faculae on their surfaces. These stellar inhomogeneities are able to impact the transmission spectrum obtained during transit and contaminate it. However, through this contamination, we are conversely able to obtain information on the state of the stellar surface. In this work, we present a framework for the simultaneous retrieval of planetary atmospheres and the associated surface of the system's host star through an analysis of the transmission spectrum. If multiple planetary transits are observed in close enough proximity, then it is likely the stellar surface has not changed in a significant way. In principle, this information could be stitched together to create a complete map of the stellar surface. For example, multiple observations of the TRAPPIST-1 system have been taken with JWST only a few days apart, during which the star's surface conditions should have been consistent (modulo the stellar rotation phase). We demonstrate the ability of this framework to disentangle these signals and show how understanding the stellar contamination is crucial to finding terrestrial planet atmospheres outside of our solar system with JWST.

[7] 迈向行星大气与恒星表面同时检索

正如过去几个月所展示的，随着JWST能力的实现，我们正步入系外行星大气表征的黄金时代。凭借这一新精度，自然要将研究重心从目前的巨行星大气研究推进到更小的类地行星。具有潜在可观测大气信号的小行星主要围绕小型M星，这类恒星以活跃著称，表面存在大量黑子和/或亮斑。这些恒星非均匀性会影响凌日时获得的透射光谱并对其造成污染。然而，通过这种污染，我们反过来可以获得恒星表面状态的信息。在本工作中，我们提出了一个框架，通过分析透射光谱，实现对行星大气及其宿主恒星表面的同时检索。如果多次行星凌日观测时间足够接近，则恒星表面状态很可能没有发生显著变化。原则上，这些信息可以拼接起来，绘制出恒星表面完整图像。例如，对TRAPPIST-1系统的多次JWST观测仅相隔几天，此期间恒星表面条件应保持一致（除恒星自转相位外）。我们展示了该框架分离这些信号的能力，并说明理解恒星污染对于利用JWST在太阳系外发现类地行星大气至关重要。

[8] Revisiting a Classic: A Comprehensive Reanalysis of the Transmission Spectrum of the Archetypal Hot Jupiter WASP-17b

2022-06-00

WASP-17b is an archetypal hot Jupiter, marked by its frequent inclusion in theoretical and atmospheric retrieval studies, and in JWST Cycle 1 observations. Its inflated radius and long transit make it one of the very best targets for atmospheric characterisation, and as such, transmission spectroscopy of the planet has been performed both from the ground and with space-based telescopes. However, the existing space-based observations, upon which many of these studies critically rely, occurred before many of the now widely used high precision observing modes and analysis techniques were developed. <P />In this talk, I will present analysis of new high precision WFC3 IR observations, along with a consistent reanalysis of the Hubble and Spitzer data previously presented in Sing et al. 2016. Our updated 0.3-5 μm transmission spectrum achieves a markedly improved average precision of 272 ppm, taking advantage of recent significant advances in exoplanet data reduction and analysis techniques. I will also discuss our comprehensive atmospheric retrieval analysis of the transmission spectrum, through which we detect H<SUB>2</SUB>O absorption at &gt;7σ, and find evidence of CO<SUB>2</SUB> absorption at &gt;3σ. These retrievals also uncovered complexities that highlight both the importance of using robust statistics for model selection and the need for high precision panchromatic observations, as we transition to a new era of atmospheric characterisation with JWST.

[8] 重访经典：对典型热木星WASP-17b透射光谱的全面再分析

WASP-17b 是典型的热木星，因其在理论和大气检索研究以及 JWST 第一周期观测中的频繁出现而著称。其膨胀的半径和较长的凌日持续时间使其成为大气特性研究的最佳目标之一，因此，该行星的透射光谱已通过地面和空间望远镜进行观测。然而，许多现有的空间观测数据——这些研究高度依赖的数据——是在许多现今广泛使用的高精度观测模式和分析技术发展之前获得的。 在本报告中，我将展示新的高精度 WFC3 IR 观测数据的分析结果，并对 Sing 等（2016）中已发表的 Hubble 和 Spitzer 数据进行了统一的再分析。我们更新后的 0.3-5 μm 透射光谱平均精度显著提升，达到 272 ppm，得益于近年来系外行星数据处理和分析技术的重大进步。我还将讨论我们对透射光谱进行的全面大气检索分析，通过该分析我们以大于 7σ 的显著性探测到 H2O 吸收，并发现大于 3σ 的 CO2 吸收证据。这些检索还揭示了模型选择中采用稳健统计方法的重要性，以及在 JWST 时代到来之际对高精度全波段观测的需求。
