Is the main result reported in "Hot Hydrogen Climates Near the Inner Edge of the Habitable Zone" robust to independent reanalysis with different modeling assumptions?
“Hot Hydrogen Climates Near the Inner Edge of the Habitable Zone”报告的主要结果在采用不同建模假设进行独立再分析时是否稳健？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Climate Bistability at the Inner Edge of the Habitable Zone due to Runaway Greenhouse and Cloud Feedbacks

2024-09-00

Understanding the climate dynamics at the inner edge of the habitable zone is crucial for predicting the habitability of rocky exoplanets. Previous studies using global climate models (GCMs) have indicated that planets receiving high stellar flux can exhibit climate bifurcations, leading to bistability between a cold (temperate) and a hot (runaway) climate. However, the mechanism causing this bistability has not been fully explained, in part due to the difficulty associated with inferring mechanisms from small numbers of expensive numerical simulations in GCMs. In this study, we employ a two-column (dayside and nightside), two-layer climate model to investigate the physical mechanisms driving this bistability. Through mechanism-denial experiments, we demonstrate that the runaway greenhouse effect, coupled with a cloud feedback on either the dayside or nightside, leads to climate bistability. We also map out the parameters that control the location of the bifurcations and size of the bistability. This work identifies which mechanisms and GCM parameters control the stellar flux at which rocky planets are likely to retain a hot, thick atmosphere if they experience a hot start. This is critical for the prioritization of targets and interpretation of observations by the James Webb Space Telescope. Furthermore, our modeling framework can be extended to planets with different condensable species and cloud types.

[1] 由于失控温室效应和云反馈导致的宜居带内缘气候双稳态

理解宜居带内缘的气候动力学对于预测类地系外行星的宜居性至关重要。此前利用全球气候模型（GCM）的研究表明，接受高恒星辐射的行星可能出现气候分岔，导致冷（温带）与热（失控）气候的双稳态。然而，导致这种双稳态的机制尚未被充分解释，部分原因在于GCM中有限且昂贵的数值模拟难以推断机制。在本研究中，我们采用一个两柱（昼面和夜面）、两层气候模型，研究驱动这种双稳态的物理机制。通过机制否定实验，我们证明了失控温室效应与昼面或夜面云反馈的耦合会导致气候双稳态。我们还绘制了控制分岔位置和双稳态范围的参数空间。本工作明确了哪些机制和GCM参数控制岩石行星在经历热起始时可能保留热且厚大气的恒星辐射阈值。这对于JWST目标优先级排序和观测解释至关重要。此外，我们的建模框架可扩展至具有不同可凝结物种和云类型的行星。

[2] Lessons from Hubble and Spitzer: 1D Self-consistent Model Grids for 19 Hot Jupiter Emission Spectra

2024-08-00

We present a population-level analysis of the dayside thermal emission spectra of 19 planets observed with Hubble WFC3 and Spitzer IRAC 3.6 and 4.5 μm, spanning equilibrium temperatures 1200─2700 K and 0.7─10.5 Jupiter masses. We use grids of planet-specific 1D, cloud-free, radiative─convective─thermochemical equilibrium models (1D-RCTE) combined with a Bayesian inference framework to estimate atmospheric metallicity, the carbon-to-oxygen ratio, and day-to-night heat redistribution. In general, we find that the secondary eclipse data cannot reject the physics encapsulated within the 1D-RCTE assumption parameterized with these three variables. We find a large degree of scatter in atmospheric metallicities, with no apparent trend, and carbon-to-oxygen ratios that are mainly consistent with solar or subsolar values but do not exhibit population agreement. Together, these indicate either (1) formation pathways vary over the hot and ultra-hot Jupiter population and/or (2) more accurate composition measurements are needed to identify trends. We also find a broad scatter in derived dayside temperatures that do not demonstrate a trend with equilibrium temperature. Like with composition estimates, this suggests either significant variability in climate drivers over the population and/or more precise dayside temperature measurements are needed to identify a trend. We anticipate that 1D-RCTE models will continue to provide valuable insights into the nature of exoplanet atmospheres in the era of JWST.

[2] 哈勃和斯皮策的经验教训：19颗热木星发射光谱的一维自洽模型网格

我们对19颗行星的日面热发射光谱进行了群体层面的分析，这些行星由Hubble WFC3和Spitzer IRAC 3.6和4.5 μm观测，平衡温度范围为1200─2700 K，质量为0.7─10.5木星质量。我们采用行星特定的1D、无云、辐射─对流─热化学平衡模型（1D-RCTE）网格，并结合贝叶斯推断框架，估算大气金属丰度、碳氧比以及昼夜热量再分布。总体而言，我们发现次食数据无法排除由这三个变量参数化的1D-RCTE假设所涵盖的物理过程。我们发现大气金属丰度存在较大离散性，未表现出明显趋势；碳氧比主要与太阳或亚太阳值一致，但未表现出群体一致性。这表明（1）热木星和超热木星的形成路径可能存在差异，和/或（2）需要更精确的成分测量以识别趋势。我们还发现推导出的日面温度也有较大离散性，且与平衡温度无明显趋势。与成分估算类似，这表明群体内气候驱动因素可能存在显著变化，和/或需要更精确的日面温度测量以识别趋势。我们预计，在JWST时代，1D-RCTE模型将继续为系外行星大气性质提供有价值的见解。

[3] Breaking degeneracies in exoplanetary parameters through self-consistent atmosphere─interior modelling

2024-12-00

Context. With a new generation of observational instruments largely dedicated to exoplanets (i.e. JWST, ELTs, PLATO, and Ariel) providing atmospheric spectra and mass and radius measurements for large exoplanet populations, the planetary models used to understand the findings are being put to the test. Aims. We seek to develop a new planetary model, the Heat Atmosphere Density Evolution Solver (HADES), which is the product of self-consistently coupling an atmosphere model and an interior model, and aim to compare its results to currently available findings. Methods. We conducted atmospheric calculations under radiative-convective equilibrium, while the interior is based on the most recent and validated ab initio equations of state. We pay particular attention to the atmosphere-interior link by ensuring a continuous thermal, gravity, and molecular mass profile between the two models. Results. We applied the model to the database of currently known exoplanets to characterise intrinsic thermal properties. In contrast to previous findings, we show that intrinsic temperatures (T<SUB>int</SUB>) of 200─400 K ─ increasing with equilibrium temperature ─ are required to explain the observed radius inflation of hot Jupiters. In addition, we applied our model to perform 'atmosphere-interior' retrievals by Bayesian inference using observed spectra and measured parameters. This allows us to showcase the model using example applications, namely to WASP-39 b and 51 Eridani b. For the former, we show how the use of spectroscopic measurements can break degeneracies in the atmospheric metallicity (Z) and intrinsic temperature. We derive relatively high values of Z = 14.79<SUB>−1.91</SUB><SUP>+ 1.80</SUP> × solar and T<SUB>int</SUB> = 297.39<SUB>−16.9</SUB><SUP>+8.95</SUP> K, which are necessary to explain the radius inflation and the chemical composition of WASP-39 b. With this example, we show th<SUP>.</SUP>e importance of using a self-consistent model with the radius being a constrained parameter of the model and of using the age of the host star to break radius and mass degeneracies. When applying our model to 51 Eridani b, we derive a planet mass M<SUB>p</SUB> = 3.13<SUB>−0.04</SUB><SUP>0.05</SUP> M<SUB>J</SUB> and a core mass M<SUB>core</SUB> = 31.86<SUB>+0.32</SUB><SUP>−0.18</SUP> M<SUB>E</SUB>, suggesting a potential formation by core accretion combined with a 'hot start' scenario. Conclusions. We conclude that self-consistent atmosphere─interior models efficiently break degeneracies in the structure of both transiting and directly imaged exoplanets. Such tools have great potential to interpret current and future observations, thereby providing new insights into the formation and evolution of exoplanets.

[3] 通过自洽大气-内部建模打破系外行星参数的简并性

背景：随着新一代观测仪器（如JWST、ELTs、PLATO和Ariel）主要用于系外行星研究，能够为大量系外行星提供大气光谱及质量和半径测量，用于理解这些发现的行星模型正面临考验。目标：我们致力于开发一种新的行星模型——热大气密度演化求解器（HADES），该模型通过自洽耦合大气模型和内部结构模型而成，并旨在将其结果与当前可用的研究成果进行比较。方法：我们在辐射-对流平衡下进行大气计算，内部结构基于最新且经过验证的从头算状态方程。我们特别关注大气与内部结构的连接，确保两者之间的热、重力和分子质量剖面连续。结果：我们将该模型应用于当前已知系外行星数据库，以刻画其内禀热性质。与以往研究不同，我们显示要解释热木星观测到的半径膨胀，需要内禀温度（T_int）为200─400 K，且随平衡温度升高而增加。此外，我们利用观测光谱和测量参数，通过贝叶斯推断进行“大气-内部结构”检索。我们以WASP-39 b和51 Eridani b为例展示模型应用。对于前者，我们展示了光谱测量如何打破大气金属丰度（Z）和内禀温度之间的简并性。我们得到Z = 14.79_{-1.91}^{+1.80} × solar和T_int = 297.39_{-16.9}^{+8.95} K的较高值，这对于解释WASP-39 b的半径膨胀和化学组成是必要的。通过该例子，我们展示了使用自洽模型、将半径作为模型约束参数以及利用母星年龄打破半径和质量简并性的重要性。将模型应用于51 Eridani b时，我们得到行星质量M_p = 3.13_{-0.04}^{+0.05} M_J和核质量M_core = 31.86_{+0.32}^{-0.18} M_E，暗示其可能通过核吸积并结合“热启动”情景形成。结论：我们认为自洽的大气-内部结构模型能够有效打破凌星和直接成像系外行星结构的简并性。这类工具对于解释当前和未来观测具有巨大潜力，从而为系外行星的形成和演化提供新见解。

[4] A Stochastic Propagator Approach to Quantum Open Systems

2021-00-00

Hot Jupiters are the only class of exoplanets for which detailed atmospheric characterization is possible with current instruments. Because of the strong horizontal temperature contrasts and the global-scale circulation expected on these planets, simulations with three-dimensional global circulation models (GCMs) are needed to place observations in context. Two effects that previously have been neglected in GCMs are transport-induced disequilibrium chemistry and photochemical hazes. Both have the potential to substantially change temperature structure, atmospheric circulation and predicted spectra.In the first part of this dissertation, I simulated the effects of disequilibrium abundances of methane, carbon monoxide and water on radiative transfer, temperature structure and phase curves in a GCM of hot Jupiter HD 189733b. The temperature changes by 50 to 100 K for the range of methane abundances expected from chemical kinetics calculations, with the dayside cooling and the nightside warming. I found little effect on the model-predicted 4.5 µm phase curve but large changes to the 3.6µm phase curve. This disproves a previous hypothesis that disequilibrium chemistry could explain the low nightside fluxes observed in the 4.5 µm band.In the second part, I developed a model simulating hazes as passive tracers in a GCM to study the 3D distribution of photochemical hazes in the atmospheres of hot Jupiters. The results show that the haze mass mixing ratio varies horizontally by over an order of magnitude. Contrary to previous predictions, hazes with small particle sizes (&lt;30 nm) are more abundant at the nightside and morning terminator than at the dayside and evening terminator.Finally, I included haze radiative feedback in the GCM to investigate how heating and cooling by photochemical hazes change temperature structure and atmospheric circulation. The dayside heats up by hundreds of Kelvin at low pressures. The response of the atmospheric circulation to the haze radiative feedback strongly depends on the assumed haze optical properties. For soot-like hazes, the jet strength somewhat decreases, and the haze distribution remains qualitatively similar to simulations without haze radiative feedback. For refractive indices resembling Titan-type hazes, the jet strength increases dramatically, leading to much higher haze abundances near the evening terminator.

[4] 量子开放系统的随机传播子方法

热木星是目前唯一能够通过现有仪器进行详细大气特性研究的系外行星类别。由于这些行星上预期存在强烈的水平温度差异和全球尺度的大气环流，因此需要利用三维全球环流模型（GCM）对观测结果进行背景分析。此前GCM中被忽略的两个效应是输运诱导的非平衡化学和光化学烟雾。这两者都有可能显著改变温度结构、大气环流和预测光谱。本论文第一部分，我在热木星HD 189733b的GCM中模拟了甲烷、一氧化碳和水的非平衡丰度对辐射传输、温度结构和相位曲线的影响。对于化学动力学计算预期的甲烷丰度范围，温度变化为50到100 K，白天面降温，夜晚面升温。我发现模型预测的4.5 µm相位曲线几乎没有变化，但3.6 µm相位曲线变化很大。这推翻了此前认为非平衡化学可以解释4.5 µm波段观测到的低夜面通量的假设。第二部分，我开发了一个将烟雾作为被动示踪物的GCM模型，用于研究热木星大气中光化学烟雾的三维分布。结果显示，烟雾的质量混合比在水平方向上变化超过一个数量级。与先前预测相反，粒径小于30 nm的烟雾在夜面和晨昏线的丰度高于白天面和傍晚昏线。最后，我在GCM中引入了烟雾的辐射反馈，研究光化学烟雾的加热和冷却如何改变温度结构和大气环流。白天面在低压下升温数百K。大气环流对烟雾辐射反馈的响应强烈依赖于假定的烟雾光学性质。对于类似烟灰的烟雾，喷流强度略有减弱，烟雾分布与无烟雾辐射反馈的模拟结果定性相似。对于折射率类似土卫六型烟雾的情况，喷流强度显著增强，导致傍晚昏线附近的烟雾丰度大幅增加。

[5] Self-Consistent Constraints for a Population of Hot Jupiters and Implications for Planet Formation and Climate

2022-06-00

Transit and eclipse spectroscopy offer insights into the chemical and radiative properties of extrasolar planet atmospheres, but inferring these properties from their spectra requires data-model comparisons. Bayesian retrieval methods are commonly used to derive atmosphere characteristics from models. Free retrievals make minimal assumptions allowing for flexible data-model fits and potentially illuminating unknown physics, but this also creates the possibility of inferring unphysical atmosphere characteristics. In this work, we combine powerful Bayesian parameter estimation tools with novel grids of self-consistent, one-dimensional, radiative-convective equilibrium models. These models are more restrictive than free retrievals, giving them more predictive power, while maintaining the computational speed that increasingly complex models lack. Using this framework, we determine metallicities, carbon-to-oxygen ratios, and heat redistributions for a population of hot Jupiters from their dayside thermal emission spectra. Our sample is derived from a uniform data-reduction analysis of Hubble Space Telescope WFC3 and Spitzer 3.6/4.5 micron observations sourced from a variety of programs. Results are presented in the context of current trend predictions in literature — metallicity and C/O closely relate to planetary formation theories, and heat redistribution has implications for climate. Finally, we present this grid modeling method as a tool for broad trend predictions that inform observations with JWST and ground-based high-resolution instruments.

[5] 热木星种群的自洽约束及其对行星形成和气候的影响

凌星和掩食光谱学为系外行星大气的化学和辐射特性提供了洞见，但要从光谱中推断这些特性需要数据与模型的对比。贝叶斯检索方法通常用于从模型中推导大气特性。自由检索假设最少，允许灵活的数据-模型拟合，并有可能揭示未知物理过程，但这也可能导致推断出不符合物理的大气特性。在本工作中，我们将强大的贝叶斯参数估计工具与新型自洽的一维辐射-对流平衡模型网格相结合。这些模型比自由检索更具约束性，因此具有更强的预测能力，同时保持了复杂模型所缺乏的计算速度。利用这一框架，我们根据热木星白昼面热发射光谱，确定了其金属丰度、碳氧比和热量再分布。我们的样本来自对哈勃空间望远镜WFC3和Spitzer 3.6/4.5微米观测数据的统一数据处理分析，数据来源于多个项目。结果在当前文献趋势预测的背景下进行展示——金属丰度和C/O与行星形成理论密切相关，热量再分布对气候有影响。最后，我们将这种网格建模方法作为一种工具，用于进行广泛的趋势预测，为JWST和地基高分辨率仪器的观测提供参考。

[6] Repeated Cyclogenesis on Hot-Exoplanet Atmospheres with Deep Heating

2023-12-00

Most current models of hot-exoplanet atmospheres assume shallow heating, a strong day-night differential heating near the top of the atmosphere. Here we investigate the effects of energy deposition at differing depths in a model tidally locked gas-giant exoplanet. We perform high-resolution atmospheric flow simulations of hot-exoplanet atmospheres forced with idealized thermal heating representative of shallow and deep heating (i.e., stellar irradiation strongly deposited at ∼10<SUP>3</SUP> Pa and ∼10<SUP>5</SUP> Pa pressure levels, respectively). Unlike with shallow heating, the flow with deep heating exhibits a new dynamic equilibrium state, characterized by repeated generation of giant cyclonic storms that move away westward once formed. The formation is accompanied by a burst of heightened turbulence, leading to the production of small-scale flow structures and large-scale mixing of temperature on a timescale of ∼3 planetary rotations. Significantly, while effects that could be important (e.g., coupled radiative flux and convectively excited gravity waves) are not included, over a timescale of several hundred days the simulations robustly show that the emergent thermal flux depends strongly on the heating type and is distinguishable by current observations.

[6] 热系外行星大气中反复发生的深层加热气旋生成

目前大多数热系外行星大气模型假设加热层较浅，即大气顶层存在强烈的昼夜加热差异。本文研究了能量在不同深度沉积对潮汐锁定气态巨型系外行星模型的影响。我们对热系外行星大气进行了高分辨率流体模拟，采用理想化的热加热，分别代表浅层和深层加热（即恒星辐射分别主要沉积在约10^3 Pa和约10^5 Pa压力层）。与浅层加热不同，深层加热下的大气流动表现出一种新的动力学平衡态，其特征是反复生成巨大的气旋风暴，并在形成后向西移动。风暴形成伴随着湍流增强，导致小尺度流动结构和大尺度温度混合的产生，时间尺度约为3个行星自转周期。值得注意的是，尽管未包含可能重要的效应（如耦合的辐射通量和对流激发的重力波），但在数百天的时间尺度上，模拟结果稳健地显示，最终的热通量强烈依赖于加热类型，并可被当前观测所区分。

[7] Self-Consistent Constraints on Elemental Abundances and Heat Redistributions for a Population of Hot Jupiters

2021-01-00

Transit and eclipse spectroscopy offer insights into the chemical and radiative properties of extrasolar planets. Inferring said properties from their spectra necessarily requires data-model comparisons. Typically, Bayesian atmospheric retrieval methods have been used to derive these properties. Retrievals generally make minimal assumptions allowing for more flexibility in the fits and potentially illuminating unknown physics, but this also creates the possibility of unphysical atmospheric determinations. In this work, we combine powerful Bayesian parameter estimation tools with a new grid of self-consistent, one dimensional radiative-convective equilibrium models. Using this framework, we determine metallicities, carbon-to-oxygen ratios, and heat redistributions for a population of hot Jupiters from their dayside thermal emission spectra. Our sample is derived from a uniform data-reduction analysis of spatial scan Hubble Space Telescope WFC3 and Spitzer 3.6/4.5 micron observations sourced from a variety of programs. The self-consistent approach mitigates the possibility of unphysical solutions, resulting in robust atmospheric inferences. We will present our self-consistent constraints on these fundamental atmospheric quantities in the context of current retrieval results and compare to atmospheric composition predictions from planet formation theories. Our nominal model fits to each object will be extrapolated to the James Webb Space Telescope wavelengths, as well as high-resolution, cross-correlation instruments, in order to identify observational setups that best address any current degeneracies.

[7] 热木星种群的元素丰度与热量再分布的自洽约束

凌星和掩食光谱学为系外行星的化学和辐射特性提供了洞见。从光谱推断这些特性必然需要数据与模型的比较。通常，贝叶斯大气检索方法被用于推导这些属性。检索通常只做最小假设，从而在拟合中更具灵活性，并有可能揭示未知物理过程，但这也带来了得到非物理大气解的可能性。在本工作中，我们将强大的贝叶斯参数估计工具与一套新的自洽一维辐射-对流平衡模型网格结合。利用这一框架，我们根据热木星的日面热发射光谱，确定了其金属丰度、C/O比和热量再分布。我们的样本来自对Hubble Space Telescope WFC3和Spitzer 3.6/4.5微米观测数据的统一数据处理分析，这些数据源自多个项目。自洽方法减小了非物理解的可能性，从而获得稳健的大气推断。我们将在当前检索结果的背景下，展示对这些基本大气参数的自洽约束，并与行星形成理论对大气组成的预测进行比较。我们对每个目标的标称模型拟合将外推到James Webb Space Telescope波段，以及高分辨率交叉相关仪器，以确定最能解决当前简并性的观测方案。

[8] Unusually Hot Interiors Could Reconcile the Missing-methane Problem for Warm-to-hot Exoplanets with Hydrogen Atmospheres

2026-06-00

JWST is revolutionizing the field of exoplanet atmospheres by delivering unprecedented spectroscopic constraints on their chemical compositions. It has provided tight constraints on the abundances of dominant carbon- and oxygen-bearing species on numerous warm-to-hot exoplanets with hydrogen-dominated atmospheres. Under thermochemical equilibrium, many of these exoplanets should have abundant methane (CH<SUB>4</SUB>); however, CH<SUB>4</SUB> has, so far, only been spotted in a few cases. Here, we present a simple, geochemistry-inspired framework to explore whether elevated intrinsic temperatures (T<SUB>int</SUB>) can account for the CH<SUB>4</SUB> depletions. Instead of using computationally expensive, forward grid models, our fast analytical framework focuses on two key chemical equilibria: CO─CH<SUB>4</SUB> and CO─CO<SUB>2</SUB>, allowing us to quickly constrain the minimum T<SUB>int</SUB> that is consistent with JWST-observed abundances of H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, and CO<SUB>2</SUB>. Applying this framework to 12 warm-to-hot exoplanets, we find that several targets require minimum T<SUB>int</SUB> values exceeding those predicted by standard evolution models, while others remain consistent with lower T<SUB>int</SUB> solutions or exhibit degeneracies with other solutions. Our sample enables an initial exploration of population-level trends: while many exoplanets broadly follow an empirical T<SUB>eq</SUB>─T<SUB>int</SUB> relation derived from the hot-Jupiter mass─radius population, a subset of targets lie well above this trend. The apparent need for hotter interiors suggests that, while Ohmic dissipation is probably an important heat source among the general population, additional heating processes, such as tidal heating, may also play important roles for some planets. Our results demonstrate the diagnostic power of atmospheric chemistry as a complementary probe of exoplanet interiors.

[8] 异常高温内部可解释富氢温暖至热系外行星的缺甲烷问题

JWST正在通过对系外行星大气化学组成的前所未有的光谱约束，彻底改变该领域。它对许多氢主导大气的温暖至高温系外行星中主要含碳和含氧物种的丰度给出了严格约束。在热化学平衡下，许多这样的系外行星应当富含甲烷（CH4）；然而，迄今为止，仅在少数案例中探测到CH4。本文提出了一个简单、受地球化学启发的框架，用于探索较高的内禀温度（Tint）是否可以解释CH4的亏损。我们没有采用计算量大的前向网格模型，而是提出了一个快速的解析框架，聚焦于两个关键化学平衡：CO─CH4和CO─CO2，从而能够快速约束与JWST观测到的H2O、CO、CH4和CO2丰度一致的最小Tint。将该框架应用于12颗温暖至高温系外行星，我们发现部分目标需要的最小Tint超过标准演化模型的预测，而其他目标则与较低Tint解一致，或与其他解存在简并性。我们的样本使得对族群层面趋势进行初步探索成为可能：虽然许多系外行星大致遵循由热木星质量-半径族群推导的经验Teq─Tint关系，但有一部分目标明显高于该趋势。对更高内热的需求表明，尽管欧姆耗散可能是一般族群中的重要热源，但如潮汐加热等额外加热过程在某些行星中也可能起重要作用。我们的结果展示了大气化学作为系外行星内部结构互补探针的诊断能力。

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Origin and Extent of the Opacity Challenge for Atmospheric Retrievals of WASP-39 b

2023-06-00

As the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) came online last summer, we entered a new era of astronomy. This new era is supported by data products of unprecedented information content that require novel reduction and analysis techniques. Recently, Niraula et al. (N22) highlighted the need for upgraded opacity models to prevent facing a model-driven accuracy wall when interpreting exoplanet transmission spectra. Here, we follow the same approach as N22 to explore the sensitivity of inferences on the atmospheric properties of WASP-39 b to the opacity models used. We find that the retrieval of the main atmospheric properties from this first JWST exoplanet spectrum is mostly unaffected by the current state of the community's opacity models. Abundances of strong opacity sources like water and carbon dioxide are reliably constrained within ~0.30 dex, beyond the 0.50 dex accuracy wall reported in N22. Assuming the completeness and accuracy of line lists, N22's accuracy wall is primarily driven by model uncertainties on broadening coefficients and far-wing behaviors, which we find to have marginal consequences for interpreting the transmission spectra of large, hot, high-metallicity atmospheres such as WASP-39 b, in opposition to emission spectra and climate modeling, which depend on deeper parts of a planetary atmosphere. The origin of the opacity challenge in the retrieval of metal-rich hot Jupiters via transmission spectroscopy will thus mostly stem from the incompleteness and inaccuracy of line lists.

[1] WASP-39 b大气检索中不透明度难题的起源与范围

随着James Webb Space Telescope (JWST)于去年夏天投入运行，天文学进入了一个新时代。这个新时代的数据产品信息量前所未有，需要新的数据处理和分析技术。近期，Niraula等人（N22）强调了升级不透明度模型的必要性，以避免在解释系外行星透射光谱时遇到模型驱动的精度瓶颈。本文采用与N22相同的方法，探讨了WASP-39 b大气性质推断对所用不透明度模型的敏感性。我们发现，从JWST首个系外行星光谱反演得到的主要大气性质基本不受当前社区不透明度模型状态的影响。强不透明度源（如水和二氧化碳）的丰度可被可靠约束在约0.30 dex以内，优于N22报告的0.50 dex精度瓶颈。在假设谱线列表完整且准确的前提下，N22的精度瓶颈主要由展宽系数和远翼行为的模型不确定性驱动，我们发现这些因素对解释WASP-39 b这类大、热、高金属丰度大气的透射光谱影响有限，而对发射光谱和气候建模影响更大，因为后者依赖于行星大气更深层。通过透射光谱对金属丰富热木星进行检索时，不透明度挑战的根源主要在于谱线列表的不完整和不准确。

[2] The effect of JWST/NIRSpec data reduction on the retrieval of WASP-39b atmospheric properties

2026-03-00

Context. The James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) provides exoplanetary transit observations with an unprecedented spectral range coverage, offering exceptional data on exoplanet atmospheres. Nevertheless, the presence of systematics in the data reduction process introduces small, but significant uncertainties that propagate into atmospheric retrievals. Understanding how these reduction choices affect interpreted atmospheric properties is essential. Aims. We aim to quantify the impact of different JWST/NIRSpec PRISM data-reduction processes, as well as the relevance of saturation on the retrieved atmospheric properties of WASP-39b. We also assess whether or not these biases are comparable to those introduced by assumptions made in atmospheric modelling, particularly in the treatment of aerosol extinction. Compared with previous similar efforts, we use the highest number of alternative data reductions and discuss the role of the saturated spectral region, without adding any additional data from other sources or random noise, in contrast to previous works. Methods. We performed a series of nested-sampling Bayesian retrievals using MultiNest and forward models generated with the Planetary Spectrum Generator. We analysed six independently reduced NIRSpec/PRISM spectra and compare retrievals using the full wavelength range as well as versions excluding the saturated 0.69-1.91 μm region. We further tested the effect of including three different cloud-opacity parametrisations. Results. Significant discrepancies arise among retrievals based on different calibrations, affecting key atmospheric parameters such as temperature, molecular abundances, and cloud opacity, often at a level exceeding one order of magnitude. Excluding the saturated region reduces the inter-pipeline dispersion; conversely, however, it also increases degeneracies between parameters. Differences introduced by data reduction are comparable in magnitude to those produced by distinct cloud-opacity models. Bayesian evidence systematically favours non-flat aerosol extinction, although the preferred spectral behaviour depends on the specific calibration used. Conclusions. Variations among JWST/NIRSpec data-reduction pipelines produce measurable and often substantial differences in retrieved atmospheric properties of WASP-39b. These biases are similar in scale to the uncertainties on modelling assumptions, underscoring the importance of robust and homogeneous calibration procedures. The results also confirm that JWST data possess the sensitivity required to probe aerosol spectral behaviour, although such constraints remain strongly dependent on the adopted data-reduction strategy.

[2] JWST/NIRSpec数据处理对WASP-39b大气性质检索的影响

背景：James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) 提供了前所未有的光谱覆盖范围的系外行星凌日观测，为系外行星大气研究带来了极佳的数据。然而，数据处理过程中的系统误差会引入小但显著的不确定性，并影响到大气检索结果。理解这些数据处理选择如何影响大气属性的解释至关重要。目的：我们旨在量化不同JWST/NIRSpec PRISM数据处理流程，以及饱和对WASP-39b大气检索属性的影响。我们还评估这些偏差是否与大气建模假设（特别是气溶胶消光处理）引入的偏差相当。与以往类似研究相比，我们采用了最多的数据处理方案，并讨论了饱和光谱区的作用，且未引入其他来源的数据或随机噪声，这与以往工作不同。方法：我们使用MultiNest进行一系列嵌套采样的贝叶斯检索，并用Planetary Spectrum Generator生成前向模型。我们分析了六组独立处理的NIRSpec/PRISM光谱，并比较了全波段和去除饱和区（0.69-1.91 μm）的检索结果。我们还测试了三种不同的云不透明度参数化方式的影响。结果：不同校准下的检索结果存在显著差异，影响关键大气参数如温度、分子丰度和云不透明度，差异常常超过一个数量级。去除饱和区可减少不同流程间的分散，但同时也增加了参数间的简并性。数据处理引入的差异与不同云不透明度模型带来的差异量级相当。贝叶斯证据系统性地支持非平坦气溶胶消光，但具体的光谱行为依赖于所用的校准。结论：不同JWST/NIRSpec数据处理流程会导致WASP-39b大气检索属性存在可测且常常显著的差异。这些偏差与建模假设带来的不确定性相当，强调了稳健且一致的数据校准流程的重要性。结果还确认JWST数据具备探测气溶胶光谱行为的灵敏度，但这种约束高度依赖于所采用的数据处理策略。

[3] ACCESS: Confirmation of a Clear Atmosphere for WASP-96b and a Comparison of Light Curve Detrending Techniques

2022-10-00

One of the strongest Na I features was observed in WASP-96b. To confirm this novel detection, we provide a new 475-825 nm transmission spectrum obtained with Magellan/IMACS, which indeed confirms the presence of a broad sodium absorption feature. We find the same result when reanalyzing the 400-825 nm VLT/FORS2 data. We also utilize synthetic data to test the effectiveness of two common detrending techniques: (1) a Gaussian processes (GP) routine, and (2) common-mode correction followed by polynomial correction (CMC+Poly). We find that both methods poorly reproduce the absolute transit depths but maintain their true spectral shape. This emphasizes the importance of fitting for offsets when combining spectra from different sources or epochs. Additionally, we find that, for our data sets, both methods give consistent results, but CMC+Poly is more accurate and precise. We combine the Magellan/IMACS and VLT/FORS2 spectra with literature 800-1644 nm HST/WFC3 spectra, yielding a global spectrum from 400 to 1644 nm. We used the PLATON and Exoretrievals retrieval codes to interpret this spectrum, and find that both yield relatively deeper pressures where the atmosphere is optically thick at log-pressures between ${1.3}_{-1.1}^{+1.0}$ and ${0.29}_{-2.02}^{+1.86}$ bars, respectively. Exoretrievals finds solar to supersolar Na I and H<SUB>2</SUB>O log-mixing ratios of $-{5.4}_{-1.9}^{+2.0}$ and $-{4.5}_{-2.0}^{+2.0}$ , respectively, while PLATON finds an overall metallicity of ${\mathrm{log}}_{10}(Z/{Z}_{\odot })=-{0.49}_{-0.37}^{+1.0}$ dex. Therefore, our findings are in agreement with the literature and support the inference that the terminator of WASP-96b has few aerosols obscuring prominent features in the optical to near-infrared (near-IR) spectrum.

[3] ACCESS：确认WASP-96b为无云大气，并对光变曲线去趋势技术进行比较

在 WASP-96b 上观测到了最强的 Na I 特征之一。为确认这一新颖探测，我们提供了使用 Magellan/IMACS 获得的新 475-825 nm 透射光谱，确实证实了宽钠吸收特征的存在。我们在重新分析 400-825 nm VLT/FORS2 数据时也得到了相同结果。我们还利用合成数据测试了两种常用去趋势技术的有效性：（1）高斯过程（GP）方法，和（2）共模校正加多项式校正（CMC+Poly）。我们发现，两种方法都难以准确再现绝对凌日深度，但能保持真实的光谱形状。这强调了在合并来自不同来源或历元的光谱时拟合偏移量的重要性。此外，我们发现对于我们的数据集，两种方法给出的结果一致，但 CMC+Poly 更准确且精度更高。我们将 Magellan/IMACS 和 VLT/FORS2 光谱与文献中的 800-1644 nm HST/WFC3 光谱结合，得到 400 到 1644 nm 的全局光谱。我们使用 PLATON 和 Exoretrievals 检索代码对该光谱进行解释，发现两者都给出了大气在 log-pressures ${1.3}_{-1.1}^{+1.0}$ 和 ${0.29}_{-2.02}^{+1.86}$ bar 之间为光学厚的较深压强。Exoretrievals 得到 Na I 和 H2O 的对数混合比为 $-{5.4}_{-1.9}^{+2.0}$ 和 $-{4.5}_{-2.0}^{+2.0}$，分别为太阳丰度到超太阳丰度；而 PLATON 得到整体金属丰度为 ${\mathrm{log}}_{10}(Z/{Z}_{\odot })=-{0.49}_{-0.37}^{+1.0}$ dex。因此，我们的发现与文献一致，并支持 WASP-96b 晨昏线在光学到近红外（近 IR）波段有很少气溶胶遮蔽显著特征的推断。

[4] WASP-39b cloud properties from JWST transit spectroscopy: a Bayesian analysis.

2024-09-00

Almost all of the detected hot Jupiters present clouds and hazes in their atmospheres. These ensembles of aerosols play an important role during primary transits blocking the stellar flux transmitted through the outer atmosphere of exoplanets, thus having an important influence on transmitted planetary spectra.Transmission spectra are very sensitive to the presence of clouds in the exoplanet atmospheres (Fortney, 2005). Depending on their size, aerosols can leave smooth effects on the observed spectra. Sometimes, cloud opacity can hide the spectral signature of different molecules that would be otherwise imprinted in the signal. When clouds become optically thick, they block all the flux that would cross the levels below them, similarly to a solid surface (Benneke &amp; Seager, 2012). Thus, a good understanding of the properties of the aerosols in exoplanets is essential to understand their gaseous envelopes.Prior to James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) observations, spectral range was relatively limited and only combining different data sources, such as Hubble and Spitzer, it was possible to cover a big enough spectral range to reveal the smooth aerosol spectral signatures. For simplicity, most of the previous studies assumed flat or Rayleigh-like cloud opacities. Figure 1 allows checking that these simplifications were valid enough for the spectral ranges covered by the Hubble Space Telescope (HST). Nevertheless, recent JWST/NIRSpec data with its wider spectral range are a game changer allowing disentanglement of gaseous absorptions and cloud extinctions.Figure 1: Simulated extinction for spherical aerosols of different radius distributions all over the complete spectral range of JWST/NIRSpec-PRISM. HST/STIS, HST/WFC3 and Spitzer/IRAC spectral ranges are delimited by grey background for comparisons. Note how some combinations of sizes and instruments would be indistinguishable from a flat extinction curve.In this work, we have studied the cloud properties of planet WASP-39b using the spectra of the primary transit obtained with JWST/NIRSpec (Rustamkulov et al., 2023). To do so, we have implemented a nested-sampling Bayesian approach, which has become popular for the atmospheric retrievals during the last decade (Benneke &amp; Seager, 2012; Fisher &amp; Heng, 2018). This framework does not just allow fitting the parameters of a certain model, but it also allows selecting which is the best model for fitting the data observed among a set, each one with a variety of atmospheric parameters and assumptions through Bayesian evidence.We tested a number of vertical distribution parameterisations found in the literature (Barstow, 2020). Following the Occam's razor reasoning, for Bayesian evidences being equal, we favor models with the lesser number of free parameters. In our study, we find that a model with a semi-infinite bottom cloud and a clear atmosphere above is the best option, while there are many similarities between the retrieved parameters for all cases (e.g., similar cloud top pressures).Then, we have studied the effect that the aerosol extinction shape has on the observed spectra. We again tested a number of extinction models and compared their Bayesian evidences. In this case, in spite of all of them achieving good fits (see figure 2), realistic simulations of a complex cloud extinction dependence with wavelength are preferred versus models assuming simpler wavelength dependencies. Extinction produced by big sized aerosols with an opacity growing with wavelength fits better the observed data and it seems to be a robust conclusion from different competing models.Figure 2: Best fitted spectra for some cloud extinction models. Observational data are shown as black/grey dots with error bars.These results also have an impact on the retrieved molecular abundances (see figure 3) suggesting possible overestimations of some chemical species or even calling into question the detection of some of them, as proposed in Lueber et al., 2024.Figure 3: Values retrieved for some atmospheric parameters when using different models relative to a flat model retrieval. [4] JWST透射光谱揭示的WASP-39b云特性：贝叶斯分析

几乎所有已探测到的热木星在其大气中都存在云和薄雾。这些气溶胶集合在初级凌日过程中起着重要作用，阻挡了穿过系外行星外层大气的恒星通量，因此对透射行星光谱有重要影响。透射光谱对系外行星大气中云的存在非常敏感（Fortney, 2005）。根据粒径不同，气溶胶会在观测光谱上留下平滑的影响。有时，云的不透明度会掩盖本应在信号中留下特征的不同分子的光谱特征。当云变得光学厚时，它们会阻挡所有本应穿过其下方层级的通量，类似于固体表面（Benneke & Seager, 2012）。因此，深入理解系外行星中气溶胶的性质对于理解其气体包层至关重要。在JWST观测之前，光谱范围相对有限，只有结合不同数据源（如Hubble和Spitzer），才能覆盖足够宽的光谱范围以揭示气溶胶的平滑光谱特征。为简化处理，大多数先前研究假设云的不透明度为平坦或类似瑞利散射。图1显示这些简化对于Hubble Space Telescope (HST)所覆盖的光谱范围来说足够有效。然而，最近JWST/NIRSpec的数据由于其更宽的光谱范围成为了改变游戏规则的工具，使得气体吸收和云消光得以区分。图1：在JWST/NIRSpec-PRISM完整光谱范围内，不同半径分布的球形气溶胶的模拟消光。HST/STIS、HST/WFC3和Spitzer/IRAC的光谱范围用灰色背景标示以便比较。注意某些粒径和仪器组合与平坦消光曲线无法区分。在本工作中，我们利用JWST/NIRSpec获得的WASP-39b初级凌日光谱研究了该行星的云性质（Rustamkulov等，2023）。为此，我们实现了嵌套采样贝叶斯方法，该方法在过去十年中已成为大气检索的主流（Benneke & Seager, 2012；Fisher & Heng, 2018）。该框架不仅允许拟合特定模型的参数，还可以通过贝叶斯证据在一组具有不同大气参数和假设的模型中选择最优模型。我们测试了文献中提出的多种垂直分布参数化方法（Barstow, 2020）。遵循奥卡姆剃刀原则，在贝叶斯证据相等时，我们倾向于选择自由参数更少的模型。在我们的研究中，我们发现底部为半无限云、上方为清晰大气的模型是最佳选择，尽管所有情形下检索参数（如云顶压强）都非常相似。随后，我们研究了气溶胶消光曲线形状对观测光谱的影响。我们再次测试了多种消光模型并比较其贝叶斯证据。在本例中，尽管所有模型都能很好地拟合数据（见图2），但与假设简单波长依赖性的模型相比，复杂云消光与波长相关的现实模拟更受青睐。由大粒径气溶胶产生的不透明度随波长增加的消光更好地拟合了观测数据，这一结论在不同竞争模型中都很稳健。图2：部分云消光模型的最佳拟合光谱。观测数据以黑色/灰色点及误差棒表示。这些结果也影响了分子丰度的检索（见图3），提示某些化学物种可能被高估，甚至质疑部分物种的探测，如Lueber等（2024）所提出。图3：采用不同模型相对于平坦模型检索时部分大气参数的取值。参考文献Barstow, J. [5] WASP-127b: a misaligned planet with a partly cloudy atmosphereand tenuous sodium signature seen by ESPRESSO

2021-09-00

During the past few years, exoplanet atmospheric studies have grown tremendously. Detections of atomic and molecular species are reported from the ultraviolet to the infrared at low- and high-resolution for Earth-mass to Jupiter-mass planets and from temperate to ultra-hot worlds. Nowadays, ground-based high-resolution spectrographs are starting to produce groundbreaking results that inform us of the temperature structure and dynamics of exoplanet atmospheres. A reference system for atmospheric studies: WASP-127bWASP-127b (Lam et al. 2017) orbits a bright (V = 10.17) G5-type star with a period of 4.18 days. The host star is at the end of its main sequence phase with an age of ~10Gyr and radius of 1.30RS and has entered the sub-giant phase. Therefore, the planet might be going through an inflation process for the second time (Lopez &amp; Fortney 2016), leading to its large radius (~1.31RJ). WASP-127b is a lukewarm (~600 times Earth irradiation) Saturn at the right mass border of the evaporation desert. Assuming a hydrogen-helium composition, its atmospheric scale height is about 2100 km. In this case, the signal in transmission for one scale height is around 420 ppm, potentially making this planet one of the best among those of its class to study exo-atmospheres through transmission spectroscopy.Near-infrared low-resolution data obtained with the Hubble Space Telescope (Spake et al. 2020) have revealed the strongest water band amplitude in an exoplanet with a mean amplitude of about 800ppm. Moreover, the presence of clouds and hazes is necessary to explain the complete spectrum. Such planets with a high-amplitude water signature in the J-band are well placed to be studied from the ground with visible and NIR spectrographs, allowing the impact of hazes and clouds to be analyzed by measuring the water content at different wavelengths. Observations of WASP-127b with ESPRESSOESPRESSO is a fiber-fed, ultra-stabilized high-resolution echelle spectrograph installed at Paranal. It can collect the light from each 8m Unit Telescope (UT) individually or the 4UTs simultaneously of the Very Large Telescope (VLT). Two transits of WASP-127b were obtained within the framework of the GTO consortium. We published our results in Allart et al. 2020, which includes the first measurement of spin-orbit alignment of this system revealing the peculiar orbital architecture of the system. The old star WASP-127 is a slow rotator while its planet has a misaligned retrograde orbit (Fig. 2). This is surprising as stars with Teff below 6250K are supposed to have aligned systems. An explanation could be that WASP-127b remained trapped in a Kozai resonance with an outer companion and only recently migrated close to its star.A view on the atmosphere of WASP-127bWe used the ESPRESSO data to analyze the transmission spectrum of the exoplanet. Visible high-resolution datasets are known to reveal the exoplanet thermosphere through atomic species as the well-known sodium doublet. The WASP-127b transit datasets have revealed an excess of absorption of 0.34 +/- 0.04% at the expected position of the sodium doublet corresponding to a small extension over 7 scale heights. However, we do not detect the presence of other atomic species but we were able to set upper limits of only a few scale heights.We also undertake a thorough search for water vapor at visible wavelengths. To do so, we used the well-known cross-correlation function technique to average thousands of water lines in velocity space. To be as model-independent as possible, we applied binary masks containing the position of water lines at different temperatures and with an increasing number of lines. We showcased that such a technique is sensitive enough to retrieve planetary signals by doing an injection recovery test. Once applied to the transit datasets of WASP-127b, we were able to put a 3-sigma upper limit on the presence of water vapor at visible wavelengths of only 38ppm on the average depth of the 1600 strongest water lines at the equilibrium temperature. This upper limit corresponds to an atmospheric extension of only 0.08 scale height. Combining low- and high-resolution spectroscopyBased on this unique precision obtained with ESPRESSO, we explored the compatibility between ground-based high-resolution spectroscopy and space-based low-resolution spectroscopy. We computed a grid of models with different grey cloud-deck pressure based on the results of Spake et al. 2020 and using the pi-eta line-by-line radiative transfer code (Pino et al 2018). On one hand, models with grey cloud deck pressure ranging from 0.3 to 1 mbar are compatible with the HST datasets, while in another hand only models with pressure below 0.5 mbar are compatible with the ESPRESSO datasets. Therefore, by combining the low- and high-resolution datasets of WASP-127b, we can constrain the grey cloud pressure between 0.3 and 0.5 mbar.ConclusionWe used two transits of WASP-127b obtained with ESPRESSO to acquire a thorough understanding of the orbital architecture of the system and the exoplanet atmosphere.We propose a new framework to search for water vapor and other molecular species at high resolution. Despite the good data quality, we did not detect water vapor. However, we combined this result with a low-resolution detection of water at 1.3 microns to constrain the presence of clouds in the atmosphere of WASP-127b.To conclude, we report for the first time that high-resolution visible data can be used to differentiate between cloudy and cloud-free exoplanets by measuring the water content and can also provide essential information on the cloud-deck pressure. The framework developed here to measure this water content will be applied to other exoplanets in the ESPRESSO GTO atmospheric survey and other surveys such as the NIRPS GTO atmospheric survey. R., et al. 2020, MNRAS

[5] WASP-127b：一颗轨道倾斜、部分多云且通过 ESPRESSO 观测到微弱钠特征的行星

在过去几年中，系外行星大气研究取得了巨大进展。从紫外到红外、从低分辨率到高分辨率、从地球质量到木星质量、从温和到超热的行星，原子和分子物种的探测不断被报道。如今，地基高分辨率光谱仪开始产出突破性成果，为我们揭示了系外行星大气的温度结构和动力学。大气研究的参考系统：WASP-127bWASP-127b（Lam et al. 2017）围绕一颗亮（V = 10.17）的G5型恒星公转，周期为4.18天。该母星正处于主序末期，年龄约10 Gyr，半径为1.30RS，已进入次巨星阶段。因此，该行星可能正经历第二次膨胀过程（Lopez & Fortney 2016），导致其半径较大（约1.31RJ）。WASP-127b是一颗温暖（约为地球照射的600倍）的土星，位于蒸发荒漠的质量边界。假设其为氢-氦组成，大气尺度高度约为2100 km。在这种情况下，一个尺度高度的透射信号约为420 ppm，使其成为同类行星中通过透射光谱研究系外大气的最佳目标之一。哈勃空间望远镜获得的近红外低分辨率数据（Spake et al. 2020）揭示了系外行星中最强的水带幅度，平均幅度约为800 ppm。此外，解释完整光谱需要考虑云和薄雾的存在。此类在J波段具有高幅度水特征的行星，非常适合用地面可见光和近红外光谱仪进行研究，通过在不同波长测量水含量，分析薄雾和云的影响。使用ESPRESSO观测WASP-127bESPRESSO是一台光纤馈送、超稳定的高分辨率echelle光谱仪，安装在Paranal，可单独收集每个8m单元望远镜（UT）的光，或同时收集甚大望远镜（VLT）4个UT的光。我们在GTO联盟框架下获得了WASP-127b的两次凌日观测。我们在Allart et al. 2020中发表了结果，包括首次测量该系统的自转-轨道对齐，揭示了该系统特殊的轨道结构。年老的母星WASP-127自转缓慢，而其行星轨道为失调的逆行轨道（见图2）。这令人惊讶，因为Teff低于6250K的恒星系统通常应为对齐系统。一种解释可能是WASP-127b曾被外伴星困在Kozai共振中，最近才迁移到靠近母星的位置。WASP-127b大气的观测我们利用ESPRESSO数据分析了该系外行星的透射光谱。可见光高分辨率数据集已知可通过原子物种（如著名的钠双线）揭示系外行星热层。WASP-127b凌日数据集在钠双线预期位置显示出0.34 +/- 0.04%的吸收过量，对应于7个尺度高度的微小扩展。然而，我们未探测到其他原子物种，但能够给出仅为几个尺度高度的上限。我们还对可见波段的水蒸气进行了全面搜索。为此，我们采用了著名的互相关函数技术，在速度空间平均数千条水线。为尽量减少模型依赖性，我们应用了包含不同温度和不同数量水线位置的二进制掩模。我们通过注入-恢复测试展示了该技术对行星信号的敏感性。应用于WASP-127b的凌日数据后，我们对平衡温度下1600条最强水线的平均深度给出了仅38 ppm的3-sigma上限。该上限对应于仅0.08个尺度高度的大气扩展。结合低分辨率和高分辨率光谱基于ESPRESSO获得的独特精度，我们探索了地基高分辨率光谱与空间低分辨率光谱之间的兼容性。我们基于Spake et al. 2020的结果，利用pi-eta逐线辐射传输代码（Pino et al 2018），计算了不同灰云层压力的模型。一方面，灰云层压力在0.3到1 mbar范围的模型与HST数据集兼容，另一方面，只有压力低于0.5 mbar的模型与ESPRESSO数据集兼容。因此，通过结合WASP-127b的低分辨率和高分辨率数据集，我们可以将灰云层压力约束在0.3到0.5 mbar之间。结论我们利用ESPRESSO获得的两次WASP-127b凌日观测，深入理解了该系统的轨道结构和系外行星大气。我们提出了一个新的高分辨率水蒸气及其他分子物种搜索框架。尽管数据质量良好，我们未探测到水蒸气。然而，我们将该结果与1.3微米低分辨率水探测结果结合，约束了WASP-127b大气中云的存在。总之，我们首次报告高分辨率可见光数据可通过测量水含量区分有云和无云系外行星，并可提供关于云层压力的重要信息。此处开发的水含量测量框架将应用于ESPRESSO GTO大气巡天及NIRPS GTO大气巡天等其他巡天的系外行星。参考文献Allart R., Pino, L., Lovis, C. 等, 2020, A&A, 644, A155Pino, L., Ehrenreich, D., Wyttenbach, A., 等. 2018, A&A, 612, A53Spake, J. [6] On the Compatibility of Ground-based and Space-based Data: WASP-96 b, an Example

2021-01-00

The study of exoplanetary atmospheres relies on detecting minute changes in the transit depth at different wavelengths. To date, a number of ground- and space-based instruments have been used to obtain transmission spectra of exoplanets in different spectral bands. One common practice is to combine observations from different instruments in order to achieve a broader wavelength coverage. We present here two inconsistent observations of WASP-96 b, one by the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and the other by the Very Large Telescope (VLT). We present two key findings in our investigation: (1) a strong water signature is detected via the HST WFC3 observations and (2) a notable offset in transit depth (&gt;1100 ppm) can be seen when the ground-based and space-based observations are combined. The discrepancy raises the question of whether observations from different instruments could indeed be combined. We attempt to align the observations by including an additional parameter in our retrieval studies but are unable to definitively ascertain that the aligned observations are indeed compatible. The case of WASP-96 b signals that compatibility of instruments should not be assumed. While wavelength overlaps between instruments can help, it should be noted that combining data sets remains risky business. The difficulty of combining observations also strengthens the need for next-generation instruments that possess broader spectral coverage. <SUP>*</SUP> These authors contributed equally to this work.

[6] 地基与空间数据的兼容性：以 WASP-96 b 为例

系外行星大气的研究依赖于在不同波长下探测凌星深度的微小变化。迄今为止，已经使用多种地面和空间仪器在不同波段获得了系外行星的透射光谱。一种常见做法是将不同仪器的观测结果结合，以获得更宽的波长覆盖范围。我们在此报告了WASP-96 b的两次不一致观测，一次来自哈勃空间望远镜（HST），另一次来自甚大望远镜（VLT）。我们的研究有两个主要发现：（1）通过HST WFC3观测检测到强烈的水吸收特征；（2）当将地面和空间观测结合时，凌星深度出现了显著偏移（>1100 ppm）。这一差异引发了一个问题：不同仪器的观测结果是否真的可以合并。我们尝试在检索研究中引入一个额外参数以对齐观测，但无法明确确定对齐后的观测确实兼容。WASP-96 b的案例表明，不应假定仪器之间具有兼容性。虽然仪器之间的波长重叠有帮助，但需要注意的是，合并数据集仍然存在风险。合并观测的难度也进一步凸显了需要下一代具有更宽光谱覆盖范围的仪器。*这些作者对本工作贡献相同。

[7] Relative abundance constraints from high-resolution optical transmission spectroscopy of WASP-121b, and a fast model-filtering technique for accelerating retrievals

2022-05-00

High-resolution Doppler-resolved spectroscopy has presented new opportunities for studying the atmospheres of exoplanets. While the 'classical' cross-correlation approach has proven to be efficient at finding atmospheric species, it is unable to perform direct atmospheric retrievals. Recent work has shown that retrievals are possible using a direct likelihood evaluation or likelihood 'mappings'. The unique aspect of high-resolution retrievals is that the data-processing methods required to remove the stellar and telluric lines also distort the underlying exoplanet's signal and therefore the forward model must be pre-processed to match this filtering. This was the key remaining limitation in our previously published framework. This paper directly addresses this by introducing a simple and fast model-filtering technique that can replicate the processing performed by algorithms such as SYSREM and PCA. This enables retrievals to be performed without having to perform expensive injection and pre-processing steps for every model. We show that we can reliably constrain quantitative measures of the atmosphere from transmission spectra including the temperature-pressure profile, relative abundances, planetary velocities, and rotational broadening parameters. Finally, we demonstrate our framework using UVES transmission spectroscopy of WASP-121b. We constrain the temperature-pressure profile and relative abundances of Fe, Cr, and V to be log<SUB>10</SUB>(χ<SUB>Fe</SUB>/χ<SUB>Cr</SUB>) = 1.66 ± 0.28, log<SUB>10</SUB>(χ<SUB>Fe</SUB>/χ<SUB>V</SUB>) = 3.78 ± 0.29, and log<SUB>10</SUB>(χ<SUB>Fe</SUB>/χ<SUB>Mg</SUB>) = -1.26 ± 0.60. The relative abundances are consistent with solar values, with the exception of Fe/Mg, where the large Mg abundance is probably explained by the escaping atmosphere of WASP-121b that is not accounted for in our atmospheric model.

[7] WASP-121b高分辨率光学透射光谱的相对丰度约束及加速反演的快速模型筛选技术

高分辨率多普勒分辨光谱为研究系外行星大气提供了新机遇。传统的互相关方法在寻找大气组分方面效率较高，但无法直接进行大气检索。近期研究表明，通过直接似然评估或似然“映射”可以实现检索。高分辨率检索的独特之处在于，为去除恒星和地面大气谱线所需的数据处理方法也会扭曲系外行星的信号，因此正向模型必须经过预处理以匹配这种滤波。这是我们此前已发表框架中剩余的关键限制。本文通过引入一种简单快速的模型滤波技术，能够复制如SYSREM和PCA等算法的处理过程，直接解决了这一问题。这使得无需对每个模型进行昂贵的注入和预处理步骤即可进行检索。我们展示了可以可靠地从透射光谱中约束大气的定量参数，包括温度-压力剖面、相对丰度、行星速度和自转展宽参数。最后，我们以WASP-121b的UVES透射光谱为例，约束了Fe、Cr和V的温度-压力剖面和相对丰度，分别为log10(χFe/χCr) = 1.66 ± 0.28，log10(χFe/χV) = 3.78 ± 0.29，log10(χFe/χMg) = -1.26 ± 0.60。除Fe/Mg外，相对丰度与太阳值一致，Fe/Mg的偏差很可能由WASP-121b未被模型考虑的大气逃逸导致。

[8] Detection of CO, H$_2$O, and OH in WASP-18b with JWST/NIRISS using Direct-Extracted Spectra and Cross-Correlation

2026-05-00

The James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) has revolutionized the characterization of exoplanetary atmospheres, offering unprecedented sensitivity to probe their chemical and physical properties. Recently, a growing trend has emerged to obtain atmospheric information directly from pixel-level planetary spectra. In this work, we re-analyzed the WASP-18b NIRISS/SOSS dataset by employing a direct extraction method. This new method preserves the spectral information at the native instrumental resolution, thereby enabling the application of cross-correlation techniques and providing atmospheric retrievals with enhanced precision and richer information content. With this methodology, we report detections of CO at $4.4σ$ significance, H$_2$O at $3.4σ$, and OH at $7.8σ$, where CO and OH were previously unseen. Building on these unambiguous detections, our subsequent retrieval analysis significantly improves the constraints on atmospheric abundances. Our results demonstrate that the cross-correlation technique effectively extracts molecular signals from medium-resolution JWST data, enhancing detection sensitivity. By revisiting JWST archival data with cross-correlation and retrieval analysis, we can achieve a more comprehensive survey of planetary atmospheric chemistry, thereby placing precise constraints on key parameters such as planetary metallicity and C/O ratio.

[8] 利用JWST/NIRISS直接提取光谱和互相关方法在WASP-18b中探测到CO、H2O和OH

詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）彻底改变了系外行星大气的特征研究，提供了前所未有的灵敏度以探测其化学和物理性质。近期，越来越多的趋势是直接从像素级行星光谱中获取大气信息。在本工作中，我们采用直接提取方法重新分析了WASP-18b的NIRISS/SOSS数据集。该新方法保留了仪器本征分辨率下的光谱信息，从而能够应用互相关技术，并为大气检索带来更高精度和更丰富的信息量。利用该方法，我们报告了CO（4.4σ）、H2O（3.4σ）和OH（7.8σ）的探测结果，其中CO和OH此前未被探测到。在这些明确探测的基础上，我们的后续检索分析显著提升了对大气丰度的约束。我们的结果表明，互相关技术能够有效从中等分辨率JWST数据中提取分子信号，提高探测灵敏度。通过对JWST档案数据进行互相关和检索分析，我们能够更全面地调查行星大气化学，从而对如行星金属丰度和C/O比等关键参数给出精确约束。

item_018 · sys_A
Analyses [2017AAS...22920206B] and [2019AGUFMSH43A..05G] of PROXIMA B's atmosphere reach potentially conflicting conclusions regarding TiO: one reports "In the infrared, the technique has unambiguously revealed the presence of complex molecules, such as water, in hot Jupiters, as well as m...", while the other reports "Meanwhile, (1) terrestrial atmospheric erosion seems unlikely even for unrealistically intense solar eruptions and (2) the likelihood of ...". Under what observational or methodological conditions can these results be reconciled, and which currently untested assumption drives the disagreement?
PROXIMA B 大气的分析 [2017AAS...22920206B] 和 [2019AGUFMSH43A..05G] 在 TiO 问题上得出可能相互矛盾的结论：一项报告称“在红外波段，该技术已明确揭示了热木星中复杂分子（如水）的存在，以及 m...”，而另一项报告称“与此同时，（1）即使极端强烈的太阳爆发，类地大气侵蚀似乎也不太可能，（2）...的可能性...”。在什么观测或方法条件下这些结果可以调和？目前尚未检验的哪个假设驱动了这种分歧？
8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] A population view of transiting hot giant exoplanets: Tracing Fe and Ti chemistry with ESPRESSO and MAROON-X

2026-06-00

Hot and ultra-hot Jupiters offer a unique laboratory to study atmospheric chemistry at the population level using ground-based high-resolution spectroscopy. Fe and Ti are key tracers of thermal and chemical structure, yet they exhibit different observational trends across the population. We present a homogeneous reanalysis of high-resolution transmission spectra of ten hot and ultra-hot Jupiters observed with VLT/ESPRESSO and Gemini-N/MAROON-X. We search for neutral Fe and Ti absorption and perform injection-recovery tests using models spanning a range of Ti-depletion levels and T-p profiles. For direct comparison across observations, we introduce the relative cross-correlation metric, $∆$Ti-Fe. We detect Fe in 7 and Ti in 4 planets above 5$σ$. Across the population, $∆$Ti-Fe decreases sharply towards lower equilibrium temperatures. Under the assumption of equal Ti depletion across planets, isothermal models fail to reproduce this trend, instead requiring a temperature-dependent depletion of Ti that increases toward cooler planets, consistent with cold-trapping processes in cooler atmospheres. Models with inverted T-p profiles naturally reproduce the decline without invoking temperature-dependent depletion. There, Ti is converted into TiO in deeper, cooler layers and then removed from the gas phase through condensation, leading to strong suppression of the observable atomic Ti signal. Nevertheless, even in the gradient models, overall depletion of Ti relative to Fe is required to match the hottest planets. Our results demonstrate that observable refractory chemistry is governed by the interplay of molecular partitioning, ionisation, condensation, and cold-trapping processes, as well as the vertical structure of ultra-hot Jupiter atmospheres. Additional observations will be necessary to distinguish between temperature-dependent cold-trapping and overall depletion.(abbrev.)

[1] 凌星热类木行星的总体视角：利用ESPRESSO和MAROON-X追踪Fe和Ti化学

热木星和超热木星为利用地基高分辨率光谱在群体层面研究大气化学提供了独特的实验室。Fe 和 Ti 是热结构和化学结构的重要示踪物，但它们在不同行星中表现出不同的观测趋势。我们对用 VLT/ESPRESSO 和 Gemini-N/MAROON-X 观测到的十颗热木星和超热木星的高分辨率透射光谱进行了同质性再分析。我们搜索中性 Fe 和 Ti 的吸收特征，并利用涵盖不同 Ti 枯竭水平和 T-p 剖面的模型进行注入-恢复测试。为便于不同观测之间的直接比较，我们引入了相对互相关指标 $∆$Ti-Fe。我们在 7 颗行星中探测到 Fe，在 4 颗行星中探测到 Ti，显著性均超过 5$σ$。在整个行星群体中，$∆$Ti-Fe 随平衡温度降低而急剧下降。在假设所有行星 Ti 枯竭程度相同的情况下，等温模型无法再现该趋势，而需要 Ti 的温度相关枯竭，即在较冷行星中枯竭程度更高，这与冷捕获过程在较冷大气中起作用一致。具有反转 T-p 剖面的模型无需引入温度相关枯竭即可自然再现该下降趋势。在这些模型中，Ti 在更深、更冷的层中转化为 TiO，并通过凝结从气相中移除，导致可观测的原子 Ti 信号被强烈抑制。然而，即使在具有梯度的模型中，为了匹配最热行星，Ti 相对于 Fe 的整体枯竭仍然是必要的。我们的结果表明，可观测的耐火元素化学受分子分配、电离、凝结和冷捕获过程以及超热木星大气垂直结构的共同影响。未来需要更多观测以区分温度相关冷捕获和整体枯竭两种情形。（摘要）

[2] Chemistry and dynamics from high-resolution emission spectra of the ultra-hot Jupiters KELT-20b and TOI-1518b from the PIRANGA project

2026-02-00

Ultra-Hot Jupiters have extremely hot dayside temperatures, making high-resolution emission spectroscopy an optimal approach to understanding their atmospheres. By detecting and characterizing the chemical species present, we gain insight into the atmospheres' composition and the existence of thermal inversions. Regarding dynamics, different species probe different layers of the atmosphere. Additionally, day-to-nightside winds driven by temperature contrast between the hot, irradiated dayside and the cooler nightside of a tidally locked planet can reveal velocity offsets of a species relative to the expected systematic velocity and radial velocity semi-amplitude of the planet. Any divergence becomes a powerful tool for modeling global circulation. <P />Two planets are explored, KELT-20b and TOI-1518b. Data was taken on PEPSI at the LBT, an optical, high-resolution spectrograph. We applied model planetary emission spectra to the observed spectra for each species, generating cross-correlation functions. <P />In KELT-20b, we detected Fe I, Ca I, and Ni I with high significance (&gt;5σ) and tentatively detected (3.5-5σ) Fe II. For TOI-1518b, we detected Fe I and Ti I and tentatively detected Ca I, Ni I, Cr I, Mg I, Fe II, and TiO. <P />The CCFs were overlaid to see if velocity shifts represent species specific phenomena or large-scale motion. The velocities of the three species detected in KELT-20b are consistent with each other, implying that they all probe the same atmospheric region or there is little vertical velocity structure in the regions probed by these species. For TOI-1518b, TiO is offset from other species, suggesting that the TiO detection may probe a different layer of the planet's atmosphere. This could be explained from cold trapping due to rain-out on the planetary nightside or photodissociation due to ultraviolet radiation of its star. <P />The detection of atomic species, along with the non-detection of many metal oxides, supports previous work that the atomic species themselves act as the dominant optical absorbers and inversion agents. The tentative detection of TiO in TOI-1518b would place this among the coolest planets with such a detection from high-resolution spectroscopy, despite years of effort. We present simple physical models to interpret the offset between TiO and other species.

[2] PIRANGA项目：超热木星KELT-20b和TOI-1518b高分辨率发射光谱中的化学与动力学

超热木星具有极高的日面温度，使高分辨率发射光谱成为理解其大气的最佳手段。通过探测和刻画存在的化学物种，我们可以深入了解大气成分及热反转的存在。动力学方面，不同物种可探测大气的不同层。此外，由于潮汐锁定行星的炽热日面与较冷夜面之间的温差驱动的昼夜面风，会导致某些物种的速度偏移，相对于预期的系统速度和行星的径向速度半振幅。任何偏离都成为模拟全球环流的有力工具。 本研究探讨了两颗行星：KELT-20b 和 TOI-1518b。数据采集自 LBT 上的 PEPSI，一台光学高分辨率光谱仪。我们对每种物种将模型行星发射光谱与观测光谱进行交叉相关。 在 KELT-20b 中，我们以高显著性（>5σ）探测到 Fe I、Ca I 和 Ni I，并以 3.5-5σ 的显著性候选探测到 Fe II。对于 TOI-1518b，我们探测到 Fe I 和 Ti I，并候选探测到 Ca I、Ni I、Cr I、Mg I、Fe II 和 TiO。 我们将 CCF 叠加以判断速度偏移是物种特有现象还是大尺度运动。KELT-20b 中三种探测到的物种速度一致，说明它们探测的是同一大气区域，或这些区域的垂直速度结构较弱。对于 TOI-1518b，TiO 的速度与其他物种有偏移，表明 TiO 探测到的是行星大气的不同层。这可能由夜面冷凝雨出或恒星紫外光致解离造成。 原子物种的探测以及许多金属氧化物的未探测到，支持了此前关于原子物种本身是主要光学吸收体和反转因子的结论。TOI-1518b 中 TiO 的候选探测使其成为通过高分辨率光谱探测到 TiO 的最冷行星之一，尽管此前多年努力未果。我们提出了简单的物理模型来解释 TiO 与其他物种之间的速度偏移。

[3] Titanium oxide and chemical inhomogeneity in the atmosphere of WASP-189 b

2022-06-00

Ultra-hot Jupiters are a class of highly irradiated planets that have fundamentally changed our view of planetary systems. Located in very short orbits around their host stars, ultra-hot Jupiters are subject to extreme conditions with permanently irradiated hot day sides and cooler, permanently dark night sides. The hot temperature regime causes a variety of different effects, such as the thermal dissociation of many molecules, partial thermal ionisation of atoms, and the occurrence of thermal inversions caused by metals and their oxides. <P />The temperature of an atmosphere decreases with increasing altitude unless there is a shortwave absorber that causes a temperature inversion. While this absorber is known to be ozone in Earth's atmosphere, titanium oxide and vanadium oxide are predicted to be shortwave observers in the atmospheres of ultra-hot Jupiters. <P />Using ground-based, high-resolution spectroscopy, we have been able to study the atmosphere of WASP-189 b, an ultra-hot Jupiter with an equilibrium temperature of 2600 K. By applying the cross-correlation technique to five independent observations, we have detected titanium oxide, as well as various different metals, including neutral and singly ionised iron and titanium, as well as chromium, magnesium, vanadium and manganese. Our detections reveal the presence of dynamical effects that show up in deviations from the true orbital and systemic velocities. We interpret these as a consequence of spatial gradients in their chemical abundances, suggesting their existence in different regions or dynamical regimes. <P />Our findings are direct observational evidence for the 3D thermochemical stratification of an exoplanet atmosphere derived from high-resolution ground-based spectroscopy. As more and more ultra-hot Jupiters are observed, our observations show empirically that successful interpretation of observations of this type of planet requires that the theory of exoplanet atmospheres accounts for the 3D nature of these atmospheres and that insights from global circulation models, atmospheric chemistry and radiative transfer are unified.

[3] WASP-189 b大气中的氧化钛及化学不均匀性

超热木星是一类受到极强辐照的行星，已经从根本上改变了我们对行星系统的认识。它们位于母星极短周期轨道上，经历极端环境，白昼面永久高温，夜晚面则持续低温。高温环境导致多种效应，如多种分子的热解离、原子的部分热电离，以及由金属及其氧化物引起的热反转现象。<P />大气温度通常随高度增加而降低，除非存在短波吸收体导致温度反转。在地球大气中，这种吸收体是臭氧，而在超热木星大气中，理论预测钛氧化物和钒氧化物为短波吸收体。<P />我们利用地基高分辨率光谱，对平衡温度为 2600 K 的超热木星 WASP-189 b 的大气进行了研究。通过对五次独立观测应用交叉相关技术，我们探测到了钛氧化物，以及多种金属，包括中性和一次电离的铁和钛，以及铬、镁、钒和锰。我们的探测揭示了动力学效应，这些效应表现为偏离真实轨道和系统速度的现象。我们将其解释为化学丰度的空间梯度所致，表明这些元素存在于不同区域或动力学环境中。<P />我们的发现是基于地基高分辨率光谱的系外行星大气三维热化学分层的直接观测证据。随着越来越多的超热木星被观测，我们的观测经验性地表明，成功解释此类行星的观测结果需要系外行星大气理论能够考虑这些大气的三维特性，并将全球环流模型、大气化学和辐射传输的见解统一起来。

[4] Titanium oxide and chemical inhomogeneity in the atmosphere of WASP-189 b

2022-07-00

Ultra-hot Jupiters are a class of highly irradiated planets that have fundamentally changed our view of planetary systems. Located in very short orbits around their host stars, ultra-hot Jupiters are exposed to extreme conditions that cause the planet to be divided into a permanently irradiated hot day side and a cooler, permanently dark night side. The hot temperature regime leads to a variety of effects such as the thermal dissociation of many molecules, the partial thermal ionisation of atoms and the presence of thermal inversions by metals and their oxides. <P />An atmosphere's temperature decreases with increasing altitude unless there exists a shortwave absorber that causes a temperature inversion. This absorber is known to be ozone in the Earth's atmosphere, whereas titanium oxide and vanadium oxide are predicted to be short-wave absorbers in the atmospheres of ultra-hot Jupiters. <P />With ground-based, high-resolution spectroscopy, we were able to study the atmosphere of WASP-189 b, an ultra-hot Jupiter with an equilibrium temperature of 2600 K. By applying the cross-correlation technique to five independent observations, we were able to detect titanium oxide as well as various metals, including both neutral and singly ionised iron and titanium, as well as chromium, magnesium, vanadium and manganese. Our detections show the presence of dynamical effects that manifest themselves in deviations from the true orbital and systemic velocities. We interpret these as resulting from gradients in their chemical abundances, suggesting their existence in different regions or dynamical regimes. <P />Our results are direct observational evidence for the three-dimensional thermochemical stratification of an exoplanet's atmosphere, derived from high-resolution ground-based spectroscopy. With more and more ultra-hot Jupiters being observed, our observations empirically show that a successful interpretation of observations of this kind of planet demands that the theory of exoplanet atmospheres considers the three-dimensional nature of these atmospheres and that insights from global circulation models, atmospheric chemistry, and radiative transfer are combined.

[4] WASP-189 b大气中的氧化钛及化学不均匀性

超热木星是一类受到强烈辐射的行星，极大地改变了我们对行星系统的认识。它们位于母星极短周期轨道上，暴露于极端环境，使行星被分为永久受照射的高温昼面和较冷、永久黑暗的夜面。高温环境导致多种效应，如多种分子的热解离、原子的部分热电离，以及金属及其氧化物引起的温度反转。 大气温度通常随高度增加而降低，除非存在短波吸收体导致温度反转。在地球大气中，这种吸收体为臭氧，而在超热木星大气中，理论预测钛氧化物和钒氧化物为短波吸收体。 通过地基高分辨率光谱，我们研究了超热木星WASP-189 b（平衡温度为2600 K）的大气。利用交叉相关技术对五次独立观测进行分析，我们探测到了钛氧化物以及多种金属，包括中性和一次电离的铁和钛，以及铬、镁、钒和锰。我们的探测结果显示出动力学效应，这些效应表现为偏离真实轨道和系统速度的现象。我们将其解释为化学丰度梯度的结果，表明这些元素存在于不同区域或动力学状态。 我们的结果是基于地基高分辨率光谱的系外行星大气三维热化学分层的直接观测证据。随着越来越多的超热木星被观测到，我们的观测经验性地表明，成功解释此类行星的观测结果需要系外行星大气理论考虑其三维特性，并结合全球环流模型、大气化学和辐射传输的见解。

[5] Detection of OH and Fe in the dayside atmosphere of the hottest ultra-hot Jupiter KELT-9b with SPIRou

2025-10-00

Simultaneous abundance measurements of volatile and refractory elements are crucial to unravelling the formation and migration history of ultra-hot Jupiters (UHJs). High-resolution infrared emission spectroscopy has recently been employed extensively to investigate the atmospheric components of UHJs, including both molecules and atoms. For the hottest known planet, KELT-9b, whose dayside atmosphere is almost completely thermally dissociated and ionized, no molecular components have been conclusively detected. Here, we present the first detection of the OH molecule in the dayside atmosphere of KELT-9b, based on two thermal emission observations conducted with the SPIRou spectrograph, and confirm the presence of Fe in the dayside hemisphere. We performed a self-consistent retrieval under the assumption of chemical equilibrium, constraining elemental abundances and atmospheric metallicity ([M/H]). We confirm the presence of a significant thermal inversion layer on the dayside. By retrieval, no significant net Doppler shift signals are identified, and the retrieved equatorial rotation speed agrees with the tidally locked rotation speed. The retrieved oxygen abundance is solar to supersolar (0.61<SUB>−0.58</SUB><SUB>+1.19</SUB> dex). The retrieval suggests a subsolar to solar [C/O] (−0.75<SUB>−0.82</SUB><SUP>+0.64</SUP> dex) and a subsolar to solar atmospheric metallicity. The low metallicity may point to a locally well-mixed envelope and interior. The constraints remain broad, and the data are still statistically consistent with supersolar C/O and subsolar oxygen abundances. Taken together, the [C/O] and [O/H] results are compatible with formation beyond the water snowline followed by inward migration, but the present data do not conclusively rule out other scenarios. The volatile-to-refractory ratios, [O/Fe] = 1.25<SUB>−0.74</SUB><SUP>+0.99</SUP> dex and [C/Fe] = 0.60<SUB>−0.74</SUB><SUP>+0.62</SUP> dex, fall within the solar to supersolar range. However, their large dispersions mean they can only provide tentative indications of volatile enrichment. Overall, the statistical significance of these constraints remains limited, making firm conclusions about the planet's formation history premature. In the future, the combination of higher-quality high-resolution optical-to-infrared observations and JWST data will enable more precise constraints on elemental abundances, providing more reliable insights into the formation and migration scenarios of UHJs. Finally, we advocate a retrieval-guided cross-correlation strategy to mitigate the risk of overlooking marginal species, exemplified by the tentative inference of CO in this study.

[5] 利用SPIRou在最热超热木星KELT-9b日面大气中探测到OH和Fe

同时测量挥发性和耐火元素的丰度对于揭示超热木星（UHJ）的形成和迁移历史至关重要。近年来，高分辨率红外发射光谱被广泛用于研究UHJ的大气成分，包括分子和原子。对于已知最热的行星KELT-9b，其日面大气几乎完全热解离和电离，目前尚未有分子成分被确证探测到。本文基于使用SPIRou光谱仪进行的两次热发射观测，首次在KELT-9b日面大气中探测到OH分子，并确认了Fe在日面半球的存在。我们在化学平衡假设下进行了自洽检索，约束了元素丰度和大气金属丰度（[M/H]）。我们确认了日面存在显著的热反转层。检索结果未发现显著的净多普勒移动信号，所检索到的赤道自转速度与潮汐锁定自转速度一致。检索得到的氧丰度为太阳到超太阳（0.61<sub>−0.58</sub><sub>+1.19</sub> dex）。检索结果显示[C/O]为亚太阳到太阳（−0.75<sub>−0.82</sub><sup>+0.64</sup> dex），大气金属丰度也为亚太阳到太阳。较低的金属丰度可能指示包层和内部局部混合良好。约束仍然较宽，数据在统计上仍与超太阳C/O和亚太阳氧丰度相容。综合[C/O]和[O/H]结果，支持在水雪线之外形成后向内迁移的情景，但现有数据尚不能排除其他方案。挥发性与耐火元素的比值[O/Fe]=1.25<sub>−0.74</sub><sup>+0.99</sup> dex和[C/Fe]=0.60<sub>−0.74</sub><sup>+0.62</sup> dex，落在太阳到超太阳范围内。然而，其较大的离散度意味着目前只能对挥发性元素富集提供初步指示。总体而言，这些约束的统计显著性有限，尚难对行星形成历史作出明确结论。未来，结合更高质量的高分辨率光学-红外观测和JWST数据，将能更精确地约束元素丰度，为UHJ的形成和迁移情景提供更可靠的见解。最后，我们建议采用检索引导的互相关策略，以降低遗漏边缘分子的风险，本研究中对CO的初步推断即为一例。

[6] Low abundances of TiO and VO on the dayside of KELT-9 b: Insights from ground-based photometric observations

2024-10-00

We present ground-based photometric observations of secondary eclipses of the hottest known planet KELT-9 b using MuSCAT2 and Sinistro. We detect secondary eclipse signals in i and <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0001" notation="LaTeX">$z_{\rm s}$</tex-math></inline-formula> with eclipse depths of <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0002" notation="LaTeX">$373^{+74}_{-75}$</tex-math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0003" notation="LaTeX">$638^{+199}_{-178}$</tex-math></inline-formula> parts per million, respectively. We perform an atmospheric retrieval on the emission spectrum combined with the data from HST/WFC3, Spitzer, TESS, and CHEOPS to obtain the temperature profile and chemical abundances, including TiO and VO, which have been thought to produce temperature inversion structures in the dayside of ultra-hot Jupiters. While we confirm a strong temperature inversion structure, we find low abundances of TiO and VO with mixing ratios of <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0004" notation="LaTeX">$\rm {log(TiO)}=-7.80^{+0.15}_{-0.30}$</tex-math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0005" notation="LaTeX">$\rm {log(VO)}=-9.60^{+0.64}_{-0.57}$</tex-math></inline-formula>, respectively. The low abundances of TiO and VO are consistent with theoretical predictions for such an ultra-hot atmosphere. In such low abundances, TiO and VO have little effect on the temperature structure of the atmosphere. The abundance of <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0006" notation="LaTeX">${\rm e}^{-}$</tex-math></inline-formula>, which serves as a proxy for <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0007" notation="LaTeX">${\rm H}^{-}$</tex-math></inline-formula> ions in this study, is found to be high, with <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0008" notation="LaTeX">$\rm {log(e^-)}=-4.89\pm {0.06}$</tex-math></inline-formula>. These results indicate that the temperature inversion in KELT-9 b's dayside atmosphere is likely not caused by <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0009" notation="LaTeX">${\rm TiO/VO}$</tex-math></inline-formula>, but rather by the significant abundance of <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0010" notation="LaTeX">${\rm H}^{-}$</tex-math></inline-formula> ions. The best-fitting model cannot fully explain the observed spectrum, and chemical species not included in the retrieval may introduce modeling biases. Future observations with broader wavelength coverage and higher spectral resolution are expected to provide more accurate diagnostics on the presence and abundances of <inline-formula><tex-math id="TM0011" notation="LaTeX">${\rm TiO/VO}$</tex-math></inline-formula>. These advanced observations will overcome the limitations of current data from HST and photometric facilities, which are constrained by narrow wavelength coverage and instrumental systematics.

[6] KELT-9 b日面TiO和VO丰度低：来自地面光度观测的启示

我们利用 MuSCAT2 和 Sinistro 对已知最热行星 KELT-9 b 进行了地面光度法次生凌星观测。在 i 和 z_s 波段分别探测到次生凌星信号，凌星深度分别为 373^{+74}_{-75} 和 638^{+199}_{-178} ppm。我们结合 HST/WFC3、Spitzer、TESS 和 CHEOPS 的数据，对发射光谱进行了大气反演，获得了温度剖面和化学丰度，包括 TiO 和 VO，这两种分子被认为可在超热木星日面产生温度反转结构。我们确认了强烈的温度反转结构，但发现 TiO 和 VO 的丰度较低，混合比为 log(TiO) = -7.80^{+0.15}_{-0.30} 和 log(VO) = -9.60^{+0.64}_{-0.57}。如此低的 TiO 和 VO 丰度与理论对超热大气的预测一致。在这种丰度下，TiO 和 VO 对大气温度结构影响很小。作为 H^- 离子代理的 e^- 丰度较高，log(e^-) = -4.89 ± 0.06。结果表明，KELT-9 b 日面大气的温度反转很可能不是由 TiO/VO 引起，而是由 H^- 离子的高丰度导致。最佳拟合模型无法完全解释观测光谱，未纳入反演的化学物种可能引入建模偏差。未来更宽波段和更高光谱分辨率的观测有望更准确诊断 TiO/VO 的存在及丰度。这些先进观测将克服 HST 和现有光度设施受限于波段覆盖和仪器系统误差的不足。

[7] Titanium oxide and chemical inhomogeneity in the atmosphere of the exoplanet WASP-189 b

2022-01-00

The temperature of an atmosphere decreases with increasing altitude, unless a shortwave absorber that causes a temperature inversion exists<SUP>1</SUP>. Ozone plays this role in the Earth's atmosphere. In the atmospheres of highly irradiated exoplanets, the shortwave absorbers are predicted to be titanium oxide (TiO) and vanadium oxide (VO)<SUP>2</SUP>. Detections of TiO and VO have been claimed using both low-<SUP>3-6</SUP> and high-<SUP>7</SUP> spectral-resolution observations, but subsequent observations have failed to confirm these claims<SUP>8-10</SUP> or overturned them<SUP>11-13</SUP>. Here we report the unambiguous detection of TiO in the ultra-hot Jupiter WASP-189 b<SUP>14</SUP> using high-resolution transmission spectroscopy. This detection is based on applying the cross-correlation technique<SUP>15</SUP> to many spectral lines of TiO from 460 to 690 nm. Moreover, we report detections of metals, including neutral and singly ionized iron and titanium, as well as chromium, magnesium, vanadium and manganese (Fe, Fe<SUP>+</SUP>, Ti, Ti<SUP>+</SUP>, Cr, Mg, V, Mn). The line positions of the detected species differ, which we interpret as a consequence of spatial gradients in their chemical abundances, such that they exist in different regions or dynamical regimes. This is direct observational evidence for the three-dimensional thermochemical stratification of an exoplanet atmosphere derived from high-resolution ground-based spectroscopy.

[7] 系外行星WASP-189 b大气中的氧化钛及化学不均匀性

大气的温度通常随高度增加而降低，除非存在导致温度反转的短波吸收体<SUP>1</SUP>。在地球大气中，臭氧起到了这一作用。在高照射的系外行星大气中，理论预测短波吸收体为TiO和VO<SUP>2</SUP>。此前有研究声称在低<SUP>3-6</SUP>和高<SUP>7</SUP>光谱分辨率观测中探测到TiO和VO，但后续观测未能证实这些结果<SUP>8-10</SUP>，甚至推翻了这些结论<SUP>11-13</SUP>。本研究报告了在超热木星WASP-189 b<SUP>14</SUP>中利用高分辨率透射光谱无歧义地探测到TiO。该探测基于对460至690 nm范围内大量TiO光谱线应用互相关技术<SUP>15</SUP>。此外，我们还报告了金属元素的探测，包括中性和一次电离的铁和钛，以及铬、镁、钒和锰（Fe, Fe<SUP>+</SUP>, Ti, Ti<SUP>+</SUP>, Cr, Mg, V, Mn）。所探测到的各物种谱线位置不同，我们将其解释为空间上化学丰度梯度的结果，即这些物种存在于不同区域或动力学状态。这是基于高分辨率地基光谱直接观测到的系外行星大气三维热化学分层的证据。

[8] Peering into Alien Skies: Building the Future of Exoplanet Atmospheric Studies

2025-12-00

The characterisation of exoplanet atmospheres has entered a transformative era, driven by breakthroughs in both space- and ground-based observations. Among these, high-resolution spectroscopy has emerged as a powerful technique—enabling the unambiguous detection of atomic and molecular species, the measurement of wind patterns, and the investigation of chemical disequilibrium in some of the most extreme planetary environments known. In this article, I summarise our contributions to the study of ultra-hot Jupiters(UHJs)—gas giants with dayside temperatures exceeding 2500 K—using high-dispersion spectrographs on the Subaru Telescope and other facilities. Our work has revealed key signatures of thermal and chemical processes at play in UHJ atmospheres, including the first detections of titanium oxide(TiO),iron(Fe I)and hydroxyl radical(OH)in emission from WASP-33b, and atomic features of Fe I and Ca II in KELT-20b.These results offer new constraints on temperature inversions, atmospheric circulation, and the energy budget of UHJs, and demonstrate the power of high-resolution spectroscopy to probe atmospheric processes in detail. With the advent of next-generation instruments on Subaru and upcoming ELTs, these efforts will lay the groundwork for comparative exoplanetology and a deeper understanding of climate, chemistry, and formation histories across a diverse range of worlds.

[8] 窥探异星天空：构建系外行星大气研究的未来

系外行星大气的特性研究已进入变革性时代，这得益于空间和地面观测的突破。其中，高分辨率光谱已成为一项强有力的技术——能够明确探测原子和分子物种、测量风场，并研究已知最极端行星环境中的化学非平衡。本文总结了我们利用Subaru望远镜及其他设施上的高分辨率光谱仪对超热木星（UHJ，日面温度超过2500 K）研究的贡献。我们的工作揭示了UHJ大气中热力和化学过程的关键特征，包括首次在WASP-33b中探测到TiO、Fe I和OH的发射，以及在KELT-20b中探测到Fe I和Ca II的原子特征。这些结果为UHJ的大气温度反转、大气环流和能量收支提供了新的约束，并展示了高分辨率光谱在细致探测大气过程中的能力。随着Subaru和即将到来的ELT等下一代仪器的出现，这些努力将为比较系外行星学以及对多样化世界的气候、化学和形成历史的深入理解奠定基础。
