8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] A JWST Optical and Infrared Phase Curve of NGTS-10b

2024-02-00

Since the first photometric phase curve observation from Spitzer, hot Jupiter atmospheres have challenged our understanding of atmospheric dynamics. Large studies have shown that many optical phase curves exhibit westward offsets, while most infrared phase curves have eastward offsets. The former is attributed to the enhanced formation of reflective clouds near the morning terminator, whereas the latter is connected primarily to equatorial jets redistributing heat from the hot dayside to the cooler nightside. This inverse correlation between the cloud and temperature maps can provide strong insights into cloud formation processes in hot Jupiters. We will present results from a full-orbit phase curve of NGTS-10b, measured simultaneously at optical and near-IR wavelengths using the unique capabilities of NIRSpec/PRISM. This includes showing the planet's wavelength-dependent hotspot offsets and heat redistribution efficiencies, phase-resolved emission spectra, and transmission spectrum. We will also discuss how our isobaric map of NGTS-10b informs current theoretical models and its implications on future JWST phase curve observations.

[1] NGTS-10b的JWST光学和红外相位曲线

自Spitzer首次获得光度相位曲线观测以来，热木星大气一直挑战着我们对大气动力学的理解。大量研究表明，许多光学相位曲线表现出西向偏移，而大多数红外相位曲线则为东向偏移。前者归因于晨昏线附近反射性云层的增强形成，后者主要与赤道喷流将热量从炽热的昼面输送到较冷的夜面有关。云层与温度分布图之间的这种反相关性可为热木星云层形成过程提供重要见解。我们将展示NGTS-10b的全轨道相位曲线结果，该曲线利用NIRSpec/PRISM的独特能力在光学和近红外波段同时测量。内容包括行星的波长依赖性热点偏移与热量再分布效率、相位分辨发射光谱和透射光谱。我们还将讨论NGTS-10b等压面分布图对当前理论模型的启示及其对未来JWST相位曲线观测的意义。

[2] From East to West : the first simultaneous temperature and cloud map of a hot Jupiter.

2021-03-00

With the discovery of 51 peg b in 1995, hot Jupiter atmospheres have challenged our understanding of atmospheric dynamics. Infrared phase curves observed by Spitzer and Hubble have shown positive offsets, meaning that the hottest point of the atmosphere is shifted east of the substellar point. On the other hand optical phase curves observed by Kepler have shown negative offsets, meaning that the cloudiest part of the planet is shifted west of the substellar point. This anti-correlation between temperature and cloud map can provide strong insights into the cloud formation process in hot Jupiters. However it relies on observations carried on two different population of planets with two different instruments. <P />We propose to observe the full orbit phase curve of NGTS-10b simultaneously from the optical to the infrared using the unique capability of the NIRSpec/PRISM instrument. We will observe the peak of the phase curve continuously shift from a negative to a positive offset on a given planet for the first time. We will map the longitudinal, latitudinal and heigh variation of the cloud, the chemical abundances and the thermal structure of the planet. We will detect chemical disequilibrium on the planet nightside and determine the condensation temperature of the clouds. <P />By combining optical and infrared phase curves we will create the first isobaric map of a hot Jupiter, a necessary step to benchmark current theoretical models. This will be crucial to inform which level of complexity will be required in future atmospheric retrieval models to obtain unbiased abundances measurements from secondary eclipse and transit observations of hot Jupiter atmospheres with JWST.

[2] 从东到西：热木星的首个同时温度与云图

自1995年51 peg b被发现以来，热木星大气一直挑战着我们对大气动力学的理解。Spitzer和Hubble观测到的红外相位曲线显示正偏移，即大气最热点位于亚恒星点以东。而Kepler观测到的光学相位曲线则显示负偏移，即行星云量最多的区域位于亚恒星点以西。温度与云图之间的这种反相关关系为理解热木星的云形成过程提供了重要线索，但目前依赖于两类不同行星、两种不同仪器的观测。<P />我们提议利用NIRSpec/PRISM仪器的独特能力，对NGTS-10b进行从光学到红外的全轨道相位曲线同时观测。我们将首次在同一颗行星上连续观测到相位曲线峰值从负偏移转变为正偏移。我们将绘制该行星云、化学丰度和热结构在经度、纬度和高度上的变化图。我们将探测行星夜侧的化学非平衡，并确定云的凝结温度。<P />通过结合光学和红外相位曲线，我们将首次创建热木星的等压面分布图，这是校准当前理论模型的必要步骤。这对于明确未来大气检索模型需要达到的复杂度水平，以便从JWST的次生凌星和凌星观测中获得无偏的丰度测量至关重要。

[3] Revisiting the Iconic Spitzer Phase Curve of 55 Cancri e: Not So Strange After All?

2022-06-00

Thermal phase curves of short-period planets provide the best constraints on the atmospheric dynamics and heat transport in exoplanet atmospheres. The published Spitzer phase curve of 55 Cancri e, an ultra-short period super-Earth, exhibits a large hot spot offset, suggesting significant eastward heat recirculation, unexpected on such a hot planet. We present our re-reduction and analysis of these iconic observations using the open source and modular Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis Pipeline (SPCA). In particular, we attempt to reproduce the published analysis using bi-linear interpolation, but also test alternative instrument detrending schemes. We study how the retrieved dayside temperature, nightside temperature, and longitudinal offset of the planet's hotspot depend on the reduction and detrending. Our re-analysis suggests that 55 Cancri e may have a negligible hot spot offset of 14 ± 10 degrees west, as expected from theoretical considerations and consistent with other ultra-short-period planets. We also report an eclipse depth of 195 ± 24 p.p.m., a phase semi-amplitude of 95 ± 13 p.p.m., a dayside brightness temperature of 3767 ± 424 K and a nightside brightness temperature of 1033 ± 253 K.

[3] 重访标志性Spitzer 55 Cancri e相位曲线：其实并不奇怪？

短周期行星的热相位曲线为系外行星大气动力学和热量输运提供了最佳约束。已发表的 Spitzer 55 Cancri e 相位曲线显示出显著的热点偏移，表明存在强烈的向东热再循环，这在如此高温的行星上是出乎意料的。我们利用开源模块化的 Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis Pipeline（SPCA）对这些标志性观测数据进行了重新归约和分析。我们尝试通过双线性插值复现已发表的分析结果，同时也测试了其他仪器去趋势方法。我们研究了所检索的日面温度、夜面温度和行星热点经度偏移如何依赖于数据归约和去趋势方法。我们的再分析表明，55 Cancri e 的热点偏移可能可以忽略不计，为 14 ± 10 度偏西，这与理论预期及其他超短周期行星一致。我们还报告了 195 ± 24 ppm 的食深、95 ± 13 ppm 的相位半振幅、3767 ± 424 K 的日面亮温和 1033 ± 253 K 的夜面亮温。

[4] A New Analysis of Eight Spitzer Phase Curves and Hot Jupiter Population Trends: Qatar-1b, Qatar-2b, WASP-52b, WASP-34b, and WASP-140b

2022-06-00

With over 30 phase curves observed during the warm Spitzer mission, the complete data set provides a wealth of information relating to trends and three-dimensional properties of hot Jupiter atmospheres. In this work we present a comparative study of seven new Spitzer phase curves for four planets with equilibrium temperatures T <SUB>eq</SUB> ~ 1300K: Qatar-2b, WASP-52b, WASP-34b, and WASP-140b, as well as a reanalysis of the 4.5 μm Qatar-1b phase curve due to the similar equilibrium temperature. In total, five 4.5 μm phase curves and three 3.6 μm phase curves are analyzed here with a uniform approach. Using these new results, in combination with literature values for the entire population of published Spitzer phase curves of hot Jupiters, we present evidence for a linear trend of increasing hotspot offset with increasing orbital period, as well as observational evidence for two classes of planets in apparent redistribution versus equilibrium temperature parameter space, and tentative evidence for a dependence of hotspot offset on planetary surface gravity in our ~1300 K sample. We do not find trends in apparent heat redistribution with orbital period or gravity. Nonuniformity in literature Spitzer data analysis techniques precludes a definitive determination of the sources or lack of trends.

[4] 八条Spitzer相位曲线的新分析及热木星种群趋势：Qatar-1b、Qatar-2b、WASP-52b、WASP-34b和WASP-140b

在Spitzer暖任务期间观测到30余条相位曲线，这一完整数据集为研究热木星大气的趋势和三维特性提供了丰富信息。本工作对4颗平衡温度T<sub>eq</sub> ~ 1300K的行星（Qatar-2b、WASP-52b、WASP-34b和WASP-140b）的7条新Spitzer相位曲线进行了对比研究，并对Qatar-1b的4.5 μm相位曲线进行了重新分析，因其平衡温度相近。本文共分析了5条4.5 μm相位曲线和3条3.6 μm相位曲线，采用统一方法处理。结合文献中所有已发表的Spitzer热木星相位曲线数据，我们提出了热点偏移随轨道周期增加而线性增加的趋势证据，以及在表观能量再分布与平衡温度参数空间中存在两类行星的观测证据，并在约1300 K样本中初步发现热点偏移与行星表面重力有关。我们未发现表观能量再分布与轨道周期或重力之间的趋势。文献中Spitzer数据分析方法的不一致性阻碍了对这些趋势来源或缺失的明确判定。

[5] Revisiting the Iconic Spitzer Phase Curve of 55 Cancri e: Hotter Dayside, Cooler Nightside, and Smaller Phase Offset

2022-11-00

Thermal phase curves of short-period exoplanets provide the best constraints on the atmospheric dynamics and heat transport in their atmospheres. The published Spitzer Space Telescope phase curve of 55 Cancri e, an ultra-short-period super-Earth, exhibits a large phase offset suggesting significant eastward heat recirculation, unexpected on such a hot planet. We present our rereduction and analysis of these iconic observations using the open source and modular Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis pipeline. In particular, we attempt to reproduce the published analysis using the same instrument detrending scheme as the original authors. We retrieve the dayside temperature ( ${T}_{\mathrm{day}}={3771}_{-520}^{+669}$ K), nightside temperature ( ${T}_{\mathrm{night}\,}\lt 1649$ K at 2σ), and longitudinal offset of the planet's hot spot, and quantify how they depend on the reduction and detrending. Our reanalysis suggests that 55 Cancri e has a negligible hot spot offset of $-{12}_{-18}^{+21}$ degrees east. The small phase offset and cool nightside are consistent with the poor heat transport expected on ultra-short-period planets. The high dayside 4.5 μm brightness temperature is qualitatively consistent with SiO emission from an inverted rock vapor atmosphere.

[5] 重访标志性Spitzer 55 Cancri e相位曲线：更热的昼面、更冷的夜面和更小的相位偏移

短周期系外行星的热相位曲线为其大气动力学和热量输运提供了最佳约束。超短周期超级地球 55 Cancri e 的已发表 Spitzer 空间望远镜相位曲线显示出较大的相位偏移，暗示存在显著的向东热再循环，这在如此高温行星上并不常见。我们利用开源模块化的 Spitzer Phase Curve Analysis 管道对这些标志性观测进行了重新归约和分析。特别地，我们尝试采用与原作者相同的仪器去趋势方案复现已发表的分析结果。我们检索了白昼面温度（${T}_{\mathrm{day}}={3771}_{-520}^{+669}$ K）、夜晚面温度（${T}_{\mathrm{night}\,}\lt 1649$ K，2σ）以及行星热点的经度偏移，并量化了这些参数对归约和去趋势方法的依赖性。我们的再分析表明，55 Cancri e 的热点偏移可以忽略，为 $-{12}_{-18}^{+21}$ 度向东。小的相位偏移和较冷的夜晚面与超短周期行星预期的低效热输运一致。白昼面 4.5 μm 的高亮温与反转岩石蒸气大气中的 SiO 发射在定性上相符。

[6] An Analysis of Spitzer Phase Curves for WASP-77Ab and WASP-121b

2024-02-00

Although Spitzer is no longer an active mission, its collection of over 30 exoplanet phase curve observations remain a valuable resource for comparative planetology of hot Jupiters and for informing future observations with JWST. For individual planets, measurements of key phase curve parameters such as amplitude and hotspot offset provide evidence for the presence of clouds, magnetic fields, etc., which affect atmospheric dynamics. WASP-77Ab and WASP-121b are two hot Jupiters with comparable orbital periods of around 1.5 days but disparate equilibrium temperatures. Spitzer observed each planet for a full orbit at both 3.6 µm and 4.5 µm. We will present an analysis of these phase curves and their derived parameters. We will discuss predicted dynamic effects on the atmospheres of both planets, as well as comparisons of calculated planetary parameters between Spitzer and JWST NIRSpec observations, whose NRS1 and NRS2 channels broadly align with the two warm-Spitzer mission channels. Finally, we will discuss an assessment of the empirical relationship between orbital period and hotspot offset for hot Jupiters.

[6] WASP-77Ab和WASP-121b的Spitzer相位曲线分析

尽管Spitzer已不再是活跃任务，其收集的30余条系外行星相位曲线观测仍是热木星比较行星学和指导未来JWST观测的重要资源。对于单个行星，相位曲线关键参数（如振幅和热点偏移）的测量为云层、磁场等影响大气动力学的因素提供了证据。WASP-77Ab和WASP-121b是两颗轨道周期约1.5天但平衡温度差异显著的热木星。Spitzer在3.6 µm和4.5 µm波段对每颗行星均进行了完整轨道的观测。我们将展示这些相位曲线及其推导参数的分析结果，讨论对两颗行星大气的动力学效应预测，以及Spitzer与JWST NIRSpec观测（NRS1和NRS2通道大致对应Spitzer两个波段）下行星参数的比较。最后，我们将评估热木星轨道周期与热点偏移之间的经验关系。

[7] Spitzer's Exoplanet Legacy: Population Trends from Phase Curve Observations

2023-01-00

While the Spitzer telescope has officially reached the end of its mission, it remains a powerful force in exoplanet characterization. Spitzer 3.6 and 4.5 micron phase curve observations allow us to measure key parameters that result from a planet's unique atmospheric circulation such as heat redistribution efficiency and hot spot shifts due to equatorial jets. Now in the era of comparative exoplanet studies, an understanding of the observed trends in these parameters is key to our understanding of the Hot Jupiter population. However, to ensure any observed trends are accurate, we must be sure that our techniques to extract these signals from the instrumental noise are reliable and reproducible. Towards those means, we have developed a more robust intrapixel sensitivity detrending method to remove the main systematic in Spitzer IRAC observations. We are using our new fixed intrapixel sensitivity map to perform as uniform of an analysis as allowed by the phase curve data. I will discuss trends in observed Hot Jupiter atmospheric parameters, as well as how these observed trends compare to three dimensional circulation model predictions to determine what they teach us about the Hot Jupiter population. Future work will continue to extend this uniform analysis to the entire population of Spitzer phase curves and improve our modeling techniques to better compare between models and data.

[7] Spitzer系外行星遗产：来自相位曲线观测的人口统计趋势

尽管Spitzer望远镜已正式结束任务，但它在系外行星特性研究中仍然具有强大作用。Spitzer 3.6和4.5微米相位曲线观测使我们能够测量由行星独特大气环流导致的关键参数，如热量再分布效率和由于赤道喷流引起的热点偏移。如今已进入系外行星对比研究时代，理解这些参数的观测趋势对于我们理解热木星种群至关重要。然而，为确保观测趋势的准确性，我们必须确保从仪器噪声中提取这些信号的方法是可靠且可复现的。为此，我们开发了一种更为稳健的像素内灵敏度去趋势方法，以去除Spitzer IRAC观测中的主要系统误差。我们正在利用新的固定像素内灵敏度图，对相位曲线数据进行尽可能统一的分析。我将讨论热木星大气参数的观测趋势，以及这些趋势与三维环流模型预测的对比，从而探讨它们对热木星种群的启示。未来工作将继续将这种统一分析扩展到全部Spitzer相位曲线样本，并改进我们的建模技术，以更好地实现模型与数据的对比。

[8] Spitzer thermal phase curve of WASP-121 b

2023-08-00

<BR /> Aims: We analyse unpublished Spitzer observations of the thermal phase-curve of WASP-121 b, a benchmark ultra-hot Jupiter. <BR /> Methods: We adopted the wavelet pixel-independent component analysis technique to remove challenging instrumental systematic effects in these datasets and we fit them simultaneously with parametric light-curve models. We also performed phase-curve retrievals to better understand the horizontal and vertical thermal structure of the planetary atmosphere. <BR /> Results: We measured planetary brightness temperatures of ~2700 K (dayside) and ~700-1100 K (nightside), along with modest peak offsets of 5.9° ± 1.6 (3.6 µm) and 5.0°<SUB>‒3.1</SUB><SUP>+3.4</SUP> (4.5 µm) after mid-eclipse. These results suggest inefficient heat redistribution in the atmosphere of WASP-121 b. The inferred atmospheric Bond albedo and circulation efficiency align well with observed trends for hot giant exoplanets. Interestingly, the measured peak offsets correspond to a westward hot spot, which has rarely been observed. We also report consistent transit depths at 3.6 and 4.5 µm, along with updated geometric and orbital parameters. Finally, we compared our Spitzer results with previous measurements, including recent JWST observations. <BR /> Conclusions: We extracted new information on the thermal properties and dynamics of an exoplanet atmosphere from an especially problematic dataset. This study probes the reliability of exoplanet phase-curve parameters obtained from Spitzer observations when state-of-the-art pipelines are adopted to remove the instrumental systematic effects. It demonstrates that Spitzer phase-curve observations provide a useful baseline for comparison with JWST observations, and shows the increase in parameters precision achieved with the newer telescope.

[8] WASP-121 b的Spitzer热相位曲线

目标：我们分析了尚未发表的WASP-121 b（一颗基准超热木星）热相位曲线的Spitzer观测数据。 方法：我们采用小波像素独立成分分析技术，以去除这些数据集中具有挑战性的仪器系统效应，并与参数化光变曲线模型同时拟合。我们还进行了相位曲线检索，以更好地理解行星大气的水平和垂直热结构。 结果：我们测得行星白昼面亮温约为2700 K，夜晚面约为700-1100 K，峰值偏移分别为5.9° ± 1.6（3.6 µm）和5.0°<SUB>‒3.1</SUB><SUP>+3.4</SUP>（4.5 µm），均发生在中掩食后。这些结果表明WASP-121 b大气中的热量再分配效率较低。推断的大气Bond反照率和环流效率与热巨行星的观测趋势一致。有趣的是，测得的峰值偏移对应于西向热点，这种现象很少被观测到。我们还报告了3.6和4.5 µm波段一致的凌日深度，以及更新后的几何和轨道参数。最后，我们将Spitzer结果与先前的观测，包括最新的JWST观测进行了比较。 结论：我们从一个特别有问题的数据集中提取了关于系外行星大气热性质和动力学的新信息。本研究检验了采用最先进数据处理流程去除仪器系统效应后，Spitzer观测获得的系外行星相位曲线参数的可靠性。结果表明，Spitzer相位曲线观测为与JWST观测的对比提供了有用的基线，并展示了新一代望远镜带来的参数精度提升。

What are the limitations of the massless planet approximation in transit light curve models, and in which parameter regimes does this approximation introduce significant errors in the inferred planetary and stellar parameters?

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Biases in Exoplanet Transmission Spectra Introduced by Limb-darkening Parametrization

2024-11-00

One of the main endeavors of the field of exoplanetary sciences is the characterization of exoplanet atmospheres on a population level. The current method of choice to accomplish this task is transmission spectroscopy, where the apparent radius of a transiting exoplanet is measured at multiple wavelengths in search of atomic and molecular absorption features produced by the upper atmosphere constituents. To extract the planetary radius from a transit light curve, it is necessary to account for the decrease in luminosity away from the center of the projected stellar disk, known as limb darkening. Physically motivated parametrizations of limb darkening, in particular of the quadratic form, are commonly used in exoplanet transit light-curve fitting. Here, we show that such parametrizations can introduce significant wavelength-dependent biases in the transmission spectra currently obtained with all instrument modes of the JWST, and thus have the potential to affect atmospheric inferences. To avoid such biases, we recommend the use of standard limb-darkening parametrizations with wide uninformative priors that allow for nonphysical stellar intensity profiles in the transit fits, and thus for a complete and symmetrical exploration of the parameter space. We further find that fitting the light curves at the native resolution results in errors on the measured transit depths that are significantly smaller compared to light curves that are binned in wavelength before fitting, thus potentially maximizing the amount of information that can be extracted from the data.

[1] 边缘暗化参数化对系外行星透射光谱引入的偏差

系外行星科学领域的主要目标之一是在群体层面刻画系外行星大气。目前实现这一目标的首选方法是透射光谱，即在多个波长测量凌日系外行星的表观半径，以寻找由高层大气成分产生的原子和分子吸收特征。要从凌日光变曲线中提取行星半径，必须考虑投影恒星盘中心向外亮度递减的现象，即边缘变暗。物理动机的边缘变暗参数化，尤其是二次型，常用于系外行星凌日光变曲线拟合。本文表明，这类参数化会在目前所有 JWST 仪器模式获得的透射光谱中引入显著的波长相关偏差，进而可能影响大气推断。为避免此类偏差，我们建议在拟合中采用标准边缘变暗参数化，并使用宽泛的无信息先验，允许出现非物理的恒星强度分布，从而对参数空间进行完整且对称的探索。我们还发现，在本征分辨率下拟合光变曲线，测得的凌日深度误差显著小于先按波长分箱后再拟合的情况，因此可最大化数据中可提取的信息量。

[2] A critical assessment of the applicability of the energy-limited approximation for estimating exoplanetary mass-loss rates

2021-06-00

Context. The energy-limited atmospheric escape approach is widely used to estimate mass-loss rates for a broad range of planets that host hydrogen-dominated atmospheres as well as for performing atmospheric evolution calculations. <BR /> Aims: We aim to study the applicability range of the energy-limited atmospheric escape approximation. <BR /> Methods: We revise the energy-limited atmospheric escape formalism and the involved assumptions. We also compare the results of the energy-limited formalism with those of hydrodynamic simulations, employing a grid covering planets with masses, radii, and equilibrium temperatures ranging between 1 M<SUB>⊕</SUB> and 39 M<SUB>⊕</SUB>, 1 R<SUB>⊕</SUB> and 10 R<SUB>⊕</SUB>, and 300 and 2000 K, respectively. <BR /> Results: Within the grid boundaries, we find that the energy-limited approximation gives a correct order of magnitude estimate for mass-loss rates for about 76% of the planets, but there can be departures from hydrodynamic simulations by up to two to three orders of magnitude in individual cases. Furthermore, we find that planets for which the mass-loss rates are correctly estimated by the energy-limited approximation to within one order of magnitude have intermediate gravitational potentials (≈2.5-5.5 ×10<SUP>8</SUP> J kg<SUP>‒1</SUP>) as well as low-to-intermediate equilibrium temperatures and irradiation fluxes of extreme ultraviolet and X-ray radiation. However, for planets with low or high gravitational potentials, or high equilibrium temperatures and irradiation fluxes, the approximation fails in most cases. <BR /> Conclusions: The energy-limited approximation should not be used for planetary evolution calculations that require computing mass-loss rates for planets that cover a broad parameter space. In this case, it is very likely that the energy-limited approximation would at times return mass-loss rates of up to several orders of magnitude above or below those predicted by hydrodynamic simulations. For planetary atmospheric evolution calculations, interpolation routines or approximations based on grids of hydrodynamic models should be used instead.

[2] 对能量限制近似法在估算系外行星质量损失率适用性的关键评估

背景：能量限制大气逃逸方法被广泛用于估算以氢为主大气的各类行星的质量损失率，并用于大气演化计算。 目的：我们旨在研究能量限制大气逃逸近似的适用范围。 方法：我们修订了能量限制大气逃逸的公式及相关假设，并将其结果与流体动力学模拟进行比较，模拟网格覆盖行星质量、半径和平衡温度分别在1 M⊕到39 M⊕、1 R⊕到10 R⊕、300到2000 K范围内。 结果：在网格范围内，能量限制近似对约76%的行星能给出质量损失率的数量级估算，但在个别情况下与流体动力学模拟结果可相差2到3个数量级。此外，我们发现，能量限制近似能在一个数量级内正确估算质量损失率的行星，其引力势（约2.5-5.5 ×10^8 J kg^‒1）为中等，且平衡温度和极紫外/X射线辐照通量为低到中等。而对于低或高引力势，或高平衡温度和高辐照通量的行星，该近似在大多数情况下失效。 结论：对于需要计算覆盖广泛参数空间行星质量损失率的行星演化计算，不应使用能量限制近似。在这种情况下，该近似很可能会给出比流体动力学模拟高出或低出几个数量级的质量损失率。对于行星大气演化计算，应采用基于流体动力学模型网格的插值方法或近似。

[3] Exploring Systematic Errors in the Inferred Parameters of the Transiting Planets

2024-04-00

Transiting planet systems offer a unique opportunity to measure precise masses and radii of planets and their host stars. However, relative photometry and radial velocity measurements alone only constrain the host star density, leaving a one-parameter mass-radius degeneracy. We assess the magnitude of systematic errors in the derived system parameters relative to their statistical precision due to different methods of breaking this degeneracy. We first model extant data for the typical hot Jupiter system KELT-15 using EXOFASTv2, considering four methods of breaking the stellar mass-radius degeneracy. We find systematic differences in the inferred physical parameters of the KELT-15 system, including a 6.5% (~1.8 σ) difference in the stellar and planetary radii based the host star mass-radius degeneracy-breaking method employed. We then evaluate the systematic errors for several systems of M-dwarfs transiting FGK host stars. M-dwarfs are the most abundant stars in the galaxy and popular targets for exoplanet searches. However, we only know of dozens of M-dwarfs with fundamental parameters of mass, radius and effective temperature characterized to better than a few percent. Similarly to transiting planets, M-dwarfs in eclipsing binaries can also be robustly characterized. Here we present several targets from the Eclipsing Binary Low Mass (EBLM) survey where we measure M-dwarf masses with precisions better than 5%, radii better than 3% and effective temperatures on order 1%. Using the same methodology employed for KELT-15, we determine that the model uncertainty in the primary star is of similar magnitude to the statistical uncertainty in the model fits of the secondary M-dwarf. Therefore, whilst these can be considered benchmark M-dwarfs, we caution the community to consider model uncertainty when pushing the limits of precise stellar characterization. Finally, we present a homogenous analysis of ~10 hot Jupiter planetary systems with secondary eclipses observed by TESS with consistent systematic uncertainties in their derived properties. Where possible we homogenously estimate albedos, temperatures, and eccentricities of these systems in addition to updated ephemerides. Understanding the eccentricity of these systems is important in understanding the formation of hot Jupiters.

[3] 探索凌星行星推断参数中的系统误差

凌星行星系统为测量行星及其宿主恒星的精确质量和半径提供了独特机会。然而，相对光度和径向速度测量只能约束宿主恒星的密度，导致质量-半径之间存在一参数简并性。我们评估了由于不同打破该简并性方法导致的系统参数系统误差相对于其统计精度的大小。我们首先利用EXOFASTv2对典型热木星系统KELT-15的现有数据进行建模，考虑了四种打破恒星质量-半径简并性的方法。我们发现KELT-15系统推断的物理参数存在系统性差异，包括根据所采用的恒星质量-半径简并性打破方法，恒星和行星半径存在6.5%（约1.8σ）的差异。随后我们评估了多颗M矮星凌越FGK型宿主星系统的系统误差。M矮星是银河系中最丰富的恒星类型，也是系外行星搜寻的热门目标。然而，目前只有几十颗M矮星的质量、半径和有效温度等基本参数被精确测量到优于几个百分点。类似于凌星行星，食双星中的M矮星也可以被稳健地表征。我们在Eclipsing Binary Low Mass（EBLM）巡天中展示了多个目标，其M矮星质量测量精度优于5%，半径优于3%，有效温度约为1%。采用与KELT-15相同的方法，我们确定主星的模型不确定性与次星M矮星模型拟合的统计不确定性相当。因此，尽管这些可以被视为基准M矮星，我们仍提醒社区在追求高精度恒星参数表征时需考虑模型不确定性。最后，我们对约10个TESS观测到次生凌星的热木星行星系统进行了同质化分析，获得了其推导属性中一致的系统不确定性。在可能的情况下，我们还同质化估算了这些系统的反照率、温度和偏心率，并更新了历元。理解这些系统的偏心率对于理解热木星的形成过程非常重要。

[4] Where does the simplified stellar contamination model fail in exoplanet transmission spectroscopy?

2026-02-00

Stellar photospheric heterogeneities (e.g., starspots and faculae) distort the apparent stellar spectrum during a transit and imprint wavelength-dependent biases on the measured planet─to─star radius ratio. This transit light source effect (TLSE) must be accounted for to obtain reliable atmospheric properties. A widely used approach is the Rackham─TLSE (R─TLSE) prescription, which applies a disk-averaged contamination correction based solely on the filling factor and spectral contrast. However, accurate transmission-spectroscopy interpretations require models that also account for limb darkening, the spatial distribution of active regions, and transit geometry. In this work, we incorporated these effects into a self-consistent, pixel-resolved framework, ECLIPSE-Xλ, and performed idealized, noise-free model─model comparisons against the R─TLSE approximation. Using three archetypal systems ─ the super-Earth LHS 1140 b, the mini-Neptune K2-18 b, and the hot Jupiter WASP-69 b ─ we show that disk-averaged TLSE corrections can differ from the self-consistent model by up to ~400 ppm in the optical for active hosts and non-equatorial transits, while remaining below ~10 ppm at near-infrared wavelengths where limb darkening is weaker. We then applied both approaches to the JWST/NIRISS SOSS transmission spectrum of LHS 1140 b. When limb darkening is artificially set to zero, ECLIPSE-Xλ recovers stellar-contamination parameters that closely match the reference R─TLSE solution, confirming that the two frameworks are consistent in the disk-averaged limit. With wavelength-dependent limb darkening included, however, reproducing the observed short-wavelength slope through stellar contamination alone requires very hot faculae (∆T<SUB>fac</SUB> ≃ 600 K) covering f<SUB>fac</SUB> ≃ 0.35 of the visible hemisphere, corresponding to an equivalent circular facular region with radius ≃0.6 R<SUB>⋆</SUB> (i.e., about 60% of the stellar radius) on the stellar disk. Such an extended, unocculted active region would be physically unlikely even for an active M dwarf. In this context, a purely stellar-contamination explanation for any residual optical slope would demand rather extreme facular populations; scenarios in which a genuine atmospheric contribution helps complement a more modest facular signal appear more physically plausible. Taken together, these results delineate the regime of validity of the R─TLSE approximation and underscore the need for geometry-aware stellar-heterogeneity models that explicitly account for limb darkening in high-precision transmission spectroscopy.

[4] 简化恒星污染模型在系外行星透射光谱中何处失效？

恒星光球非均匀性（如恒星黑子和亮斑）会在凌日过程中扭曲表观恒星光谱，并在测量的行星-恒星半径比上引入波长相关的偏差。这种凌日光源效应（TLSE）必须加以考虑，才能获得可靠的大气性质。广泛使用的方法是Rackham-TLSE（R-TLSE）方案，仅基于填充因子和光谱对比度进行盘面平均污染修正。然而，准确的透射光谱解释需要模型同时考虑边缘变暗、活跃区空间分布和凌日几何。在本工作中，我们将这些效应纳入自洽、像素分辨的ECLIPSE-Xλ框架，并与R-TLSE近似进行了理想化、无噪声的模型-模型对比。以三种典型系统——超级地球LHS 1140 b、迷你海王星K2-18 b和热木星WASP-69 b为例，我们发现对于活跃宿主和非赤道凌日，盘面平均TLSE修正与自洽模型在光学波段可相差高达约400 ppm，而在边缘变暗较弱的近红外波段则低于约10 ppm。随后我们将两种方法应用于LHS 1140 b的JWST/NIRISS SOSS透射光谱。当边缘变暗被人为设为零时，ECLIPSE-Xλ恢复的恒星污染参数与R-TLSE参考解高度一致，证实两种框架在盘面平均极限下是一致的。然而，若考虑波长相关的边缘变暗，仅通过恒星污染重现观测到的短波斜率需非常高温的亮斑（∆T<SUB>fac</SUB> ≃ 600 K），覆盖可见半球f<SUB>fac</SUB> ≃ 0.35，相当于恒星盘面上半径约0.6 R<SUB>⋆</SUB>（即约60%恒星半径）的等效圆形亮斑区域。即使对于活跃M矮星，如此大面积未被遮挡的活跃区在物理上也极不可能。在此背景下，任何残余光学斜率若仅用恒星污染解释，则需极端的亮斑分布；而大气贡献与适度亮斑信号共同作用的情景更为合理。综上，这些结果明确了R-TLSE近似的适用范围，并强调了在高精度透射光谱中需采用显式考虑边缘变暗的几何敏感恒星非均匀性模型。

[5] On the Effect of Stellar Activity on Low-resolution Transit Spectroscopy and the use of High Resolution as Mitigation

2022-05-00

We present models designed to quantify the effects of stellar activity on exoplanet transit spectroscopy and atmospheric characterization at low (R = 100) and high (R = 100,000) spectral resolution. We study three model classes mirroring planetary system archetypes: a hot Jupiter around an early-K star (HD 189733 b); a mini-Neptune around an early-M dwarf (K2-18 b); and terrestrial planets around a late-M dwarf (TRAPPIST-1). We map photospheres with temperatures and radial velocities (RV) and integrate specific intensity stellar models. We obtain transit spectra affected by stellar contamination, the Rossiter-McLaughlin effect (RME), and center-to-limb variations (CLV). We find that, at low resolution, for later-type stars, planetary water features become difficult to distinguish from contamination. Many distributions of unocculted active regions can induce planetary-like features of similar amplitudes in the case of a late-M dwarf. Atmospheric characterization of planets around late-type stars will likely continue to suffer from degeneracy with stellar activity unless active regions' parameters can be constrained using additional information. For the early-K star, stellar contamination mostly manifests itself through a slope at optical wavelengths similar to Rayleigh scattering. In all cases, contamination induces offsets in measured planet radii. At high resolution, we show that we can determine the origin of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO detection signals and lift the degeneracy observed at low resolution, provided sufficient planet RV variation during transit and adequate correction for the RME and CLV when required. High-resolution spectroscopy may therefore help resolve issues arising from stellar contamination for favorable systems.

[5] 恒星活动对低分辨率凌星光谱的影响及高分辨率作为缓解手段的应用

我们提出了旨在量化恒星活动对系外行星凌日光谱和大气表征影响的模型，涵盖低（R = 100）和高（R = 100,000）光谱分辨率。我们研究了三类反映行星系统原型的模型：围绕早K型恒星的热木星（HD 189733 b）；围绕早M型矮星的迷你海王星（K2-18 b）；以及围绕晚M型矮星的类地行星（TRAPPIST-1）。我们绘制了具有不同温度和径向速度（RV）的光球，并整合了特定强度的恒星模型。我们获得了受恒星污染、Rossiter-McLaughlin效应（RME）和中心到边缘变化（CLV）影响的凌日光谱。我们发现，在低分辨率下，对于晚型恒星，行星水特征变得难以与污染区分。在晚M型矮星的情况下，许多未被遮挡的活跃区分布可诱发与行星特征幅度相当的信号。除非能利用额外信息约束活跃区参数，否则围绕晚型恒星的行星大气表征很可能继续受到与恒星活动的退化影响。对于早K型恒星，恒星污染主要表现为类似瑞利散射的光学波段斜率。在所有情况下，污染都会引入行星半径测量的偏移。在高分辨率下，我们展示了只要凌日过程中行星RV变化足够大，并对RME和CLV进行充分校正，就能确定H<sub>2</sub>O和CO探测信号的起源，并消除低分辨率下的退化。因此，高分辨率光谱有望帮助解决有利系统中由恒星污染引发的问题。

[6] Phase-curve Pollution of Exoplanet Transmission Spectra

2021-04-00

The occurrence of a planet transiting in front of its host star offers the opportunity to observe the planet's atmosphere filtering starlight. The fraction of occulted stellar flux is roughly proportional to the optically thick area of the planet, the extent of which depends on the opacity of the planet's gaseous envelope at the observed wavelengths. Chemical species, haze, and clouds are now routinely detected in exoplanet atmospheres through rather small features in transmission spectra, i.e., collections of planet-to-star area ratios across multiple spectral bins and/or photometric bands. Technological advances have led to a shrinking of the error bars down to a few tens of parts per million (ppm) per spectral point for the brightest targets. The upcoming James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) is anticipated to deliver transmission spectra with precision down to 10 ppm. The increasing precision of measurements requires a reassessment of the approximations hitherto adopted in astrophysical models, including transit light-curve models. Recently, it has been shown that neglecting the planet's thermal emission can introduce significant biases in the transit depth measured with the JWST/Mid-InfraRed Instrument, integrated between 5 and 12 μm. In this paper, we take a step forward by analyzing the effects of the approximation on transmission spectra over the 0.6-12 μm wavelength range covered by various JWST instruments. We present open-source software to predict the spectral bias, showing that, if not corrected, it may affect the inferred molecular abundances and thermal structure of some exoplanet atmospheres.

[6] 系外行星透射光谱的相位曲线污染

行星凌越其母星的事件为观测行星大气对恒星光的过滤提供了机会。被遮挡的恒星通量比例大致与行星的光学厚面积成正比，而该面积取决于行星气体包层在观测波长下的不透明度。通过透射光谱中的较小特征（即多个光谱通道和/或光度带的行星-恒星面积比集合），目前已能常规探测系外行星大气中的化学组分、薄雾和云。技术进步使得最亮目标的每个光谱点误差条缩小到几十ppm。即将发射的James Webb Space Telescope（JWST）预计将实现精度达10 ppm的透射光谱。测量精度的提升要求重新评估迄今在天体物理模型（包括凌星光变曲线模型）中采用的近似。近期研究表明，忽略行星的热发射会在JWST/Mid-InfraRed Instrument（5–12 μm波段积分）测得的凌星深度中引入显著偏差。本文进一步分析了该近似对JWST各仪器覆盖的0.6–12 μm波段透射光谱的影响。我们发布了开源软件用于预测光谱偏差，结果显示若不加以修正，该偏差可能影响部分系外行星大气的分子丰度和热结构的推断。

[7] An Analytical Model for Transit Light Curves

2024-11-00

Transits and secondary eclipses of an exoplanet orbiting a host star generate dips in the light curve. This article proposes a new class of mathematical functions that provide simple and computationally efficient models for such light curves. The transit and secondary eclipse functions account for ingress/egress, and the transit function incorporates curvilinear limb darkening. Their parameters are estimated using least squares methods. These models can be used to test for the statistical significance of dips, potentially revealing the existence of an exoplanet. In addition, they can be used to estimate features such as duration and depth of a transit or a secondary eclipse, limb darkening curvature and ingress/egress slope, and the orbital period. Examples using publicly available light curve data are presented, demonstrating the accuracy and efficiency of the proposed functions as fast analytical models for transits and/or secondary eclipses.

[7] 凌星光变曲线的解析模型

一颗系外行星绕主星运行时，其凌星和次生食会在光变曲线上产生下降。本文提出了一类新的数学函数，为此类光变曲线提供了简单且计算高效的建模方法。凌星和次生食函数均考虑了进/出食过程，凌星函数还包含了曲线状边缘变暗。其参数通过最小二乘法估算。这些模型可用于检验光变曲线下降的统计显著性，从而有可能揭示系外行星的存在。此外，还可用于估算凌星或次生食的持续时间和深度、边缘变暗曲率、进/出食斜率以及轨道周期。文中给出了使用公开光变曲线数据的示例，展示了所提出函数作为凌星和/或次生食快速解析模型的准确性和高效性。

[8] Power of wavelets in analyses of transit and phase curves in the presence of stellar variability and instrumental noise. I. Method and validation

2023-07-00

Context. Stellar photometric variability and instrumental effects, such as cosmic ray hits, data discontinuities, data leaks, instrument aging, and so on, lead to difficulties in the characterisation of exoplanets. Therefore, they can impact the accuracy and precision of the modelling and the detectability of their transits, occultations, and phase curves. <BR /> Aims: This paper is aimed at improving the transit, occultation, and phase-curve modelling in the presence of strong stellar variability and instrumental noise. To this end, we invoke the wavelet formulation. <BR /> Methods: We explored the capabilities of the software package Transit and Light Curve Modeller (TLCM). It is able to perform (1) a joint radial-velocity and light-curve fit or (2) a light curve-only fit. It models the transit, occultation, beaming, ellipsoidal, and reflection effects in the light curves (including the gravity-darkening effect). Here, the red noise, stellar variability, and instrumental effects were modelled via wavelets. The wavelet fit was constrained by prescribing that the final white noise level must be equal to the average of the uncertainties of the photometric data points. This helps to avoid overfitting and regularises the noise model. The approach was tested by injecting synthetic light curves into short-cadence Kepler data and modelling them. <BR /> Results: The method performs well over a certain signal-to-noise (S/N) ratio. We provide limits in terms of the S/N for every studied system parameter that is needed for accurate parameter retrieval. The wavelet approach is able to manage and remove the impact of data discontinuities, cosmic ray events, and long-term stellar variability and instrument ageing, as well as short-term stellar variability, pulsation, and flares (among others). <BR /> Conclusions: We conclude that precise light-curve models combined with the wavelet method and with well-prescribed constraints on the white noise are able to retrieve the planetary system parameters, even in the presence of strong stellar variability and instrumental noise, including data discontinuities.

[8] 在恒星变化性和仪器噪声存在下利用小波分析凌星和相位曲线的能力。I. 方法与验证

背景：恒星光度变化和仪器效应（如宇宙射线击中、数据不连续、数据泄漏、仪器老化等）导致系外行星特性刻画变得困难。因此，这些因素会影响凌日、掩食和相位曲线建模的准确性和精度，以及其可探测性。 目标：本文旨在提升在强恒星变化和仪器噪声存在下的凌日、掩食和相位曲线建模。为此，我们引入了小波方法。 方法：我们探索了Transit and Light Curve Modeller (TLCM)软件包的能力。该软件能够进行（1）径向速度与光变曲线联合拟合，或（2）仅光变曲线拟合。它可对光变曲线中的凌日、掩食、光束效应、椭球效应和反射效应（包括重力变暗效应）进行建模。本文中，红噪声、恒星变化和仪器效应通过小波建模。小波拟合通过规定最终白噪声水平等于光度数据点不确定性的平均值来约束，以避免过拟合并正则化噪声模型。该方法通过将合成光变曲线注入Kepler短时序数据并进行建模进行测试。 结果：该方法在一定信噪比（S/N）范围内表现良好。我们为每个研究的系统参数提供了准确参数检索所需的S/N下限。小波方法能够处理并消除数据不连续、宇宙射线事件、长期恒星变化和仪器老化，以及短期恒星变化、脉动和耀斑等影响。 结论：我们认为，精确的光变曲线模型结合小波方法，并对白噪声有良好约束，即使在强恒星变化和仪器噪声（包括数据不连续）存在的情况下，也能准确检索行星系统参数。

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] Atmospheric Metallicity and C/O of HD 189733 b from High-resolution Spectroscopy

2024-01-00

We present high-resolution K-band emission spectra of the quintessential hot Jupiter HD 189733 b from the Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer. Using a Bayesian retrieval framework, we fit the dayside pressure-temperature profile, orbital kinematics, mass-mixing ratios of H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, NH<SUB>3</SUB>, HCN, and H<SUB>2</SUB>S, and the <SUP>13</SUP>CO/<SUP>12</SUP>CO ratio. We measure mass fractions of ${\mathrm{logH}}_{2}{\rm{O}}=-{2.0}_{-0.4}^{+0.4}$ and $\mathrm{logCO}=-{2.2}_{-0.5}^{+0.5}$ , and place upper limits on the remaining species. Notably, we find logCH<SUB>4</SUB> &lt; -4.5 at 99% confidence, despite its anticipated presence at the equilibrium temperature of HD 189733 b assuming local thermal equilibrium. We make a tentative (~3σ) detection of <SUP>13</SUP>CO, and the retrieved posteriors suggest a <SUP>12</SUP>C/<SUP>13</SUP>C ratio similar to or substantially less than the local interstellar value. The possible <SUP>13</SUP>C enrichment would be consistent with accretion of fractionated material in ices or in the protoplanetary disk midplane. The retrieved abundances correspond to a substantially substellar atmospheric C/O = 0.3 ± 0.1, while the carbon and oxygen abundances are stellar to slightly superstellar, consistent with core-accretion models which predict an inverse correlation between C/O and metallicity. The specific combination of low C/O and high metallicity suggests significant accretion of solid material may have occurred late in the formation process of HD 189733 b.

[1] 利用高分辨率光谱测量HD 189733 b的大气金属丰度和C/O

我们展示了利用 Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer 获得的典型热木星 HD 189733 b 的高分辨率 K 波段发射光谱。通过贝叶斯检索框架，我们拟合了白天面压力-温度剖面、轨道运动学、H2O、CO、CH4、NH3、HCN 和 H2S 的质量混合比，以及 13CO/12CO 比值。我们测得 H2O 的质量分数为 ${\mathrm{logH}}_{2}{\rm{O}}=-{2.0}_{-0.4}^{+0.4}$，CO 为 $\mathrm{logCO}=-{2.2}_{-0.5}^{+0.5}$，并对其余分子给出了上限。值得注意的是，尽管在假设局部热平衡下 HD 189733 b 的平衡温度下应存在 CH4，但我们在 99% 置信度下得到 logCH4 < -4.5。我们对 13CO 做出了约 3σ 的初步探测，检索的后验分布表明 12C/13C 比值与本地星际值相当或显著低于该值。13C 的可能富集与在冰或原行星盘中部吸积分馏物质一致。检索得到的大气丰度对应于明显亚恒星的 C/O = 0.3 ± 0.1，而碳和氧的丰度为恒星到略高于恒星水平，这与核吸积模型预测的 C/O 与金属丰度反相关一致。低 C/O 和高金属丰度的特定组合表明，在 HD 189733 b 的形成后期可能发生了大量固体物质的吸积。

[2] Atmospheric metallicity and C/O of HD 189733 b from Keck/KPIC high-resolution spectroscopy

2024-02-00

We present high-resolution K-band emission spectra of the prototypical hot Jupiter HD 189733 b from the Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer (KPIC). Using a Bayesian retrieval framework, we fit the dayside pressure-temperature profile, orbital kinematics, mass-mixing ratios of H2O, CO, CH4, NH3, HCN, and H2S, and the 13CO/12CO ratio. We measure mass fractions of H2O and CO, and place upper limits on the remaining species. Notably, we find log10 CH4 &lt; -4.5 at 99% confidence, despite its expected presence at the equilibrium temperature of HD 189733 b. We make a tentative (~3sigma) detection of 13CO, and the retrieved posteriors suggest a 12C/13C ratio similar to or substantially less than the local interstellar value. The possible13C enrichment would be consistent with accretion of fractionated material in ices or in the protoplanetary disk midplane. The retrieved abundances correspond to a substantially sub-stellar atmospheric C/O = 0.3±0.1, while the carbon and oxygen abundances are stellar to slightly super-stellar, consistent with core-accretion models which predict an inverse correlation between C/O and metallicity. The specific combination of low C/O and high metallicity suggests significant accretion of solid material may have occurred late in the formation process of HD 189733 b.

[2] 利用Keck/KPIC高分辨率光谱测量HD 189733 b的大气金属丰度和C/O

我们利用Keck行星成像仪与表征仪（KPIC）获得了典型热木星HD 189733 b的高分辨率K波段发射光谱。采用贝叶斯检索框架，我们拟合了昼面压强-温度剖面、轨道运动学、H2O、CO、CH4、NH3、HCN、H2S的质量混合比以及13CO/12CO比值。我们测量了H2O和CO的质量分数，并对其余分子给出了上限。值得注意的是，尽管在HD 189733 b的平衡温度下预期存在甲烷，我们却以99%置信度得到log10 CH4 < -4.5。我们对13CO做出了初步（~3 sigma）探测，检索后验分布显示12C/13C比值与本地星际值相当或显著更低。13C的可能富集与在冰或原行星盘中吸积分馏物质一致。检索到的丰度对应大气C/O = 0.3±0.1，显著低于恒星值，而碳和氧的丰度为恒星到略高于恒星水平，这与核吸积模型预测的C/O与金属丰度反相关一致。低C/O与高金属丰度的组合表明，在HD 189733 b形成后期可能发生了大量固体物质的吸积。

[3] Hydrogen sulfide and metal-enriched atmosphere for a Jupiter-mass exoplanet

2024-08-00

As the closest transiting hot Jupiter to Earth, HD 189733b has been the benchmark planet for atmospheric characterization<SUP>1─3</SUP>. It has also been the anchor point for much of our theoretical understanding of exoplanet atmospheres from composition<SUP>4</SUP>, chemistry<SUP>5,6</SUP>, aerosols<SUP>7</SUP> to atmospheric dynamics<SUP>8</SUP>, escape<SUP>9</SUP> and modelling techniques<SUP>10,11</SUP>. Previous studies of HD 189733b have detected carbon and oxygen-bearing molecules H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO (refs. <SUP>12,13</SUP>) in the atmosphere. The presence of CO<SUB>2</SUB> and CH<SUB>4</SUB> has been claimed<SUP>14,15</SUP> but later disputed<SUP>12,16,17</SUP>. The inferred metallicity based on these measurements, a key parameter in tracing planet formation locations<SUP>18</SUP>, varies from depletion<SUP>19,20</SUP> to enhancement<SUP>21,22</SUP>, hindered by limited wavelength coverage and precision of the observations. Here we report detections of H<SUB>2</SUB>O (13.4σ), CO<SUB>2</SUB> (11.2σ), CO (5σ) and H<SUB>2</SUB>S (4.5σ) in the transmission spectrum (2.4─5.0 μm) of HD 189733b. With an equilibrium temperature of about 1,200 K, H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO and H<SUB>2</SUB>S are the main reservoirs for oxygen, carbon and sulfur. Based on the measured abundances of these three main volatile elements, we infer an atmospheric metallicity of three to five times stellar. The upper limit on the methane abundance at 5σ is 0.1 ppm, which indicates a low carbon-to-oxygen ratio (&lt;0.2), suggesting formation through the accretion of water-rich icy planetesimals. The low oxygen-to-sulfur and carbon-to-sulfur ratios also support the planetesimal accretion formation pathway<SUP>23</SUP>.

[3] 一颗类木行星的硫化氢和金属富集大气

作为距离地球最近的凌日热木星，HD 189733b 一直是大气特性研究的基准行星。它也是我们对系外行星大气从组成、化学、气溶胶到大气动力学、大气逃逸和建模技术等理论理解的重要锚点。此前对 HD 189733b 的研究已在其大气中探测到含碳和含氧分子的 H2O 和 CO。关于 CO2 和 CH4 的存在曾有报道，但后续被质疑。基于这些观测推断出的金属丰度——追踪行星形成位置的关键参数——从贫化到富集不等，受限于观测波段覆盖和精度。本文我们报告在 HD 189733b 的透射光谱（2.4─5.0 μm）中探测到 H2O（13.4σ）、CO2（11.2σ）、CO（5σ）和 H2S（4.5σ）。在约 1,200 K 的平衡温度下，H2O、CO 和 H2S 分别是氧、碳和硫的主要储库。基于这三种主要挥发性元素的丰度，我们推断其大气金属丰度为恒星的三到五倍。对甲烷丰度的 5σ 上限为 0.1 ppm，表明碳氧比（<0.2）较低，暗示其形成过程涉及富水冰星子吸积。低氧硫比和碳硫比也支持星子吸积的形成路径。

[4] A Deep Molecular Survey of HD 189733b

2021-03-00

HD 189733b is the archetype of hot Jupiters. We propose to measure the molecular inventory and cloud composition in its atmosphere to high sensitivity. We will observe its transit spectrum over a large range of wavelength, using 5 transits: two observed with NIRCam grisms, and three with MIRI MRS. The resulting composite transit spectrum spans from 2.4 to beyond 20 microns, and includes strong bands of the major atmospheric molecules (water, methane, carbon monoxide, and carbon dioxode), as well as strong bands of important minor species (HCN, ammonia, and hydrogen sulfide). There is evidence that exoplanets show a mass-metallicity trend that differs from planets in our Solar System when water vapor (oxygen) is used as the proxy for metallicity. We will measure the atmospheric metallicity of HD 189733b to a precision of 0.025-dex using CNO elements, and determine unequivocally whether it is consistent with the Solar System's relation, or not. We will also probe atmospheric chemical disequilibrium by detecting minor species that are depleted (methane) or enhanced by vertical transport (HCN), or produced by photochemistry (hydrogen sulfide, from sulfur photochemistry). Our analysis will successfully deal with possible interference due to stellar activity, and we will measure the cloud composition using vibrational modes in the thermal IR. Although this planet transits a very bright star (Kmag=5.54), we are using moderately high spectral resolution and the observations will not be hindered by saturation. These are low-risk observations for Cycle-1 because the signals to be measured are large, and the scientific return is substantial.

[4] HD 189733b 的深度分子巡天

HD 189733b是热木星的典型代表。我们拟以高灵敏度测量其大气中的分子成分和云的组成。我们将利用5次凌星观测其透射光谱，覆盖宽广波长范围：其中两次使用NIRCam光栅，三次使用MIRI MRS。最终合成的透射光谱将覆盖2.4微米至20微米以上，包含主要大气分子（水、甲烷、一氧化碳和二氧化碳）的强吸收带，以及重要微量组分（HCN、氨和硫化氢）的强吸收带。有证据表明，当以水蒸气（氧）作为金属丰度代理时，系外行星表现出的质量-金属丰度关系与太阳系行星不同。我们将利用CNO元素将HD 189733b的大气金属丰度测量精度提升至0.025 dex，并明确判定其是否与太阳系的关系一致。我们还将通过探测因垂直输运而耗减（甲烷）或增强（HCN）、或由光化学产生（硫化氢，源自硫光化学）的微量组分，研究大气化学非平衡。我们的分析将有效处理恒星活动可能带来的干扰，并通过热红外中的振动模测量云的组成。尽管该行星凌越一颗非常明亮的恒星（Kmag=5.54），我们采用中等高光谱分辨率，观测不会受到饱和影响。这些是Cycle-1中低风险观测，因为信号强，科学回报大。

[5] Osiris revisited: Confirming a solar metallicity and low C/O in HD 209458 b

2025-08-00

HD 209458 b is the prototypical hot Jupiter and one of the best targets available for precise atmosphere characterisation. Now that spectra from both the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) are available, we can reveal the atmospheric properties in unprecedented detail. For this study we performed a new data reduction and analysis of the original HST/WFC3 spectrum, accounting for the wavelength dependence of the instrument systematics that was not considered in previous analyses. This allowed us to precisely and robustly measure the much-debated H<SUB>2</SUB>O abundance in HD 209458 b's atmosphere. We combined the newly reduced spectrum with archival JWST/NIRCam data and ran free chemistry atmospheric retrievals over the 1.0 − 5.1 μm wavelength range, covering possible features of multiple absorbing species, including CO<SUB>2</SUB>, CO, CH<SUB>4</SUB>, NH<SUB>3</SUB>, HCN, Na, SO<SUB>2</SUB>, and H<SUB>2</SUB>S. We detected H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO<SUB>2</SUB> robustly at above 7σ significance, and found a 3.6σ preference for cloudy models compared to a clear atmosphere. For all the other absorbers we tested, only the upper limits of the abundance can be measured. We used Bayesian model averaging to account for a range of different assumptions about the cloud properties, resulting in a water volume mixing ratio of 0.95<SUB>−0.17</SUB><SUP>+0.35</SUP> × solar and a carbon dioxide abundance of 0.94<SUB>−0.09</SUB><SUP>+0.16</SUP> × solar. Both results are consistent with solar values and are comparable to predictions from the VULCAN 1D photochemistry model. Combining these values with a prior on the CO abundance from ground-based measurements, we derive an overall atmospheric composition comparable to solar metallicity of [M/H] = 0.10<SUB>−0.40</SUB><SUP>+0.41</SUP> and very low C/O of 0.054<SUB>−0.034</SUB><SUP>+0.080</SUP> with a 3 σ upper limit of 0.454. This indicates a strong enrichment in oxygen and depletion in carbon during HD 209458 b's formation.

[5] 重访Osiris：确认HD 209458 b的太阳金属丰度和低C/O

HD 209458 b是热木星的原型，也是目前最适合精确大气表征的目标之一。随着哈勃空间望远镜（HST）和詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）光谱的获取，我们能够以前所未有的细节揭示其大气性质。本研究对原始HST/WFC3光谱进行了新的数据处理和分析，考虑了此前分析未涉及的仪器系统误差的波长依赖性。这使我们能够精确且稳健地测量HD 209458 b大气中备受争议的H2O丰度。我们将新处理的光谱与归档的JWST/NIRCam数据结合，在1.0−5.1 μm波段范围内进行了自由化学大气检索，涵盖了包括CO2、CO、CH4、NH3、HCN、Na、SO2和H2S等多种吸收组分的可能特征。我们以超过7σ的显著性稳健探测到H2O和CO2，并发现与无云大气相比，云模型有3.6σ的偏好。对于其他吸收体，仅能测得丰度的上限。我们采用贝叶斯模型平均法，考虑了不同云属性假设，得到水体积混合比为0.95_{−0.17}^{+0.35} × solar，二氧化碳丰度为0.94_{−0.09}^{+0.16} × solar。两者均与太阳值一致，并与VULCAN 1D光化学模型预测相当。结合地面观测对CO丰度的先验，我们推导出整体大气组成与太阳金属丰度[M/H]=0.10_{−0.40}^{+0.41}相当，C/O极低，为0.054_{−0.034}^{+0.080}，3σ上限为0.454。这表明HD 209458 b在形成过程中氧显著富集、碳显著亏损。

[6] The Watery Atmosphere of HD 209458 b Revealed by Joint K- and L-band High-resolution Spectroscopy

2025-10-00

We present a joint analysis of high-resolution K- and L-band observations of the benchmark hot Jupiter HD 209458 b from the Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer. One half-night of observations was obtained in each bandpass, covering similar preeclipse phases. The two epochs were then jointly analyzed using our atmospheric retrieval pipeline based on petitRADTRANS to constrain the atmospheric pressure─temperature profile and chemical composition. Consistent with recent results from JWST observations at lower spectral resolution, we obtain an oxygen-rich composition for HD 209458 b (C/O &lt; 10<SUP>−3</SUP> at 95% confidence) and a lower limit on the volatile metallicity similar to the solar value ([(C + O)/H] &gt; −0.2 at 95% confidence). Leveraging the large spectral grasp of the multiband observations, we constrain the atmospheric H<SUB>2</SUB>O mixing ratio to <inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mi>log</mml:mi><mml:msub><mml:mi>H</mml:mi><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:msub><mml:mi>O</mml:mi><mml:mi>V MR</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>&gt;</mml:mo><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>3.1</mml:mn></mml:math> </inline-formula> at 95% confidence, and obtain 95% upper limits on the atmospheric mixing ratios of CO (&lt;10<SUP>−4.8</SUP>), CH<SUB>4</SUB> (&lt;10<SUP>−4.5</SUP>), NH<SUB>3</SUB> (&lt;10<SUP>−5.8</SUP>), H<SUB>2</SUB>S (&lt;10<SUP>−3.3</SUP>), and HCN (&lt;10<SUP>−5.6</SUP>). The limits on CH<SUB>4</SUB>, NH<SUB>3</SUB>, and HCN are consistent with recent results from JWST transmission spectroscopy, demonstrating the value of multiband, ground-based high-resolution spectroscopy for precisely constraining trace-species abundances in exoplanet atmospheres. The retrieved low-C/O, moderate-metallicity composition for HD 209458 b is consistent with formation scenarios involving late accretion of substantial quantities of oxygen-rich refractory solids and/or ices.

[6] 通过联合K波段和L波段高分辨率光谱揭示HD 209458 b的富水大气

我们对基准热木星 HD 209458 b 的 Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer 获得的高分辨率 K 波段和 L 波段观测进行了联合分析。每个波段各获得了半夜的观测，覆盖了类似的凌前相位。随后，我们利用基于 petitRADTRANS 的大气检索流程对这两个历元的数据进行了联合分析，以约束大气的压强-温度剖面和化学组成。与近期 JWST 低光谱分辨率观测结果一致，我们获得了 HD 209458 b 的富氧组成（C/O < 10^−3，95%置信度）和与太阳值相当的挥发性金属丰度下限（[(C + O)/H] > −0.2，95%置信度）。借助多波段观测的广泛波长覆盖，我们将大气 H2O 混合比约束为 log(H2O_VMR) > −3.1（95%置信度），并获得了 CO（<10^−4.8）、CH4（<10^−4.5）、NH3（<10^−5.8）、H2S（<10^−3.3）和 HCN（<10^−5.6）的大气混合比 95% 上限。CH4、NH3 和 HCN 的上限与近期 JWST 透射光谱结果一致，显示多波段地基高分辨率光谱在精确约束系外行星大气微量组分丰度方面的价值。HD 209458 b 检索得到的低 C/O、中等金属丰度组成与其形成过程中晚期吸积大量富氧难挥发固体和/或冰的情景一致。

[7] Characterizing Exoplanetary Atmospheres at High Resolution with SPIRou: Detection of Water on HD 189733 b

2021-12-00

We present the first exoplanet atmospheric detection made as part of the SPIRou Legacy Survey, a Large Observing Program of 300 nights exploiting the capabilities of SPIRou, the new near-infrared high-resolution (R ~ 70,000) spectropolarimeter installed on the Canada-France-Hawaii Telescope (3.6 m). We observed two transits of HD 189733b, an extensively studied hot Jupiter that is known to show prominent water vapor absorption in its transmission spectrum. When combining the two transits, we successfully detect the planet's water vapor absorption at 5.9σ using a cross-correlation t-test, or with a ∆BIC &gt; 10 using a log-likelihood calculation. Using a Bayesian retrieval framework assuming parameterized temperature-pressure (T-P) profile atmospheric models, we constrain the planet atmospheric parameters, in the region probed by our transmission spectrum, to the following values: ${\mathrm{log}}_{10}\mathrm{VMR}[{{\rm{H}}}_{2}{\rm{O}}]$ = $-{4.4}_{-0.4}^{+0.4}$ , and P <SUB>cloud</SUB> ≳ 0.2 bar (gray clouds), both of which are consistent with previous studies of this planet. Our retrieved water volume-mixing ratio is slightly subsolar; although, combining it with the previously retrieved super-solar CO abundances from other studies would imply a super-solar C/O ratio. We furthermore measure a net blueshift of the planet signal of $-{4.62}_{-0.44}^{+0.46}$ km s<SUP>-1</SUP>, which is somewhat larger than many previous measurements and unlikely to result solely from winds in the planet's atmosphere, although it could possibly be explained by a transit signal dominated by the trailing limb of the planet. This large blueshift is observed in all of the different detection/retrieval methods that were performed and in each of the two transits independently.

[7] 利用 SPIRou 高分辨率表征系外行星大气：在 HD 189733 b 上探测到水

我们报告了SPIRou Legacy Survey（300夜大型观测项目，利用加拿大-法国-夏威夷望远镜3.6 m新近安装的近红外高分辨率（R ~ 70,000）光谱偏振仪SPIRou）中首次系外行星大气探测结果。我们观测了HD 189733b的两次凌星，这是一颗已被广泛研究、在透射光谱中表现出显著水蒸气吸收的热木星。将两次凌星数据合并后，我们利用互相关t检验成功以5.9σ探测到行星的水蒸气吸收，或通过对数似然计算得到∆BIC > 10。采用参数化温度-压强（T-P）剖面的大气模型进行贝叶斯检索，我们约束了透射光谱探测区域内的大气参数：log10VMR[H2O] = -4.4_{-0.4}^{+0.4}，P_cloud ≳ 0.2 bar（灰云），两者均与此前研究一致。我们检索到的水体积分混合比略低于太阳值；但结合其他研究中已检索到的超太阳CO丰度，则暗示超太阳C/O比。此外，我们测得行星信号的净蓝移为-4.62_{-0.44}^{+0.46} km s^-1，略高于许多先前测量，且不太可能仅由行星大气风引起，但可能由凌星信号主要来自行星后缘主导所致。该大蓝移在所有不同的探测/检索方法及两次凌星中均独立出现。

[8] Atmospheric escape in HD 189733b: Variable signatures of hydrogen, oxygen and carbon measured with HST

2023-01-00

One of the most well-studied exoplanets to date, HD 189733b stands out as an archetypal hot Jupiter with a wide collection of observations and theoretical models aimed at characterizing its atmosphere, interior, host star, and environment. We report here on the results of an extensive campaign to observe atmospheric escape signatures in HD 189733b using the Hubble Space Telescope and its unique ultraviolet capabilities. We have found a repeatable in-transit absorption of singly-ionized carbon and neutral oxygen in the epoch of June-July/2017. This result is, however, in tension with a previous observation with the same configuration performed in 2009. Finally, we also reproduce the observation of escaping hydrogen using a different instrument than previous detections. Our results reinforce the conclusion that the exosphere of HD 189733b is highly variable from epoch to epoch, likely in response to the changes in the high-energy input from its variable host star.

[8] HD 189733b的大气逃逸：利用HST测量的氢、氧和碳的可变特征

HD 189733b是迄今研究最为深入的系外行星之一，作为典型的热木星，其大气、内部结构、母星及环境均有大量观测和理论模型。我们在此报告利用哈勃空间望远镜及其独特的紫外能力，对HD 189733b大气逃逸特征进行的大规模观测活动结果。我们在2017年6-7月期间发现了可重复的凌日内单电离碳和中性氧吸收。然而，这一结果与2009年采用相同配置的先前观测存在张力。最后，我们还用不同于以往探测的仪器复现了逃逸氢的观测。我们的结果进一步支持HD 189733b外逸层在不同历元间高度可变，这很可能是对其变异母星高能输入变化的响应。

8 篇截止日之后发表的候选摘要 / candidate abstracts

[1] On Spectroscopic Phase-curve Retrievals: H<SUB>2</SUB> Dissociation and Thermal Inversion in the Atmosphere of the Ultrahot Jupiter WASP-103 b

2022-03-00

This work presents a reanalysis of the spectroscopic phase-curve observations of the ultrahot Jupiter WASP-103 b obtained by the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and the Spitzer Telescope. Traditional 1D and unified 1.5D spectral retrieval techniques are employed, allowing to map the thermal structure and the abundances of trace gases in this planet as a function of longitude. On the dayside, the atmosphere is found to have a strong thermal inversion, with indications of thermal dissociation traced by continuum H<SUP>-</SUP> opacity. Water vapor is found across the entire atmosphere but with depleted abundances of around 10<SUP>-5</SUP>, consistent with the thermal dissociation of this molecule. Regarding metal oxide and hydrides, FeH is detected on the hot spot and the dayside of WASP-103 b, but TiO and VO are not present in detectable quantities. Carbon-bearing species such as CO and CH<SUB>4</SUB> are also found, but since their detection is reliant on the combination of HST and Spizer, the retrieved abundances should be interpreted with caution. Free and Equilibrium chemistry retrievals are overall consistent, allowing to recover robust constraints on the metallicity and C/O ratio for this planet. The analyzed phase-curve data indicates that the atmosphere of WASP-103 b is consistent with solar elemental ratios.

[1] 关于光谱相位曲线反演：WASP-103 b超热木星大气中的H2解离与热反转

本工作对Hubble Space Telescope (HST) 和 Spitzer Telescope 获取的超热木星 WASP-103 b 的光谱相位曲线观测数据进行了重新分析。采用了传统的1D和统一的1.5D光谱检索技术，从而能够绘制该行星热结构和微量气体丰度随经度的分布。在日面，大气表现出强烈的热反转，并通过连续H<sup>-</sup>不透明度显示出热解离的迹象。整个大气中都检测到水蒸气，但丰度约为10<sup>-5</sup>，与该分子的热解离一致。关于金属氧化物和氢化物，FeH在WASP-103 b的热点和日面被探测到，但TiO和VO未达到可探测水平。还检测到含碳分子如CO和CH<sub>4</sub>，但由于其探测依赖于HST和Spitzer的联合观测，检索到的丰度应谨慎解读。自由和化学平衡检索结果总体一致，使得对该行星的金属丰度和C/O比获得了稳健约束。分析的相位曲线数据表明，WASP-103 b的大气与太阳元素比一致。

[2] On the Dayside Atmosphere of WASP-12b

2022-06-00

The atmospheric structure of WASP-12b has been hotly contested for years, with disagreements on the presence of a thermal inversion as well as the carbon-to-oxygen ratio, C/O, due to retrieved abundances of H<SUB>2</SUB>O, CO<SUB>2</SUB>, and other included species such as HCN and C<SUB>2</SUB>H<SUB>2</SUB>. Previously, these difficult-to-diagnose discrepancies have been attributed to model differences; assumptions in these models were thought to drive retrievals toward different answers. Here, we show that some of these differences are independent of model assumptions and are instead due to subtle differences in the inputs, such as the eclipse depths and line-list databases. We replicate previously published retrievals and find that the retrieved results are data driven and are mostly unaffected by the addition of species such as HCN and C<SUB>2</SUB>H<SUB>2</SUB>. We also propose a new physically motivated model that takes into consideration the formation of H<SUP>-</SUP> via the thermal dissociation of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and H<SUB>2</SUB> at the temperatures reached in the dayside atmosphere of WASP-12b, but the data's current resolution does not support its inclusion in the atmospheric model. This study raises the concern that other exoplanet retrievals may be similarly sensitive to slight changes in the input data.

[2] 关于WASP-12b的昼面大气

WASP-12b的大气结构多年来一直存在激烈争议，主要集中在是否存在热反转以及碳氧比（C/O），这与通过检索得到的H2O、CO2及其他分子（如HCN和C2H2）丰度有关。此前，这些难以诊断的差异被归因于模型差异；人们认为模型中的假设导致检索结果出现不同答案。在本研究中，我们展示了其中一些差异与模型假设无关，而是由于输入参数的细微差别，例如食深和谱线数据库。我们复现了先前发表的检索，并发现检索结果主要由数据驱动，且加入如HCN和C2H2等分子对结果影响不大。我们还提出了一个新的物理动机模型，考虑了在WASP-12b日面大气达到的高温下，H2O和H2热解离形成H-的过程，但目前数据的分辨率尚不足以支持在大气模型中加入该过程。本研究引发了一个担忧，即其他系外行星的检索结果也可能对输入数据的微小变化同样敏感。

[3] A Comprehensive Analysis of the Panchromatic Transmission Spectrum of the Hot-Saturn WASP-96 b: Nondetection of Haze, Possible Sodium Limb Asymmetry, Stellar Characterization, and Formation History

2026-03-00

We conduct a reanalysis of the JWST NIRISS/SOSS observation of the hot-Saturn WASP-96 b. Initial analysis of this data revealed an enhanced Rayleigh scattering slope at the blue end of the transmission spectrum, suggesting the presence of hazes at high altitudes. In this work, we report nondetection of this slope, confirming an atmosphere clear of high-altitude aerosols consistent with the pre-JWST results. Also contrary to the initial result, our results indicate the presence of gray cloud deck, although at relatively low altitudes/high pressures. We further combined the NIRISS/SOSS spectrum with the Very Large Telescope, Hubble Space Telescope, and Spitzer to produce a transmission spectrum from 0.35─5 μm. C/O ratio and metallicity are tentatively constrained at substellar values (<inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mspace></mml:mspace><mml:mtext>C/O</mml:mtext><mml:mspace></mml:mspace></mml:mrow><mml:mi>planet</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.5</mml:mn><mml:msubsup><mml:mn>7</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.12</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.07</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> and <inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mspace></mml:mspace><mml:mtext>[Fe/H]</mml:mtext><mml:mspace></mml:mspace></mml:mrow><mml:mi>planet</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.0</mml:mn><mml:msubsup><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.52</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.46</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> compared to C/O<SUB>star</SUB> = 0.92 ± 0.25 and [Fe/H]<SUB>star</SUB> = 0.24 ± 0.05). Inputting these composition constraints to interior structure models, we constrain a core mass of <inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mn>4</mml:mn><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>3</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>15</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>8</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math> </inline-formula> M<SUB>⊕</SUB>. We find evidence of atmospheric leading-trailing terminator asymmetries in the broadened sodium absorption feature with a transit time offset of 50 s, while the water features appear symmetric. CH<SUB>4</SUB>, CO, and CO<SUB>2</SUB> remain unconstrained due to spectral coverage limits. Upcoming JWST NIRSpec/G395H observations (ID 4082, PI: M. Radica) will be crucial for constraining these carbon-bearing species.

[3] 热土星WASP-96 b全波段透射光谱的综合分析：未探测到薄雾，可能的钠晨昏线不对称，恒星特征与形成历史

我们对热土星WASP-96 b的JWST NIRISS/SOSS观测进行了重新分析。该数据的初步分析在透射光谱蓝端显示出增强的Rayleigh散射斜率，提示高层存在薄雾。在本工作中，我们报告未探测到该斜率，确认大气中不存在高层气溶胶，这与JWST前的结果一致。同样与初步结果相反，我们的结果显示存在灰色云层，但位于相对较低高度/高压处。我们进一步将NIRISS/SOSS光谱与甚大望远镜、哈勃空间望远镜和Spitzer的数据结合，获得了0.35─5 μm的透射光谱。我们对多种化学组分的质量分数进行了约束，包括：H2O = −2.6−0.42+0.43，K = −5.7−1.13+1.05，Na = −3.4−0.92+0.90。C/O比和金属丰度初步约束为亚恒星值（C/Oplanet = 0.57−0.12+0.07，[Fe/H]planet = 0.01−0.52+0.46，相比C/Ostar = 0.92 ± 0.25，[Fe/H]star = 0.24 ± 0.05）。将这些组成约束输入内部结构模型，我们约束了核心质量为4−15+8 M⊕。此外，我们推断出超恒星的耐火元素与氧的比值（Δlog10(R/O) = 1.48−0.62+0.57）和亚恒星的C/O比，表明WASP-96 b的核心很可能形成于水冰线之外，经历了盘驱动迁移，并在碳烟线内吸积了大气。我们在钠吸收特征的展宽中发现了晨昏线不对称性，凌日时间偏移为50秒，而水的特征则表现为对称。由于光谱覆盖范围的限制，CH4、CO和CO2仍未被约束。即将进行的JWST NIRSpec/G395H观测（ID 4082，PI: M. Radica）将对这些含碳分子的约束至关重要。

[4] On the Compatibility of Ground-based and Space-based Data: WASP-96 b, an Example

2021-01-00

The study of exoplanetary atmospheres relies on detecting minute changes in the transit depth at different wavelengths. To date, a number of ground- and space-based instruments have been used to obtain transmission spectra of exoplanets in different spectral bands. One common practice is to combine observations from different instruments in order to achieve a broader wavelength coverage. We present here two inconsistent observations of WASP-96 b, one by the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and the other by the Very Large Telescope (VLT). We present two key findings in our investigation: (1) a strong water signature is detected via the HST WFC3 observations and (2) a notable offset in transit depth (&gt;1100 ppm) can be seen when the ground-based and space-based observations are combined. The discrepancy raises the question of whether observations from different instruments could indeed be combined. We attempt to align the observations by including an additional parameter in our retrieval studies but are unable to definitively ascertain that the aligned observations are indeed compatible. The case of WASP-96 b signals that compatibility of instruments should not be assumed. While wavelength overlaps between instruments can help, it should be noted that combining data sets remains risky business. The difficulty of combining observations also strengthens the need for next-generation instruments that possess broader spectral coverage. <SUP>*</SUP> These authors contributed equally to this work.

[4] 地基与空间数据的兼容性：以 WASP-96 b 为例

系外行星大气的研究依赖于在不同波长下探测凌星深度的微小变化。迄今为止，已经使用多种地面和空间仪器在不同波段获得了系外行星的透射光谱。一种常见做法是将不同仪器的观测结果结合，以获得更宽的波长覆盖范围。我们在此报告了WASP-96 b的两次不一致观测，一次来自哈勃空间望远镜（HST），另一次来自甚大望远镜（VLT）。我们的研究有两个主要发现：（1）通过HST WFC3观测检测到强烈的水吸收特征；（2）当将地面和空间观测结合时，凌星深度出现了显著偏移（>1100 ppm）。这一差异引发了一个问题：不同仪器的观测结果是否真的可以合并。我们尝试在检索研究中引入一个额外参数以对齐观测，但无法明确确定对齐后的观测确实兼容。WASP-96 b的案例表明，不应假定仪器之间具有兼容性。虽然仪器之间的波长重叠有帮助，但需要注意的是，合并数据集仍然存在风险。合并观测的难度也进一步凸显了需要下一代具有更宽光谱覆盖范围的仪器。*这些作者对本工作贡献相同。

[5] Confirmation of Water Absorption in the Thermal Emission Spectrum of the Hot Jupiter WASP-77Ab with HST/WFC3

2022-06-00

Secondary eclipse observations of hot Jupiters can reveal both their compositions and thermal structures. Previous observations have shown a diversity of hot Jupiter eclipse spectra, including absorption features, emission features, and featureless blackbody-like spectra. We present a secondary eclipse spectrum of the hot Jupiter WASP-77Ab observed between 1 and 5 μm with the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and the Spitzer Space Telescope. The HST observations show signs of water absorption indicative of a noninverted thermal structure. We fit the data with both a one-dimensional free retrieval and a grid of one-dimensional self-consistent forward models to confirm this noninverted structure. The free retrieval places a 3σ lower limit on the atmospheric water abundance of $\mathrm{log}({n}_{{{\rm{H}}}_{2}{\rm{O}}})\gt -4.78$ and cannot constrain the CO abundance. The grid fit produces a slightly superstellar metallicity and constrains the carbon-to-oxygen ratio to less than or equal to the solar value. We also compare our data to recent high-resolution observations of WASP-77Ab taken with the IGRINS/IGRINS spectrograph. We find that the best-fit model to the IGRINS data gives a reduced chi squared of χ<SUP>2</SUP> <SUB>ν</SUB> = 1.32 when compared to the WFC3 data. However, the metallicity derived from the IGRINS data is significantly lower than that derived from our self-consistent model fit. We find that this difference may be due to disequilibrium chemistry, and the varying results between the models applied here demonstrate the model dependence of derived metallicities when comparing to low-resolution, low-wavelength coverage data alone. Future work to combine observations from IGRINS, HST, and the James Webb Space Telescope will improve our estimate of the atmospheric composition of WASP-77Ab.

[5] 利用HST/WFC3确认热木星WASP-77Ab热发射光谱中的水吸收

对热木星的次生凌星观测可以揭示其组成和热结构。以往观测显示热木星的凌星光谱多样，包括吸收特征、发射特征和无特征的黑体型光谱。我们展示了用Hubble Space Telescope (HST) 和 Spitzer Space Telescope在1至5 μm波段观测的热木星WASP-77Ab的次生凌星光谱。HST观测显示出水吸收特征，表明其热结构未发生反转。我们用一维自由检索和一维自洽前向模型网格拟合数据，均证实了这一非反转结构。自由检索对大气水丰度给出了3σ下限：$\mathrm{log}({n}_{{{\rm{H}}}_{2}{\rm{O}}})\gt -4.78$，但无法约束CO丰度。模型网格拟合结果显示金属丰度略高于太阳值，并将C/O比约束在不高于太阳值。我们还将数据与近期IGRINS/IGRINS光谱仪对WASP-77Ab的高分辨率观测进行了比较。我们发现，IGRINS数据的最佳拟合模型与WFC3数据相比，得到的归一化卡方为χ<sup>2</sup><sub>ν</sub> = 1.32。然而，IGRINS数据推导的金属丰度显著低于我们的自洽模型拟合结果。我们认为这种差异可能源于非平衡化学，不同模型的结果也表明仅依赖低分辨率、窄波段数据时推导金属丰度具有模型依赖性。未来结合IGRINS、HST和James Webb Space Telescope观测将有助于改进对WASP-77Ab大气组成的估计。

[6] A Combined Ground-based and JWST Atmospheric Retrieval Analysis: Both IGRINS and NIRSpec Agree that the Atmosphere of WASP-77A b Is Metal-poor

2024-03-00

Ground-based high-resolution and space-based low-resolution spectroscopy are the two main avenues through which transiting exoplanet atmospheres are studied. Both methods provide unique strengths and shortcomings, and combining the two can be a powerful probe into an exoplanet's atmosphere. Within a joint atmospheric retrieval framework, we combined JWST NIRSpec/G395H secondary eclipse spectra and Gemini South/IGRINS pre- and post-eclipse thermal emission observations of the hot Jupiter WASP-77A b. Our inferences from the IGRINS and NIRSpec data sets are consistent with each other, and combining the two allows us to measure the gas abundances of H<SUB>2</SUB>O and CO, as well as the vertical thermal structure, with higher precision than either data set provided individually. We confirm WASP-77A b's subsolar metallicity ([(C+O)/H] = −0.61 ${}_{-0.09}^{+0.10})$ and solar C/O ratio (C/O = 0.57 ${}_{-0.06}^{+0.06})$ . The two types of data are complementary, and our abundance inferences are mostly driven by the IGRINS data, while inference of the thermal structure is driven by the NIRSpec data. Our ability to draw inferences from the post-eclipse IGRINS data is highly sensitive to the number of singular values removed in the detrending process, potentially due to high and variable humidity. We also search for signatures for atmospheric dynamics in the IGRINS data and find that propagated ephemeris error can manifest as either an orbital eccentricity or a strong equatorial jet. Neither are detected when using more up-to-date ephemerides. However, we find moderate evidence of thermal inhomogeneity and measure a cooler nightside that presents itself in the later phases after secondary eclipse.

[6] 地面与JWST联合大气检索分析：IGRINS和NIRSpec均认为WASP-77A b大气金属贫乏

地基高分辨率和空间低分辨率光谱是研究凌日系外行星大气的两种主要途径。两种方法各有独特优势和局限，结合使用可成为探测系外行星大气的有力手段。在联合大气检索框架下，我们结合了 JWST NIRSpec/G395H 次生凌星发射光谱和 Gemini South/IGRINS 次生凌星前后热发射观测数据，对热木星 WASP-77A b 进行了分析。IGRINS 和 NIRSpec 数据集的推断结果相互一致，联合分析使我们能比单独数据集更高精度地测量 H2O 和 CO 的气体丰度及垂直温度结构。我们确认 WASP-77A b 的亚太阳金属丰度（[(C+O)/H] = −0.61 ${}_{-0.09}^{+0.10}$）和太阳 C/O 比（C/O = 0.57 ${}_{-0.06}^{+0.06}$）。两类数据互为补充，丰度推断主要由 IGRINS 数据驱动，温度结构推断则由 NIRSpec 数据驱动。我们从次生凌星后 IGRINS 数据中推断的能力对去趋势过程中去除奇异值的数量高度敏感，可能与高且变化的湿度有关。我们还在 IGRINS 数据中搜索大气动力学特征，发现历元误差传播可表现为轨道偏心率或强赤道喷流，但使用最新历元时均未探测到。然而，我们发现中等证据表明存在热不均匀性，并测得次生凌星后期呈现较冷的夜面。

[7] Spatial characterization of the trailing and leading limbs of WASP-76b: Detection of H2O and HCN at high-resolution

2021-09-00

Extreme temperature contrasts between the day and nightside of ultra-hot Jupiters (UHJ) result in significantly asymmetric atmospheres, with a region of extreme atmospheric expansion appearing over a small range of latitudes around the terminator. Over the course of a transit, WASP-76 b rotates by about 30° and hence temporal variations of the observable atmosphere could significantly affect the detectability of its constituents. Specifically, the trailing limb of this planet allows us to probe a significant portion of the inflated dayside, resulting in a higher atmospheric detectability. This geometric effect could mimic the observed time-variability of absorption signals due to condensation in the nightside of these planets, which has been recently reported for neutral iron in WASP-76 b. By studying molecules that are not expected to condense in the nightside of UHJs (~1000K), we can isolate the possible effect of different day and nightside scale heights. Here, we will analyze a stronger water vapor signal during the egress of the planet than at ingress, which cannot be explained by condensation and suggests that the extreme geometry of UHJ manifests itself as time-dependent absorption signals. Additionally, we report a redshifted HCN signature arising from the leading limb (i.e., observable in the first half of the transit and absent from the second half) and a weak evidence of ammonia using high-resolution observations of WASP-76 b with CARMENES.

[7] WASP-76b 前后肢的空间特征：高分辨率下 H2O 和 HCN 的探测

超热木星（UHJ）昼夜温度极端反差导致其大气显著不对称，在晨昏线附近一小范围纬度区域出现极端大气膨胀。在凌星过程中，WASP-76 b自转约30°，因此可观测大气的时变性可能显著影响其组分的可探测性。具体而言，该行星的后缘允许我们探测到膨胀的白昼面的大部分区域，从而提高了大气的可探测性。这一几何效应可能会模拟由于这些行星夜面冷凝导致的吸收信号的时间变化，近期在WASP-76 b中性铁的观测中已有报道。通过研究在UHJ夜面（约1000K）不易冷凝的分子，我们可以区分昼夜面不同尺度高度的可能影响。本文分析了该行星凌出阶段水蒸气信号强于凌入阶段的现象，这无法用冷凝解释，表明UHJ极端几何结构表现为时变吸收信号。此外，我们报告了来自前缘的红移HCN特征（即仅在凌星前半段可见，后半段消失），以及利用CARMENES对WASP-76 b高分辨率观测获得的氨的微弱证据。

[8] Parameter degeneracies associated with interpreting HST WFC3 transmission spectra of exoplanetary atmospheres

2025-04-00

The Wide Field Camera 3 (WFC3) instrument on the Hubble Space Telescope has provided an abundance of exoplanet spectra over the years. These spectra have enabled analysis studies using atmospheric retrievals to constrain the properties of these objects. However, follow-up observations from the JWST have called into question some of the results from these older datasets, and highlighted the need to properly understand the degeneracies associated with retrievals of WFC3 spectra. In this study, we perform atmospheric retrievals of 38 transmission spectra from WFC3 and use model comparison to determine the complexity required to fit the data. We explore the effect of retrieving system parameters such as the stellar radius and planet's surface gravity, and thoroughly investigate the degeneracies between individual model parameters - specifically the temperature, abundance of water, and cloud-top level. We focus on three case studies (HD 209458b, WASP-12b, and WASP-39b) in an attempt to diagnose some of the issues with these retrievals, in particular the low retrieved temperatures when compared to the equilibrium values. Our study advocates for the careful consideration of parameter degeneracies when interpreting retrieval results, as well as the importance of wider wavelength coverage to break these degeneracies, in agreement with previous studies. The combination of data from multiple instruments, as well as analysis from multiple data reductions and retrieval codes, will allow us to robustly characterize the atmosphere of these exoplanets.

[8] 解释系外行星大气HST WFC3透射光谱时的参数简并性

哈勃空间望远镜上的宽视场相机3（WFC3）仪器多年来提供了大量系外行星光谱。这些光谱使得通过大气检索分析这些天体的性质成为可能。然而，JWST的后续观测对这些早期数据集的一些结果提出了质疑，并强调了充分理解WFC3光谱检索相关参数退化的重要性。在本研究中，我们对38个WFC3透射光谱进行了大气检索，并通过模型比较确定拟合数据所需的模型复杂度。我们探讨了检索系统参数（如恒星半径和行星表面重力）的影响，并详细研究了各模型参数之间的退化关系，特别是温度、水丰度和云顶高度。我们重点分析了三个案例（HD 209458b、WASP-12b和WASP-39b），试图诊断这些检索中存在的问题，尤其是检索到的温度低于平衡温度的情况。我们的研究建议在解释检索结果时需谨慎考虑参数退化，并强调拓宽波长覆盖范围以打破这些退化的重要性，这与先前研究一致。多仪器数据的结合，以及多种数据处理和检索代码的分析，将有助于我们对这些系外行星大气进行稳健表征。

Analyses [2016A&A...596A..90V] and [2017A&A...606A.144A] of HD 189733B's atmosphere reach potentially conflicting conclusions regarding water: one reports "We find good agreement between our models and the estimated water abundance for WASP-43b.", while the other reports "We place a 5-σ upper limit of 100 ppm on the strength of the 6500 Å water vapor band.". Under what observational or methodological conditions can these results be reconciled, and which currently untested assumption drives the disagreement?
