[1] Characterizing Exoplanetary Atmospheres at High Resolution with SPIRou: Detection of Water on HD 189733 b

2021-12-00

We present the first exoplanet atmospheric detection made as part of the SPIRou Legacy Survey, a Large Observing Program of 300 nights exploiting the capabilities of SPIRou, the new near-infrared high-resolution (R ~ 70,000) spectropolarimeter installed on the Canada-France-Hawaii Telescope (3.6 m). We observed two transits of HD 189733b, an extensively studied hot Jupiter that is known to show prominent water vapor absorption in its transmission spectrum. When combining the two transits, we successfully detect the planet's water vapor absorption at 5.9σ using a cross-correlation t-test, or with a ∆BIC &gt; 10 using a log-likelihood calculation. Using a Bayesian retrieval framework assuming parameterized temperature-pressure (T-P) profile atmospheric models, we constrain the planet atmospheric parameters, in the region probed by our transmission spectrum, to the following values: ${\mathrm{log}}_{10}\mathrm{VMR}[{{\rm{H}}}_{2}{\rm{O}}]$ = $-{4.4}_{-0.4}^{+0.4}$ , and P <SUB>cloud</SUB> ≳ 0.2 bar (gray clouds), both of which are consistent with previous studies of this planet. Our retrieved water volume-mixing ratio is slightly subsolar; although, combining it with the previously retrieved super-solar CO abundances from other studies would imply a super-solar C/O ratio. We furthermore measure a net blueshift of the planet signal of $-{4.62}_{-0.44}^{+0.46}$ km s<SUP>-1</SUP>, which is somewhat larger than many previous measurements and unlikely to result solely from winds in the planet's atmosphere, although it could possibly be explained by a transit signal dominated by the trailing limb of the planet. This large blueshift is observed in all of the different detection/retrieval methods that were performed and in each of the two transits independently.

[1] 利用 SPIRou 高分辨率表征系外行星大气：在 HD 189733 b 上探测到水

我们报告了SPIRou Legacy Survey（300夜大型观测项目，利用加拿大-法国-夏威夷望远镜3.6 m新近安装的近红外高分辨率（R ~ 70,000）光谱偏振仪SPIRou）中首次系外行星大气探测结果。我们观测了HD 189733b的两次凌星，这是一颗已被广泛研究、在透射光谱中表现出显著水蒸气吸收的热木星。将两次凌星数据合并后，我们利用互相关t检验成功以5.9σ探测到行星的水蒸气吸收，或通过对数似然计算得到∆BIC > 10。采用参数化温度-压强（T-P）剖面的大气模型进行贝叶斯检索，我们约束了透射光谱探测区域内的大气参数：log10VMR[H2O] = -4.4_{-0.4}^{+0.4}，P_cloud ≳ 0.2 bar（灰云），两者均与此前研究一致。我们检索到的水体积分混合比略低于太阳值；但结合其他研究中已检索到的超太阳CO丰度，则暗示超太阳C/O比。此外，我们测得行星信号的净蓝移为-4.62_{-0.44}^{+0.46} km s^-1，略高于许多先前测量，且不太可能仅由行星大气风引起，但可能由凌星信号主要来自行星后缘主导所致。该大蓝移在所有不同的探测/检索方法及两次凌星中均独立出现。

[2] Water observed in the atmosphere of τ Boötis Ab with CARMENES/CAHA

2022-08-00

Characterizing the atmospheres of hot Jupiters is important in understanding the formation and migration of these exotic planets. However, there are still many open questions about the chemical and physical properties of these atmospheres. Here, we confirm the detection of water vapour in thermal emission from the non-transiting hot Jupiter τ Boötis Ab with the high-resolution NIR CARMENES spectrograph. Combining over 17 h of observations (560 spectra) and using a Bayesian cross-correlation to log-likelihood approach, we measure a systemic velocity of $V_{\mathrm{sys}} = -11.51^{+0.59}_{-0.60}$ km s<SUP>-1</SUP> and a radial velocity semi-amplitude of $K_{\mathrm{P}} = 106.21^{+1.76}_{-1.71}$ km s<SUP>-1</SUP> for the planet, which results in an absolute mass of $M_{\mathrm{P}} = 6.24^{+0.17}_{-0.18}\, \mathrm{M_{J}}$ and an orbital inclination of $41.6^{+1.0}_{-0.9}$ degrees. Our retrieved V<SUB>sys</SUB> shows a significant shift (+5 km s<SUP>-1</SUP>) from the literature value, which could be caused by an inaccurate time of periastron. Within the explored model grid, we measure a preference for solar water abundance (VMR = 10<SUP>-3</SUP>) and find no evidence for additional minor species in the atmosphere. Given the extensive orbital coverage of the data, we searched for a phase dependency in the water signal but found no strong evidence of variation with orbital phase. This detection is at odds with recent observations from SPIRou/CFHT and their tight upper limit on water abundance. We recommend further observations of the atmosphere τ Boötis Ab to try and resolve these discrepancies.

[2] 利用CARMENES/CAHA在τ Boötis Ab大气中观测到水

对热木星大气的刻画对于理解这些特殊行星的形成和迁移过程至关重要。然而，这些大气的化学和物理性质仍存在许多未解之谜。在本研究中，我们利用高分辨率近红外CARMENES光谱仪，确认了非凌星热木星τ Boötis Ab热发射中的水蒸气探测。我们结合超过17小时（560个光谱）的观测数据，并采用贝叶斯互相关到对数似然的方法，测得行星的系统速度为V_sys = -11.51^{+0.59}_{-0.60} km s^-1，径向速度半振幅为K_P = 106.21^{+1.76}_{-1.71} km s^-1，对应行星绝对质量M_P = 6.24^{+0.17}_{-0.18} M_J，轨道倾角为41.6^{+1.0}_{-0.9}度。我们反演得到的V_sys与文献值相比有显著偏移（+5 km s^-1），可能由近日点时刻不准确导致。在所探索的模型网格中，我们测得太阳丰度的水（VMR = 10^-3）最为优选，且未发现大气中存在其他微量分子的证据。鉴于数据覆盖了广泛的轨道相位，我们搜索了水信号的相位依赖性，但未发现随轨道相位有显著变化。该探测结果与SPIRou/CFHT的最新观测及其对水丰度的严格上限不符。我们建议对τ Boötis Ab大气进行进一步观测，以尝试解决这些差异。

[3] Where are the Water Worlds?: Self-consistent Models of Water-rich Exoplanet Atmospheres

2023-08-00

It remains to be ascertained whether sub-Neptune exoplanets primarily possess hydrogen-rich atmospheres or whether a population of H<SUB>2</SUB>O-rich water worlds lurks in their midst. Addressing this question requires improved modeling of water-rich exoplanetary atmospheres, both to predict and interpret spectroscopic observations and to serve as upper boundary conditions on interior structure calculations. Here, we present new models of hydrogen-helium-water atmospheres with water abundances ranging from solar to 100% water vapor. We improve upon previous models of high-water-content atmospheres by incorporating updated prescriptions for water self-broadening and a nonideal gas equation of state. Our model grid (https://umd.box.com/v/water-worlds) includes temperature─pressure profiles in radiative-convective equilibrium, along with their associated transmission and thermal emission spectra. We find that our model updates primarily act at high pressures, significantly impacting bottom-of-atmosphere temperatures, with implications for the accuracy of interior structure calculations. Upper-atmosphere conditions and spectroscopic observables are less impacted by our model updates, and we find that, under most conditions, retrieval codes built for hot Jupiters should also perform well on water-rich planets. We additionally quantify the observational degeneracies among both thermal emission and transmission spectra. We recover standard degeneracies with clouds and mean molecular weight for transmission spectra, and we find thermal emission spectra to be more readily distinguishable from one another in the water-poor (i.e., near-solar) regime.

[3] 水世界在哪里？：水丰富系外行星大气的自洽模型

目前尚不清楚亚海王星类系外行星是否主要拥有富氢大气，或其间是否存在一类富H2O的水世界。解决这一问题需要改进对富水系外行星大气的建模，以预测和解释光谱观测结果，并为内部结构计算提供上边界条件。本文提出了新的氢-氦-水大气模型，水丰度从太阳丰度到100%水蒸气。我们在以往高水含量大气模型基础上，加入了更新的水自展宽处理和非理想气体状态方程。我们的模型网格（https://umd.box.com/v/water-worlds）包括辐射-对流平衡下的温度-压力剖面及其对应的透射和热发射光谱。我们发现，模型更新主要影响高压区，显著改变大气底部温度，这对内部结构计算的准确性有重要影响。高层大气条件和光谱可观测量受模型更新影响较小，我们发现，在大多数情况下，为热木星开发的检索代码同样适用于富水行星。我们还量化了热发射和透射光谱之间的观测退化性。我们重现了透射光谱中与云和平均分子量的标准退化性，并发现热发射光谱在贫水（即近太阳丰度）情形下更易于区分。

[4] Confirmation of Water Absorption in the Thermal Emission Spectrum of the Hot Jupiter WASP-77Ab with HST/WFC3

2022-06-00

Secondary eclipse observations of hot Jupiters can reveal both their compositions and thermal structures. Previous observations have shown a diversity of hot Jupiter eclipse spectra, including absorption features, emission features, and featureless blackbody-like spectra. We present a secondary eclipse spectrum of the hot Jupiter WASP-77Ab observed between 1 and 5 μm with the Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and the Spitzer Space Telescope. The HST observations show signs of water absorption indicative of a noninverted thermal structure. We fit the data with both a one-dimensional free retrieval and a grid of one-dimensional self-consistent forward models to confirm this noninverted structure. The free retrieval places a 3σ lower limit on the atmospheric water abundance of $\mathrm{log}({n}_{{{\rm{H}}}_{2}{\rm{O}}})\gt -4.78$ and cannot constrain the CO abundance. The grid fit produces a slightly superstellar metallicity and constrains the carbon-to-oxygen ratio to less than or equal to the solar value. We also compare our data to recent high-resolution observations of WASP-77Ab taken with the IGRINS/IGRINS spectrograph. We find that the best-fit model to the IGRINS data gives a reduced chi squared of χ<SUP>2</SUP> <SUB>ν</SUB> = 1.32 when compared to the WFC3 data. However, the metallicity derived from the IGRINS data is significantly lower than that derived from our self-consistent model fit. We find that this difference may be due to disequilibrium chemistry, and the varying results between the models applied here demonstrate the model dependence of derived metallicities when comparing to low-resolution, low-wavelength coverage data alone. Future work to combine observations from IGRINS, HST, and the James Webb Space Telescope will improve our estimate of the atmospheric composition of WASP-77Ab.

[4] 利用HST/WFC3确认热木星WASP-77Ab热发射光谱中的水吸收

对热木星的次生凌星观测可以揭示其组成和热结构。以往观测显示热木星的凌星光谱多样，包括吸收特征、发射特征和无特征的黑体型光谱。我们展示了用Hubble Space Telescope (HST) 和 Spitzer Space Telescope在1至5 μm波段观测的热木星WASP-77Ab的次生凌星光谱。HST观测显示出水吸收特征，表明其热结构未发生反转。我们用一维自由检索和一维自洽前向模型网格拟合数据，均证实了这一非反转结构。自由检索对大气水丰度给出了3σ下限：$\mathrm{log}({n}_{{{\rm{H}}}_{2}{\rm{O}}})\gt -4.78$，但无法约束CO丰度。模型网格拟合结果显示金属丰度略高于太阳值，并将C/O比约束在不高于太阳值。我们还将数据与近期IGRINS/IGRINS光谱仪对WASP-77Ab的高分辨率观测进行了比较。我们发现，IGRINS数据的最佳拟合模型与WFC3数据相比，得到的归一化卡方为χ<sup>2</sup><sub>ν</sub> = 1.32。然而，IGRINS数据推导的金属丰度显著低于我们的自洽模型拟合结果。我们认为这种差异可能源于非平衡化学，不同模型的结果也表明仅依赖低分辨率、窄波段数据时推导金属丰度具有模型依赖性。未来结合IGRINS、HST和James Webb Space Telescope观测将有助于改进对WASP-77Ab大气组成的估计。

[5] Helium Atmospheres May Hide in Current Exoplanet Analysis Frameworks

2026-01-00

The increasing number of detailed exoplanet observations offers an opportunity to refine our analyses and interpretations. Here, we show that atmospheres that appear volatile-rich and/or cloudy may instead be helium-rich. As transmission spectra constrain the atmospheric scale height (H), a He-enriched atmosphere can be misinterpreted as H<SUB>2</SUB>-dominated water-rich to bring the mean molecular weight (μ) to intermediate values (∼4−10) when He/H<SUB>2</SUB> is fixed. Similarly, a cloud deck can reduce the spectral features, and thus the apparent (i.e., cloud-free equivalent) H. We present a proof-of-concept reanalysis of HD 209458 b's JWST transmission spectrum treating He/H<SUB>2</SUB> as a free parameter, resulting in sets of He-rich solutions. We argue that He enhancement must be considered to reliably constrain atmospheric composition, be sensitive to a more diverse planetary population, and ultimately yield robust trends to inform formation and evolution pathways. Looking ahead, we suggest leveraging insights from differences in pressure-broadening effects, outflow measurements, and atmospheric chemistry to disentangle reliably between He-, volatile-rich, and cloudy atmospheres—while recognizing that associated models need targeted upgrades to reach the fidelity level required to this end.

[5] 氦大气可能隐藏在当前系外行星分析框架中

越来越多的系外行星详细观测为我们改进分析和解释提供了机会。本文展示了那些看似富含挥发物和/或多云的大气，实际上可能是富含He的大气。由于透射光谱约束了大气的尺度高度（H），当He/H2固定时，He富集的大气可能会被误解为H2主导的富水大气，从而使平均分子量（μ）达到中间值（约4−10）。类似地，云层也会减弱光谱特征，从而降低表观（即无云等效）H。我们以HD 209458 b的JWST透射光谱为例，进行概念验证性再分析，将He/H2作为自由参数，得到了多组富He解。我们认为，必须考虑He的增强，才能可靠约束大气组成，对更为多样的行星族群保持敏感，并最终获得稳健的趋势以指导形成和演化路径。展望未来，我们建议利用压强展宽效应差异、外流测量和大气化学等方面的见解，来可靠区分He富、富挥发物和多云大气——同时认识到相关模型需要有针对性地升级，以达到所需的精度水平。

[6] Methods for Incorporating Model Uncertainty into Exoplanet Atmospheric Analysis

2024-04-00

A key goal of exoplanet spectroscopy is to measure atmospheric properties, such as abundances of chemical species, in order to connect them to our understanding of atmospheric physics and planet formation. In this new era of high-quality JWST data, it is paramount that these measurement methods are robust. When comparing atmospheric models to observations, multiple candidate models may produce reasonable fits to the data. Typically, conclusions are reached by selecting the best-performing model according to some metric. This ignores model uncertainty in favour of specific model assumptions, potentially leading to measured atmospheric properties that are overconfident and/or incorrect. In this talk, we will present a case study highlighting these effects. The hot Jupiter HD 209458b is one of the most extensively studied exoplanets, boasting one of the highest-quality spectra obtained with HST. Despite this, past efforts to measure the abundance of water vapor in its atmosphere have led to a range of different conclusions, with some studies finding that water is depleted relative to equilibrium expectations and others disputing this. We finally resolved this apparent contradiction using a new ensemble approach to atmospheric retrieval, whereby results from multiple models are combined in a statistically sound manner to account for model uncertainty. We showed that when a range of reasonable models are considered, the uncertainty on the measured water abundance ranges from depleted to slightly enhanced relative to equilibrium. In this case, the results are strongly influenced by the chosen approach to modeling clouds and hazes. As we start to analyse JWST data, it is critical that we do not fall into the same trap, and that we consider effects of model uncertainty to avoid overconfident and biased inferences.

[6] 将模型不确定性纳入系外行星大气分析的方法

系外行星光谱学的一个关键目标是测量大气属性，如化学组分的丰度，以便将其与我们对大气物理和行星形成的理解联系起来。在JWST高质量数据的新时代，确保这些测量方法的稳健性至关重要。在将大气模型与观测数据进行比较时，多个候选模型可能都能较好地拟合数据。通常，结论是通过根据某种指标选择表现最好的模型得出的。这种做法忽略了模型不确定性，偏向于特定模型假设，可能导致对大气属性的测量过于自信和/或不正确。在本报告中，我们将通过一个案例研究来突出这些影响。热木星HD 209458b是研究最为深入的系外行星之一，拥有用HST获得的最高质量光谱之一。尽管如此，过去对其大气中水汽丰度的测量却得出了不同的结论，一些研究发现水丰度低于平衡预期，另一些则对此提出异议。我们最终通过一种新的集合反演方法解决了这一表面上的矛盾，该方法以统计上合理的方式结合多个模型的结果，从而考虑模型不确定性。我们显示，当考虑一系列合理模型时，测得的水丰度的不确定性范围从低于到略高于平衡值。在本案例中，结果受云和薄雾建模方法的影响很大。随着我们开始分析JWST数据，必须避免重蹈覆辙，充分考虑模型不确定性，以避免过度自信和有偏推断。

[7] The Watery Atmosphere of HD 209458 b Revealed by Joint K- and L-band High-resolution Spectroscopy

2025-10-00

We present a joint analysis of high-resolution K- and L-band observations of the benchmark hot Jupiter HD 209458 b from the Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer. One half-night of observations was obtained in each bandpass, covering similar preeclipse phases. The two epochs were then jointly analyzed using our atmospheric retrieval pipeline based on petitRADTRANS to constrain the atmospheric pressure─temperature profile and chemical composition. Consistent with recent results from JWST observations at lower spectral resolution, we obtain an oxygen-rich composition for HD 209458 b (C/O &lt; 10<SUP>−3</SUP> at 95% confidence) and a lower limit on the volatile metallicity similar to the solar value ([(C + O)/H] &gt; −0.2 at 95% confidence). Leveraging the large spectral grasp of the multiband observations, we constrain the atmospheric H<SUB>2</SUB>O mixing ratio to <inline-formula> <mml:math><mml:mi>log</mml:mi><mml:msub><mml:mi>H</mml:mi><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:msub><mml:mi>O</mml:mi><mml:mi>V MR</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>&gt;</mml:mo><mml:mo>−</mml:mo><mml:mn>3.1</mml:mn></mml:math> </inline-formula> at 95% confidence, and obtain 95% upper limits on the atmospheric mixing ratios of CO (&lt;10<SUP>−4.8</SUP>), CH<SUB>4</SUB> (&lt;10<SUP>−4.5</SUP>), NH<SUB>3</SUB> (&lt;10<SUP>−5.8</SUP>), H<SUB>2</SUB>S (&lt;10<SUP>−3.3</SUP>), and HCN (&lt;10<SUP>−5.6</SUP>). The limits on CH<SUB>4</SUB>, NH<SUB>3</SUB>, and HCN are consistent with recent results from JWST transmission spectroscopy, demonstrating the value of multiband, ground-based high-resolution spectroscopy for precisely constraining trace-species abundances in exoplanet atmospheres. The retrieved low-C/O, moderate-metallicity composition for HD 209458 b is consistent with formation scenarios involving late accretion of substantial quantities of oxygen-rich refractory solids and/or ices.

[7] 通过联合K波段和L波段高分辨率光谱揭示HD 209458 b的富水大气

我们对基准热木星 HD 209458 b 的 Keck Planet Imager and Characterizer 获得的高分辨率 K 波段和 L 波段观测进行了联合分析。每个波段各获得了半夜的观测，覆盖了类似的凌前相位。随后，我们利用基于 petitRADTRANS 的大气检索流程对这两个历元的数据进行了联合分析，以约束大气的压强-温度剖面和化学组成。与近期 JWST 低光谱分辨率观测结果一致，我们获得了 HD 209458 b 的富氧组成（C/O < 10^−3，95%置信度）和与太阳值相当的挥发性金属丰度下限（[(C + O)/H] > −0.2，95%置信度）。借助多波段观测的广泛波长覆盖，我们将大气 H2O 混合比约束为 log(H2O_VMR) > −3.1（95%置信度），并获得了 CO（<10^−4.8）、CH4（<10^−4.5）、NH3（<10^−5.8）、H2S（<10^−3.3）和 HCN（<10^−5.6）的大气混合比 95% 上限。CH4、NH3 和 HCN 的上限与近期 JWST 透射光谱结果一致，显示多波段地基高分辨率光谱在精确约束系外行星大气微量组分丰度方面的价值。HD 209458 b 检索得到的低 C/O、中等金属丰度组成与其形成过程中晚期吸积大量富氧难挥发固体和/或冰的情景一致。

[8] WASP-127b: a misaligned planet with a partly cloudy atmosphereand tenuous sodium signature seen by ESPRESSO

2021-09-00

During the past few years, exoplanet atmospheric studies have grown tremendously. Detections of atomic and molecular species are reported from the ultraviolet to the infrared at low- and high-resolution for Earth-mass to Jupiter-mass planets and from temperate to ultra-hot worlds. Nowadays, ground-based high-resolution spectrographs are starting to produce groundbreaking results that inform us of the temperature structure and dynamics of exoplanet atmospheres. A reference system for atmospheric studies: WASP-127bWASP-127b (Lam et al. 2017) orbits a bright (V = 10.17) G5-type star with a period of 4.18 days. The host star is at the end of its main sequence phase with an age of ~10Gyr and radius of 1.30RS and has entered the sub-giant phase. Therefore, the planet might be going through an inflation process for the second time (Lopez &amp; Fortney 2016), leading to its large radius (~1.31RJ). WASP-127b is a lukewarm (~600 times Earth irradiation) Saturn at the right mass border of the evaporation desert. Assuming a hydrogen-helium composition, its atmospheric scale height is about 2100 km. In this case, the signal in transmission for one scale height is around 420 ppm, potentially making this planet one of the best among those of its class to study exo-atmospheres through transmission spectroscopy.Near-infrared low-resolution data obtained with the Hubble Space Telescope (Spake et al. 2020) have revealed the strongest water band amplitude in an exoplanet with a mean amplitude of about 800ppm. Moreover, the presence of clouds and hazes is necessary to explain the complete spectrum. Such planets with a high-amplitude water signature in the J-band are well placed to be studied from the ground with visible and NIR spectrographs, allowing the impact of hazes and clouds to be analyzed by measuring the water content at different wavelengths. Observations of WASP-127b with ESPRESSOESPRESSO is a fiber-fed, ultra-stabilized high-resolution echelle spectrograph installed at Paranal. It can collect the light from each 8m Unit Telescope (UT) individually or the 4UTs simultaneously of the Very Large Telescope (VLT). Two transits of WASP-127b were obtained within the framework of the GTO consortium. We published our results in Allart et al. 2020, which includes the first measurement of spin-orbit alignment of this system revealing the peculiar orbital architecture of the system. The old star WASP-127 is a slow rotator while its planet has a misaligned retrograde orbit (Fig. 2). This is surprising as stars with Teff below 6250K are supposed to have aligned systems. An explanation could be that WASP-127b remained trapped in a Kozai resonance with an outer companion and only recently migrated close to its star.A view on the atmosphere of WASP-127bWe used the ESPRESSO data to analyze the transmission spectrum of the exoplanet. Visible high-resolution datasets are known to reveal the exoplanet thermosphere through atomic species as the well-known sodium doublet. The WASP-127b transit datasets have revealed an excess of absorption of 0.34 +/- 0.04% at the expected position of the sodium doublet corresponding to a small extension over 7 scale heights. However, we do not detect the presence of other atomic species but we were able to set upper limits of only a few scale heights.We also undertake a thorough search for water vapor at visible wavelengths. To do so, we used the well-known cross-correlation function technique to average thousands of water lines in velocity space. To be as model-independent as possible, we applied binary masks containing the position of water lines at different temperatures and with an increasing number of lines. We showcased that such a technique is sensitive enough to retrieve planetary signals by doing an injection recovery test. Once applied to the transit datasets of WASP-127b, we were able to put a 3-sigma upper limit on the presence of water vapor at visible wavelengths of only 38ppm on the average depth of the 1600 strongest water lines at the equilibrium temperature. This upper limit corresponds to an atmospheric extension of only 0.08 scale height. Combining low- and high-resolution spectroscopyBased on this unique precision obtained with ESPRESSO, we explored the compatibility between ground-based high-resolution spectroscopy and space-based low-resolution spectroscopy. We computed a grid of models with different grey cloud-deck pressure based on the results of Spake et al. 2020 and using the pi-eta line-by-line radiative transfer code (Pino et al 2018). On one hand, models with grey cloud deck pressure ranging from 0.3 to 1 mbar are compatible with the HST datasets, while in another hand only models with pressure below 0.5 mbar are compatible with the ESPRESSO datasets. Therefore, by combining the low- and high-resolution datasets of WASP-127b, we can constrain the grey cloud pressure between 0.3 and 0.5 mbar.ConclusionWe used two transits of WASP-127b obtained with ESPRESSO to acquire a thorough understanding of the orbital architecture of the system and the exoplanet atmosphere.We propose a new framework to search for water vapor and other molecular species at high resolution. Despite the good data quality, we did not detect water vapor. However, we combined this result with a low-resolution detection of water at 1.3 microns to constrain the presence of clouds in the atmosphere of WASP-127b.To conclude, we report for the first time that high-resolution visible data can be used to differentiate between cloudy and cloud-free exoplanets by measuring the water content and can also provide essential information on the cloud-deck pressure. The framework developed here to measure this water content will be applied to other exoplanets in the ESPRESSO GTO atmospheric survey and other surveys such as the NIRPS GTO atmospheric survey. R., et al. 2020, MNRAS

[8] WASP-127b：一颗轨道倾斜、部分多云且通过 ESPRESSO 观测到微弱钠特征的行星

在过去几年中，系外行星大气研究取得了巨大进展。从紫外到红外、从低分辨率到高分辨率、从地球质量到木星质量、从温和到超热的行星，原子和分子物种的探测不断被报道。如今，地基高分辨率光谱仪开始产出突破性成果，为我们揭示了系外行星大气的温度结构和动力学。大气研究的参考系统：WASP-127bWASP-127b（Lam et al. 2017）围绕一颗亮（V = 10.17）的G5型恒星公转，周期为4.18天。该母星正处于主序末期，年龄约10 Gyr，半径为1.30RS，已进入次巨星阶段。因此，该行星可能正经历第二次膨胀过程（Lopez & Fortney 2016），导致其半径较大（约1.31RJ）。WASP-127b是一颗温暖（约为地球照射的600倍）的土星，位于蒸发荒漠的质量边界。假设其为氢-氦组成，大气尺度高度约为2100 km。在这种情况下，一个尺度高度的透射信号约为420 ppm，使其成为同类行星中通过透射光谱研究系外大气的最佳目标之一。哈勃空间望远镜获得的近红外低分辨率数据（Spake et al. 2020）揭示了系外行星中最强的水带幅度，平均幅度约为800 ppm。此外，解释完整光谱需要考虑云和薄雾的存在。此类在J波段具有高幅度水特征的行星，非常适合用地面可见光和近红外光谱仪进行研究，通过在不同波长测量水含量，分析薄雾和云的影响。使用ESPRESSO观测WASP-127bESPRESSO是一台光纤馈送、超稳定的高分辨率echelle光谱仪，安装在Paranal，可单独收集每个8m单元望远镜（UT）的光，或同时收集甚大望远镜（VLT）4个UT的光。我们在GTO联盟框架下获得了WASP-127b的两次凌日观测。我们在Allart et al. 2020中发表了结果，包括首次测量该系统的自转-轨道对齐，揭示了该系统特殊的轨道结构。年老的母星WASP-127自转缓慢，而其行星轨道为失调的逆行轨道（见图2）。这令人惊讶，因为Teff低于6250K的恒星系统通常应为对齐系统。一种解释可能是WASP-127b曾被外伴星困在Kozai共振中，最近才迁移到靠近母星的位置。WASP-127b大气的观测我们利用ESPRESSO数据分析了该系外行星的透射光谱。可见光高分辨率数据集已知可通过原子物种（如著名的钠双线）揭示系外行星热层。WASP-127b凌日数据集在钠双线预期位置显示出0.34 +/- 0.04%的吸收过量，对应于7个尺度高度的微小扩展。然而，我们未探测到其他原子物种，但能够给出仅为几个尺度高度的上限。我们还对可见波段的水蒸气进行了全面搜索。为此，我们采用了著名的互相关函数技术，在速度空间平均数千条水线。为尽量减少模型依赖性，我们应用了包含不同温度和不同数量水线位置的二进制掩模。我们通过注入-恢复测试展示了该技术对行星信号的敏感性。应用于WASP-127b的凌日数据后，我们对平衡温度下1600条最强水线的平均深度给出了仅38 ppm的3-sigma上限。该上限对应于仅0.08个尺度高度的大气扩展。结合低分辨率和高分辨率光谱基于ESPRESSO获得的独特精度，我们探索了地基高分辨率光谱与空间低分辨率光谱之间的兼容性。我们基于Spake et al. 2020的结果，利用pi-eta逐线辐射传输代码（Pino et al 2018），计算了不同灰云层压力的模型。一方面，灰云层压力在0.3到1 mbar范围的模型与HST数据集兼容，另一方面，只有压力低于0.5 mbar的模型与ESPRESSO数据集兼容。因此，通过结合WASP-127b的低分辨率和高分辨率数据集，我们可以将灰云层压力约束在0.3到0.5 mbar之间。结论我们利用ESPRESSO获得的两次WASP-127b凌日观测，深入理解了该系统的轨道结构和系外行星大气。我们提出了一个新的高分辨率水蒸气及其他分子物种搜索框架。尽管数据质量良好，我们未探测到水蒸气。然而，我们将该结果与1.3微米低分辨率水探测结果结合，约束了WASP-127b大气中云的存在。总之，我们首次报告高分辨率可见光数据可通过测量水含量区分有云和无云系外行星，并可提供关于云层压力的重要信息。此处开发的水含量测量框架将应用于ESPRESSO GTO大气巡天及NIRPS GTO大气巡天等其他巡天的系外行星。参考文献Allart R., Pino, L., Lovis, C. 等, 2020, A&A, 644, A155Pino, L., Ehrenreich, D., Wyttenbach, A., 等. 2018, A&A, 612, A53Spake, J. J., Sing, D. K., Wakeford, H. R., 等. 2020, MNRAS
